### [1] [Agentic schema-guided extraction of materials process knowledge from scientific literature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06322)
*Sameer Sadruddin,Jennifer D'Souza*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SciKGExtract 是一个模式引导（schema-guided）框架，结合大语言模型（large language model, LLM）抽取、化学归一化（chemical normalization）与智能体（agent）评估精炼，用于将材料文献转化为可复用的知识图谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料文献蕴含丰富实验知识，但其流程、化学实体与测量数据以异构形式报道且依赖具体工艺语境，导致难以聚合。

Method: 框架整合 LLM 抽取、化学规范化（PubChem normalization）以及基于智能体的评估与精炼，随后进行知识图谱（knowledge graph）集成；在 176 篇原子层沉积（atomic layer deposition, ALD）论文（ZnO 与 IGZO）及专家标注的全模式子集上评测。

Result: PubChem 归一化提升所有测试模型的精确匹配抽取 F1；ZnO 最佳 F1 由直接归一化抽取的 0.591 提升至智能体精炼后的 0.805，而 IGZO 最佳结果仅 0.344，显示多组分超循环（supercycle）流程难度更大；在含 65 项实验属性与 155 个定量测量节点的深层嵌套模式下，暴露出流程分割（process segmentation）与数值归属错误。

Conclusion: 化学规范化（chemical canonicalization）与针对性智能体验证为将复杂材料文献转化为可复用、机器可执行的实验知识提供了互补性控制手段。

Abstract: Materials literature contains detailed experimental knowledge, but procedures, chemical entities and measurements remain difficult to aggregate because they are reported in heterogeneous forms and depend on process-specific context. We present SciKGExtract, a schema-guided framework that combines large-language-model extraction with chemical normalization and agent-based evaluation and refinement before knowledge-graph integration. We evaluate the framework on 176 atomic-layer-deposition papers describing zinc oxide (ZnO) and indium--gallium--zinc oxide (IGZO), together with an expert-annotated full-schema subset. PubChem normalization improves exact-match extraction F1 for every tested model. For ZnO, the best F1 increases from 0.591 for direct normalized extraction to 0.805 with agentic refinement, whereas the best IGZO result is 0.344, revealing the greater difficulty of multicomponent supercycle processes. Evaluation against a deeply nested schema containing 65 experimental properties and 155 quantitative measurement nodes further exposes errors in process segmentation and numerical assignment. These results show that chemical canonicalization and targeted agentic verification provide complementary controls for converting complex materials literature into reusable, machine-actionable experimental knowledge.

</details>

### [2] [A simple Lagrangian for shallow water equations with constant potential vorticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05904)
*Anton Galajinsky*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文在近期浅水方程（shallow water equations）规范场描述的基础上，针对常位涡（constant potential vorticity）情形构建了一个简洁的拉格朗日表述，其特点在于无需 Clebsch 类参数化或辅助场。研究进一步指出赤道处科里奥利参数（Coriolis parameter）消失时方程具有额外的 SO(2,1) 共形不变性（conformal invariance），并借助群论方法得到与共形群标度对称变换相关的新精确解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机源于近期浅水方程的规范场描述工作，本文希望为常位涡这一特定情形提供更简洁的拉格朗日刻画，并避免传统方法对 Clebsch 型参数化或辅助场的依赖。此外，赤道处科里奥利参数 vanishes 所引出的额外共形对称性也构成研究动因。

Method: 构建适用于常位涡情形的拉格朗日表述（Lagrangian formulation），该构造不诉诸 Clebsch 类参数化或辅助场（auxiliary fields）。在此基础上识别赤道处因科里奥利参数消失而出现的额外 SO(2,1) 共形不变性，并采用群论方法（group-theoretic method）求解。

Result: 所构建的拉格朗日表述具有不依赖 Clebsch 型参数化与辅助场的优点；并确认在赤道（科里奥利参数为零）处浅水方程呈现额外的 SO(2,1) 共形不变性。通过群论方法获得了一个新的精确解，该解与共形群中的标度对称变换（scaling symmetry transformation）相关联。

Conclusion: 常位涡情形的浅水方程可通过无需 Clebsch 参数化与辅助场的简洁拉格朗日形式描述，且赤道处存在额外的 SO(2,1) 共形对称性。该对称性结构可借助群论方法导出新的精确解，表明共形群标度对称性在求解中具有构造性作用。

Abstract: Inspired by a recent gauge field description of shallow water equations [SciPost Phys. 14 (2023) 102], in this work we build a simple Lagrangian formulation for the case of constant potential vorticity. An attractive feature of the construction is that it does not appeal to a Clebsch-like parametrization or the use of auxiliary fields. It is also emphasized that on the equator, where the Coriolis parameter vanishes, the shallow water equations exhibit extra SO(2,1) conformal invariance. Using the group-theoretic method, a new exact solution is found, which is associated with the scaling symmetry transformation entering the conformal group.

</details>


### [3] [Landau damping and mode-selective instability of a vortex gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04841)
*H. Terças,J. L. Figueiredo,J. T,Mendonça*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 点涡气体（point-vortex gas）在无碰撞极限下可视为真正的 Vlasov 系统，可在物理平面上直接导出约化的 Klimontovich 方程与 Vlasov 描述，并预言涡旋罗斯贝类波、朗道阻尼（Landau damping）类比、模式选择机制及束—等离子体不稳定性（beam–plasma instability）类比等集体现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点涡动力学的非正则哈密顿结构（non-canonical Hamiltonian structure）已知，但其无碰撞集体行为的动理学（kinetic theory）框架尚未系统建立。作者旨在不引入辅助动量变量的前提下，直接在物理平面上给出该系统的 Vlasov 描述，以统一解释涡旋集体现象。

Method: 从点涡动力学的非正则哈密顿结构出发，推导约化的 Klimontovich 方程及相应的 Vlasov 描述，全程在物理平面上进行，无需引入辅助动量变量；并借助类比等离子体与自引力系统的方法分析集体模、共振与介质响应。

Result: 理论预言了丰富的集体现象谱：涡旋罗斯贝类波（vortex Rossby-like waves）、全局振荡、共振临界层（resonant critical layers），以及与等离子体和自引力系统类似的介电响应算符。对光滑涡旋分布，差分旋转导致类似朗道阻尼（Landau damping）的机制，使集体模在无碰撞、无耗散下向共振涡旋环带传递能量与角动量。差分旋转还起严格模式选择作用：高斯涡旋云中仅存单个四极准模（quadrupolar quasi-mode），更高多极子均被相位混合（phase mixing）破坏。此外预言了束—等离子体不稳定性（beam–plasma instability）的涡旋对应版本：绕 Onsager 斑块运行的薄涡环可选择性激发 Kelvin 模，其方位对称性由环的粒子数决定。

Conclusion: 结果表明动理学理论是描述涡旋集体动力学的自然框架，并为涡旋输运、涡旋雪崩（vortex avalanches）以及中子星超流体中的旋转突变（rotational glitches）提供了新的机制性解释。

Abstract: We show that a collisionless point-vortex gas behaves as a genuine Vlasov system. Starting from the non-canonical Hamiltonian structure of point-vortex dynamics, we derive a reduced Klimontovich equation and the corresponding Vlasov description directly on the physical plane, without introducing auxiliary momentum variables. The theory predicts a rich spectrum of collective phenomena, including vortex Rossby-like waves, global oscillations, resonant critical layers, and a dielectric response operator analogous to that of plasmas and self-gravitating systems. For smooth vortex distributions, differential rotation gives rise to a vortex analogue of Landau damping, whereby collective modes transfer energy and angular momentum to resonant vortex annuli in the absence of collisions or dissipation. Remarkably, differential rotation acts as a stringent mode-selection mechanism: for a Gaussian vortex cloud, only a single quadrupolar quasi-mode survives, while all higher multipoles are destroyed by phase mixing. We further predict a vortex counterpart of the beam--plasma instability, in which a thin vortex ring orbiting an Onsager patch selectively excites Kelvin modes with an azimuthal symmetry controlled by the ring population. These results establish kinetic theory as a natural framework for collective vortex dynamics and suggest new mechanisms for vortex transport, vortex avalanches, and rotational glitches in neutron-star superfluids.

</details>

### [4] [Quasi-solitons of the 1D Bose gas at arbitrary-strength contact repulsion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04492)
*Anahita Sarvi,Cesare Vianello,Jean-Sébastien Caux*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 利用一维接触排斥玻色气体（Lieb-Liniger 模型）的可积性（integrability），通过多空穴本征态的选择性叠加构造出长寿命、空间局域的类孤子激发（soliton-like excitations），并给出可在热力学极限下无界延长寿命的标度方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是在远离半经典区域、且相互作用无法做微扰处理的强关联区间，获得类经典的激发行为，从而将可积性用于构造任意相互作用强度下的稳定局域激发。

Method: 基于一维接触排斥玻色气体（Lieb-Liniger 模型）的可积性，将多空穴本征态（multi-hole eigenstates）进行选择性叠加来构造密度分布；并通过调节色散宽度（dispersion width）与空穴数目等参数控制其寿命，另提出一种热力学极限下的标度方案（scaling protocol）。

Result: 所构造的密度分布呈现长寿命且空间局域的特征，寿命可通过色散宽度和空穴数等参数调节；所提标度方案原则上可在热力学极限（thermodynamic limit）下实现寿命无界增长。

Conclusion: 该结果表明，类经典激发可以源自幺正量子动力学（unitary quantum dynamics），并出现在半经典区域之外以及相互作用不可做微扰处理的参数区间，凸显可积性在构造长寿命局域激发方面的作用。

Abstract: Harnessing integrability at arbitrary interaction strength, we build long-lived and spatially localized soliton-like excitations in the 1D Bose gas with contact repulsion (Lieb-Liniger model). We show how these density profiles, built from selective superpositions of multi-hole eigenstates, display long lifetimes tunable through parameters like dispersion width and number of holes. We further illustrate a scaling protocol that in principle enables unbounded increase in lifetimes in the thermodynamic limit. Our results showcase classical-like excitations originating from unitary quantum dynamics, away from the semi-classical regime, and in regions where interactions cannot be treated perturbatively.

</details>


### [5] [Rotation Breaks Kibble-Zurek Universality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05934)
*Shiva Dwivedi,Kenichi Kasamatsu,Adolfo Del Campo,Mithun Thudiyangal*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该研究通过随机 Gross-Pitaevskii 模拟发现，势阱旋转会压制二维玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体有限时间淬火中的 Kibble-Zurek（KZ）普适性；旋转诱导的手性偏置使涡旋数趋近 Feynman-Onsager（FO）值且不依赖淬火时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究二维玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体（Bose-Einstein condensate）在有限时间淬火形成时，势阱旋转是否以及如何改变 Kibble-Zurek（KZ）临界动力学普适性。

Method: 采用随机 Gross-Pitaevskii 模拟（stochastic Gross-Pitaevskii simulations），考察旋转与淬火的时间协议对涡旋生成的影响，包括在淬火过程中提升旋转频率，并引入钉扎势（pinning potential）检验涡旋晶格的有序化。

Result: 旋转诱导的手性偏置（chiral bias）使涡旋数接近 Feynman-Onsager（FO）值，且与淬火时间无关；在淬火同时提升旋转时，低频下 KZ 标度（KZ scaling）得以恢复，涡旋数低于 FO 值；涡旋形成无序晶格，钉扎势可将其有序化为 Abrikosov 晶格（Abrikosov lattice），即使在低转速下亦然。

Conclusion: 势阱旋转可作为调控临界动力学（critical dynamics）的控制手段，超越传统 KZ 普适性描述。

Abstract: We show that trap rotation overrides Kibble-Zurek (KZ) universality when a 2D Bose-Einstein condensate forms via a finite-time quench. In stochastic Gross-Pitaevskii simulations, the rotation-induced chiral bias drives the vortex number close to the Feynman-Onsager (FO) value, independent of the quench time. Ramping up rotation with the quench restores the KZ scaling at low frequencies, with vortex numbers below the FO value. The vortices form a disordered lattice that a pinning potential can order into an Abrikosov lattice, even at low rotation. Trap rotation thus offers a control knob for critical dynamics.

</details>


### [6] [Imaginary magnetic fields for ultracold atoms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06793)
*Saulius Alminas,Domantas Burba,Gediminas Juzeliūnas*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该文提出一种利用拉曼耦合（Raman coupling）与态依赖粒子损耗（state-dependent particle losses）为超冷原子产生均匀虚磁场（imaginary magnetic field）的四能级方案，并解析研究了其波包动力学与输运行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在超冷原子体系中实现合成规范场（synthetic gauge field）是量子模拟的重要方向，但均匀虚磁场（imaginary synthetic magnetic field）的产生方案尚待发展，且非厄米（non-Hermitian）效应对原子输运的影响需要刻画。

Method: 采用包含两个损耗辅助态的四能级方案：通过消除损耗辅助态并投影到剩余两个拉曼缀饰态（Raman dressed states）之一，使原子运动受具有恒定旋度（constant curl）的虚矢量势（imaginary vector potential）影响。随后解析推导该合成磁场中原子高斯波包（Gaussian wavepacket）的动力学，并分析其宽度依赖的输运行为。

Result: 解析给出了虚磁场下高斯波包动力学及宽度依赖输运的结果；两能级非厄米哈密顿量（two-level non-Hermitian Hamiltonian）的数值结果与绝热投影（adiabatic projection）描述一致，验证了后者的适用性。

Conclusion: 该四能级方案可生成空间均匀的虚合成磁场，且其绝热投影描述与两能级非厄米哈密顿量在数值上吻合，表明该绝热方法有效，并揭示了非厄米系统中的宽度依赖输运特征。

Abstract: We propose a four-level scheme for generating a uniform imaginary magnetic field for ultracold atoms using Raman coupling and state-dependent particle losses. Eliminating two lossy auxiliary states and subsequently projecting onto one of the two remaining Raman dressed atomic states, the atomic motion is affected by an imaginary vector potential with a constant curl corresponding to a spatially uniform imaginary synthetic magnetic field. We analytically derive the Gaussian wavepacket dynamics for atoms in such a synthetic magnetic field and study the width-dependent transport, which is unique to non-Hermitian systems. We find numerical agreement between the two-level non-Hermitian Hamiltonian and the adiabatic projection description, confirming the applicability of the latter adiabatic approach.

</details>

### [7] [Exact resummation of hydrodynamics for arbitrary elastic scattering integral](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05930)
*Lorenzo Gavassino*

Main category: nucl-th

TL;DR: 该文针对三维空间中单能粒子受外场弹性散射的输运问题，在任意旋转不变散射核（scattering kernel）下推导出频率与波数相关的精确扩散系数（diffusivity），将流体力学梯度展开（hydrodynamic gradient expansion）重求和至所有阶，结果表现为无穷连分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规扩散仅探测碰撞算符的第一个角谐波（angular harmonic），无法反映完整散射核的微观信息；作者希望借助对所有阶梯度项的重求和，保留散射过程的完整细节。

Method: 在三个空间维度中考虑单能粒子的弹性散射，针对任意旋转不变散射核，通过重求和流体力学梯度展开求解含频率与波数依赖的扩散系数；该结果写成无穷连分数，其逐级结构由碰撞算符更高角谐波的弛豫率（relaxation rate）决定。

Result: 扩散系数具有非平凡的解析结构，其微观信息体现在解析结构与对局域外源的响应中；对菲克定律（Fick's law）的偏离随距离指数衰减，衰减率取决于逆扩散系数在复波数平面内最近的奇点。

Conclusion: 对流体力学行为的指数小偏离仍携带关于底层散射过程的详细微观信息，表明重求和后的本构关系（constitutive relation）可超越常规扩散的近似描述。

Abstract: We consider monoenergetic particles propagating in three spatial dimensions and undergoing elastic scattering off an external medium. For an arbitrary rotationally invariant scattering kernel, we derive an exact expression for the frequency- and wave-number-dependent diffusivity, thereby resumming the hydrodynamic gradient expansion to all orders. The result is an infinite continued fraction, with each successive level determined by the relaxation rate of a higher angular harmonic of the collision operator. Thus, while ordinary diffusion probes only the first angular harmonic, the resummed constitutive relation retains information about the full scattering kernel. We show how this microscopic information manifests itself in the analytic structure of the diffusivity and in the response to a localized external source. In particular, deviations from Fick's law decay exponentially with distance, at a rate determined by the nearest complex-wavenumber singularity of the inverse diffusivity. Thus, exponentially small departures from hydrodynamics retain detailed information about the underlying scattering process.

</details>

# cs.CE [[Back]](#toc)

### [8] [AnchorPose for Geometry-Aware MOF Assembly through Meso-Grained Pose Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05843)
*Zhonglong Peng,Rui Jiao,Chang Chen,Geng Zhong,Qiuliang Liu,Shifeng Jin*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: AnchorPose 是一种中尺度（meso-grained）位姿生成框架，通过将几何依赖性纳入生成式表示，从给定构建单元预测金属有机框架（metal-organic framework, MOF）结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从给定构建单元预测 MOF 结构需恢复其在周期晶体中的位置与取向。角度误差的空间效应依赖几何且各向异性（anisotropic），相同角度误差会因单元尺寸、形状和旋转轴不同而产生不同原子位移，故仅凭角度误差无法完整描述位姿误差的空间后果。

Method: AnchorPose 以少量代表性原子表示每个构建单元，将其局部几何与当前空间状态结合，并用贝叶斯流网络（Bayesian Flow Networks）生成这些原子坐标。借助已知的原子对应关系，通过刚性对齐（rigid alignment）恢复完整构建单元位姿，并将几何一致的点返回生成过程，从而连接点级空间预测与单元级结构约束。

Result: 在 MOF 基准（benchmark）上，AnchorPose 相比所对比的单元级（block-level）与全原子（all-atom）基线，提升了单候选匹配率（single-candidate match rates）。

Abstract: We study the spherical collapse of a homogeneous dust cloud on a three-brane in the Shtanov--Sahni braneworld, in which the extra dimension is timelike. Every turning point of the scale factor lies above $\astar=\sqrt{3/4πλ}$, a bounce exists if and only if a simple condition on the comoving density holds, and all curvature invariants stay below bounds fixed by $λ$ alone along the entire cycle. Comoving worldlines are geodesically complete. We then match the cloud across a comoving boundary to a spherically symmetric exterior with a trace-free effective source and solve the Israel conditions in closed form. The exterior is a charged Vaidya geometry whose mass function and tidal charge are fixed by the boundary radius. The tidal charge is positive, opposite in sign to the Randall--Sundrum case, and the exterior cannot be static. A trapped region forms on the boundary if and only if the mass exceeds $2/(3\sqrt{πλ})$, and in that case the bounce still takes place in an untrapped neighbourhood of the boundary. For trapped clouds we construct an exterior over the full contraction, bounce and re-expansion by joining an advanced and a retarded charged Vaidya patch through a static wedge. The matched metric is of class $C^{1,1}$, the effective energy conditions hold, and the cloud re-emerges through a white hole region into a second asymptotic region. In the general relativistic limit $λ\to\infty$ the Oppenheimer--Snyder solution is recovered.

  <summary>Details</summary>

The geometry of $V^*_4(N)$ is determined by the geometry of the three-dimensional homogeneous Riemannian space $V_3(N)$, which is invariant under the action of the three-parameter motions group $G_3(N)$ ($N$ corresponds to the group number in the Bianchi classification). The canonical frame of the space $V_3(N)$ is used to construct the metric tensor of the space $V^*_4(N)$ and the components of the vector potential of the invariant electromagnetic field. Using this reper, we obtain Maxwell vacuum equations containing only the structure constants of the groups, the non-holonomic components of the contravariant metric tensor and vector potential, and their derivatives with respect to the wave variable. All non-equivalent exact solutions of Maxwell vacuum equations are found for the case of solvable motions groups $G_3(I)-G_3(VII)$.
  All solutions of Maxwell vacuum equations obtained in this article are written explicitly in terms of arbitrary independent functions of wave variable. Therefore, when integrating the Einstein-Maxwell equations, it has to consider only the remaining systems of Einstein equations which reduce to systems of ordinary differential equations that include only these independent functions.

</details>

Abstract: Black-hole (BH) quasinormal modes encode the properties of merger remnants and provide powerful tests of strong-field gravity. The BH spectroscopy has predominantly treated them as independent linear oscillations. We establish two structural results. First, at an isolated, nondegenerate intermediate pole, a cubic strain residue factorizes into an excitation amplitude and a quadratic conversion coefficient. This factorization imposes rank constraints across output channels and choices of second-order initial data, providing consistency tests of the intermediate-mode description. Second, the intermediate pole residue can be reconstructed from its horizon and infinity radiation. With the first-order field and third-order data held fixed, this reconstruction extends to the full cubic waveform difference generated by changing the second-order initial data. QCS thus connects lower-order radiation to higher-order observables and provides a framework for probing the interaction structure of nonlinear ringdown.

For the analytic models considered here, the linear scalar spectra depend on the $f(R)$ theory only through the dimensionless combination $μM$. For this purpose, we employ hyperboloidal Rácz--Tóth evolution and a stationary axisymmetric zero-mode computation. The resulting thresholds are compared with the time-domain response at representative points. For $\hatγ=0$, we find two asymmetric threshold branches in $η$, while for $\hatγ=η$, the two branches have opposite signs and unequal magnitudes. Abstract: In this work, we present the Simpson-Visser black-bounce (SV-BB) spacetime with both electric and magnetic charges (the dyonic SV-BB) as an exact solution of general relativity coupled to Maxwell electrodynamics and an anisotropic fluid with vanishing radial pressure. We assume the anisotropic fluid represents an Einstein cluster, which consists of a dense system of particles moving in circular orbits that generate only tangential pressure and zero radial pressure. </details>


### [41] [Cherenkov Bursts are Memory Steps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06528)

TL;DR: 本文提出，有限长超光速电荷径迹（superluminal charge track）产生的切伦科夫辐射（Cherenkov burst）可理解为一对相互干涉的电磁记忆跃迁（electromagnetic memory transitions）。该视角将辐射与记忆效应统一，并为利用阿斯卡里安效应（Askaryan effect）分辨宏观记忆跃迁提供新实验平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，切伦科夫辐射与电磁记忆效应（electromagnetic memory effect）通常被视作彼此独立的现象。作者希望揭示二者之间的内在联系，从而为记忆相关的红外物理（infrared physics）寻找新的实验观测途径。

Method: 以有限长超光速电荷径迹为对象，分析其端点加速（endpoint accelerations）在横向矢势（transverse vector potential）中产生的两个方向相反的记忆阶跃（memory steps），并考察二者在观测者时间（observer time）中的干涉行为随切伦科夫方向（Cherenkov direction）趋近的变化。

Result: 在低于端点加速尺度（endpoint-acceleration-scale）时，相应的有限频辐射由记忆幅度（memory amplitude）与临时记忆偏移（temporary memory offset）的持续时间共同决定。趋近切伦科夫方向时，记忆幅度增大而其在观测者时间中的间隔缩小，最终两个矢势阶跃重叠，其发散贡献相消为有限的切伦科夫辐射。

Conclusion: 该框架表明切伦科夫辐射可视为两个电磁记忆跃迁的干涉结果，并提示可将阿斯卡里安效应作为分辨单个宏观记忆跃迁的实验场景，为记忆相关红外物理开辟新的实验领域。

Abstract: We show that the familiar Cherenkov burst from a finite superluminal charge track can be understood as a pair of interfering electromagnetic memory transitions. Endpoint accelerations of the track produce opposite memory steps in the transverse vector potential, such that the corresponding finite-frequency radiation is universally fixed, below the endpoint-acceleration-scale, by the memory amplitude and the time for which the temporary memory offset persists. As the Cherenkov direction is approached, the memory amplitudes grow while their separation in observer time shrinks, until the two vector-potential steps overlap and their divergent contributions cancel into a finite Cherenkov burst. This perspective opens the door to using the Askaryan effect as a setting in which individual macroscopic memory transitions may be resolved, providing a new experimental arena for memory-linked infrared physics.

</details>

*David Wei*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于借助随机游走编码与随机平面图渗流的对应关系，刻画均匀迂回系统中交叉事件的统计行为，从而理解其临界渗流结构。

Method: 方法上采用数值研究（numerical study），利用均匀迂回系统与简单随机游走对的编码，并在游走编码中通过局部峰/谷翻转（local peak/valley flips）构造一个保律动力学（law-preserving dynamics）。

Result: 结果给出了相反开交叉（opposite open crossings）之间强负相关（strong negative association）的数值证据，并据此提出关于其渐近行为的猜想；模拟显示渗流结构去相关速度远快于底层随机平面图。

Conclusion: 结论指出均匀迂回系统可作为随机平面图上临界渗流的研究框架，其渗流结构相对于底层平面图具有更快的去相关特性，并给出了若干待证猜想。

Abstract: We perform a numerical study of uniform meandric systems via their encoding by pairs of simple random walks and their interpretation as a model of critical percolation on random planar maps. We investigate certain natural box-crossing events and provide numerical evidence for a strong negative association between opposite open crossings and formulate conjectures on their asymptotic behavior. Our second contribution is a simple law-preserving dynamics on meandric systems, by local peak/valley flips in the walk encoding. Our simulations suggest that the percolation structure decorrelates much faster than the underlying random planar map.

</details>


### [43] [Harnessing Measurement Error as a Thermodynamic Resource in Two-Reservoir Information Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03778)
*Yang Xiao,Qian Zeng,Jin Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该工作提出一类双热库信息引擎（two-reservoirs information engines, TRIEs），证明其功输出、效率与功涨落同测量误差（measurement error）之间的单调关系取决于“麦克斯韦妖”（demon）的控制参数，并给出在任意测量误差下仍能产生有用功及同时优化多性能指标的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常认为测量误差（measurement error）会限制信息引擎（information engine）的性能，但这一限制未必普遍成立。本文旨在探究测量误差是否可在某些条件下不损害甚至改善信息引擎性能。

Method: 构建由二能级系统（two-level system）与双热库（two reservoirs）组成的TRIE模型，考查功输出、效率与功涨落随测量误差的变化，并分析妖的控制参数（demon's control parameters）所起的作用；进一步设计双Otto循环（dual Otto cycles）TRIE，并提出基于囚禁$^{40}\mathrm{Ca}^+$离子（trapped ion）的实验实现方案。

Result: 结果显示，上述性能参数与测量误差的单调关系由妖的控制参数决定；据此可确定在任意测量误差水平下产生有用功的条件，以及测量误差可同时优化各性能参数的工作区间。当妖的能耗仅由冷库（cold reservoir）提供时，计入该能耗的效率可超越标准卡诺极限（Carnot bound）而不违反热力学第二定律（second law）。

Conclusion: 测量误差对信息引擎性能的制约并非普遍规律，其影响可由妖的控制参数调控；所提TRIE框架可拓展应用于多能级系统信息引擎（multi-level system information engines）。

Abstract: In general, measurement error constrains the performance of information engines. However, this may not hold universally. In this work, we propose a class of two-reservoirs information engines (TRIEs) with a two-level system and demonstrate that the monotonic relationships between the work output, the efficiency, as well as the work fluctuations of the TRIEs and measurement error are determined by the demon's control parameters. Utilizing this, we establish the conditions that allow the TRIEs to generate useful work under any level of measurement error, and identify the regimes in which these performance parameters can be optimized simultaneously by the measurement error. Furthermore, we demonstrate that when the demon's energy cost is supplied solely by the cold reservoir, the efficiency that accounts for this cost can surpass the standard Carnot bound without violating the second law. Finally, we design a dual Otto cycles TRIE and provide an experimental scheme for its implementation based on a trapped $^{40}\mathrm{Ca}^+$ ion. Significantly, our results can be applied to the multi-level system information engines.

</details>


### [44] [Information Entropy and Dissipation Potential in Stochastic Nambu Nonequilibrium Thermodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03790)
*So Katagiri,Tatsuaki Wada*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文在随机 Nambu 非平衡热力学（NNET）框架下，论证耗散势 $S_{\mathrm{NB}}$ 并非 Shannon 熵本身，而是支配非平衡稳态分布的平稳准势（stationary quasipotential）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于厘清 NNET 中耗散势 $S_{\mathrm{NB}}$ 与信息熵之间的关系，明确其统计物理含义。作者指出 $S_{\mathrm{NB}}$ 不应被等同于 Shannon 熵，而需赋予其平稳准势的角色。

Method: 方法上，从纯扩散、梯度流系统（gradient-flow systems）以及含 Hamiltonian 旋转流的系统出发，推导包含 Nambu 流的 Fokker--Planck 方程的平稳分布；并借助开放气体-活塞系统（gas--piston system）与热浴及外压库耦合的案例，区分热力学熵与耗散势。

Result: 结果显示，当 Nambu 流保持耗散势不变、且扩散矩阵与输运张量之间满足 Einstein 型关系（Einstein-type relation）时，平稳分布由 $S_{\mathrm{NB}}$ 决定的指数权重支配，局部自信息因而直接与耗散势相关。进一步给出 $S_{\mathrm{NB}}$ 的 Massieu 解释，引入 Nambu 正则分布（Nambu canonical distribution），并在鞍点近似（saddle-point approximation）下推导涨落的协方差。

Conclusion: 结论将 NNET 的耗散势识别为非平衡稳态（nonequilibrium steady states）下的平稳准势，从而为其提供统计解释。该工作澄清了 $S_{\mathrm{NB}}$ 与 Shannon 熵的区别，并给出其 Massieu 与正则分布层面的含义。

Abstract: We investigate the relationship between the dissipation potential $S_{\mathrm{NB}}$ and information entropy in stochastic Nambu nonequilibrium thermodynamics (NNET). Starting from pure diffusion, gradient-flow systems, and systems with Hamiltonian rotational currents, we derive the stationary distribution of the Fokker--Planck equation including a Nambu flow. We show that, when the Nambu flow preserves the dissipation potential and an Einstein-type relation holds between the diffusion matrix and the transport tensor, the stationary distribution is governed by an exponential weight determined by $S_{\mathrm{NB}}$. Consequently, the local self-information is directly related to the dissipation potential, demonstrating that $S_{\mathrm{NB}}$ should not be identified with the Shannon entropy itself but instead serves as the stationary quasipotential governing the nonequilibrium steady-state distribution. Using the distinction between the thermodynamic entropy and the dissipation potential established for an open gas--piston system coupled to a thermal bath and an external-pressure reservoir, we further clarify the Massieu interpretation of $S_{\mathrm{NB}}$, introduce a Nambu canonical distribution, and derive the covariance of fluctuations within the saddle-point approximation. These results provide a statistical interpretation of the NNET dissipation potential by identifying it as the stationary quasipotential underlying nonequilibrium steady states.

</details>


### [45] [An Absorbing State Model with an Emergent Ising Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03936)
*Shen Ai,Dov Levine,Paul Chaikin*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究正方格子上中心固定的排斥性针状体（repulsive needles）动力学模型，系统呈现吸收态相变（absorbing state transition），并在一定针长与转角参数范围内自组织出棋盘对称相；该相随参数增大熔化，且相界临界指数与平衡态二维伊辛模型（2D Ising model）一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究非平衡动力学系统中是否存在与平衡态临界行为等价的普适类，尤其是格点上几何受限的排斥性各向异性粒子模型的集体行为与相变性质。

Method: 构建针状体中心固定于正方格子的动力学模型：发生重叠的针为“活跃”并接受 $θ\in[0,ε)$ 的排斥性转动，无重叠的针保持静止；通过调节针长与 $ε$ 考察系统的稳态、序参量与相变行为。

Result: 模型表现出吸收态相变；长针下观察到向列序（nematic order）；在特定针长与 $ε$ 范围内系统自组织形成棋盘对称（checkerboard symmetry）态；增大 $ε$ 时该相熔化并转为无序相；该相界展示出与平衡态二维伊辛模型完全相同的临界指数（critical exponents）。

Conclusion: 该非平衡针状体动力学模型中的自组织棋盘相到无序相的转变属于与平衡态二维伊辛模型相同的普适类，表明非平衡几何受限系统可再现平衡临界普适行为。

Abstract: We study a dynamical model of repulsive needles with centers fixed on a square lattice. Needles that overlap are active and receive a repulsive rotation $ θ\in [0,ε)$; needles with no overlap do not move. This model exhibits an absorbing state transition. Nematic order was observed for long needles. For a range of needle lengths and $ε$, the system self-organizes into a state with checkerboard symmetry. As $ε$ is increased, this phase melts and becomes disordered. Remarkably, we find that this phase boundary exhibits critical exponents identical to those of the equilibrium 2D Ising model.

Abstract: Nonreciprocal interactions have emerged as a fundamental driver of collective behavior far from equilibrium. Here, we show that nonreciprocal couplings between spin systems whose isolated dynamics favors ordered states, beyond the previously identified oscillatory (swap) phase, give rise to a richer nonequilibrium phenomenology, including a multistable regime where ordered and disordered states coexist, a scenario strictly forbidden in equilibrium. Crucially, we show that this nonequilibrium phenomenology is inherited directly from the critical properties of the underlying isolated model. In particular, tricriticality in the isolated system gives rise to multistability, hysteresis, and hard excitations in the oscillatory dynamics, while non-normal interactions further enrich the phase diagram by generating multistability. Moving beyond mean-field theory, we derive a universal field equation governing the onset of these nonreciprocal oscillations in finite dimensions. Our results establish a unified theoretical framework connecting critical phenomena, dynamical systems, and non-reciprocal collective dynamics, with applications ranging from opinion dynamics to active matter

Result: 数值结果显示原模型相变实为混合渗流相变，兼具连续相变特征（临界簇发散）与一阶相变特征（不连续性）；改为各向同性后该混合相变依然存在；引入额外局域随机移动以消除吸收态后，一阶相变被强烈抑制或消除，仅留下表观连续的渗流相变，但所观测到的临界指数在各模型间变化不显著。

Abstract: We investigate a class of dynamic percolation models on a lattice, in which connections are added and removed by a set of local stochastic rules. In particular, the removal of any connection that can result in one connected cluster becoming two (or more) separate clusters is strictly forbidden. These models include and are inspired by a model for local quantum error correction for a toric code studied by Chirame, et al., PRX Quantum 6, 030363 (2025), who identified a phase transition with first-order characteristics, such as bistability and a discontinuous order parameter. We show numerically that this transition is a hybrid percolation transition (HPT), having characteristics of a continuous percolation transition such as diverging critical clusters while at the same transition having discontinuities like a first-order transition. We then modify this model to be fully isotropic, showing that the HPT remains. In both of these models, the steady state on one side of the HPT is an absorbing state; by turning on additional isotropic local stochastic moves, we remove the absorbing state and strongly suppress or remove the first-order transition, leaving an apparently continuous percolation transition. Curiously, the numerically-observed percolation critical exponents do not change significantly between the models with hybrid transitions and the model with an apparently continuous transition. These exponents are significantly different from known percolation transitions, indicating that this may be a new universality class.

Conclusion: 结果为代谢动力学中的双稳态与突变切换（abrupt switching）提供了一种可能的机制。

Abstract: We generalize the Furusawa--Kaneko model, a simple model of intracellular catalytic reaction networks, to multicatalyst reactions and analyze the generalized model using dynamical mean-field theory. In the thermodynamic and dense-network limit, we derive exact effective equations and analyze their fixed points for arbitrary degree distributions. Under nutrient-poor conditions, network heterogeneity suppresses the metabolic--starvation transition irrespective of the number of catalysts per reaction. In contrast, under nutrient-rich conditions, the bifurcation structure changes with the number of catalysts. With one catalyst per reaction, only continuous transitions occur, whereas two or more catalysts give rise to bistable phases and discontinuous transitions. The bifurcation structure also differs between two and three catalysts per reaction, with three catalysts allowing bistability between metabolic states with different cell growth rates. Our results provide a possible mechanism for bistability and abrupt switching in metabolic dynamics.

</details>


### [51] [Parisi's solution of the Sherrington--Kirkpatrick model from synchronous Monte Carlo dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05557)
*Yoshiyuki Kabashima*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文在离散时间动力学框架下（所有 Ising 自旋同步更新，即 SyncMC），重做了 Sompolinsky–Zippelius 从弛豫动力学推导 SK 模型 Parisi 解的程序，证明其长时间结构与 Parisi 方程一致，并通过数值模拟支持 Parisi 解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sompolinsky 与 Zippelius 曾通过连续时间软自旋弛豫动力学、在时间尺度广泛分离假设下得到 Sherrington-Kirkpatrick（SK）模型的 Parisi 解；本文旨在将这一程序推广到离散时间、所有 Ising 自旋同时更新的动力学情形。

Result: SyncMC 的关联与响应函数在平衡态离散时间下满足精确的涨落-耗散关系（fluctuation-dissipation relation）。其 SK 模型动力学平均场方程比连续时间情形多出两项——每个自旋的自耦合及通过耦合关联的噪声，二者仅影响快弛豫。长时间结构重现了 Parisi 方程，包括 Parisi 偏微分方程与局域场分布方程。复制交换（replica exchange）模拟（至 N=2048）排除复本对称的局域场分布，与 Parisi 解一致；由重叠分布及随机构型弛豫响应估计的 x(q)（至 N=4096）亦与 Parisi 结果一致。

Conclusion: 对离散时间同步动力学，只要假设时间尺度广泛分离、且在每一慢尺度上满足带约化比值 x 的相同关系，其长时间行为即重现 Parisi 解；数值模拟支持 Parisi 解并排除复本对称解。

Abstract: Sompolinsky and Zippelius obtained Parisi's solution of the Sherrington-Kirkpatrick (SK) model from the relaxational dynamics of soft spins in continuous time, by assuming a hierarchy of widely separated time scales. Here we carry out the same programme for a discrete-time dynamics in which all Ising spins are updated at once. This dynamics, synchronous Monte Carlo (SyncMC), introduces Gaussian auxiliary fields so that the spins become conditionally independent, and it samples the Boltzmann distribution exactly for any system with symmetric pairwise couplings. We show that, for any such system, the correlation and response functions of SyncMC obey an exact fluctuation-dissipation relation in discrete time in equilibrium. The dynamical mean-field equations of SyncMC for the SK model contain two terms absent in continuous-time dynamics, a self-coupling of each spin and a noise correlated through the couplings; both affect only the fast relaxation. As a result, under the same assumptions as in the Sompolinsky-Zippelius construction (widely separated time scales, and on each slow scale the same relation with a reduced ratio x, as expected in relaxation from random initial conditions), the long-time structure of SyncMC reproduces the Parisi equations, including the Parisi partial differential equation and the equation for the distribution of local fields. Equilibrium simulations with replica exchange for up to N=2048 spins exclude the replica-symmetric distribution of the actual local fields and are consistent with the Parisi solution. The function x(q) estimated from the overlap distribution, and from the response in relaxation from random configurations for up to N=4096 spins, is also consistent with Parisi's.

</details>


### [52] [Confined Riesz gas: From Newtonian Dynamics to Emergent Hydrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06626)
*Indranil Mukherjee,Abhishek Dhar,Manas Kulkarni*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文结合大规模牛顿动力学模拟与含密度、速度、温度三个粗粒化场的水动力学框架，定量验证了Riesz气体（Riesz gas, RG）中微观动力学与宏观演化的对应关系，并证明该涌现水动力描述在较宽的体黏度与热导率参数窗口内具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Riesz气体是一类具有幂律相互作用的典型多体经典系统，其平衡态性质已被广泛研究，但微观动力学、宏观演化与热化（thermalization）之间的联系仍基本未被探索。

Method: 将大规模牛顿模拟（Newtonian simulations）与基于密度、速度、温度三个粗粒化场的水动力学（hydrodynamic）理论相结合，后者引入体黏度（bulk viscosity）ξ与热导率（thermal conductivity）κ两个唯象输运系数以刻画宏观耗散效应；并针对圆顶型（dome-like）与牛顿摆型（Newton-cradle-type）等代表性初始条件进行分析。

Result: 从相对早期直至达到稳态的广泛时间尺度上，微观演化与水动力学演化均表现出良好一致性；该一致性在相当宽的ξ与κ参数窗口内持续存在，说明涌现水动力描述的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 结果系统性地验证了Riesz气体中水动力学的有效性，并从第一性原理出发建立了幂律相互作用系统中牛顿动力学与连续介质理论之间的具体桥梁。

Abstract: We investigate the real-time dynamics of the Riesz gas (RG), a paradigmatic system of particles with power-law interactions that encapsulates several many-body interacting classical systems. While the equilibrium properties of the system have been extensively studied the connection between microscopic dynamics, macroscopic evolution and thermalization has remained largely unexplored. Combining extensive large-scale Newtonian simulations with a hydrodynamic framework formulated in terms of three coarse grained fields -- density, velocity, and temperature -- we establish a direct and quantitative correspondence between the two descriptions. The hydrodynamic theory incorporates two phenomenological transport coefficients, the bulk viscosity $ξ$ and thermal conductivity $κ$, which encode dissipative effects at a macroscopic level. By analyzing representative classes of initial conditions, such as dome-like and Newton-cradle-type profiles, we find excellent agreement between microscopic and hydrodynamic evolution over a broad range of timescales, starting from relatively early all the way up to times at which steady state is attained. Notably, this agreement persists across a substantial window of $ξ$ and $κ$, demonstrating the robustness of the emergent hydrodynamic description. Our results provide a systematic validation of hydrodynamics in the RG and establish a concrete bridge between Newtonian dynamics and continuum theories in systems with power-law interactions, starting from first principles.

</details>

Abstract: In this paper we use machine learning methods to predict radiation damage in the lead tungstate crystals of the CMS electromagnetic calorimeter at the Large Hadron Collider. We analyze LHC Open Data collected from 2016 to 2018 and study the time evolution of the crystal optical transparency. We apply deep neural network models to predict its behavior over different future time intervals, and find that encoder-decoder sequence-to-sequence architectures can effectively describe crystal aging.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 塑料闪烁体因快响应、机械柔性和制备简便，仍是大面积粒子探测器的理想选择。受 ALICE 3 Muon Identifier（MID）与 ALICE Fast Interaction Trigger（FIT）升级等未来应用驱动，需要研究 PU–PPO–POPOP 体系，但该材料体系的优化与辐射响应仍缺乏系统信息。

Method: 制备了一系列不同配比的 PU 基塑料闪烁体样品，采用最小电离粒子（MIP）、光谱学（optical spectroscopy）和时间相关单光子计数（time-correlated single-photon counting）进行表征；并将最佳配方放大为 20 cm 与 1 m 闪烁条，配合单根嵌入式波长移位（wavelength-shifting）光纤与 SiPM 读出进行测试，另在 CERN IRRAD 设施开展质子辐照测量。

Result: 测量表明闪烁响应主要由 PPO 浓度主导，POPOP 主要改变光谱响应，掺杂样品保持约 1.6–1.75 ns 的快有效衰减时间。最佳配方放大至 20 cm 与 1 m 闪烁条后可成功运行，验证了米级光收集的可行性。质子辐照显示光学与闪烁响应随累积剂量系统性退化，并在约两个月后出现显著恢复。

Conclusion: 优化后的 PU–PPO–POPOP 闪烁体可兼具快时间响应、高光产额、可扩展探测器几何结构与可恢复的辐射响应，支持其在未来大面积粒子探测器系统中的进一步开发。

Abstract: Plastic scintillators remain attractive for large-area particle detectors because of their fast response, mechanical flexibility, and relatively simple fabrication. Motivated by future applications including the ALICE~3 Muon Identifier (MID) and the upgrade of the ALICE Fast Interaction Trigger (FIT), this work investigates polyurethane (PU)-based plastic scintillators doped with 2,5-diphenyloxazole (PPO) and 1,4-bis(5-phenyloxazol-2-yl)benzene (POPOP). Systematic information on the optimization and radiation response of this material system remains limited. A series of compositions was therefore fabricated and characterized using minimum-ionizing particles (MIP), optical spectroscopy, and time-correlated single-photon counting. The measurements show that the scintillation response is governed primarily by the PPO concentration, while POPOP mainly modifies the spectral response, and fast effective decay times of approximately 1.6--1.75~ns are maintained across the doped samples. The best-performing formulation was subsequently scaled to 20-$cm$ and 1-m scintillator bars and successfully operated with a single embedded wavelength-shifting fiber and SiPM readout, demonstrating its suitability for meter-scale light collection. Proton-irradiation measurements at the CERN IRRAD facility further revealed systematic degradation of the optical and scintillation responses with increasing exposure, together with substantial recovery after approximately two months. These results demonstrate that optimized PU--PPO--POPOP scintillators can combine fast timing, high light output, scalable detector geometry, and recoverable radiation response, supporting their further development for future large-area particle-detector systems.

</details>

### [55] [Origin of the inhomogeneous linewidth in a solid state $^{229}$Th:CaF$_2$ nuclear clock](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04205)
*Kai Li,Tian Ooi,Jack F. Doyle,Konstantin Herb,Emil Pellett,Martin Pimon,Thorsten Schumm,Steven M. Girvin,Leo Radzihovsky,Jun Ye*

Main category: physics.atom-ph

TL;DR: 该研究系统测量了 $^{229}$Th:CaF$_2$ 中跃迁线宽随温度、钍浓度与四极态的变化，确认局域电场梯度（EFG）起伏导致的非均匀展宽为主导机制，并揭示 EFG 无序随钍浓度线性增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固态 $^{229}$Th 核钟的实现依赖窄线宽（高 Q 值）量子鉴别器，但实验观测到的跃迁线宽为数十至数百千赫兹，远高于核同质异能素辐射寿命所决定的亚赫兹线宽，其展宽来源尚不明确。

Method: 对 $^{229}$Th:CaF$_2$ 进行系统测量，考察线宽随温度、钍浓度与四极态（quadrupole state）的依赖关系，并结合理论分析与第一性原理（first-principle）模拟，包括原子尺度与密度泛函理论（density-functional-theory, DFT）计算。

Result: 线宽与温度无关，随钍浓度线性增加，且强烈依赖四极态；数据分析表明局域电场梯度（electric-field gradient, EFG）的变化是非均匀展宽的主导来源，EFG 无序随钍浓度线性增加；洛伦兹线型（Lorentzian line shape）与线宽的线性浓度依赖可由掺杂诱导的长程扰动随机分布的 $1/r^3$ 标度自然解释；原子尺度与从头计算复现了这些特征。

Conclusion: 结果确立了固态 $^{229}$Th 中的主导展宽机制，并指出其微观缺陷构型来源，为工程化设计具有更低非均匀展宽的核钟材料提供指导。

Abstract: Narrow linewidth (high-Q) quantum discriminators are essential for realizing solid-state $^{229}$Th nuclear clocks, yet the observed transition linewidths remain at the tens-to-hundreds-of-kilohertz level, far exceeding the sub-Hertz linewidth set by the radiative lifetime of the nuclear isomer. Here, we investigate the origin of this broadening in $^{229}$Th:CaF$_2$ through systematic measurements of the linewidth as functions of temperature, thorium concentration, and quadrupole state, accompanied by theoretical analysis and first-principle simulations. We find that the linewidth is independent of temperature, increases linearly with thorium concentration, and depends strongly on the quadrupole state. Data analysis identifies variations of the local electric-field gradient (EFG) as the dominant source of inhomogeneous broadening and reveals that the EFG disorder increases linearly with thorium concentration. The observed Lorentzian line shapes and linewidth linear concentration dependence are explained naturally from the $1/r^3$ scaling of a random distribution of long-range dopant-induced perturbations. Complementary atomistic and \textit{ab initio} density-functional-theory calculations reproduce these characteristic features and provide insights into the microscopic defect configurations responsible for the broadening. These results establish the dominant broadening mechanism in solid-state $^{229}$Th and provide guidance for engineering nuclear-clock materials with reduced inhomogeneous broadening.

</details>


### [56] [Onset of Melting in Finite Ion Crystals: The Role of Structural Isomerization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05624)
*Boris V. Pashinsky,Boris Blinov*

Main category: physics.atom-ph

TL;DR: 该研究基于分子动力学（molecular dynamics）与Metropolis-Hastings平衡采样，系统分析N=4至N=101的有限二维离子晶体熔化机制，揭示结构异构体（structural isomer）对熔化路径与非均匀熔化概率的决定性影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有工作已研究熔化概率随温度与各向异性（anisotropy）的变化，但结构异构化（structural isomerization）如何改变熔化路径尚不清晰，需在更宽的晶体尺寸范围内建立统一的微观熔化图像。

Method: 采用分子动力学模拟结合Metropolis-Hastings平衡采样，分析各向异性势阱中库仑晶体（Coulomb crystals）的径向涨落、角度无序、Lindemann参数以及依赖于异构体的能量地形（energy landscape）。

Result: 相同粒子数与势阱各向异性的晶体，因所处的亚稳态结构异构体不同而表现出显著不同的熔化行为；熔化并非简单的软模失稳（soft-mode instability），而是径向与角度无序逐渐热驱动的累积过程，结构异构化重塑涨落通道，导致熔化概率在温度–各向异性空间中非均匀分布。

Conclusion: 研究提供了有限离子晶体熔化的统一微观图像，并阐明结构重排（structural rearrangement）在有限尺寸相变（finite-size phase transitions）中的作用。

Abstract: We theoretically investigate the microscopic mechanism of melting in finite two-dimensional ion crystals confined in anisotropic traps. Building on our previous studies of melting probability as a function of temperature and anisotropy, we extend the analysis to crystal sizes ranging from $N=4$ to $N=101$ ions and examine how structural isomerization modifies the melting pathways. Using molecular dynamics simulations together with Metropolis-Hastings equilibrium sampling, we analyze radial fluctuations, angular disorder, Lindemann parameters, and isomer-dependent energy landscapes in these Coulomb crystals. We find that crystals with identical particle number and trap anisotropy can exhibit substantially different melting behavior depending on the metastable structural isomer they occupy. Contrary to a simple soft-mode instability picture, the observed melting process is characterized by a gradual thermally-driven accumulation of radial and angular disorder. Structural isomerization reshapes the fluctuation channels through which delocalization develops, leading to nonuniform melting probabilities across temperature and anisotropy space. These results provide a unified microscopic picture of melting in finite ion crystals and clarify the role of structural rearrangements in finite-size phase transitions.

</details>


### [57] [Bifurcation of laser-dressed band extrema in solids under intense low-frequency fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05895)
*Alexander Flegel,Liang-Wen Pi,Liang-You Peng,Mikhail Frolov*

Main category: physics.atom-ph

TL;DR: 强低频激光场可定性改变晶体带间能隙的局部极值结构：在临界场强下能隙发生分岔，单一对称极小值分裂为两个对称相关极小值并被局域极大值分隔；该机制源于多带带间极化重分布引起的场修饰带曲率贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在揭示强场修饰（strong-field dressing）如何改变电子能谱的局部几何结构，即激光场对晶体带间能隙极值结构的影响机制。作者关注该效应是否具有普遍性，及其对光谱响应的可观测后果。

Method: 基于多带晶体哈密顿量的非微扰对角化构造瞬时激光修饰基（instantaneous laser-dressed basis），并在高对称点附近推导修饰能隙的四次正规形（quartic normal form）。通过将二次部分分解为均匀斯塔克贡献（homogeneous Stark contribution）与场修饰带曲率贡献（field-modified band-curvature contribution）来辨识分岔的微观起源，并以 Kronig--Penney 模型进行验证。

Result: 在临界场强下正规形二次部分变号，能隙出现分岔：单一对称极小值分裂为两个对称相关极小值，其间由局域极大值分隔。该分岔的机制来自多带带间极化重分布所提供的场修饰带曲率贡献。Kronig--Penney 模型在多个高对称点、不同带间能隙下均重现同一分岔，并在吸收边之上产生额外的范霍夫特征（van Hove feature）。

Conclusion: 研究确立了一种普遍机制：强场修饰可修改电子能谱的局部几何结构，并可通过范霍夫特征等光谱信号加以体现。

Abstract: We show that an intense low-frequency laser field can qualitatively modify the local extremal structure of an interband energy gap in a crystal. Using an instantaneous laser-dressed basis obtained from the nonperturbative diagonalization of the multiband crystal Hamiltonian, we derive a quartic normal form for the dressed gap near a high-symmetry point. At a critical field, the quadratic part of this normal form changes sign, and the gap experiences a bifurcation: a single symmetric minimum splits into two symmetry-related minima separated by a local maximum. We identify the microscopic origin of this bifurcation by decomposing the quadratic part into a homogeneous Stark contribution and a field-modified band-curvature contribution. The latter originates from the multiband redistribution of interband polarizations and provides the mechanism for the field-induced change of the gap extrema. A realization for the Kronig--Penney model demonstrates the same bifurcation at multiple high-symmetry points and for different interband gaps. As an illustration of its spectral consequences, we show that the bifurcation gives rise to an additional van Hove feature in the instantaneous interband susceptibility above the absorption edge. Our results establish a general mechanism by which strong-field dressing modifies the local geometry of the electronic spectrum.

</details>

### [58] [A proof of GW/DT4 conjecture on local projective plane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06024)
*Yalong Cao*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该论文证明了一维稳定层（stable sheaves）在 $\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}^2}(-1,-2)$ 全空间上的 DT4 不变量（DT4 invariants）对任意次数曲线类（degree curve classes）的闭公式，并据此验证了作者与 Maulik、Toda 提出的一个猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在为一维稳定层的 DT4 不变量给出闭公式，并通过与 Klemm-Pandharipande 提出并计算的 Gopakumar-Vafa 型不变量（Gopakumar-Vafa type invariants）相结合，验证作者、Maulik 与 Toda 提出的猜想。

Method: 在证明过程中，模空间（moduli spaces）的定向（orientations）以规范方式（canonical way）选取；对 $\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}^2}(-1,-2)$ 全空间上的一维稳定层，针对所有次数曲线类推导 DT4 不变量的闭公式。

Result: 得到了一维稳定层在 $\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}^2}(-1,-2)$ 全空间上 DT4 不变量的闭公式，适用于所有次数曲线类；该结果与 Gopakumar-Vafa 型不变量结合后，验证了所提猜想。

Conclusion: 论文通过闭公式与 Gopakumar-Vafa 型不变量的结合，完成了对作者、Maulik 和 Toda 所提猜想的验证。

Abstract: We prove a closed formula for DT4 invariants of one dimensional stable sheaves on the total space of $\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}^2}(-1,-2)$ for all degree curve classes. During the proof, orientations of moduli spaces are chosen in a canonical way. Combining with Gopakumar-Vafa type invariants proposed and computed by Klemm-Pandharipande, we verify a conjecture proposed by the author, Maulik and Toda.

</details>

### [59] [Reinforcement Learning on the Discrete Composition Channel of a Crystal Generator: Validated Gains and Reward Hacking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03880)
*Pawan Prakash,Philipp Höllmer,Addis Fuhr,Peter Hirschfeld,P. Ganesh,Stefano Martiniani,Richard Hennig*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作将组相对策略优化（group-relative policy optimization, GRPO）引入基于随机插值（stochastic interpolants）与离散流匹配（discrete flow matching）的晶体生成模型，通过强化学习对齐黑盒奖励函数。所提奖励将亚稳、独特且新颖结构产率（mSUN）由 13.4% 提升至 45.5%，同时揭示奖励被利用风险与社区指标的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆向材料设计（inverse materials design）是计算材料发现的长期目标，但现有晶体生成模型的训练目标仅拟合结构数据库分布，并未指向特定设计目标（如目标性质）。因此需要一种能将生成模型与通用黑盒奖励对齐的强化学习机制。

Method: 采用组相对策略优化（GRPO）对基于随机插值（stochastic interpolants）和离散流匹配（discrete flow matching）的生成模型进行强化学习对齐；原子类型由离散流生成，其广义 GRPO 的策略梯度直接作用于原子类型转移的似然（likelihoods of atom-type transitions），这一点区别于以往面向扩散与流模型的强化学习方法。同时引入以提升亚稳、独特且新颖结构产率（mSUN）为目标的奖励函数。

Result: 在社区基准（community benchmark）评估下，mSUN 由预训练模型的 13.4% 提升至强化模型的 45.5%；该奖励同样提升了基于潜在去噪扩散模型（latent denoising diffusion models）的晶体材料强化学习框架的性能。此外发现，直接强化原子类型转移似然会导致奖励被利用（reward exploitation），需以显式防护（explicit guards）加以阻止。

Conclusion: 分析同时暴露了社区指标的缺口：不同堆积方式（packings）下的单元素结构被计为亚稳、独特且新颖材料，从而虚增 mSUN 却不产生任何新化合物。稳定性声明取决于其参考凸包（reference hull），因此所有结果均按背后参考相（reference phases）数量分组报告，并主张基准评测也应采用同样做法。

Abstract: Inverse materials design is a long-standing goal of computational materials discovery. Generative models for crystalline materials are typically trained to match the distribution of a structure database, while nothing in their training objective points them at specific design goals such as targeted properties. We use group-relative policy optimization (GRPO) to align a generative model based on stochastic interpolants and discrete flow matching with general black-box reward functions through reinforcement learning. Atom types are generated by a discrete flow and the policy gradient of our generalization of GRPO directly acts on the likelihoods of the atom-type transitions, which differentiates our work from previous reinforcement-learning approaches for diffusion and flow-based generative models of crystalline materials. We introduce a reward function that raises the yield of metastable, unique and novel structures (mSUN) from 13.4% for the pretrained model to 45.5% for the reinforced model, as evaluated by a community benchmark. Our reward also improves the performance of a reinforcement learning framework for crystalline materials based on latent denoising diffusion models. At the same time, we find that directly reinforcing atom-type transition likelihoods enables reward exploitation that has to be prevented with explicit guards. The same analysis also exposes a gap in the community metric. Single-element structures in distinct packings are counted as metastable, unique and novel materials and inflate mSUN without yielding any new compounds. A stability claim is only as good as its reference hull. We report every result split by the number of reference phases behind it and argue that benchmarks should do the same.

</details>


### [60] [Probabilistic Algorithms for Ising Machines from Optimization to Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03972)
*Corentin Delacour,Xiuqi Zhang,Abdelrahman S. Abdelrahman,Saleh Bunaiyan,Kyle Lee,Shuvro Chowdhury,Kerem Y. Camsari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该综述从算法与硬件协同设计（co-design）视角，系统梳理面向多种伊辛平台（Ising platforms）的可移植概率算法（probabilistic algorithms），并展望生成式人工智能（generative AI）与伊辛机器的相互促进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伊辛机器（Ising machines）被视为求解难解优化与采样问题的硬件加速器，但其实际影响力日益取决于算法与硬件的协同设计；算法需求塑造新架构，新硬件能力又催生全新算法。因此需要以自顶向下（top-down）的视角，梳理具备可证明保证（provable guarantees）的算法方法。

Method: 采用综述（Review）形式，聚焦可跨不同伊辛平台移植的概率算法。涵盖模拟退火（simulated annealing）、并行回火（parallel tempering）等基础方法及其可原生编码硬约束（hard constraints）的二维扩展；并考察从团簇平均场方法（cluster mean-field methods）到变分采样器（variational samplers）等扩展可解问题规模的方法。

Result: 重点介绍了概率近似优化算法（Probabilistic Approximate Optimization Algorithm, PAOA）——一种直接源自概率硬件发展的 QAOA 经典类比；并探讨生成式 AI 与伊辛机器的相互强化机制：学习模型提出全局移动（global moves）以加速优化，而概率技术改善大语言模型（large language models）中的推断。

Conclusion: 正如量子计算（quantum computing）中算法与硬件的共同演化，概率计算与伊辛计算正处于类似的转折点。文章提出了一个协同设计框架，以加速下一代伊辛机器能力的提升与采用。

Abstract: Ising machines have emerged as promising hardware accelerators for intractable optimization and sampling problems, yet their practical impact increasingly hinges on the co-design of algorithms and hardware, where algorithmic demands shape new architectures and new hardware capabilities inspire entirely new algorithms. In this Review, we survey probabilistic algorithms designed for portability across diverse Ising platforms, advocating a top-down perspective that prioritizes principled methods with provable guarantees. We cover foundational methods such as simulated annealing and parallel tempering, including two-dimensional extensions that natively encode hard constraints, and examine approaches that expand the scale of solvable problems from cluster mean-field methods to variational samplers. We highlight the Probabilistic Approximate Optimization Algorithm (PAOA), a classical analog of QAOA that emerged directly from probabilistic hardware development, and explore how generative AI and Ising machines might reinforce each other: learned models propose global moves to accelerate optimization, while probabilistic techniques improve inference in large language models. Much as quantum computing has seen algorithms co-evolve with hardware, probabilistic and Ising computing stand at a similar inflection point. We outline a co-design framework for accelerating the capabilities and adoption of next-generation Ising machines.

</details>

### [61] [How dust particles orbiting white dwarfs can reveal undetected co-orbiting exoplanets II. Exoplanets on elliptic orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04358)
*Kyriaki I. Antoniadou,Dimitri Veras*

Main category: astro-ph.EP

TL;DR: 本文（Paper II）将 Paper I 的研究扩展到次天体轨道偏心的情形，分析椭圆限制性三体问题（elliptic restricted three-body problem, ERTBP）。作者计算了二维与三维 ERTBP 中 1/1 共振的对称与非对称周期轨道族，发现多条新非对称轨道族，并通过 N 体模拟（N-body simulation）探讨其与白矮星（white dwarf, WD）附近碎片的观测联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在把 Paper I 的结果推广到次天体轨道偏心的情形，即椭圆限制性三体问题（ERTBP）。所选取的次天体质量涵盖谷神星（Ceres）、水星、地球与海王星，以便据此推断大质量小行星与行星的存在。该分析同时面向白矮星（WD）附近碎片观测的推断需求。

Method: 在平面（2D-ERTBP）与非共面（3D-ERTBP）两种情形下研究尘埃粒子，计算 1/1 共振的对称与非对称周期轨道族，并据此生成详细相图（phase portrait）。最后运行设定合适初始条件的 N 体模拟（N-body simulation），与周期轨道结果进行比对。

Result: 在高偏心率 2D-ERTBP 区域发现新非对称周期轨道族 E^A_41、E^A_42、E^A_43，由 Paper I 中新 A_2 族上的新分岔点（bifurcation point）B_T^4 产生；另发现孤立的非对称轨道族 E^A_44、E^A_45、E^A_46，以及自 L_4 出发的短周期与长周期非对称轨道族 E^A_S 与 E^A_L。在 2D 与 3D-ERTBP 中，多数对称或非对称周期轨道族的尘埃粒子均表现出高偏心率运动。

Doing so produces novel results in their own right and diagnostic tools for precise guidance for our next steps. Based on them, we generated detailed phase portraits. Finally, we run suitably seeded $N$-body simulations to compare with the periodic orbits, and to provide observational links with debris close to WDs. New families of asymmetric periodic orbits, called $E^A_{41}$, $E^A_{42}$, and $E^A_{43}$ in the high-eccentricity 2D-ERTBP regime were discovered. They are generated by the new bifurcation point, $B_T^4$, along the new $A_2$ family, showcased in Paper I. Moreover, new isolated families of asymmetric periodic orbits, namely $E^A_{44}$, $E^A_{45}$, and $E^A_{46}$ were discovered. Families of short- and long- period asymmetric orbits starting from $L_4$, called $E^A_S$ and $E^A_L$, were also computed. In both the 2D- and 3D-ERTBP regimes, highly eccentric motion is exhibited for the dust particles in the majority of the families of either symmetric or asymmetric periodic orbits. The $N$-body simulations reveal that over ten years, the orbital period deviations of the dust are higher than in the circular restricted three-body problem (Paper I); in some architectures, Mercury-mass planets can regularly generate deviations exceeding $\sim$100 s, which potentially aids observational inferences of hidden asteroids or planets orbiting WDs.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NaCl 是地外海洋的主要成分，但学界普遍假设其难以进入富水行星的高压冰幔，盐—冰相互作用机制及对行星内部化学交换的影响尚不明确。

Conclusion: 含盐冰可能通过深部对流幔输送，并在约 5 GPa 边界释放盐分，其密度较高的产物可抑制进一步上升输送；这种路径依赖的盐捕获（pathway-dependent salt trapping）可能对岩石内部与次表层海洋之间的化学交换构成此前未被认识的控制机制。

Synchrotron x-ray diffraction and neutron diffraction reveals an expanded and strongly disordered ice VII lattice with no detectable crystalline NaCl. Upon decompression at 300 K, the phase expels crystalline NaCl at $5.1 \pm 0.3$ GPa and does not reform upon recompression, demonstrating that its formation is controlled by the thermodynamic pathway. These properties suggest that salty ice may be transported through deep convecting mantles but release salt near the 5 GPa boundary, where the denser products could inhibit further upward transport. Main category: math.PR

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dubédat 关于多 SLE（Schramm–Loewner evolution）的交换关系引出了 chordal Belavin–Polyakov–Zamolodchikov（BPZ）方程，其线性解空间结构此前缺乏系统分类，尤其在不施加增长条件时。

Conclusion: 该分类在不施加增长条件的情形下统一刻画了 BPZ 解空间维数与结构，并在更弱假设下重现既有 Catalan 维数结果，同时刻画了 κ=8 与 κ=4 情形下的完备性与显式解。

Abstract: We classify the linear solution spaces of the chordal Belavin--Polyakov--Zamolodchikov (BPZ) equations, which arise in Dubédat's commutation relations for multiple Schramm--Loewner evolutions (SLE). Without imposing growth conditions, we determine the subspaces selected by successive conformal Ward identities and their exact dimensions. For every $κ>0$ and BPZ spectral parameter $λ\in\mathbb{R}$, we determine the spectrum and Jordan structure of the translation operator. When $λ=0$ and $κ\in(0,8)$, we further determine all admissible scaling exponents and the dimensions of the corresponding translation-invariant solution spaces. A triangular change of derivative coordinates yields a first-order system of rational Knizhnik--Zamolodchikov type, allowing us to represent the Ward operators explicitly and reduce the classification to finite-dimensional linear algebra.
  For $2N$ boundary points and $κ\in(0,8)$, translation-invariant BPZ solutions with $λ=0$ and the homogeneity required by Möbius covariance automatically satisfy the third Ward identity and the usual power-law bound. This recovers, under weaker assumptions, the Catalan dimension formula previously established by Flores and Kleban. At $κ=8$, the third Ward identity imposes an additional constraint when $N\ge2$, but the full Möbius-covariant solution space still has Catalan dimension, establishing completeness of the partition functions constructed from uniform spanning trees. Finally, at $κ=4$ and $λ>0$, we construct an explicit basis of positive BPZ solutions and identify its elements as partition functions for level lines of a Gaussian free field with suitable harmonic means.

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文研究离散 $d$ 维环面上中心平稳随机场（centered stationary random fields）经齐次常系数微分算子作用后的高斯涨落，证明了在负 Sobolev 空间中依分布收敛到协方差乘子为 $|p(k)|^2\gamma(k)$ 的中心高斯随机分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是刻画微分可观测量（differential observables）在中心平稳随机场上的涨落极限，并厘清微分算子与场本身对极限协方差结构的贡献，尤其是微分能否补偿低频谱奇异性。

Method: 采用傅里叶分析（Fourier analysis）与矩和累积量方法（method of moments and cumulants），并结合紧性论证（tightness argument）；假设涉及协方差的谱标度极限 $\gamma$ 与 $r\geq3$ 阶归一化累积量。

Result: 在适当假设下证明收敛于具有协方差乘子 $|p(k)|^2\gamma(k)$ 的中心高斯随机分布；该乘子将算子与场的效应分离，且微分可补偿低频谱奇异。对格点高斯自由场（lattice Gaussian Free Field），得到梯度的投影白噪声极限。

Conclusion: 给出累积量假设的两个充分判据：树–图界（tree–graph bound）与有限程 Gibbs 场的 Dobrushin 唯一性条件；并据此在高温度下重新得到中心 Ising 磁化与 Potts 颜色涨落场的白噪声极限。

Abstract: We study Gaussian fluctuations of differential observables of centered stationary random fields on the discrete $d$-dimensional torus of mesh $1/N$. Our main result is a Central Limit Theorem for the fields $f\mapsto N^{-d/2-m}\sum_xφ_x^N(Pf)(x/N)$, where $P$ is a scalar homogeneous constant-coefficient differential operator of order $m\geq0$ with Fourier symbol $p$, e.g., $P=Δ$ and $m=2$. Under suitable assumptions on the spectral scaling limit $γ$ of the covariance and the normalized cumulants of order $r\geq3$, we prove convergence in law in a negative Sobolev space to a centered Gaussian random distribution with covariance multiplier $|p(k)|^2γ(k)$. The two factors separate the effects of the operator and the field and, notably, differentiation can compensate a low-frequency spectral singularity. For the lattice Gaussian Free Field, this gives a projected white-noise limit for the gradient. The proofs use Fourier analysis and the method of moments and cumulants, together with a tightness argument. Finally, we give two sufficient criteria for the cumulant assumption, namely a tree--graph bound and the Dobrushin uniqueness condition for finite-range Gibbs fields, and recover the white-noise limits of the centered Ising magnetization and Potts colour fluctuation fields at high temperature.

</details>

*Jianping Jiang,Sike Lang*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该论文证明：在 $\mathbb{Z}^d$（$d\ge2$）上的最近邻反铁磁 Ising 模型（antiferromagnetic Ising model）中，对任意 $\beta>\beta_c(d)$，当外场 $|h|$ 足够小时 Gibbs 态（Gibbs state）不唯一，从而将 Dobrushin（1968）关于充分大 $\beta$ 的结果推广至所有超临界逆温。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dobrushin（1968）已证明在充分大的逆温 $\beta$ 下，小外场中 Gibbs 态不唯一；本文旨在将该结论的适用范围从"充分大 $\beta$"扩展至全部 $\beta>\beta_c(d)$，即越过相变点的整个低温区域。核心问题是考察非唯一性（non-uniqueness）是否仅依赖于零场相变条件。

Method: 研究对象为 $\mathbb{Z}^d$（$d\ge2$）上的最近邻（nearest-neighbor）反铁磁 Ising 模型，参数为逆温 $\beta$ 与均匀外场 $h$，并以零场 Ising 模型的临界逆温 $\beta_c(d)$ 作为阈值。证明策略为在任意 $\beta>\beta_c(d)$ 及足够小的 $|h|$ 条件下建立 Gibbs 态的非唯一性，属数学物理中的严格证明工作。

Result: 主要结果为：对每个 $\beta>\beta_c(d)$，只要 $|h|$ 足够小，Gibbs 态即不唯一。该结论在参数范围上严格强于 Dobrushin（1968）的既有结果，后者仅覆盖充分大的 $\beta$。

Conclusion: 零场临界逆温 $\beta_c(d)$ 构成小外场下 Gibbs 态非唯一性的完整阈值：一旦 $\beta>\beta_c(d)$，小外场即可导致非唯一性。该结果确立了反铁磁 Ising 模型中相变点与外部场诱导非唯一性之间的紧密对应关系。

Abstract: Consider the nearest-neighbor antiferromagnetic Ising model on $\mathbb{Z}^d$, $d\ge2$, at inverse temperature $β$ and in a uniform external field $h$. Let $β_c(d)$ be the critical inverse temperature of the zero-field Ising model. We prove that for every $β>β_c(d)$, the Gibbs state is not unique whenever $|h|$ is sufficiently small. This extends a previous result of Dobrushin (1968) from sufficiently large $β$ to every $β>β_c(d)$.

</details>


### [66] [Walk/Zeta correspondence for quantum walks on path graphs: a pre-geometric structure implied by a generalized reflection principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05393)
*Jiro Akahori,Riki Kitano,Norio Konno,Iwao Sato*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该论文在有限路径图上为反射随机游走（reflected random walks）与 Szegedy 游走建立了局部 Konno-Sato 对应：借助广义反射原理得到 zeta 函数分解，并由此推出热力学极限与两类极限（高斯求和公式、Dirac comb 恒等式，以及大规模 Dirac 型极限）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在将 Konno-Sato 对应从既有情形推广到有限路径图上的反射随机游走与 Szegedy 游走，以统一刻画经典游走与量子游走之间的局部对应关系及其极限行为。

Method: 采用广义反射原理（generalized reflection principle），在非对称情形中以显式对合（involutions）替代点反射，将 zeta 函数精确分解为边界因子（boundary factor）与以无限二面体群（infinite dihedral group）为指标的局部因子（local factors）；经对称化（symmetrization）后逐群元素比较经典与量子的局部因子。

Result: 恒等元贡献决定归一化热力学极限（normalized thermodynamic limits）；对称情形下扩散标度（diffusive scaling）与弹道标度（ballistic scaling）分别恢复高斯 Poisson 求和公式与 Dirac comb 恒等式；在非对称性与谱参数的双标度（double scaling）下，量子分解给出大质量 Dirac 型极限，其中非恒等偶字（even words）产生周期轨道因子，恒等元产生自由场因子，奇字（odd words）与边界共同产生有限边界修正。

Conclusion: 结果建立了有限路径图上经典与量子游走的局部 Konno-Sato 对应，表明经典、量子两侧的局部因子在对称化后逐群元素一致，并在双标度极限下导出含边界修正的大质量 Dirac 型有效描述。

Abstract: We establish a local Konno-Sato correspondence for reflected random and Szegedy walks on a finite path graph. A generalized reflection principle, using explicit involutions rather than point reflections in the asymmetric case, yields exact zeta factorizations into a boundary factor and local factors indexed by the infinite dihedral group. After symmetrization, the classical and quantum local factors correspond for each group element. The identity contribution determines the normalized thermodynamic limits. In the symmetric case, diffusive and ballistic scalings recover the Gaussian Poisson summation formula and the Dirac comb identity, respectively. Under a double scaling of the asymmetry and spectral parameter, the quantum factorization yields a massive Dirac-type limit: nonidentity even words produce the periodic-orbit factor, the identity produces the free-field factor, and odd words together with the boundary produce a finite boundary correction.

</details>


### [67] [Strong freezing in the binary perceptron model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06275)
*Shuyang Gong,Shuangping Li*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该论文证明了二元感知机（binary perceptron）模型在任意固定正约束密度和任意固定边缘（margin）下，以高概率呈现强冻结（strong freezing）现象，即典型解是孤立的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强冻结是二元感知机模型中一个重要的物理预测，且被进一步猜想为典型解之间相距约 n 的汉明距离（Hamming distance）。该现象与统计物理（statistical physics）和计算复杂性（computational complexity）有深刻联系，但此前尚未在一般参数下得到证明。

Method: 证明基于植入模型（planted model）与一个受限配分函数（restricted partition function），后者只统计与植入解具有非负重叠（nonnegative overlap）的解。与完整配分函数不同，该受限量沿植入方向单调，因而可用 FKG 不等式（FKG inequality）将其与孤立性解耦，而限制仅带来 n+1 的因子代价。

Result: 作者证明二元感知机模型在每个固定正约束密度与每个固定边缘下，均以高概率表现出强冻结。通过上述解耦，可将植入解的孤立性转移至典型解。

Conclusion: 受限配分函数的单调性使 FKG 不等式得以将孤立性从植入解传递到典型解，从而完成强冻结猜想的证明。

Abstract: The binary perceptron model studies the set of solutions obtained by intersecting random Gaussian half-spaces with the discrete hypercube {-1,+1}^n. Pioneered in the work by Krauth and M{é}zard in the 1980s [KM89], one of the most striking physics predictions for this model is strong freezing, which says that typical solutions are isolated. Moreover, the conjecture was sharpened later to assert that typical solutions are at Hamming distance of order $n$ from every other solution [HWK13],[HK14]. This phenomenon has attracted a lot of attention over the years, partly due to its intricate connections to statistical physics and computational complexity. We prove that the binary perceptron model exhibits strong freezing with high probability at every fixed positive constraint density and every fixed margin. Our proof is based on the planted model and a restricted partition function that counts solutions having nonnegative overlap with the planted solution. Unlike the full partition function, this restricted one is monotone in the planted direction, so the FKG inequality decouples it from isolation, while the restriction costs only a factor of n+1. Together, these allow us to transfer the isolation of the planted solution to typical solutions, which completes the proof of strong freezing.

</details>


### [68] [Upper deviations of the Sherrington-Kirkpatrick free energy: the fifth-order law and the onset of constrained Parisi measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06480)
*Yan Ru Pei*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文研究 Sherrington-Kirkpatrick（SK）模型配分函数的大偏差（large deviation）速率，证明约束极小值超出平衡自由能 $c_\beta\theta^5(1+o(1))$，从而给出指数 $\frac{5}{6}(6c_\beta)^{-1/5}\delta^{6/5}$ 的精确常数，并刻画约束极小元的结构及若干推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复制理论（replica theory，Kondor 及 Parisi、Rizzo）预测 $N^{-1}\log Z_N$ 超出典型值 $\delta$ 的概率以 $\exp(-Na\delta^{6/5})$ 衰减，Aronow 与 Lopatto 已证明该阶的界，但显式常数的精确刻画尚待给出。该指数为带零处原子约束的 Parisi 问题的 Legendre 变换（Legendre transform）。

Method: 在 Parisi 问题中施加零处携带原子 $\theta$ 的约束，计算约束极小值与平衡自由能之差，并分析约束极小元的结构（首次正接触、原子大小及分片函数（plateau function）在接触点附近的普适三次行为）。进一步借助分数矩公式（fractional-moment formula）与 Lopatto 关于 Parisi 测度在零处累积的定理，将结果推广至一般混合模型。

Result: 约束极小值超出平衡自由能 $c_\beta\theta^5(1+o(1))$，其中 $c_\beta=\frac{9}{640}\beta^3\rho_\beta(0)^{-3}$，$\rho_\beta(0)>0$ 为 Parisi 测度在零处的密度；由此得指数 $\frac56(6c_\beta)^{-1/5}\delta^{6/5}(1+o(1))$（$N\to\infty$ 后 $\delta\to0$）。约束极小元的边际首次正接触至首阶位于 $3\theta/(2\rho_\beta(0))$，原子为 $\theta/2$，其下的分片函数与接触点恒等函数相差一个普适三次项。该分片演算适用于所有满足 $\xi'''(0)=0$ 且 Parisi 测度在零处累积的混合模型；小场 $h$ 打开量级 $|h|^{2/3}$ 的间隙并向自由能加入 $|h|^{10/3}$ 量级的项；零处原子使速率线性离开零，斜率等于该原子。近临界时常数趋于 Kondor 值 $9/5120$，边际区终止于约束中的 de Almeida-Thouless 阈值，首次接触之上约束测度与 Parisi 测度相差 $O(\theta^5)$。

Conclusion: 对每一 $\beta>1$，结论依赖 Lopatto 的定理（Parisi 测度在零处累积）。研究给出了 SK 模型大偏差指数的显式常数及约束极小元的结构刻画，并将分片演算推广至满足相应条件的混合模型，同时揭示小场与零处原子对自由能和速率的影响。

Abstract: Let $Z_N$ be the partition function of the Sherrington-Kirkpatrick model at inverse temperature $β>1$. Replica theory (Kondor near the critical temperature, Parisi and Rizzo at all temperatures) predicts that the probability that $N^{-1}\log Z_N$ exceeds its typical value by $δ$ decays like $\exp(-Naδ^{6/5})$ with an explicit constant $a$; Aronow and Lopatto recently proved bounds of this order. The exponent is the Legendre transform of the Parisi problem constrained to carry an atom $θ$ at zero. We show that the constrained minimum exceeds the equilibrium free energy by $c_βθ^5(1+o(1))$, where $c_β=\frac{9}{640}β^3ρ_β(0)^{-3}$ and $ρ_β(0)>0$ is the density at zero of the Parisi measure. With the fractional-moment formula, valid for this model, this gives the exponent $\frac56(6c_β)^{-1/5}δ^{6/5}(1+o(1))$ as $N\to\infty$ and then $δ\to0$. The constrained minimizer has a marginal first positive contact, to leading order at $3θ/(2ρ_β(0))$ with an atom $θ/2$, below which a function equal to the identity at the contacts differs from it by a universal cubic to leading order. This plateau calculus applies to every mixture with $ξ'''(0)=0$ whose Parisi measure accumulates at zero; it also shows that a small field $h$ opens a gap of order $|h|^{2/3}$ and adds a term of order $|h|^{10/3}$ to the free energy, and that an atom of the Parisi measure at zero, present in every pure $p$-spin model with $p\ge3$, makes the rate leave zero linearly, with slope equal to the atom. Near criticality the constant tends to Kondor's value $9/5120$, the marginal regime ends at a de Almeida-Thouless threshold in the constraint, and above its first contact the constrained measure agrees with the Parisi measure up to $O(θ^5)$. The results at every $β>1$ use Lopatto's theorem that the Parisi measure accumulates at zero.

</details>


### [69] [TAP states at upper deviations of the free energy: existence, localization and marginal stability in Ising spin glasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06490)
*Yan Ru Pei*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文通过广义 TAP 自由能（generalized TAP free energy）研究混合 p-spin Ising 自旋玻璃（spin glass）自由能的上大偏差（upper large deviations），证明在收敛半径大于一的混合模型下 max F_TAP 与 log Z_N 具有相同的大偏差率，并在凸混合情形刻画了达到上偏差的广义 TAP 临界点及其几何与谱性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究自旋玻璃自由能上大偏差的结构性来源，尤其是澄清广义 TAP 自由能与配分函数（partition function）在大偏差层面的对应关系，并在不依赖 Boursier 严格 Plefka 条件（strict Plefka condition）的前提下给出临界点刻画。

Method: 以 Chen–Panchenko–Subag 的广义 TAP 自由能 F_TAP 为工具；对收敛半径大于一的混合模型，结合其带定理（band theorem）与 Ramsey 定理（Ramsey's theorem）证明 max F_TAP 与 log Z_N 的大偏差在速度 N 下相同；对满足 ∑_p 2^p β_p^2 < ∞ 的凸混合，分析广义 TAP 临界点、约束 Parisi 障碍（constrained Parisi obstacle）的接触集（contact set）、Auffinger–Chen 场律（field law）以及 Hessian 的反射 Pastur 律（reflected Pastur law）。

Result: 对收敛半径大于一的混合，max F_TAP 与 log Z_N 在速度 N 下具有相同大偏差；对凸混合，上偏差由广义 TAP 临界点承担，且这些临界点在自由能速率下存在、无需严格 Plefka 条件。在指数阶更小的事件之外，级别 f 的近极大点（near-maximizers）位于约束 Parisi 障碍的接触集附近，服从 Auffinger–Chen 场律，其 Hessian 体分布接近反射 Pastur 律，该律的边缘非正且在障碍二阶平坦的接触处恰好为零；当所有接触均为此类时，固定稳定性余量（stability margin）会带来速率代价。

Conclusion: 广义 TAP 自由能可作为刻画混合 p-spin 自旋玻璃上大偏差的有效工具，实现其与配分函数大偏差的对应，并在凸混合情形给出上偏差的临界点图景及接触集—谱结构刻画；稳定接触的固定稳定性余量对应明确的大偏差速率。

Abstract: We study upper large deviations of the free energy of mixed $p$-spin Ising spin glasses through the generalized TAP free energy $F_{\mathrm{TAP}}$ of Chen, Panchenko and Subag. For every mixture with radius of convergence greater than one, $\max F_{\mathrm{TAP}}$ and $\log Z_N$ have the same large deviations at speed $N$; the proof combines their band theorem with Ramsey's theorem. For convex mixtures with $\sum_p 2^pβ_p^2<\infty$, upper deviations at level $f$ are carried by generalized TAP critical points, which exist at the free-energy rate without Boursier's strict Plefka condition. Outside an event of smaller exponential order, near-maximizers at level $f$ lie near the contact set of a constrained Parisi obstacle, follow the Auffinger-Chen field law, and have Hessian bulk near a reflected Pastur law whose edge is nonpositive and vanishes exactly at contacts where the obstacle is flat to second order. Where every contact is of this kind, a fixed stability margin costs rate.

</details>

### [70] [Finite-dimensional Lie subalgebras of the universal enveloping algebra of $\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathbb{C})$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04001)
*Tim Heib*

Main category: math.RA

TL;DR: 该工作完整分类了特殊线性李代数（special linear Lie algebra）$\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathbb{C})$ 的万有包络代数（universal enveloping algebra）中所有非零有限维复李子代数（complex Lie subalgebra），并给出实斜埃尔米特（real skew-hermitian）情形及 Weyl 代数实现的相关讨论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在刻画 $\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathbb{C})$ 的万有包络代数中非零有限维李子代数的完整分类结构，并将相应结果推广至实斜埃尔米特设定，以关联单模与双模 Weyl 代数中的实现。

Method: 方法为代数结构分类：证明幂零（nilpotent）李子代数均为交换（abelian）的，非可解（non-solvable）李子代数均为约化（reductive）的，其半单部分（semisimple component）同构于 $\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathbb{C})$；并借助参数族 $\mathfrak{r}(\boldsymbol{d},\boldsymbol{m})$ 刻画非幂零可解（non-nilpotent solvable）李子代数。

Result: 分类结果显示：非幂零可解李子代数可表示为交换中心（abelian center）与可解李代数族 $\mathfrak{r}(\boldsymbol{d},\boldsymbol{m})$ 中成员的直和（direct sum）；该族由严格递增、最大公约数为 1 的正整数向量 $\boldsymbol{d}$ 及记录其重数的等长正整数向量 $\boldsymbol{m}$ 参数化。

Conclusion: 论文完成了该包络代数中有限维复李子代数的完整分类，并进一步讨论了实斜埃尔米特情形下的对应分类及其与单模和双模 Weyl 代数实现的关系。

Abstract: In this work we provide the complete classification of all non-zero finite-dimensional complex Lie subalgebras of the universal enveloping algebra of the special linear Lie algebra $\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathbb{C})$. That is, we show that every such nilpotent Lie subalgebra is abelian and every non-solvable Lie subalgebra is reductive, with semisimple component isomorphic to $\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathbb{C})$ itself. Every non-nilpotent solvable Lie subalgebra is the direct sum of an abelian center and a member of the family of solvable Lie algebras $\mathfrak{r}(\boldsymbol{d},\boldsymbol{m})$, parameterized by a vector $\boldsymbol{d}$ whose entries are strictly increasing positive integers that have greatest common divisor one together with a positive integer vector $\boldsymbol{m}$ of the same length recording their multiplicities. Moreover, we discuss the corresponding classification in a real skew-hermitian setting and its relation to the realizations in the single- and two-mode Weyl algebras.

</details>

### [71] [Simultaneous measurement of the Higgs boson decay rates to WW and ZZ in fully hadronic final states at FCC-ee](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04397)
*Jan Eysermans,Mila Pandurovic,Christoph Paus,Michele Selvaggi,Aman Desai*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 在 FCC-ee 的 Higgsstrahlung 过程中，针对六喷注末态同时提取 H→WW 与 H→ZZ 信号强度，预期给出 σ(ZH)×BR 的相对不确定度为 1.48% 与 8.20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测定希格斯玻色子（Higgs boson）与电弱规范玻色子（electroweak gauge bosons）的耦合是 FCC-ee 物理计划的核心目标，其中 HWW 与 HZZ 耦合对模型无关地确定希格斯总衰变宽度（total decay width）具有关键作用。

Method: 研究 Higgsstrahlung 产生过程中 H→WW 与 H→ZZ 衰变，聚焦关联 Z 玻色子与希格斯衰变产生的矢量玻色子均作强子衰变的六喷注（six-jet）末态；采用喷注聚类（jet-clustering）与专门的喷注配对（jet-pairing）技术优化强子衰变规范玻色子重建，并因 WW 与 ZZ 衰变模式间显著的动力学重叠而同时提取两者信号强度。

Result: 在质心能量（center-of-mass energy）240 GeV、积分亮度（integrated luminosity）10.8 ab^-1 假设下，H→WW 与 H→ZZ 衰道的 σ(ZH)×BR 预期相对不确定度分别为 1.48% 与 8.20%。

Conclusion: 全强子衰道中 H→WW 因分支比大而具备高信号统计量，H→ZZ 则部分由 Z 玻色子较大的强子分支比补偿；该分析表明在 FCC-ee 条件下可对两个衰道的 σ(ZH)×BR 实现高精度测量。

Abstract: The precise determination of the Higgs boson couplings to electroweak gauge bosons is a key objective of the FCC-ee physics program. In particular, measurements of the Higgs boson couplings to W and Z bosons play a central role in the model-independent determination of its total decay width. This paper presents a study of Higgs boson decays to H -> WW and H -> ZZ in the Higgsstrahlung production process. The analysis focuses on events in which the associated Z boson and the vector bosons from the Higgs decay all decay hadronically, resulting in six-jet final states. The large H -> WW branching fraction leads to high signal statistics in the fully hadronic channel, while for H -> ZZ the much smaller Higgs branching fraction is partly compensated by the large hadronic branching fractions of the Z bosons. Due to the substantial kinematic overlap between the WW and ZZ decay modes, their signal strengths are extracted simultaneously. Jet-clustering and dedicated jet-pairing techniques are employed to optimize the reconstruction of the hadronically decaying gauge bosons and enhance the separation of the two signal components. Assuming a center-of-mass energy of 240 GeV and an integrated luminosity of 10.8 ab^-1, expected relative uncertainties of 1.48% and 8.20% are obtained on the production cross section times branching fraction, sigma(ZH) x BR, for the H -> WW and H -> ZZ decay modes, respectively.

</details>


### [72] [Search for Higgs boson compositeness effects in high-mass dilepton final states in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 and 13.6 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04782)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于 CMS 探测器在 LHC 13 与 13.6 TeV 质心能量、207 fb⁻¹ 积分亮度数据中搜寻高质标双电子与双缪子末态的新物理效应，重点关注 Higgs 玻色子复合性（compositeness），并以有效场论（effective field theory）对斜参数（oblique parameters）W、Y 给出严格上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准模型（standard model）无法解释 Higgs 玻色子的基本性或复合性本质，若 Higgs 为复合粒子则可能伴随新的大质量矢量玻色子共振态，并在双轻子质量谱（dilepton mass spectrum）中留下畸变，因此需要在高质标双轻子末态中检验超越标准模型（beyond the standard model）效应。

Method: 利用 CMS 探测器在 LHC 上以 13 与 13.6 TeV 质心能量（center-of-mass energy）采集的、总积分亮度为 207 fb⁻¹ 的数据，搜寻高质标双电子与双缪子末态；在假定 TeV 标度以上存在新玻色子态的前提下，采用有效场论方法约束斜参数 W 与 Y，并在假设可见双轻子质量谱内或其略上方存在新 Z' 共振态的情形下设置复合性上限。

Result: 单参数一维拟合（仅允许一个斜参数偏离零）在 95% 置信水平（confidence level）下给出：−3.9×10⁻⁴ &lt; Y &lt; 3.5×10⁻⁴，以及 −2.1×10⁻⁴ &lt; W &lt; 1.6×10⁻⁴；在若干新物理情景下于 TeV 标度得到更严格的复合性上限，并首次探索了大共振衰变宽度（large resonance decay widths）的情形。

Conclusion: 高质标双轻子数据未显示与标准模型预言一致的偏差证据，但对 Higgs 复合性所预言的新物理给出了严格的斜参数约束与 TeV 标度复合性上限，且首次涵盖宽共振衰变宽度情景。

Abstract: A search for beyond the standard model effects in high-mass dielectron and dimuon final states with the CMS detector is performed, with a specific focus on Higgs boson compositeness. The search uses data collected at the LHC at 13 and 13.6 TeV center-of-mass energies, corresponding to a total integrated luminosity of 207 fb$^{-1}$. Compositeness would manifest as distortions in the observed dilepton mass spectrum and the presence of bound states, such as the Higgs boson and new massive vector boson resonances. Assuming the existence of new boson states with masses well above the TeV scale, an effective field theory approach provides stringent bounds on the oblique parameters $\mathcal{W}$ and $\mathcal{Y}$, which are zero in the standard model. One-dimensional fits, in which only one of the oblique parameters is allowed to deviate from zero, provide the following limits: $-$3.9 $\times$ 10$^{-4}$$\lt$ $\mathcal{Y}$ $\lt$ 3.5 $\times$ 10$^{-4}$ and $-$2.1 $\times$ 10$^{-4}$ $\lt$ $\mathcal{W}$ $\lt$ 1.6 $\times$ 10$^{-4}$ at 95% confidence level. More stringent compositeness limits at the TeV scale are set in several new physics scenarios under the assumption that a new Z' resonance is physically present within or slightly above the visible dilepton mass spectrum. In this context, cases with large resonance decay widths are explored for the first time.

</details>
