Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于 BESIII 探测器在 3.773 GeV 采集的 20.3 fb⁻¹ e⁺e⁻ 碰撞数据，首次对双 Cabibbo 压低衰变 D⁺ → K⁺π⁺π⁻π⁰ 进行振幅分析，并首次观测到 D⁺ → K*⁰ρ⁺ 与 D⁺ → K*⁺ρ⁰ 两个衰变道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双 Cabibbo 压低（doubly Cabibbo-suppressed）衰变 D⁺ → K⁺π⁺π⁻π⁰ 此前缺乏振幅分析，且 D → VV 末态极化行为具有研究价值，因此需要高统计量数据开展首次振幅分析以确认相关衰变道及其极化特性。

Method: 利用 BESIII 探测器在质心能量 3.773 GeV 处采集的积分亮度 20.3 fb⁻¹ 的 e⁺e⁻ 碰撞数据样本，对 D⁺ → K⁺π⁺π⁻π⁰ 衰变进行首次振幅分析（amplitude analysis）。

Result: 首次观测到 D⁺ → K*⁰ρ⁺ 与 D⁺ → K*⁺ρ⁰，分支比（branching fraction）分别为 (5.67±0.41_stat±0.17_syst)×10⁻⁴ 和 (5.32±0.57_stat±0.24_syst)×10⁻⁴；两者均具有较大的横向极化（transverse polarization），纵向极化分数（longitudinal polarization fraction）分别为 0.111±0.024_stat±0.008_syst 与 0.263±0.049_stat±0.015_syst。此外测得 D⁺ → K⁺ω 的分支比为 (4.76±0.84_stat±0.13_syst)×10⁻⁵。

Conclusion: 该研究首次给出 D⁺ → K⁺π⁺π⁻π⁰ 的振幅分析结果，并首次观测到 D⁺ → K*⁰ρ⁺ 和 D⁺ → K*⁺ρ⁰ 两个 D → VV 衰变道，二者均表现出较大的横向极化，同时给出了 D⁺ → K⁺ω 的分支比测量值，为理解该双 Cabibbo 压低衰变的动力学与极化机制提供了实验依据。

Abstract: By analyzing an $e^+e^-$ collision data sample with an integrated luminosity of 20.3 fb$^{-1}$ collected with the BESIII detector at the center-of-mass energy of 3.773 GeV, we perform the first amplitude analysis on the doubly Cabibbo-suppressed decay $D^+ \to K^+π^+π^-π^0$ and report the first observation of $D^+ \to K^{*0}ρ^+$ and $D^+\to K^{*+}ρ^0$. The corresponding branching fractions are $(5.67\pm0.41_{\rm stat}\pm0.17_{\rm syst})\times10^{-4}$ and $(5.32\pm0.57_{\rm stat}\pm0.24_{\rm syst})\times10^{-4}$, respectively. These two $D\to VV$ decay both have large transverse polarizations. The longitudinal polarization fractions are measured to be $0.111\pm0.024_{\rm stat}\pm0.008_{\rm syst}$ and $0.263\pm0.049_{\rm stat}\pm0.015_{\rm syst}$, respectively. The branching fraction of the decay $D^+\to K^+ω$ is measured to be $(4.76\pm0.84_{\rm stat}\pm0.13_{\rm syst})\times 10^{-5}$.

</details>


### [74] [Precision measurement of ocean tides using cosmic-ray muons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05294)
*Jiwon Seo,Jongseok Chung,Chang Hyon Ha,TongZhou Huang,Jinyoung Kim,Sooa Kim,Hani Kimku,Byoung-cheol Koh,Minsu Kwak,Sihyun Lee,Yujin Lee*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 利用水下隧道中的宇宙线缪子（cosmic-ray muons）探测海水潮汐引起的覆盖层变化，观测到的调制与独立验潮仪数据高度一致，可实现米级海水覆盖层变化的分辨。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙线缪子可自然探测其轨迹上的积分质量，因此具备监测动态自然系统中时间相关覆盖层变化的潜力；与仅测量局地海平面的传统验潮仪不同，缪子成像（muography）测量探测器上方的积分覆盖层。

Method: 在水下隧道中监测由海洋潮汐引起的海水覆盖层变化，并与独立验潮仪测量进行对比；同时开展空间缪子成像扫描以验证覆盖层模型。

Result: 观测到的调制紧密跟踪独立验潮仪测量，得到衰减响应系数 k=43.2±1.0 counts/h/m，并可分辨米级海水覆盖层变化；空间缪子成像扫描验证了覆盖层模型，确认海水贡献对重现观测衰减不可或缺。

Conclusion: 缪子成像能够定量分辨环境中微小的时间相关覆盖层变化，为监测动态自然系统提供了新途径。

Abstract: Cosmic-ray muons provide a natural probe of the integrated mass along their trajectories. Here we show that cosmic-ray muons can monitor ocean-tide-induced variations in seawater overburden within an underwater tunnel. The observed modulation closely tracks independent tide-gauge measurements, yielding an attenuation response coefficient of $k=43.2\pm1.0$~counts$\rm /h/m$ and resolving meter-scale variations in seawater overburden. A spatial muography scan validates the overburden model, confirming that the seawater contribution is essential to reproduce the observed attenuation. Unlike conventional tide gauges that measure sea level locally, muography measures the integrated overburden above the detector. These results demonstrate that muography can quantitatively resolve small, time-dependent variations in environmental overburden, providing a new approach to monitoring dynamic natural systems.

</details>


### [75] [Search for Diffuse Supernova Neutrino Background of All Flavors with PandaX-4T](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05980)
*Yingxin Zhang,PandaX Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于PandaX-4T液氙探测器的相干弹性中微子-原子核散射（coherent elastic neutrino-nucleus scattering, CEvNS）数据，首次开展弥散超新星中微子背景（diffuse supernova neutrino background, DSNB）搜寻，未发现显著超出本底的事件，给出90%置信水平下的通量上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弥散超新星中微子背景是宇宙中所有历史超新星中微子累积形成的信号，其探测有助于理解恒星演化与中微子物理。本文首次尝试利用相干弹性中微子-原子核散射通道，并借助液氙探测器低阈值、低本底的特性开展该搜寻。

Method: 使用中国锦屏地下实验室（China Jinping Underground Laboratory）的PandaX-4T液氙探测器，基于调试运行与首次科学运行累计1.54吨·年（tonne·year）的曝光量，通过相干弹性中微子-原子核散射信号通道分析事件，并与预期本底比较。

Result: 在预期本底之上未观测到显著事件超出。对入射中微子能量高于19.3 MeV的ν_x通量（分别为ν_μ、\barν_μ、ν_τ、\barν_τ中每一种），在90%置信水平下给出(2.27–2.43)×10^3 cm^-2 s^-1每味的上限。

Conclusion: 该结果为利用相干弹性中微子-原子核散射搜寻弥散超新星中微子背景的首个结果，其灵敏度与当前世界最佳上限相当，表明液氙探测器在该物理目标上具有竞争力。

Abstract: We present the first search for the diffuse supernova neutrino background utilizing coherent elastic neutrino-nucleus scattering with the PandaX-4T liquid xenon detector at the China Jinping Underground Laboratory. Based on a total exposure of $1.54~\mathrm{tonne \cdot year}$ from the commissioning and first science runs, we observe no significant event excess over the expected background. We therefore set an upper limit of $(2.27 - 2.43) \times 10^3~\mathrm{cm^{-2}s^{-1}}$ per flavor at 90\% confidence level on the $ν_x$ flux (each of $ν_μ, \barν_μ, ν_τ, \barν_τ$) of the diffuse supernova neutrino background for incident neutrino energies above 19.3~MeV. The result is comparable to the current world best limit.

</details>


### [76] [A graph neural network for deep learning-based full event interpretation at FCC-ee](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06054)
*Elena Graverini,Samuel Morand,Rafael Silva Coutinho,Felipe Luan Souza de Almeida*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该文提出一个基于深度学习（deep learning）的全事件解释原型，用于未来环形正负电子对撞机（FCC-$ee$）中 $e^{+}e^{-}\rightarrow Z\rightarrow b\bar{b}$ 事例的 $b$-强子（$b$-hadron）衰变树重建，并以 IDEA 探测器概念为案例。测试中 90% 的重建衰变链包含完全正确的粒子集合，最长三代衰变链的聚类效率超过 93%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向 FCC-$ee$ 的全事件解释（full event interpretation）需要从带电粒子信息中同时识别并重建 $b$-强子的层级衰变树，而现有方法在此类任务上的能力构成研究动因。论文以 $\sqrt{s}=91\ \mathrm{GeV}$ 的模拟事例为对象，探索深度学习方案的可行性。

Method: 采用图神经网络（graph neural network）框架：将事例表示为图（graph），重建带电粒子构成节点（node），粒子间的成对可观测（pairwise observables）编码为描述粒子关系的边特征（edge feature）。该框架旨在同时识别并重建每个事例中 $b$-强子的层级衰变树，研究基于 IDEA（Innovative Detector for Electron positron Accelerators）探测器概念开展。

Result: 在测试样本上，90% 的重建衰变链（decay chain）包含完全正确的粒子集合，且与推断出的衰变层级无关。在一系列基准 $b$-强子衰变中，对于最多跨越三代衰变的衰变链，聚类效率（clustering efficiency）超过 93%。

Conclusion: 该原型表明图神经网络方法可有效实现 FCC-$ee$ 环境下 $b$-强子层级衰变的识别与重建，并支持在 IDEA 探测器概念下的后续应用拓展。

Abstract: This paper presents a deep learning-based full event interpretation prototype developed for the electron-positron Future Circular Collider (FCC-$ee$) using simulated $e^{+}e^{-}\rightarrow Z\rightarrow b\bar{b}$ events at $\sqrt{s} = 91 \ \mathrm{GeV}$. The study was carried out using the Innovative Detector for Electron positron Accelerators (IDEA) detector concept as case study for future application. The proposed graph neural network framework aims to simultaneously identify and reconstruct the hierarchical decay trees of $b$-hadrons in each event using information from reconstructed charged particles. Events are represented as graphs, where reconstructed charged particles form the nodes and pairwise observables are encoded as edge features describing the relations between particles. On a test sample, 90% of reconstructed decay chains contain exactly the correct set of particles, irrespective of their inferred decay hierarchy. Across a range of benchmark $b$-hadron decays, this clustering efficiency exceeds 93% for decay chains spanning up to three generations of decays.

</details>


### [77] [SysVar: Consistent and scalable treatment of systematic uncertainties in template-based analyses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06117)
*Ilias Tsaklidis,Agrim Aggarwal,Georgios Alexandris,Tristan Fillinger,Giacomo De Pietro,Daniel Ivanov,Melisa-Melek Akdag,Markus Prim,Florian Bernlochner*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: SysVar 是一个开源、可通过 pip 安装且不依赖特定实验的轻量级 API，用于在模拟数据生成的模板（template）中传播系统不确定性（systematic uncertainties），并通过协方差压缩保持相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着分析规模扩大，将校正权重（correction weights）引入模拟数据生成的模板、同时保持信号提取变量（signal extraction variables）中相关性，变得日益困难。此类工作流程易出错但对高精度测量至关重要。

Method: SysVar 通过轻量级 API 隐藏应用校正权重、构建系统变分（systematic variations）以及对名义与变分模板进行直方图化（histogramming）的大部分簿记复杂度；通过在整个分析中传播完整协方差（covariance）并将其压缩为一组少量正交特征变分（orthogonal eigenvariations），每个对应一个普通形状干扰参数（shape nuisance parameter），以保持相关性。

Result: 通过结合两个独立的伪测量（pseudo-measurements）展示了 SysVar 的工作流程与效果，二者受益于模板形状中以一致方式编码的相关系统不确定性。

Conclusion: SysVar 为高精度测量中系统不确定性的模板传播提供了简化且可靠的工作流程；该工具开源、可通过 pip 安装，并与具体实验无关。

Abstract: Propagating systematic uncertainties from correction weights into templates generated from simulated data while preserving correlations in the signal extraction variables becomes increasingly challenging as analyses scale in size. SysVar's lightweight API hides most of the bookkeeping complexity of applying correction weights, building systematic variations, and histogramming nominal and varied templates. This streamlines workflows that are otherwise error-prone but essential for high-precision measurements. Correlations are preserved by propagating the full covariance across the analysis and compressing it into a small set of orthogonal eigenvariations, each defining an ordinary shape nuisance parameter. In this work, we demonstrate SysVar's workflow and impact by combining two independent pseudo-measurements that benefit from consistently correlated systematic uncertainties encoded in the template shapes. SysVar is open-source, pip-installable, and experiment-agnostic.

</details>


### [78] [FPGA-based muon shower identification and graph neural network tracking algorithms for HL-LHC triggers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06379)
*Daniel Estrada,Elena Aller,Andrea Cardini,Santiago Folgueras,Pelayo Leguina,Javier Prado*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该工作为 CMS 一级触发（Level-1 Trigger, L1T）升级提出两种硬件加速策略，分别针对高能辐射缪子的径迹效率损失与长寿命粒子（Long-Lived Particles, LLP）等非常规物理信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CMS 一级触发升级需应对高能辐射缪子造成的效率损失以及长寿命粒子等非常规物理特征，因此需要新的触发策略来识别此类非标准缪子信号。

Method: 第一种策略直接作用于低层探测器击中（hit），在 CMS 桶部区域实现专用缪子簇射识别算法，通过监测漂移管（Drift Tube）腔室中的击中多重数生成簇射原语（Shower Primitives）。第二种策略针对桶部-端盖过渡（overlap）区域，评估图神经网络（Graph Neural Networks, GNN）以重建位移缪子径迹，采用分离式方法先验证硬件可行性，基于 GraphSAGE 架构构建代理模型并在 FPGA 上验证。

Result: 簇射识别算法以亚束团交叉（sub-bunch-crossing）延迟和超低资源消耗运行，可成功标记簇射缪子并为下游径迹寻找器提供上下文。GNN 代理模型采用 INT8-PO2 量化与数据驱动的位宽优化，以编译期算术位移替代依赖 DSP 的定点乘法，将 DSP 占用降至 20%，并实现仅 19 个时钟周期的确定性推理延迟。

Conclusion: 上述两类发展为在一级触发层面触发非标准缪子信号（如来自 LLP 衰变的信号）奠定了基础。

Abstract: This work presents two hardware-accelerated strategies for the upgrade of the CMS Level-1 Trigger (L1T) to target efficiency losses due to highly energetic radiating muons and unconventional physics signatures, such as Long-Lived Particles (LLPs). Operating directly on low-level detector hits, the first strategy implements a dedicated muon shower identification algorithm in the CMS barrel region that monitors hit multiplicities in the Drift Tube chambers to generate Shower Primitives. Executing with sub-bunch-crossing latency and ultra-low resource consumption, this algorithm successfully tags showered muons and provides essential context for downstream track finders. The second approach targets the barrel-endcap transition (overlap) region by evaluating Graph Neural Networks to reconstruct displaced muon tracks. Following a divided methodology that first isolates hardware feasibility from evolving physics refinements, an initial proxy model based on a GraphSAGE architecture is evaluated to validate its viability on FPGAs within strict latency limits. This implementation utilizes an INT8-PO2 quantization technique coupled with a data-driven bit-width optimization. By replacing DSP-heavy fixed-point multiplications with fast compile-time arithmetic bit-shifts and allocating the minimum required bits per signal, this hardware-software co-design reduces DSP utilization to $20\%$ and achieves a deterministic inference latency of just $19$ clock cycles. Together, these developments pave the way for triggering on non-standard muon signatures, such as those from LLP decays, at L1T.

</details>


### [79] [Design optimization of the inter-electrode structures in DC-RSD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06676)
*N. Cartiglia,R. Arcidiacono,G. Bardelli,M. Boscardin,A. Cassese,M. Centis Vignali,T. Croci,M. Durando,M. Ferrero,A. Fondacci,S. Gaiero,O. Hammad Ali,L. Lanteri,A. Losana,L. Menzio,A. Morozzi,F. Moscatelli,D. Passeri,G. Paternoster,G. Sguazzoni,F. Siviero,V. Sola,L. Viliani*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该论文开发了基于ROOT的DCRSD_SurfaceSimulator，将电阻表面建模为二维基尔霍夫电阻网络（Kirchhoff resistive network），用于定量比较沟槽、电阻条与无结构三种电极间结构对C耦合电阻硅探测器（RSD）位置分辨率的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: C耦合电阻硅探测器（RSD）通过电阻n$^+$表面层在电极间共享电荷，而电极间结构（沟槽、电阻条或无结构）直接决定可实现的位置分辨率及其均匀性，但何种结构最优尚无定量答案。

Method: 开发DCRSD_SurfaceSimulator，将电阻表面建模为二维基尔霍夫电阻网络，求解电荷共享随击中位置、表面电阻率与电极间结构的变化，并用FBK DC-RSD传感器（300、500、1000 μm像素间距）的测试束数据进行验证。

Result: 模拟器正确复现了位置分辨率、其空间均匀性与信号共享模板。在1000 Ω/□表面电阻率下，电阻条在全部测试电阻（500–2000 Ω）与输入阻抗（0–100 Ω）范围内的分辨率均优于沟槽约25%。

Conclusion: 分辨率差异源于电荷共享分数（charge-sharing fraction）的空间梯度：沟槽产生的梯度明显更弱且更不均匀，尤其在远离像素中心处，而电阻条或裸电阻表面则更优。

Abstract: C-coupled Resistive Silicon Detectors (RSD) share charge among electrodes through a resistive n$^+$ surface layer, and the choice of inter-electrode structure --- a trench, a resistive strip, or no structure at all --- directly determines the achievable position resolution and its uniformity. This paper addresses the question of which structure best optimises the spatial resolution. To answer it quantitatively, DCRSD_SurfaceSimulator was developed, a ROOT-based tool that models the resistive surface as a two-dimensional Kirchhoff resistive network and solves for charge sharing as a function of hit position, surface resistivity, and inter-electrode structure. The simulator is validated against test-beam data from FBK DC-RSD sensors with 300, 500, and 1000~$μ$m pixel pitch, reproducing the position resolution, its spatial uniformity, and the signal-sharing templates correctly. Using the validated simulator, trenches, resistive strips, and no containment are then compared on position resolution and its spatial uniformity, inter-electrode resistance, and signal leakage to outer pixels. Resistive strips are found to outperform trenches in resolution by approximately 25\% for all tested strip resistances (500--2000~$Ω$) and input impedances (0--100~$Ω$), at a surface resistivity of 1000~$Ω/\square$, and trace the origin of this difference to the spatial gradient of the charge-sharing fraction: trenches produce a markedly weaker and less uniform gradient, particularly away from the pixel centre, than either a resistive strip or a bare resistive surface.

*Matthias Vigl,Nikita Pond,Jackson Barr,Alexander Froch,Dan Guest,Nicole Hartman,Michael Kagan,Lukas Heinrich*

TL;DR: 该工作提出一套系统性流程，用于在高能物理（high-energy physics, HEP）任务中推导稳健的缩放定律（scaling laws）并比较不同设计选择；该流程在玩具问题上验证后，应用于 ATLAS JetSet2（约 110 亿喷注）多任务 Transformer，覆盖算力受限与数据受限两种机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型等机器学习领域的进展多来自以训练投入预测性能的缩放定律，高能物理中也已观察到类似行为，因此需要系统化方法以进一步研究 HEP 任务中的缩放规律与设计取舍。

Result: 在数据受限机制下，首次预测了早停（early stopping）条件下模型规模、训练时长、学习率与批量大小的联合最优值；在算力最优缩放时，模型规模与数据集规模呈近似相等的 $\sqrt{C}$ 依赖，辅助目标（auxiliary objectives）在同等算力预算下降低主喷注分类损失，向更低层级数据扩展输入可系统性降低损失而缩放指数几乎不变。

Conclusion: 幂律（power-law）机制的出现本身由尺度决定：当数据集规模低于某阈值时，损失几乎不携带关于高算力缩放的信息，凸显大规模高质量全模拟数据集作为 HEP 缩放研究与基础模型（foundation models）发展基础的价值。

Abstract: Much of the recent progress in machine learning domains such as language models has come from scaling laws that predict performance as a function of training effort. In high-energy physics (HEP) similar behavior has now been observed. To aid further study, we present a systematic procedure to derive robust scaling laws and compare design choices on the relevant budget axes for HEP tasks. We first validate the full scaling trajectory on toy problems and then apply the procedure to multi-task transformers on the ~11 billion-jet ATLAS JetSet2 dataset, in both the compute- and data-constrained regimes. For the latter, we predict, to the best of our knowledge for the first time, the jointly optimal model size, training horizon, learning rate and batch size under early stopping. At compute-optimal scaling, we recover a near-equal $\sqrt{C}$ dependence of model and dataset size, and find that auxiliary objectives lower the primary jet-classification loss at equal compute budget. Expanding the inputs toward lower-level data systematically lowers the loss while leaving the scaling exponent nearly unchanged. The onset of the power-law regime is itself set by scale: below a threshold in dataset size the loss carries little information about high-compute scaling, underscoring the value of large, high-quality full-simulation datasets as a foundation for scaling studies and the development of foundation models in HEP.

</details>


### [81] [Reconfigurable On-Chip AI for Particle Physics Detectors with Logic Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06816)
*Mila Bileska,Lino Gerlach,Jennet Dickinson,Lindsey Gray,Giuseppe Di Guglielmo,Peter Elmer,Isobel Ojalvo*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该工作评估逻辑神经网络（logic neural networks, LNNs）用于粒子物理像素探测器（pixel detector）的片上（on-chip）推理，指出其资源效率可支撑更复杂模型、对辐射导致的随机比特翻转（bit flip）更鲁棒，并提出可重构（reconfigurable）LNN 以适应部署后的条件变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端数据率或资源受限场景使集中式处理不可行，端侧机器学习（on-device machine learning）因而愈发重要。粒子物理下一代像素探测器的高粒度与高读出频率导致数据率无法集中处理，且空间与功耗限制严格；同时辐射损伤会持续改变探测器响应，要求模型在部署后可重新训练与重构，而硬连线（hard-wired）方案通常只针对窄定义任务且难以适应数据分布变化。

Method: 面向像素探测器的片上推理评估 LNNs；LNNs 直接学习布尔逻辑（Boolean logic）而非传统算术运算，据称适合定制硅（custom silicon）部署。此外开展了可重构 LNNs 的新工作，使模型能在部署后适应变化的运行条件。

Result: LNNs 展现出优越的资源效率（resource efficiency），可能带来模型能力的跃升，使更复杂架构在可用硬件预算内可行；并显示对辐射诱导的随机比特翻转具有更高鲁棒性（robustness）。

Conclusion: 在粒子物理像素探测器的片上推理场景中，LNNs 有望兼顾资源效率与抗辐射鲁棒性，并通过可重构设计实现部署后的自适应，从而回应集中式处理不可行与辐射损伤导致的响应漂移问题。

Abstract: On-device machine learning is increasingly important in applications where extreme data rates or resource constraints make centralized processing infeasible. Logic neural networks (LNNs), which directly learn Boolean logic instead of conventional arithmetic operations, have recently emerged as a state-of-the-art approach for efficient inference and are particularly well suited for deployment in custom silicon. However, hard-wired solutions are typically optimized for narrowly defined tasks and lack the ability to adapt to changing data distributions. Next-generation pixel detectors in particle physics present a particularly challenging use case: their high granularity and readout frequency generate data rates that cannot be centrally processed, while stringent space and power constraints necessitate highly efficient on-chip inference. Moreover, radiation damage progressively alters detector response, requiring models that can be re-trained and reconfigured after deployment. In this work, we evaluate LNNs for on-chip inference in pixel detectors. We show that their superior resource efficiency may enable a step change in model capability, making more complex architectures feasible within the available hardware budget. LNNs also provide increased robustness against radiation-induced random bit flips. Finally, we present novel work on reconfigurable LNNs, enabling models to adapt to changing operating conditions after deployment.

</details>

### [82] [An optimal observable for polarization measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03855)
*Erik Bachmann,Mareen Hoppe*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出以部分子层面（parton-level）微分极化分数作为最优观测量，刻画极化截面测量的基本灵敏度极限，并通过神经网络学习其条件期望实现无分箱或有分箱推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矢量玻色子极化（vector-boson polarization）是电弱部门新物理的敏感探针，但稀有过程与受抑制的纵向贡献使得最优极化分离成为充分利用数据的关键。

Method: 将部分子层面微分极化分数定义为最优观测量，其条件期望保留全部实验可及极化信息；采用神经网络学习该条件期望，支持无分箱（unbinned）或有分箱（binned）推断，并测量其分布。

Result: 该分布测量将极化灵敏度与基准截面（fiducial cross section）的模型无关性结合，可探测超出极化贡献常数重标度（constant rescalings）的偏离；在包容性 ZZ 产生（inclusive ZZ production）中进行了演示。

Conclusion: 微分极化分数构成极化截面测量的基本灵敏度极限，为电弱部门新物理探测提供兼具最优灵敏度与模型无关性的框架。

Abstract: Vector-boson polarization provides a sensitive probe of new physics in the electroweak sector. Rare processes and suppressed longitudinal contributions make optimal polarization separation essential to exploit the available data. We show that parton-level differential polarization fractions define optimal observables and a fundamental sensitivity limit for polarized cross-section measurements. Their conditional expectations retain all experimentally accessible polarization information, yielding the best achievable sensitivity within the assumed model, and can be learned by neural networks for unbinned or binned inference. Measuring their distributions combines polarization sensitivity with the model independence of fiducial cross sections, probing deviations beyond constant rescalings of polarized contributions. We demonstrate the approach in inclusive ZZ production.

</details>

The generator, publicly available in the POWHEG BOX RES framework, describes the hadro-production of a $b$-jet and a leptonically decaying $W$-boson, accompanied by an additional jet, i.e. $pp\to \ell^{+} ν_{\ell}\, j_b\, j $ and $pp\to \ell^{-} \barν_{\ell}\, j_{\bar{b}}\, j $, where $j_b$ ($j_{\bar{b}}$) is a jet containing a bottom (anti)quark, and $j$ is a generic jet. Our matching is designed to combine the advantages of a five-flavour calculation with the description of bottom quark mass effects in final-state jets. Conclusion: 重味标记的重新诠释相对非味标记包容性双喷注搜索与常规单顶诠释提供了对该模型的互补敏感性，并在 λ₁-λ₂ 耦合平面得到具有约束力的 95% 置信水平排除限，为同时解释重子不对称与暗物质的重子数破坏模型提供了实验限制。

Abstract: A reinterpretation of ATLAS searches targeting $b$-tagged dijet resonances and monotop-alike signatures at a center-of-mass energy of 13 TeV is presented using $pp$ collision data corresponding to integrated luminosities of 139 $\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$. By exploiting heavy-flavor signatures and allowing for jet-rich final states, this reinterpretation provides complementary sensitivity to the model relative to non-flavor-tagged inclusive dijet searches and conventional monotop reinterpretations. Assuming the mediators decay either into a pair of down-type quarks or into a top quark and a dark matter candidate, we translate the experimental upper limits into the model's parameter space. 95% confidence level exclusion limits are derived in the $λ_1$-$λ_2$ coupling plane for benchmark mediator masses of 1.5, 1.75, and 2.0 TeV. For all three benchmark masses, the $b$-tagged dijet reinterpretation excludes $λ_1 > 0.45$, while the bottom-squark reinterpretation excludes the region with $λ_1 > 0.3$ and $λ_2 > 0.6$, within the respective validated parameter domains.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 基于 Z 极点处极化 Λ_b 的稀有衰变 Λ_b → Λ(→pπ^-)νν̄，推导微分衰变分布并提取五个独立角观测量（其中四个需要初始 Λ_b 极化）；结合格点量子色动力学（lattice QCD）形状因子给出标准模型（Standard Model）预言，并评估 FCC-ee 上的新物理敏感度。

Abstract: We study the rare decay $Λ_b \rightarrow Λ(\rightarrow pπ^-)ν\barν$ of polarized $Λ_b$ baryons produced at the $Z$ pole. We find that the differential decay distribution is characterized by five independent angular observables, four of which require initial $Λ_b$ polarization. While standard observables in mesonic $b \rightarrow s ν\barν$ decays ($B \rightarrow K^{(*)} ν\barν$, $B_s \rightarrow φν\barν$) probe only two combinations of short-distance Wilson coefficients, $|C_L + C_R|$ and $|C_L - C_R|$, the angular observables in $Λ_b \rightarrow Λ(\rightarrow pπ^-)ν\barν$ provide access to the orthogonal combinations $|C_R|^2 - |C_L|^2$ and $\text{Im}(C_L C_R^*)$. In particular, we identify a $T$-odd CP asymmetry, $A_5$, that directly probes CP-violating phases in the short-distance effective Hamiltonian. We present Standard Model predictions for the branching ratio and all angular observables using lattice QCD form factors, and evaluate the projected new physics sensitivity at the FCC-ee. Our results demonstrate that baryonic angular observables are essential for resolving parameter degeneracies and for completely characterizing the chiral and CP structure of possible new physics in $b \rightarrow s ν\barν$ transitions.

</details>


### [86] [Boosted top tagging via lepton-in-jet topology for vector-like quark searches in FCC-hh](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05104)
*Sanchari Bhattacharyya,Biplob Bhattacherjee,Kirana K. K.,Swagata Mukherjee*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于机器学习（machine learning）的框架，用于在100 TeV的FCC-hh上识别高洛伦兹boost的轻子衰变顶夸克（leptonic top quark）t→bℓν，并将标记器（tagger）应用于矢量样B′夸克（vector-like B′ quark）单产生的寻找，发现可达约7 TeV（窄宽度）或约10 TeV（放宽宽度）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在FCC-hh的高能环境下，顶夸克衰变产物因大洛伦兹boost而高度准直，轻子嵌入顶夸克喷注（top-quark jet）内部；此时依赖单个喷注成分（jet constituent）信息的成分级标记器是否仍保持LHC能量下的判别能力尚不确定，需要加以检验并构建适配的识别策略。

Conclusion: 在所研究的FCC-hh条件下，成分级标记器仍具与变量级方法相当的判别能力；所提出的标记与事件级搜索策略对3–10 TeV范围内矢量样B′夸克具有显著灵敏度，且发现范围对宽度要求敏感。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a machine-learning-based framework for identifying leptonically decaying, highly boosted top quarks, $t\to b\ellν$, at the Future Circular Collider: hadron-hadron mode (FCC-hh) operating at a center-of-mass energy of $\sqrt{s}=100$~TeV. At these energies, the large Lorentz boost causes the top-quark decay products to become highly collimated, with the lepton embedded within the resulting top-quark jet. In this regime, it is not a priori clear whether constituent-based jet taggers, which rely on information from individual jet constituents, retain the high discrimination power achieved at LHC energies. To answer this question, we employ two complementary approaches: a variable-based tagger trained with a deep neural network (DNN) using physics-motivated observables and a constituent-based Particle Transformer (ParT) acting on the jet constituents. Both approaches are optimised for the characteristic non-isolated lepton signature of boosted leptonic top decays and achieve comparable performance in discriminating leptonic top jets from hadronic top jets and heavy-flavor $b$- and $c$-jet backgrounds. We then incorporate the tagging framework into an event-level search for single production of a vector-like $B^\prime$ quark decaying through $B^\prime\to tW$, and use it to evaluate sensitivity to $B^\prime$ in the range of $3$ to $10$~TeV. For the reference $B^\prime$ benchmark, the proposed search strategy achieves a $5σ$ discovery reach of approximately $7$~TeV under the width requirement $Γ_{B^\prime}/m_{B^\prime}<25\%$, in both the statistics-only and $10\%$ background systematic uncertainty scenarios. Relaxing the width requirement to $Γ_{B^\prime}/m_{B^\prime}\lesssim 50\%$ extends the discovery reach to approximately $10$~TeV.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LHC数据中出现的95 GeV与650 GeV过量聚集现象需要一致的理论解释；同时存在一种普遍成见认为KK引力子共振质量极高、超出LHC能区，作者意在反驳这一成见并解释相关信号被忽视的原因。

Method: 作者提出以Randall-Sundrum KK引力子共振序列解释650 GeV信号及若干附加迹象，并分析现有搜索为何排除2 TeV以下此类粒子，指出其选择条件仅对标量共振（scalar resonance）有效，从而漏掉KK引力子情形；进而建议在ZZ、hh、WW等通道中补充特定末态选择。

Result: 作者认为650 GeV共振可能是KK引力子之一，而非被现有搜索排除的对象；现有分析因套用仅适用于标量共振的选择而忽视该情景，诸如衰变为SM希格斯对（SM Higgs pairs）的共振搜索也未考虑KK引力子情景。

Conclusion: 若采用正确处理，ZZ与hh通道的探测效率可显著提升，并有望在现有数据下获得有力证据；应将h(95)h(125)与h(95)h(95)加入现有选择条件，并利用ZZ、WW通道的半轻子（semi-leptonic）与纯强子（purely hadronic）末态，确认延伸至3 TeV的KK共振序列，从而可能实现发现。

Abstract: Evidence for excesses observed at LHC tend to cluster mostly around 95 GeV and 650 GeV. We claim that a consequent interpretation of the 650 GeV and some additional indications calls for a Randall Sundrum sequence of Kaluza Klein graviton resonances. A common prejudice claims that such resonances should be very heavy, out of reach for LHC. We argue that this is no so. Although present LHC searches seem to exclude these particles below 2 TeV, we explain why this 650 GeV resonance could be one of them. We also explain why ongoing analyses ignore this scenario and apply selections which are only valid for scalar resonances. This could also apply to ongoing searches for Higgs pairs where the search for resonances decaying into SM Higgs pairs ignores the KK graviton scenario. We expect that with a correct treatment, one could very significantly increase the detection efficiency for ZZ and hh channels and achieve, with present data, a convincing proof of our expectations. Channels like h(95)h(125) and h(95)h(95) should be added to present selections. Such improvements should allow to reach a discovery. For the ZZ and WW channels, one can use semi-leptonic and purely hadronic final states, to confirm the tower of KK resonances indicated up to 3 TeV.

</details>


### [88] [Learning Astrophysical Uncertainties in Dark Matter Direct Detection with Simulation-Based Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06159)
*Felix Kahlhoefer,Niklas Reus*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文将神经比率估计（neural ratio estimation, NRE）应用于 XENONnT 的 WIMP 直接探测，验证其作为基于模拟推断（simulation-based inference）方法在质量 $m_\chi$ 与自旋无关质子耦合 $c_p$ 推断上的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于似然比（likelihood ratio）的推断依赖显式似然建模，且在实验深入中微子雾（neutrino fog）时面临扩展性挑战；同时晕模型（halo model）等天体物理不确定性需要额外处理成本。

Method: 以二分类器在模拟的参数-观测对上训练，逼近 $m_\chi$ 与 $c_p$ 的似然比，观测数据来自 fuse 探测器模拟生成的闪烁与电离信号；通过在训练数据中纳入多个晕模型实现天体物理不确定性的隐式边缘化（marginalization）。

Result: 训练于组合数据集时，即使在 $\pm5\sigma$ 极端标准晕模型（Standard Halo Model）变化下仍可恢复良好校准的后验，而更真实的晕模型给出的结果近乎无差异；信号加本底分类器给出的排除上限与 XENONnT 预期灵敏度在定性上一致，且在考虑频率派与贝叶斯方法差异后定量一致。

Conclusion: 结果表明 NRE 可作为剖面似然比（profile likelihood ratio）方法的可扩展补充，在实验进一步触及中微子雾时具有特别优势。

Abstract: We present a proof-of-concept study of neural ratio estimation (NRE) - a simulation-based inference algorithm - applied to WIMP direct detection using the XENONnT experiment as an example. A binary classifier is trained on simulated parameter-observation pairs to approximate the likelihood ratio for the WIMP mass $m_χ$ and spin-independent proton coupling $c_p$, using realistic scintillation and ionization signals produced by the fuse detector simulation. We demonstrate that astrophysical uncertainties from halo model variations are marginalized over implicitly and at no additional inference cost by including multiple halo models in the training data. Using extreme $\pm5σ$ Standard Halo Model variations, we show that training on a combined dataset recovers well-calibrated posteriors even under severe misspecification, while more realistic halo models produce nearly indistinguishable results. The signal-plus-background classifier yields an exclusion limit that is both qualitatively and (after accounting for differences between frequentist and Bayesian approaches) quantitatively consistent with the XENONnT expected sensitivity. These results establish NRE as a scalable complement to the profile likelihood ratio approach, with particular advantages as experiments probe deeper into the neutrino fog.

</details>


### [89] [Phenomenological Estimate for the Longitudinal Unpolarized Structure Function in Semi-inclusive Deep Inelastic Scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06628)
*Alessandro Bacchetta,Matteo Cerutti,Leonard Gamberg,Richard Whitehill*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文对半包含深度非弹性散射（semi-inclusive deep inelastic scattering, SIDIS）中纵向非极化结构函数 $F_{UU,L}$ 及其与横向结构函数之比 $R_{\,\rm SIDIS}=F_{UU,L}/F_{UU,T}$ 进行唯象研究，首次结合低与高横向动量（transverse momentum）贡献，指出 $F_{UU,L}$ 在低横向动量区可显著，并对横向动量积分观测量产生约 $10\%$ 的修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于刻画 $F_{UU,L}$ 与 $R_{\,\rm SIDIS}$ 的横向动量依赖，并填补以往分析中未同时纳入低与高横向动量贡献的空白，从而评估纵向贡献对 SIDIS 测量解释的重要性。

Method: 采用唯象（phenomenological）方法研究 $F_{UU,L}$ 与 $R_{\,\rm SIDIS}$，在 SIDIS 测量相关运动学范围内首次将低横向动量与高横向动量贡献相结合；低横向动量处 $F_{UU,L}$ 受幂次压低（power suppressed），使其对非极化扇区的次次领头幂（next-to-next-to-leading-power）动力学敏感。

Result: 该贡献可能相当可观：在低横向动量与硬标度（hard scale）下 $R_{\,\rm SIDIS}\simeq 0.3$，并对横向动量积分观测量产生量级约 $10\%$ 的修正；文中指出这些估计仍受理论不确定性（theoretical uncertainties）影响。

Conclusion: 结果提示，纳入 $F_{UU,L}$ 很可能对 SIDIS 多重数（multiplicities）的解释具有重要性，并为进一步约束纵向贡献，建议开展针对 $R_{\,\rm SIDIS}$ 的专门测量。

Abstract: We present a phenomenological study of the longitudinal unpolarized structure function $F_{UU,L}$ in semi-inclusive deep inelastic scattering (SIDIS) and its ratio with the transverse structure function, $R_{\, \rm SIDIS}=F_{UU,L}/F_{UU,T}$. We study their transverse-momentum dependence, combining for the first time low- and high-transverse-momentum contributions across the kinematic range relevant to SIDIS measurements. At low transverse momentum, $F_{UU,L}$ is power suppressed, making it sensitive to next-to-next-to-leading-power dynamics in the unpolarized sector. We find that this contribution can be sizable, with $R_{\, \rm SIDIS}\simeq 0.3$ at low transverse momentum and hard scale, producing a correction of the order of $10\%$ to transverse-momentum-integrated observables. Although these estimates remain subject to theoretical uncertainties, our results indicate that accounting for $F_{UU,L}$ is likely to be important for the interpretation of SIDIS multiplicities and motivate dedicated measurements of $R_{\, \rm SIDIS}$ to better constrain the longitudinal contribution.

</details>


### [90] [Novel dependence between neutrino mass splittings strongly supported by initial JUNO results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06738)
*I. Alikhanov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 基于 JUNO 合作组最新测量结果，检验了近期经验性预测的中微子（neutrino）振荡参数关系式；实验数据给出 1.4143，与 √2 预测高度吻合，为中心值偏差小于 0.03 个标准差提供强有力支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在标准三味（three-flavor）框架下，三个中微子质量平方差（mass-squared difference）中仅两个相互独立，而近期有研究基于经验理由预测这两个振荡参数间还存在额外依赖关系。该预测此前缺乏高精度实验检验，JUNO 的高精度测量为验证提供了契机。

Method: 采用 JUNO 合作组迄今最精确的 Δm²₂₁ 与 Δm²₃₁ 测量结果，计算预测关系式左端表达式，并与理论预测值 √2 进行对比，评估中心值偏差与相对精度。

Result: 实验数据给出该比例为 1.4143₋₀.₀₀₃₈⁺⁰·⁰⁰³⁶，对应相对精度 0.27%；其中心值与预测值 √2 ≈ 1.4142 的偏差小于 0.03 个标准差（standard deviation），显示出极好的一致性。

Conclusion: 结果为该新约束提供了强有力的实验支持，暗示中微子质量谱（neutrino mass spectrum）可能受某种潜在对称性（symmetry）支配，而非由完全独立的参数描述。

Abstract: In the standard three-flavor paradigm, two of the three neutrino mass-squared differences are independent. The existence of an additional dependence between these oscillation parameters was recently predicted on empirical grounds, taking the form $$\frac{\sqrt{Δm^2_{31}}+\sqrt{Δm^2_{21}}}{\sqrt{Δm^2_{31}}-\sqrt{Δm^2_{21}}}=\sqrt{2}.$$ The JUNO Collaboration has now reported the most precise measurements to date of both $Δm^2_{21}$ and $Δm^2_{31}$. We demonstrate that this relation is in excellent agreement with the experimental data, which yield $1.4143_{-0.0038}^{+0.0036}$ corresponding to a relative precision of $0.27\%$. Remarkably, the central value differs by less than $0.03$ standard deviations from the prediction ($\sqrt{2}\approx1.4142$). This provides strong experimental support for the novel constraint and thereby suggests an underlying symmetry governing the neutrino mass spectrum, rather than purely independent parameters.

</details>


### [91] [No Einstein Relation, No Problem: How Relativistic Weakly Coupled Heavy Quarks Equilibrate Beyond Leading Logarithm Anyway](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03863)
*Jean F. Du Plessis,Bruno Scheihing-Hitschfeld*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 重夸克（heavy quark）在微观拖曳（drag）与动量扩散（momentum diffusion）违反爱因斯坦关系（Einstein relation）时仍能达到平衡。本文在弱耦合非阿贝尔等离子体（non-Abelian plasma）中严格计算至 \(\mathcal{O}(g^4)\) 的动量转移核（momentum-transfer kernel），揭示其非高斯（non-Gaussian）结构对平衡过程的关键作用，并与强耦合 \(\mathcal{N}=4\) SYM 结果作对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重夸克平衡过程通常依赖爱因斯坦关系的假设与简化 Fokker-Planck 模型，而实际微观拖曳与动量扩散可违反该关系。作者旨在从第一性原理场论出发，厘清平衡如何随耦合强度演化，并检验简化模型的适用边界。

Method: 在弱耦合非阿贝尔等离子体中，严格计算至 \(\mathcal{O}(g^4)\) 阶的动量转移核，并在重夸克质量倒数（inverse heavy-quark mass）领头阶研究其动力学。进一步分析演化核（evolution kernel）的解析结构、动量转移累积量（cumulants）的大阶渐近行为，并与强耦合 \(\mathcal{N}=4\) SYM 的核性质作比较。

Result: 超出领头对数（leading logarithm）后，核的非高斯结构对平衡起关键作用：核的有界解析域决定了动量转移概率分布的不对称指数尾（exponential tails）。强耦合 \(\mathcal{N}=4\) SYM 亦呈现近似高斯核心与指数尾，但其核奇异性性质、输运系数与尾部指数（tail exponents）的速度依赖不同。动力学后果有别：极弱耦合下陡降谱中相对论性粒子群的持续主要由拖曳解释；强耦合下涨落诱导的存活者偏置（survivor bias）则带来较大相对增强。

Conclusion: 弱耦合与强耦合虽在微观机制与速度依赖上存在差异，但共享的定性结构为重夸克提供了以第一性原理场论为基础的实用唯象描述（phenomenological description）途径。

Abstract: Relativistic heavy quarks equilibrate even when their microscopic drag and momentum diffusion violate the Einstein relation. We investigate how equilibration proceeds as a function of the coupling and compared to simplified Fokker-Planck models. To do this, we present a detailed calculation of the momentum-transfer kernel in weakly coupled non-Abelian plasmas through strict $\mathcal{O}(g^4)$, and study the resulting dynamics at leading order in the inverse heavy-quark mass. Beyond leading logarithm, its non-Gaussian structure plays a crucial role in equilibration. We examine closely the analytic structure of the evolution kernel, determine the large-order asymptotics of the momentum-transfer cumulants, and show how the kernel's bounded analytic domain fixes asymmetric exponential tails at the level of the momentum transfer probability. Strongly coupled $\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM also features an approximately Gaussian core and exponential tails, but differs in the nature of the kernel's singularities and the velocity dependence of the transport coefficients and tail exponents. These differences have distinct dynamical consequences: for the parameters studied, the persistence of the relativistic population in steeply falling spectra is largely explained by drag alone at very weak coupling, whereas fluctuation-induced survivor bias produces a large relative enhancement at strong coupling. Nevertheless, the shared qualitative structure between weak and strong coupling points the way towards a practical phenomenological description of heavy quarks anchored in first-principles field theory.

</details>


### [92] [A non-invertible Fibonacci selection rule for two-Higgs-doublet models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06014)
*Yu Hamada*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在双希格斯二重态模型（two-Higgs-doublet model, 2HDM）中，以源自弦紧化（string compactification）的非可逆 Fibonacci 选择定则（non-invertible Fibonacci selection rule）替代传统离散 $\mathbb{Z}_2$ 对称性，可在匹配标度 $Λ$ 处抑制味改变（flavor-changing）相互作用，并导出可被 HL-LHC 检验的希格斯物理信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准模型（Standard Model, SM）之外的 2HDM 通常依靠离散 $\mathbb{Z}_2$ 对称性抑制树级味改变中性流（flavor-changing neutral current），但该机制缺乏更基础的理论依据。本文受弦紧化启发，尝试用非可逆选择定则实现同一目的，并同时处理附加希格斯粒子质量与宇宙畴壁（cosmological domain wall）问题。

Method: 在匹配标度 $Λ$ 处施加非可逆 Fibonacci 选择定则，禁止通常允许附加希格斯粒子任意变重的独立质量参数；通过重整化群演化（renormalization-group evolution）在 $Λ$ 以下重新生成该参数。同时引入一个允许的 $\mathbb{Z}_2$ 破缺四重相互作用（quartic interaction）以规避稳定的宇宙畴壁。在精确对齐极限（exact alignment limit）下，要求微扰幺正性（perturbative unitarity）与势的下界性（boundedness from below）直至匹配标度成立，从而约束参数空间。

Result: 上述幺正性与有界性条件限定了三个非 SM 希格斯态的质量；当 $Λ=10^{19}\,\mathrm{GeV}$ 时，其质量低于约 $150\,\mathrm{GeV}$。对参数空间的扫描预测，希格斯双光子（diphoton）信号强度受到压低，且落在 HL-LHC 的预期探测范围内。

Conclusion: 结果表明，弦论中的非可逆选择定则可在希格斯物理中留下可检验的印记（testable signature），为 2HDM 的味结构与质量起源提供了一种有别于传统 $\mathbb{Z}_2$ 对称性的理论框架。

Abstract: We study two-Higgs-doublet models, extensions of the Standard Model (SM) containing two Higgs doublets rather than one. Tree-level Higgs-mediated interactions that change quark or lepton flavor are conventionally suppressed by a discrete $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry. Motivated by string compactifications, we instead impose a non-invertible Fibonacci selection rule at a matching scale $Λ$ for that purpose. This rule also forbids the independent mass parameter that normally allows the additional Higgs particles to become arbitrarily heavy, while renormalization-group evolution regenerates it below $Λ$. An allowed $\mathbb{Z}_2$-breaking quartic interaction simultaneously avoids stable cosmological domain walls. In the exact alignment limit, requiring perturbative unitarity and boundedness from below up to the matching scale bounds the masses of the three non-SM Higgs states; for $Λ=10^{19}\,\mathrm{GeV}$, they lie below about $150\,\mathrm{GeV}$. The scanned parameter space predicts a suppressed Higgs diphoton signal strength within the projected reach of the HL-LHC. This illustrates how a stringy non-invertible selection rule can leave testable signatures in Higgs physics.

</details>

### [93] [Reaction mechanisms of sodium carbonate activation in ultra-high-volume slag-cement blends](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03748)
*Samira Hossain,Zhanzhao Li,Kai Gong*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 在90 wt.%矿渣+10 wt.%硅酸盐水泥（PC）的单组分体系中干混入Na2CO3（0-20 wt.%），发现碳酸盐介导的碱度持久性（alkalinity persistence）是维持超高矿渣掺量体系中矿渣持续反应与强度发展的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超高掺量矿渣-水泥（UHVS）体系可大幅降低熟料比例，但矿渣反应缓慢制约强度发展；Na2CO3虽为低腐蚀性、低碳活化剂，其碱度适中亦会延迟强度发展，故需探究将二者整合能否缓解该问题。

Method: 采用抗压强度测试、等温量热（isothermal calorimetry）、时间分辨定量X射线衍射、傅里叶变换红外光谱（FTIR）、水溶液分析与热力学建模（thermodynamic modeling）相结合，考察Na2CO3如何控制物相组合、碱度、计算孔隙率与强度发展。

Result: 5-10 wt.% Na2CO3时，方解石主导的碳酸盐反应促进碱度快速上升，加速早龄期反应与强度发展，但随后碱度下降限制后期矿渣反应与凝胶生成；20 wt.% Na2CO3则促进gaylussite与碳酸盐-AFm相早期大量生成，抑制初始pH上升、延缓早期凝胶积累，却因更大碱储量与物相-溶液分配演化维持后期碱度，实现矿渣持续溶解与凝胶生成，反应程度更高、计算孔隙率更低，抗压强度接近纯PC。筛选级从摇篮到大门生命周期评估（cradle-to-gate LCA）显示，相对PC全球变暖潜势（GWP）降低66-72%。

Conclusion: 碳酸盐介导的碱度持久性控制是维持UHVS体系中矿渣持续反应的关键，20 wt.% Na2CO3路径可在保持强度的同时显著降低碳排放。

Abstract: Ultra-high-volume slag-cement (UHVS) blends can substantially reduce clinker ratio, but slow slag reaction limits strength development; Na2CO3 is a less caustic and lower-carbon activator for alkali-activated materials, yet its moderate alkalinity can similarly delay strength development. Here, dry-blended Na2CO3 (0-20 wt.% relative to water) was incorporated into a one-part binder with 90 wt.% ground-granulated blast-furnace slag and 10 wt.% Portland cement (PC) to determine whether this integration could mitigate slow strength development in both systems. Compressive strength testing, isothermal calorimetry, time-resolved quantitative X-ray diffraction, Fourier-transform infrared spectroscopy, aqueous solution analysis, and thermodynamic modeling were combined to determine how Na2CO3 controls phase assemblage, alkalinity, calculated porosity and strength development. At 5-10 wt.% Na2CO3, calcite-dominated carbonate reactions promoted a rapid alkalinity increase and accelerated early-age reaction and strength development; however, subsequent alkalinity decline limited later-age slag reaction and gel formation. In contrast, 20 wt.% Na2CO3 favored substantial early formation of gaylussite and carbonate-AFm phases, which moderated the initial pH rise and delayed early gel accumulation. The larger alkali inventory and evolving phase-solution partitioning helped sustain alkalinity at later ages, enabling continued slag dissolution and gel formation. This pathway produced higher reaction extent and lower calculated porosities, with compressive strength approaching that of neat PC. Screening-level cradle-to-gate life-cycle assessment indicates a 66-72% reduction in global warming potential relative to PC, depending on Na2CO3 production pathways. These results identify carbonate-mediated control of alkalinity persistence as key to sustaining slag reaction in UHVS systems.

</details>


### [94] [Dielectric Response of Short and Long Range Models of Nonuniform Liquids and Implications for Machine Learned Interatomic Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04224)
*Atul C. Thakur,Harender S. Dhattarwal,Richard C. Remsing*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该研究借助局域分子场（local molecular field, LMF）理论，量化受限水（confined water）中短程与长程静电的作用，揭示短程模型在纵向介电响应上与长程模型存在定性差异，并推导出适用于LMF理论的涨落关系与积分方程以准确预测非均匀介电响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 界面上电场介电屏蔽（dielectric screening）因界面与受限效应而改变，准确描述界面化学需正确建模非均匀介电张量。然而局部机器学习原子间势（machine learned interatomic potentials, MLIPs）等短程相互作用模型被广泛用于分子界面模拟，其非均匀介电响应缺乏定量理解。

Method: 采用局域分子场（LMF）理论框架，区分短程与长程静电在受限水中的作用；发展朗道理论（Landau theory）解释长程相互作用对极化涨落（polarization fluctuations）的硬化效应；推导适用于LMF理论的涨落关系与积分方程；并考察短程MLIPs与对称保持平均场（symmetry preserving mean field, SPMF）理论的结合。

Result: 短程与长程模型预测出相同的横向介电剖面（transverse dielectric profile），但短程模型预测的纵向介电剖面（longitudinal dielectric profile）与长程模型存在定性差异。朗道理论表明长程相互作用以体相介电常数（bulk dielectric constant）为因子硬化极化涨落，并将界面极化关联长度缩短一个数量级。推导的涨落关系与积分方程可准确预测纵向介电响应；包含界面构型的MLIPs本质上学习到界面静态平均场修正，可用积分方程由极化涨落预测其非均匀介电剖面。

Conclusion: LMF理论可修正结构与介电响应，但标准涨落关系不再成立，需采用新推导的涨落关系与积分方程。短程MLIPs可结合SPMF理论，为长程相互作用及非均匀介电函数的预测提供线性标度（linear-scaling）的替代方案。

Abstract: Dielectric screening of electric fields is modified by interfaces and confinement, and any accurate description of interfacial chemistry necessitates properly modeling the nonuniform dielectric tensor. However, models with short range interactions, such as local machine learned interatomic potentials (MLIPs), are increasingly used in simulations of molecular interfaces without quantitative understanding of their nonuniform dielectric response. To build this understanding, we use the framework provided by local molecular field (LMF) theory to quantify the roles of short and long range electrostatics in confined water. Short and long range models predict the same transverse dielectric profile but short range models predict qualitatively different longitudinal dielectric profiles than their long range counterparts. To understand these differences, we develop a Landau theory that shows that long range interactions stiffen polarization fluctuations by a factor of the bulk dielectric constant and reduce the interfacial polarization correlation length by an order of magnitude. LMF theory, which captures long range interactions with an averaged field, can correct the structure and dielectric response, but the standard fluctuation relation no longer holds. We derive a fluctuation relation and integral equation for LMF theory and demonstrate their accuracy for predicting longitudinal dielectric response. MLIPs that incorporate interfacial configurations in their training essentially learn a static mean field correction at the interface, such that the integral equation can be used to predict their nonuniform dielectric profile from polarization fluctuations. We also show that short range MLIPs can be combined with symmetry preserving mean field (SPMF) theory to provide a linear-scaling alternative for long range interactions and predicting nonuniform dielectric functions.

</details>
