# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [95] [Interplay between Excitability and Noise in Analog Spiking Neurons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06720)
*Léopold Van Brandt,Alon Ascoli,Michele Bonnin,Grégoire Brandsteert,Denis Flandre,Jean-Charles Delvenne*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 该工作利用基于工业物理的瞬态噪声 SPICE 仿真框架，在面向神经形态计算的 CMOS 模拟脉冲神经元中重现了生物神经元在时变刺激下放电时间更可靠的现象，并借助随机热力学不确定性关系揭示了振荡（速率编码）模式在可靠性与耗散权衡上的物理限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bryant 与 Segundo、Mainen 与 Sejnowski 的神经科学实验表明，部分生物神经元在时变刺激下的放电时间比恒定刺激下更可靠。作者希望验证 CMOS 模拟脉冲神经元是否也具备该特性，并厘清其在神经形态硬件中的可靠性—耗散权衡问题。

Method: 采用与代工厂晶体管紧凑模型（compact model）兼容的工业级基于物理的瞬态噪声 SPICE 仿真框架，对设计用于神经形态计算硬件平台的 CMOS 模拟脉冲神经元进行仿真；并引入随机热力学不确定性关系（stochastic thermodynamic uncertainty relation）作为理论分析工具。

Result: 在恒定激励电流下，神经元处于振荡区，其经验周期（脉冲间间隔）在连续循环中因晶体管热噪声而累积相位噪声（抖动，jitter）。时变输入可通过受控状态转移机制触发输出脉冲，从而在兴奋性区显著降低脉冲时间抖动。

Conclusion: CMOS 模拟脉冲神经元可重现生物神经元中时变刺激提升放电时间可靠性的行为；随机热力学不确定性关系（适用于一般过阻尼耗散随机动力学系统）进一步佐证了振荡或速率编码（rate-coding）模式在可靠性—耗散权衡方面存在物理限制。

Abstract: A spiking neuron is a dynamical system exhibiting a limit cycle (a spike) when subject to sufficiently excitatory input. Famous neuroscience experiments by Bryant and Segundo as well as Mainen and Sejnowski revealed that the spike times of some biological neurons are more reliable when subject to time-varying stimuli compared to constant ones. Provided with an industrial physics-based transient noise SPICE simulation framework compatible with foundry transistor compact models, we have demonstrated similar behaviours for a CMOS analog spiking neuron designed for neuromorphic computing hardware platforms. Under constant excitation current, the neuron operates in oscillatory regime and the empirical periods (interspike intervals) suffer from accumulated phase noise (jitter) during successive cycles, mainly due to the thermal noise of transistors. A time-varying input is reported to be capable of triggering output spikes according to a controlled statetransition mechanism, thereby significantly reducing the spike time jitter in the excitability regime. The physical limitation of the oscillatory or rate-coding regime in terms of reliabilitydissipation tradeoff is further evidenced by a stochastic thermodynamic uncertainty relation, a theorem that applies to general overdamped dissipative stochastic dynamical systems.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mtrl-sci'></div>

# cond-mat.mtrl-sci [[Back]](#toc)

### [96] [Space Charge Layer and Facile Halide Rearrangement Enable Fast Lithium-Ion Transport at Halide Solid Electrolyte Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03821)
*Md Salman Rabbi Limon,Matthew Beltran,Boyu Wang,Laisuo Su,Zeeshan Ahmad*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究结合密度泛函理论（DFT）、缺陷热力学、机器学习驱动分子动力学（MLMD）与振动/拉曼光谱，阐明卤化物电解质 LZC 与 LYC 异质界面（heterointerface）提升锂离子电导率的原子尺度机理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卤化物固态电解质（halide solid electrolytes）兼具高电压稳定性、易加工与快锂离子传导，但 LZC 与 LYC 物理混合后电导率提升 46% 与 58% 的界面增强机制尚不明确。该工作旨在揭示其来源。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论计算、缺陷热力学、机器学习驱动分子动力学，以及振动与拉曼光谱（Raman spectroscopy）实验相结合的手段；并使用代表构型无序的低能有序超胞（supercell）建模 LZC、LYC 及其相干界面。

Result: Li 与 Cl 间隙缺陷（interstitials）为两种材料中的主导带电缺陷；LZC|LYC 界面促进界面 Li Frenkel 对（Frenkel pairs）生成，在 LZC 中产生 Li 间隙、在 LYC 中产生 Li 空位，提高界面附近载流子浓度。分子动力学轨迹中 Cl 取向分析显示界面形成新的稳定 Cl 构型，可容纳 Li 跳跃引起的骨架重排；模拟振动功率谱与实测拉曼谱表明界面存在晶格软化与八面体畸变，与此一致。

Conclusion: 结果支持一种协同机制：空间电荷层（space charge layer）形成与动态柔性的界面卤化物骨架共同增强 Li 离子传导，为超离子卤化物异质结构（superionic halide heterostructures）与多组分固态电解质提供原子尺度设计原则。

Abstract: Halide solid electrolytes are promising candidates for energy-dense all-solid-state batteries because they combine high-voltage stability, facile processing, and fast Li-ion conduction. A recent study showed that physically mixing two halide solid electrolytes, Li$_2$ZrCl$_6$ (LZC) and Li$_3$YCl$_6$ (LYC), raises the Li-ion conductivity by 46% and 58% relative to LZC and LYC, respectively, suggesting that halide heterointerfaces offer a new route to faster ion transport; however, the origin of this enhancement remains unknown. Here, we combine density functional theory calculations, defect thermodynamics, machine-learning-driven molecular dynamics, and vibrational and Raman spectroscopy to elucidate Li-ion transport in LZC, LYC, and their heterointerface. Using low-energy ordered supercells that represent the configurational disorder of these halides, we find that Li and Cl interstitials are the dominant charged defects in both materials. Forming an LZC|LYC interface favors the generation of interfacial Li Frenkel pairs, producing Li interstitials in LZC and Li vacancies in LYC, thereby increasing the charge-carrier concentration near the interface. By analyzing the angular orientations of Cl around the metal cations in molecular dynamics trajectories of a coherent interface, we show that the interface creates new stable Cl configurations that accommodate the framework rearrangement accompanying Li hops. Simulated vibrational power spectra and measured Raman spectra indicate lattice softening and octahedral distortion at the interface, consistent with the formation of these Cl configurations. These results support a cooperative mechanism in which space charge layer formation and a dynamically flexible interfacial halide framework enhance Li-ion conduction, providing atomistic design principles for superionic halide heterostructures and multicomponent solid electrolytes.

</details>


### [97] [Equivariant generative diffusion learns and generalizes the structural ensemble of amorphous oxides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03973)
*Jun Jiang,Ian Berry,James N. Fry,Hai-Ping Cheng*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出一种 $SE(3)$ 等变去噪扩散模型，直接学习非晶氧化物的构型分布，使模型本身充当结构数据库；仅用 1,781 个 DFT 构型训练即可复现结构统计量并生成超过 $3\times10^{5}$ 原子的大尺度模型，且可外推至非化学计量比、不同密度、界面与掺杂等条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非晶材料本质上是统计系综（statistical ensembles）而非确定结构，而传统密度泛函理论（DFT）与机器学习势（machine-learned potential）模拟仅采样该系综的一小部分。因此需要一个能够学习并表征整个构型分布的方法。

Method: 采用 $SE(3)$ 等变（$SE(3)$-equivariant）的去噪扩散模型（denoising-diffusion model）学习非晶氧化物的构型分布，训练具有数据高效性，仅使用 1,781 个 DFT 构型。模型可用于提出候选非晶结构以供第一性原理弛豫（first-principles relaxation）探索构型空间。

Result: 训练后的模型能复现部分径向分布函数（partial radial distribution functions）、配位数统计与键角分布，并生成超过 $3\times10^{5}$ 原子的模型，成本与最廉价的经典对势（classical pair potentials）相当。模型定位到比已知最低能量低 36 meV/atom 的非晶 Zr-Ta-O 结构；生成能力可外推至非化学计量组成、其他质量密度、界面与掺杂。第一性原理验证表明生成可被引导至目标能量，且去噪训练损失（denoising training loss）不能排序生成质量，因为二者度量不同对象。

Conclusion: 该工作表明生成式扩散模型可充当非晶材料的构型数据库，以数据高效方式捕捉构型系综并支持大规模生成与条件外推，同时提示训练损失与生成质量之间不存在直接对应关系。

Abstract: Amorphous materials are statistical ensembles rather than definitive structures, and conventional density-functional (DFT) and machine-learned-potential simulations sample only a small part of that ensemble. We present an $SE(3)$-equivariant denoising-diffusion model that learns the configurational distribution of amorphous oxides, so the model itself is the structure database. The learning is data efficient. A model trained on $1{,}781$ DFT configurations suffices to reproduce partial radial distribution functions, coordination statistics and bond-angle distributions, and to generate models of over $3\times10^{5}$ atoms at a cost comparable to that of the cheapest classical pair potentials. The trained model can propose amorphous atomic structures for first-principles relaxation to explore the configuration space. For example, it locates an amorphous Zr-Ta-O structure $36$ meV/atom below the previously known minimum. Generation can also extend beyond trained conditions to non-stoichiometric compositions, other mass densities, interfaces, and doping. First-principles verification confirms that generation can be steered to a requested energy, and shows that the denoising training loss does not rank generative quality, because the two measure different things.

</details>


### [98] [Studying the evolution of exchange and dipolar interactions and spin correlation in vdW magnet CrCl3 across magnetic phases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04080)
*Prem B. Karki,Ayodimeji E. Aregbesola,Karishma Prasad,Jonathan Skeels,Avani Inamdar,Pooja Kalal,Jian Wang,Kapildeb Ambal**

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究利用多频宽带（1-16 GHz）磁共振技术，研究范德华磁体 CrCl3 块体单晶中交换相互作用与偶极-偶极相互作用的温度演化，发现交换作用随降温呈指数增强，而偶极作用保持恒定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解范德华磁体（van der Waals magnets）中的量子相互作用，对于实现其在下一代磁性、电子与自旋电子学（spintronic）应用中的潜力至关重要。

Method: 对块体单晶 CrCl3 样品开展多频宽带（multifrequency broadband，1-16 GHz）磁共振（magnetic resonance）测量，以研究交换（exchange）与偶极-偶极（dipole-dipole）相互作用的演化。

Result: 偶极展宽的低场共振线宽在较高共振场因交换窄化（exchange narrowing）而变窄；交换相互作用在室温降至 50 K 过程中呈指数演化，而偶极-偶极相互作用在同一温区保持恒定。室温下检测到半场磁共振跃迁（half-field magnetic resonance transition），测得自旋间距为 2.4(3) Å。

Conclusion: 偶极相互作用的演化、半场磁共振的观测以及强交换作用表明，CrCl3 在远高于全局磁有序温度的温区即存在短程有序/关联（short-range ordering/correlation）；该研究亦展示了多频电子自旋共振（electron spin resonance）技术在研究范德华磁性材料量子相互作用与磁性关联方面的敏感性与重要性。

Abstract: Understanding quantum interactions in van der Waals magnets are critical to realizing their potential for next-generation magnetic, electronic, and spintronic applications. In this study, we investigated the evolution of exchange and dipole-dipole interactions in van der Waals magnet CrCl3 using multifrequency broadband (1-16 GHz) magnetic resonance on a bulk single-crystal CrCl3 sample. We found that the dipolar-broadened low-field resonance linewidth narrows due to exchange narrowing at higher resonance fields. Our findings show that the exchange interaction evolves exponentially as we lower the sample temperature from room temperature to 50 K. However, we found that the dipole-dipole interaction remains constant in the same temperature range. We have also detected the half-field magnetic resonance transition at room temperature, indicating a robust dipole-dipole interaction among spins. The measured inter-spin distance is 2.4(3) A at room temperature. The evolution of dipole interactions, the observation of half-field magnetic resonance, and the strong exchange interaction indicate short-range ordering/correlation in CrCl3 at temperatures far above the magnetic global ordering temperature. This study also demonstrates the significance and sensitivity of the multifrequency electron spin resonance technique for understanding quantum interactions and magnetic correlations in van der Waals magnetic materials.

</details>


### [99] [Distilling universal machine-learning potentials for moiré lattices across one million atoms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04115)
*Thomas Huang,Yueyao Fan,Kaichen Xie,Bolun Li,Kaijie Yang,Jenna A. Bilbrey,Eric Bylaska,Peter V. Sushko,Di Xiao,Ting Cao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出 MoiréMLIP：在 DFT 标注的 Moiré Kaleidoscope 数据集上微调通用原子模型，实现大尺度莫尔重构的高精度建模；经知识蒸馏得到 MoiréMLIP-mini，可在单 GPU 上扩展至百万原子，并揭示 MoTe₂ 三层结构中的层级极化网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原子重构（atomic reconstruction）在多个尺度上重塑莫尔材料，但直接从头算（ab initio）建模对近魔角莫尔及莫尔之莫尔（moiré-of-moirés）等大超结构计算代价过高。现有原子模型在精度、可迁移性与规模扩展方面存在瓶颈。

Method: 构建 MoiréMLIP：在覆盖过渡金属硫族化合物（transition metal dichalcogenide）组分、对称性、堆叠方式与扭转角的 DFT 标注 Moiré Kaleidoscope 数据集上微调通用原子模型；并通过知识蒸馏（knowledge distillation）得到轻量版 MoiréMLIP-mini。

Result: MoiréMLIP 以 6–8 meV/Å 的力误差复现从头算重构，并可迁移至更小扭转角与未见结构；MoiréMLIP-mini 在保持精度的同时将单 GPU 推理扩展至一百万原子。应用于交替扭转 MoTe₂ 三层结构时，揭示出源于莫尔之莫尔畴壁处高度局域化的大莫尔胞畸变、跨越数十纳米的层级极化网络。

Conclusion: 该结果克服了现有原子模型在精度、可迁移性与规模化方面的关键瓶颈，使跨新兴莫尔长度尺度的预测性模拟成为可能。

Abstract: Atomic reconstruction reshapes moiré materials across multiple scales, from local structure and polarization textures to global electronic topology, yet direct \textit{ab initio} modeling becomes prohibitive for large superstructures such as marginal-twist-angle moirés and moiré-of-moirés. We develop MoiréMLIP by fine-tuning a universal atomistic model on the density functional theory labeled Moiré Kaleidoscope dataset, which spans transition metal dichalcogenide compositions, symmetries, stackings, and twist angles. MoiréMLIP reproduces \textit{ab initio} reconstruction with force errors of 6--8\,meV/Å and transfers to smaller twist angles and unseen structures. Knowledge distillation yields MoiréMLIP-mini, which retains this accuracy while extending single-GPU inference to one million atoms. Applied to an alternate-twist MoTe$_2$ trilayer, it reveals a hierarchical polarization network spanning tens of nanometers arising from large moiré-cell distortions sharply localized along the moiré-of-moiré domain walls. These results overcome key accuracy, transferability, and scaling bottlenecks of existing atomistic models and enable predictive simulations across emergent moiré length scales.

</details>


### [100] [Agentic Resource Allocation for Batch Multi-Objective Bayesian Optimization in Autonomous Materials Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04134)
*Robert Robinson,Shakti Prasad Padhy,Sushant Sinha,Sk Md Ahnaf Akif Alvi,Juan Florez Coronel,Brent Vela,Trevor Hastings,Douglas Allaire,Raymundo Arróyave*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作面向资源约束下的合金设计，构建多目标贝叶斯优化（Bayesian optimization, BO）框架，并对多目标优化中的自适应策略调优进行基准测试。结果表明，混合分配策略比纯利用型基线累积更多互信息（mutual information），而基于大语言模型（LLM）的自适应策略通过响应优化进展与资源约束调整批次分配，优于固定混合策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先进材料发现受合成、加工与表征的高时间与高经济成本限制，其设计空间巨大且常含多个竞争目标。多数贝叶斯优化流程依赖固定的探索-利用（exploration-exploitation）策略，难以适应自驱动实验室（self-driving laboratories）动态实验中不断变化的约束。

Method: 开发面向资源约束合金设计的多目标贝叶斯优化框架，并在每次迭代中对比多种自适应策略调优方法。评估涵盖两个体系：以最大化熔点、最小化密度为目标的七元难熔高熵合金（septenary refractory high-entropy alloy, RHEA）体系，以及针对饱和磁化强度、矫顽力和硬度的 Fe-Co-Ni 基软磁合金体系。所比较的策略包括利用导向策略、固定混合探索/利用策略，以及两种不同的大语言模型（LLM）自适应策略（在批次分配与实验信号解读上方法不同），并在基线及多种中程资源事件条件（预算削减、时间线压缩及二者叠加）下评估。

Result: 混合分配策略相较利用导向基线可累积显著更多互信息，同时仅以成比例更小的超体积（hypervolume）与优化速度代价换取。自适应策略通过依据不断演化的优化统计与资源约束调整分配，表现优于固定混合分配策略。

Conclusion: 自适应资源分配为材料发现流程提供了有利的权衡，可降低对帕累托最优（Pareto-optimal）组分预测的不确定性。

Abstract: The discovery and development of advanced materials is a challenging process constrained by the high time and monetary costs of synthesis, processing, and characterization. The underlying design spaces can be enormous, often with multiple competing objectives. Bayesian optimization (BO) provides a principled approach for efficiently navigating such spaces, but most workflows rely on fixed exploration-exploitation policies that lack the capacity to adapt to shifting constraints in dynamic campaigns typical of self-driving laboratories. In this work, we develop a multi-objective BO framework for alloy design under resource constraints, benchmarking strategies for adaptive policy tuning at each iteration. Our evaluation covers a septenary refractory high-entropy alloy (RHEA) system focused on maximizing melting temperature and minimizing density, and an Fe-Co-Ni-based soft magnetic alloy system targeting saturation magnetization, coercivity, and hardness. We compare an exploitation-focused strategy, a fixed mixed exploratory/exploitative policy, and two distinct LLM-based adaptive strategies with different approaches to batch allocation and campaign signal interpretation, evaluated across baseline and mid-campaign resource event conditions including budget reductions, timeline cuts, and combined disruptions. Our results show that mixed allocation strategies accumulate substantially more mutual information than the exploitation-focused baseline at a proportionally smaller cost to hypervolume and optimization speed, with adaptive strategies outperforming a fixed-mixed allocation policy by adjusting their allocation in response to both evolving campaign statistics and resource constraints. These findings suggest that adaptive resource allocation offers a favorable tradeoff for materials discovery campaigns in reducing predictive uncertainty on Pareto-optimal compositions.

</details>


### [101] [MOSAIC: Addressing Space for Local, Steerable, and Scalable Atomistic Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04161)
*Ishan Nadkarni,Sebastien Hamel,Flynn Walsh,Fei Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: Mosaic 提出一种混合网格-粒子（hybrid mesh-particle）表示，将空间划分为锚定区域并在其中设置原子槽位，从而为原子放置提供持久空间地址，支持分子与材料的多尺度可控生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子发现与材料科学中的生成模型潜力巨大，但实现该潜力需同时具备对原子位置的局部控制能力，以及组装大型异质结构的能力，现有方法在这一点上存在不足。

Method: Mosaic 是一种表示而非模型：空间被划分为锚定区域，每个槽位编码占据状态（occupancy）、原子种类（atomic species）及相对锚点的连续位移（continuous displacement），因而可与不同生成骨干（generative backbone）搭配使用。

Result: 在分子骨干固定时，其局部支撑与坐标先验（coordinate prior）提升了分子连通性与有效性，可跨分子尺寸高效采样并实现细粒度空间控制；在材料尺度上，可依据粗粒度空间布局生成原子级多晶（atomistic polycrystals），并借助局部性将大规模生成转化为有界组装问题，实现超百万原子的结构生成。

Conclusion: 局部空间寻址（local spatial addressing）可作为跨尺度物质可操控生成（steerable generation）的实用基础，覆盖从单个分子到具有工程意义的大型结构。

Abstract: Generative models are opening new possibilities in molecular discovery and materials science. Realizing this potential, however, requires both local control over atomic placement and the ability to assemble large, heterogeneous structures. We introduce Mosaic, a hybrid mesh-particle representation that partitions space into anchored regions containing atom slots. Each slot encodes occupancy, atomic species, and a continuous displacement from its anchor, providing persistent spatial addresses for local conditioning, hierarchical generation, and scalable assembly. Because Mosaic defines a representation rather than a model, it can be paired with different generative backbones. With the molecular backbone held fixed, its local support and coordinate prior improve molecular connectivity and validity, the resulting model samples efficiently across molecular sizes and enables fine-grained spatial control. At larger material scales, the same representation enables generation of atomistic polycrystals conditioned on coarse spatial layouts. Most notably, locality turns large-scale generation into a bounded assembly problem, enabling structure generation with more than one million atoms. Together, this establishes local spatial addressing as a practical foundation for steerable generation of matter across scales, from individual molecules to large-scale structures of broader engineering significance.

</details>


### [102] [Molecular Crystal Structure Prediction from Conditional Flow on the Unit Cells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04193)
*Qiang Zhu,Yihan Weng*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作将分子晶体结构预测解耦为三步生成流程：先由分子图、Hall设置与Z′预测晶格不变量，再经准随机采样重建晶胞参数，最后在给定对称性与晶胞约束下进行分子堆积搜索，在84个体系中成功复现83个实验结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子晶体结构由空间群对称性（space group symmetry）、晶胞（unit cell）与不对称单元内分子排布共同决定，三者同时预测需混合离散对称性选择与连续高维搜索，难度较大。作者旨在通过解耦这三类变量来降低预测复杂度。

Method: 训练一个流模型（flow model），从分子图、Hall设置及不对称单元分子数（Z′）中学习不变晶格描述符（invariant lattice descriptors，如正、倒格矢逐次极小值与Selling标量）的条件分布；随后通过两步顺序准随机采样（quasi-random sampling），先重建符合预测晶格不变量与密度要求的晶胞参数，再在给定对称性与晶胞约束下进行分子堆积搜索。

Result: 在84个Z′ ≤ 1的单组分体系中，该方法对83个体系复现了与实验相符的结构；唯一失败案例源于力场（force-field）在保持实验结构方面的局限。

Conclusion: 学习得到的晶胞提议可有效支持给定Hall设置与Z′条件下的晶体结构预测（crystal structure prediction, CSP），未来有望扩展至对称性与Z′均可变的完全盲预测。

Abstract: A molecular crystal structure is jointly described by its space group symmetry, unit cell, and the molecular alignment within the asymmetric unit. Concurrently predicting all three variables is a daunting task, as it mixes discrete symmetry choices with a high-dimensional search in the continuous space. To address this challenge, we decouple these variables using a three-step generation process. Specifically, we train a flow model to learn the conditional distribution of invariant lattice descriptors (e.g. direct- and reciprocal-lattice successive minima and Selling scalars) from a molecular graph, a Hall setting, and the number of molecules in the asymmetric unit ($Z'$). Using a two sequential quasi-random sampling processes, we first reconstruct the cell parameters that match the predicted lattice invariants and density requirements, and then conduct a molecular packing search within the give symmetry and unit cell constraint. On 84 single-component systems with $Z' \le 1$, our approach reproduces experimental matches for 83 systems; the remaining failure stems from force-field limitations in preserving the experimental structure. These results demonstrate that learned cell proposals can effectively support crystal structure prediction (CSP) for a given Hall setting and $Z'$, which may be extended to fully blind prediction with variable symmetry and $Z'$ settings in the future.

</details>


### [103] [Materials and Metrology for Out-of-Plane Damping-Like Spin-Orbit Torque](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04248)
*Xiaoxi Huang,Yuhan Liang,Orion Smedley,Daniel C. Ralph*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该展望（Perspective）批判性综述了具有强非常规面外分量的电流诱导阻尼型自旋轨道矩（damping-like spin-orbit torque, SOT），梳理其材料来源、量化方法及面向可扩展磁技术的效率进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 若能为具有垂直磁各向异性（perpendicular magnetic anisotropy）的纳米磁性器件开发更高效的磁化取向翻转机制，将有望实现新一代高能效、高密度、非易失存储器件；其中非常规面外分量自旋轨道矩被视为有前景的策略，但该领域研究分散、结论不一。

Method: 采用展望（Perspective）形式的批判性综述，围绕三类可产生此类力矩的对称性破缺来源（单晶低晶体对称性、反铁磁序、铁磁序）分析材料类别，并评述力矩的量化实验方法。

Result: 已有研究在广泛材料平台中报道了此类面外力矩；然而不同测量方法结果常相互矛盾，伪影（artifact）被误读，定性观测有时被过度解读为技术可行性的证据。

Conclusion: 该领域仍呈碎片化状态，需在材料来源、量化方法与效率评估上建立一致认识，并评估其力矩效率是否足以支撑可扩展的磁性技术。

Abstract: A new generation of energy-efficient, high-density, and non-volatile memory devices could be enabled if a more efficient mechanism is developed for switching the magnetization orientation of nanoscale magnetic devices possessing perpendicular magnetic anisotropy. A promising strategy under widespread investigation is current-induced damping-like spin-orbit torque with a strong unconventional out-of-plane component, rather than only the conventional in-plane component. In recent years, such torques have been reported in a broad range of materials platforms, arising primarily from three classes of symmetry breaking within the material generating the torque: low crystal symmetry in single-crystalline materials, antiferromagnetic order, and ferromagnetic order. Despite this progress, the field remains fragmented. Measurements using different methods often disagree, artifacts have been misinterpreted, and qualitative observations are sometimes overinterpreted as evidence of technological promise. This Perspective will provide a critical overview of out-of-plane damping-like spin-orbit torques, analyzing the classes of materials which can generate such torques, the experimental methods by which the torques can be quantified, and the progress toward achieving torque efficiencies sufficient for scalable magnetic technologies.

</details>


### [104] [Band Structure Modulation in Ternary-Based InAs/ AlAs1-xSbx /InAs1-xSbx Superlattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04308)
*Arash Dehzangi,Jiakai Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究采用修正的 sp3s* 经验紧束缚方法（empirical tight-binding method），结合虚晶近似（virtual crystal approximation）与 s 位点能量弯曲参数（bowing parameter），构建超晶格哈密顿量，用以计算含 InAs/InAs1-xSbx 及 InAs/InAs1-xSbx/AlAs1-xSbx 的三元应变层超晶格的电子能带结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在扩展经验紧束缚方法对三元应变层超晶格电子能带结构的计算能力，覆盖从长波红外到宽带隙势垒等多种超晶格构型。

Method: 采用修正的 sp3s* 经验紧束缚方法并构建超晶格哈密顿量（Hamiltonian），将虚晶近似与 s 在位能量（s-on-site energy）的弯曲参数结合以预测三元超晶格行为，并开展多种构型的数值模拟。

Result: 模拟得到的能级与三元超晶格及其结构变体的实验带隙（bandgap）测量结果高度一致。

Conclusion: 将虚晶近似与 s 在位能量弯曲参数相结合，可准确预测三元超晶格的电子能带行为，验证了该经验紧束缚建模途径的有效性。

Abstract: This study demonstrates an overall view of recent quantum mechanical based modeling effort using empirical tight-binding method to expand the ability of calculation of electronic band structure for strained-layer superlattices composed of ternary InAs/InAs1-xSbx and InAs/InAs1-xSbx/AlAs1-xSbx materials. Using a modified sp3s* empirical tight-binding method and building the superlattice Hamiltonian, we demonstrate that combining the virtual crystal approximation with a bowing parameter for the s-on-site energy accurately predicts ternary superlattice behavior. We provide a comprehensive overview of our numerical simulations, which model diverse superlattice configurations ranging from long wavelength infrared range to wide-bandgap barrier architectures. Notably, the simulated energy levels strongly agree with experimental bandgap measurements of the ternary superlattices and their structural variants.

</details>


### [105] [Emergent magnetic nonanalyticity in Dirac altermagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04496)
*Xizhi Fu,Zhenqiao Huang,Fei Yang,Mengli Hu,Xingkai Cheng,Junwei Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作指出对称性所允许的响应未必是系统实际涌现的响应，在交错磁体（altermagnet）的无能隙狄拉克费米子（Dirac fermion）中，狄拉克点位移触发 Lifshitz 重构，产生内禀非解析有效势，导致磁化以 M∝sgn(ε)ε² 的非线性压磁响应出现，而对称性允许的线性响应反而缺失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统材料响应理论以诺伊曼原理（Neumann's principle）与解析的朗道展开（Landau expansion）为基石，将对称性视为响应的组织原则。本文动机在于揭示“对称性允许”与“实际涌现”之间的区别，并考察这一区别在对称性相关的无能隙狄拉克费米子体系中带来的重要后果。

Method: 通过分析对称性相关无能隙狄拉克费米子（如交错磁体）中狄拉克点位移所驱动的 Lifshitz 重构，推导内禀非解析有效势；在狄拉克交错磁体中引入 C 配对的自旋极化狄拉克锥，考察配对对称性 C 破缺对简并及重构的影响，并结合第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）寻找材料实现。

Result: 在狄拉克交错磁体中，非解析性通过 C 配对的狄拉克锥获得磁性表现：破缺配对对称性 C 会解除简并并将 Lifshitz 重构转化为巡游磁化（itinerant magnetization）。压磁效应（piezomagnetism）因此变为非线性非解析，满足 M∝sgn(ε)ε²，而对称性允许的线性响应缺失。第一性原理计算指认单层 Fe₂S 为候选材料。

Conclusion: 该结果挑战了基于诺伊曼原理与解析朗道展开的传统对称性响应范式，指出主导响应无法由该范式推断；并提出量子奇异性（quantum singularity）可作为设计材料响应的新自由度。

Abstract: Symmetry has long provided the organizing principle for understanding how materials respond to external perturbations. Here we show that what is permitted by symmetry need not be what actually emerges from the underlying system, and this distinction becomes particularly consequential for symmetry-related gapless Dirac fermions such as in altermagnets. A perturbation that shifts the Dirac points drives a Lifshitz reconstruction and generates an intrinsically nonanalytic effective potential, giving rise to undiscovered singular responses. In Dirac altermagnets, this nonanalyticity acquires a magnetic manifestation through $C$-paired spin-polarized Dirac cones: breaking the pairing symmetry $C$ lifts their degeneracy and converts the associated Lifshitz reconstruction into an itinerant magnetization. As a consequence, piezomagnetism, the induction of a net magnetization by a mechanical strain, becomes nonlinear and nonanalytic, with $M\propto\mathrm{sgn}(\varepsilon)\varepsilon^2$, whereas the linear response is absent despite being allowed by symmetry. Accordingly, the leading response cannot be inferred from the conventional symmetry-based paradigm of material response built upon the cornerstones of Neumann's principle and analytic Landau expansion. First-principles calculations identify the monolayer Fe$_2$S as a candidate material realization for such singular magnetic responses. These results suggest quantum singularities as a new degree of freedom for engineering material responses.

</details>


### [106] [Development and Application of a Magnetoelectric Coupling Measurement System for Strain-Mediated Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04599)
*Samanway Mohanta,Poonacha C. T,Hemant Singh,Gunjan Verma,A. Ahlawat,S. C. Das,G. Sinha,V. G. Sathe,D. K. Shukla*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文开发了基于动态锁相（lock-in）的磁电（magnetoelectric, ME）耦合测量系统，并用于研究PMN-PT/NFO与CFO/BTO复合材料的应变介导耦合，结果显示PMN-PT/NFO的ME系数远高于CFO/BTO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立动态锁相ME耦合测量手段，并厘清应变介导磁电耦合中压电强度与磁各向异性对耦合输出的影响。

Method: 采用动态锁相（lock-in）技术构建磁电耦合测量系统，对0.65Pb(Mg$_{1/3}$Nb$_{2/3}$)O$_3$-0.35PbTiO$_3$/NiFe$_2$O$_4$（PMN-PT/NFO）与CoFe$_2$O$_4$/BaTiO$_3$（CFO/BTO）复合材料进行应变介导耦合表征，并分析其场依赖性与相位行为。

Result: PMN-PT/NFO的ME系数约为1.85 mV·mm$^{-1}$·Oe$^{-1}$，显著高于CFO/BTO的约0.147 mV·mm$^{-1}$·Oe$^{-1}$；两种样品的反对称场依赖关系（antisymmetric field dependence）与180°相位反转（phase reversal）证实耦合源于应变驱动。

Conclusion: PMN-PT/NFO的优异响应源于PMN-PT的高压电强度与NFO的低磁各向异性（magnetic anisotropy）的协同作用；CFO的高磁各向异性与BTO较弱的压电性限制了ME输出，表明磁软性与压电强度在优化应变介导ME耦合中起决定性作用。

Abstract: This paper reports about development of a dynamic lock-in based magnetoelectric (ME) coupling measurement system and its application to investigate strain-mediated coupling in 0.65Pb(Mg$_{1/3}$Nb$_{2/3}$)O$_3$-0.35PbTiO$_3$/NiFe$_2$O$_4$ (PMN-PT/NFO) and CoFe$_2$O$_4$/BaTiO$_3$ (CFO/BTO) composites. The PMN-PT/NFO composite exhibits a significantly enhanced ME coefficient ($α_{\mathrm{ME}} \approx 1.85$~mV\,mm$^{-1}$\,Oe$^{-1}$) compared to CFO/BTO ($\approx 0.147$~mV\,mm$^{-1}$\,Oe$^{-1}$). The antisymmetric field dependence and $180^\circ$ phase reversal confirm the strain-driven origin of coupling in both the samples. The superior response of PMN-PT/NFO arises from the combined effect of high piezoelectric strength in PMN-PT and low magnetic anisotropy of NFO, whereas the high magnetic anisotropy of CFO and weaker piezoelectricity of BTO limit the ME output. These results highlight the decisive role of magnetic softness and piezoelectric strength in optimizing strain-mediated ME coupling.

</details>


### [107] [Site-Specific Orbital Symmetry Breaking in Epitaxial Graphene on Silicon Carbide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04760)
*Vinod K. Paidi,Yves Joly,Alex Taekyung Lee,Guillaume Radtke,Vlado K. Lazarov,Demie Kepaptsoglou,Quentin M. Ramasse,Matthieu Bugnet*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作利用像差校正扫描透射电子显微镜（STEM）中的单色电子能量损失谱（EELS）在原子尺度研究 SiC(0001) 上外延石墨烯缓冲层的电子结构，并结合从头算（ab initio）计算揭示其成键改变与轨道特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外延石墨烯在 SiC 界面的电子输运性质调控是石墨烯电子器件应用的关键问题，而缓冲层与自由悬浮石墨烯的电子结构差异需在原子尺度加以阐明。

Method: 采用像差校正扫描透射电子显微镜（STEM）中的单色电子能量损失谱（EELS）获取 C K 边精细结构，并辅以从头算（ab initio）计算，实现位点分辨的轨道特征探测。

Result: C K 边谱精细结构与自由悬浮石墨烯明显不同，直接证明键合环境发生改变；缓冲层中四分之一碳原子与衬底硅原子形成共价键，诱导部分 sp³ 杂化，淬灭面外 π 态并破坏界面层的 π 共轭；衬底施加的对称性破缺进一步解除面内轨道简并。

Conclusion: 以 π 态淬灭与位点特异性对称性破缺为基础的轨道工程（orbital-engineering）框架，为面向下一代低能耗晶体管应用的石墨烯-衬底界面调控提供了定量依据。

Abstract: The epitaxial graphene grown on SiC interface has been the subject of tremendous research efforts to control its electronic transport properties in the quest for graphene electronic applications. The electronic structure of the epitaxial graphene buffer layer on SiC(0001) is investigated at the atomic scale using monochromated electron energy loss spectroscopy (EELS) in an aberration-corrected scanning transmission electron microscope (STEM). The C $K$-edge reveals spectral fine structures distinctly different from free-standing graphene layer, providing direct experimental evidence of a modified bonding environment. Ab initio calculations providing a site-resolved probe of orbital character to elucidate the origin of these spectral features: covalent bonding between one-quarter of the buffer-layer carbon atoms and the substrate silicon atoms induces partial $\mathrm{sp}^3$ hybridization, quenching the out-of-plane $π$ states and disrupting $π$-conjugation across the interfacial layer. Substrate-imposed symmetry breaking further lifts the in-plane orbital degeneracy. This orbital-engineering framework, rooted in $π$-state quenching and site-specific symmetry breaking, establishes a quantitative basis for tailoring graphene-substrate interfaces toward next-generation low-energy transistor applications.

</details>


### [108] [Quantitative near-atomic-resolution oxidation-state mapping in complex oxide heterostructures by electron energy-loss spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04797)
*Ioannis Iatrakis,Zitao Zhang,Ricardo Egoavil,Emma van der Minne,Christoph Baeumer,Frank de Groot*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出了一个基于近原子分辨电子能量损失谱（EELS）的定量氧化态（oxidation state）映射理论框架，通过结合弛豫狄拉克-哈特里-福克（Dirac-Hartree-Fock）电离截面与配体场多重态（ligand-field multiplet）理论，成功解析复杂氧化物异质结中过渡金属与稀土元素的重叠谱特征并恢复其本征价态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂氧化物异质结中过渡金属与稀土元素谱特征高度重叠，传统方法难以在近原子尺度上定量区分各元素的氧化态（oxidation state），亟需一个通用且定量的价态映射方法。

Method: 通过将弛豫狄拉克-哈特里-福克（relaxed Dirac-Hartree-Fock）电离截面与配体场多重态（ligand-field multiplet）理论相结合，计算过渡金属 L$_{2,3}$ 与稀土 M$_{4,5}$ 边的总非弹性微分截面；并将该方法应用于两个关联氧化物异质结体系。

Result: 方法分辨出 Cr、Mn、Fe、Co 的氧化态，并识别出 LaCr$_{0.2}$Mn$_{0.2}$Fe$_{0.2}$Co$_{0.2}$Ni$_{0.2}$O$_3$ 层中的 Ni 贡献，发现以 Co$^{2+}$ 为主并伴随混合 Mn$^{2+}$/Mn$^{3+}$/Mn$^{4+}$，显示层内位点特异性的价态重分布；同时分离了 NdNiO$_3$/La$_{0.67}$Sr$_{0.33}$MnO$_3$/NdGaO$_3$ 中重叠的 La-M$_{4,5}$ 与 Ni-L$_{2,3}$ 边，恢复出本征 Ni-L$_{2,3}$ 激发谱，发现 NdNiO$_3$ 中有效 Ni 价态偏离简单离子图像。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一种从近原子分辨 EELS 进行定量价态映射的通用方法，可适用于广泛的实验条件，用于研究复杂氧化物的局域电子结构。

Abstract: We present a theoretical framework for quantitative oxidation state mapping of transition-metal and rare-earth elements from near-atomic-resolution electron energy-loss spectroscopy (EELS) in complex oxide heterostructures. The total inelastic differential cross section of transition-metal L$_{2,3}$ and rare-earth M$_{4,5}$ edges is calculated by combining the relaxed Dirac-Hartree-Fock ionization cross section with ligand-field multiplet theory for the discrete transitions. We apply the approach to two correlated-oxide heterostructures, La$_{0.67}$Sr$_{0.33}$MnO$_3$/LaCr$_{0.2}$Mn$_{0.2}$Fe$_{0.2}$Co$_{0.2}$Ni$_{0.2}$O$_3$/SrTiO$_3$ and NdNiO$_3$/La$_{0.67}$Sr$_{0.33}$MnO$_3$/NdGaO$_3$, which include a wide range of transition-metal and rare-earth elements with overlapping features. The method resolves the oxidation states of Cr, Mn, Fe and Co and identifies the Ni contribution within the LaCr$_{0.2}$Mn$_{0.2}$Fe$_{0.2}$Co$_{0.2}$Ni$_{0.2}$O$_3$ layer at near-atomic resolution. We find predominantly Co$^{2+}$ together with mixed Mn$^{2+}$/Mn$^{3+}$/Mn$^{4+}$ character, indicating site-specific valence redistribution within the layer. We also separate overlapping spectral features, such as La-M$_{4,5}$ and Ni-L$_{2,3}$ edges in the NdNiO$_3$/La$_{0.67}$Sr$_{0.33}$MnO$_3$/NdGaO$_3$ heterostructure, thereby recovering the intrinsic Ni-L$_{2,3}$ excitation spectrum. We find that the effective Ni valence in NdNiO$_3$ deviates from the simple ionic picture. More broadly, this work establishes a generic method for quantitative valence mapping from near-atomic-resolution EELS, applicable to a wide range of experimental conditions for the study of local electronic structure of complex oxides.

</details>


### [109] [Phenomenon-first problem formulation with language models in metal-organic frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04866)
*Jihan Kim*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种“现象优先（phenomenon-first）”的材料发现框架，由大语言模型（LLM）分别接收客体物理、主体能力与操作情境描述，主动提出定性的非常规行为及其机制、判别特征与可证伪条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是将 LLM 推理从执行既定目标向上游推进，转为前瞻性地构想定性的材料现象，从而拓展材料发现中机制探索的广度。

Method: 在金属有机框架（metal-organic frameworks, MOFs）的 80 组主客体组合中，模型在评估或材料筛选前先行冻结 57 项提案；并设置重复实验与仅给客体信息的基线进行对照。

Result: 评估识别出九个计算上可行的候选；结构化表述可复现地拓宽机制探索，原始与重复运行各覆盖 21 个机制大类，仅客体表述为八个。MC-010 在实验已报道 MOFs 中产生可复现的水合驱动保留乙醇重取向，MC-015 得到相对匹配经典描述的量子修正 H₂ 吸附区域偏好反转。

Conclusion: 结构化 LLM 推理可使模型从执行给定目标转向前瞻性提出定性材料现象。

Abstract: Here we introduce a phenomenon-first framework for materials discovery in which an LLM receives separate descriptions of guest physics, host capabilities and operating context and proposes an unusual qualitative behavior with its mechanism, distinguishing signature and falsifier. Across 80 guest-host combinations in metal-organic frameworks, the model generated 57 proposals that were frozen before evaluation or materials selection; assessment identified nine computationally tractable candidates. In a repeat and guest-only baseline, structured formulation reproducibly broadened mechanistic exploration: the original and repeat runs each covered 21 broad mechanism families versus eight for guest-only formulation despite fewer proposals. From the original run, MC-010 yielded reproducible hydration-driven reorientation of retained ethanol in experimentally reported MOFs, while MC-015 yielded a quantum-corrected reversal of H$_2$ adsorption-region preference relative to the matched classical description. These results show how structured LLM reasoning can move upstream from executing supplied objectives toward prospectively formulating qualitative materials phenomena.

</details>


### [110] [Identification and Structural Decomposition of Hidden Defect Configurations: A Case Study of Charged Oxygen Divacancies in HfO$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04892)
*Hyunjin Lee,Yoon Kyeung Lee,Keun Heo,Shi Liu,Taehun Lee*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究以氧化铪（HfO$_2$）中$c$轴氧双空位复合体为对象，结合密度泛函理论（DFT）局部畸变搜索与Smooth Overlap of Atomic Positions（SOAP）结构分解，揭示了一族隐藏的低能构型（$T_c^{*}$）。其混合M/T/PO特征改变了向单斜（M）与极性正交（PO）相变的转变势垒，使PO取代M成为低势垒分支。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有广泛构型搜索揭示了晶态化合物中的隐藏缺陷结构，但缺陷局部键合构型如何改变其周围原子环境仍缺乏理解。HfO$_2$同时存在能量竞争的单斜（M）、四方（T）与极性正交（PO）相，是研究该问题的合适体系。

Method: 在四方晶格约束下开展密度泛函理论（DFT）弛豫的局部畸变搜索，识别$T_c$附近隐藏的低能构型族$T_c^{*}$；采用SOAP描述符对全带电复合体（$2\mathrm{V}_{\mathrm{O}}^{2+}$）进行结构分解，并辅以Hf配位与键几何分析；随后释放晶格约束，用固态nudged elastic band（NEB）计算以胞弛豫端点为基准的M相与PO相转变路径及势垒。

Result: 理想化$T_c$参考构型无障碍地连接到最低能量的混合基元$T_c^{*}$构型，在受限势能面上能量降低18.4 meV/f.u.。SOAP分解显示该复合体具有M、T、PO混合特征。释放约束后，向M与PO的势垒分别由理想参考的6.9与10.1 meV/f.u.升至$T_c^{*}$衍生结构的26.9与15.3 meV/f.u.；M相势垒增幅更大，使PO成为低势垒分支。

Conclusion: 该案例表明结构分解能够将隐藏缺陷构型与周围原子环境的变化及竞争相之间的动力学分支联系起来，为理解缺陷调控的相变路径提供了方法学范式。

Abstract: Extensive configurational searches have revealed hidden defect structures in crystalline compounds, yet how their local bonding arrangements modify the surrounding atomic environment remains less well understood. We examine this question in hafnium oxide (HfO$_2$), which hosts energetically competing monoclinic (M), tetragonal (T), and polar orthorhombic (PO) polymorphs. For the $c$-axis oxygen-divacancy complex ($T_c$), a local-distortion search with density-functional theory (DFT) relaxation under tetragonal-lattice confinement identifies a hidden family of low-energy configurations ($T_c^{*}$). For the fully charged complex ($2\mathrm{V}_{\mathrm{O}}^{2+}$), structural decomposition using Smooth Overlap of Atomic Positions (SOAP) descriptors reveals mixed M-, T-, and PO-like character, supported by independent analyses of Hf coordination and bond geometry. The idealized $T_c$ reference connects barrierlessly to the lowest-energy mixed-motif $T_c^{*}$ configuration, lowering the energy by $18.4$ meV/f.u. within the confined landscape. After releasing the lattice constraint, we compare transformation pathways to M and PO using solid-state nudged elastic band calculations with cell-relaxed endpoints. The respective barriers increase from $6.9$ and $10.1$ meV/f.u. for the idealized reference to $26.9$ and $15.3$ meV/f.u. for the $T_c^{*}$-derived structure. The larger increase for M makes PO, rather than M, the lower-barrier branch. This case study shows how structural decomposition connects hidden defect configurations with changes in the surrounding atomic environment and kinetic branching between competing polymorphs.

</details>


### [111] [Anisotropic mesoscale magnetic scattering across the spin-reorientation transition in TbMn6Sn6](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04903)
*Zhuang Xu,Fan Chen,Hui Cheng,Xinmin Wang,Taisen Zuo,He Cheng,Haitao Hu,Cuiping Zhang,Qian Zhao,Michel Kenzelmann,Jonathan S. White,Junying Shen,Xin Tong*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究利用小角中子散射（small-angle neutron scattering, SANS）探测块体 TbMn6Sn6 单晶的介观磁性，未观察到长程有序斯格明子晶格（skyrmion lattice）的六重布拉格花样，而发现了具有各向异性 twofold 低-Q 散射响应，该响应在自旋重取向（spin-reorientation transition, SRT）区域随温度与磁场显著演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TbMn6Sn6 是兼具拓扑电子态、强磁各向异性与近室温自旋重取向转变的中心对称亚铁磁 kagome 金属（kagome metal），其实空间成像已在薄样品中揭示螺旋条纹、双斯格明子（biskyrmion）类织构与斯格明子泡（skyrmion-bubble）类结构，但此类局域织构能否在块体中发展为长程有序斯格明子晶格尚不明确。

Method: 采用小角中子散射（SANS）对块体 TbMn6Sn6 单晶的介观磁性进行探测，涵盖零场条件以及 345 K（H || b）与 310 K（H || c，接近 TSR）等不同温度与磁场取向的扫描。

Result: 在所探索条件下未观察到指示长程有序斯格明子晶格的六重 SANS 布拉格花样；主要磁响应为各向异性 twofold 低-Q 散射分量。零场下，宽且水平拉长的低-Q 信号在 TSR 以上出现并随接近顺磁态而减弱；345 K 且 H || b 时信号呈弥散状并在约 700 Oe 以下迅速被抑制；310 K 且 H || c 时响应更集中、呈线状并持续至更高磁场。

Conclusion: 结果确立了跨越自旋重取向区域的块体各向异性介观磁响应，与实空间成像报道的条纹或畴相关织构一致，但无法唯一确定其真实空间形貌。该差异推测源于 TSR 附近快速演化的磁各向异性与磁场几何的联合效应。

Abstract: TbMn6Sn6 is a centrosymmetric ferrimagnetic kagome metal that combines topological electronic states with strong magnetic anisotropy and a near-room-temperature spin-reorientation transition (SRT). Real-space imaging studies of thin specimens have revealed helical stripes, biskyrmion-like textures, and skyrmion-bubble-like structures in the spin-reorientation regime. Whether such local textures develop into a bulk long-range-ordered skyrmion lattice remains unresolved. Here we present small-angle neutron scattering (SANS) results probing the mesoscale magnetism of a bulk TbMn6Sn6 single crystal. We observe no sixfold SANS Bragg pattern indicative of a long-range-ordered skyrmion lattice under the explored conditions. Instead, the dominant magnetic response is an anisotropic twofold low-Q scattering component whose intensity distribution and field dependence evolve markedly across the spin-reorientation regime. In zero field, a broad and horizontally elongated low-Q signal develops above TSR and weakens as the system approaches the paramagnetic state. At 345 K with H || b, the signal is diffuse and is suppressed rapidly below about 700 Oe. By contrast, at 310 K with H || c, close to TSR, the response is more concentrated and line-shaped and persists to higher fields. The contrasts between these scans likely reflect the combined effects of rapidly evolving magnetic anisotropy near TSR and the field geometry. Our results therefore establish a bulk anisotropic mesoscale magnetic response across the spin-reorientation regime, consistent with stripe- or domain-related textures reported by real-space imaging, while not uniquely identifying their real-space morphology.

</details>


### [112] [Computing at the Edge Enabled by Indoor Photovoltaics and Ferroelectrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05133)
*Robert L. Z. Hoye,Markus Hellenbrand,Nuno Estrócio,Austin M. Kay,Alice Scardina,Alessandro Mezzetti,Ignasi Fina,Jorge Íñiguez-González,Luís S. Marques,Francesco Matteucci,Quanxi Jia,Judith L. MacManus-Driscoll,Bert Offrein,Florencio Sánchez,Beatriz Noheda,George Koutsourakis,Marina Freitag,Gregory Burwell,Giulia Grancini,Jose P. B. Silva*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该观点文章（perspective）论证了以边缘计算（edge computing）或分布式智能（distributed intelligence, DI）替代集中式人工智能（AI）计算的可持续性路径，指出室内光伏（indoor photovoltaics, IPV）等本地能量收集与低功耗计算器件的协同设计是关键，并主张（反）铁电材料（(anti)ferroelectric materials）可突破储能与内存内神经形态计算的瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集中式大型数据中心承载的AI计算规模急剧扩张，正逼近电力与水资源等基础设施的承载极限。在数十亿小型自主节点上本地执行部分计算可节省大量通信能耗，但该范式长期受制于缺乏与计算基础设施能耗需求相匹配的可靠本地能源供给。

Method: 采用观点性综述（perspective）方式，先梳理分布式智能对室内光伏（IPV）的要求，继而评估新兴材料对这些要求的满足程度，并探讨现有差距的弥补途径。

Result: 室内光伏的快速进展与神经形态计算（neuromorphic computing）、内存内计算（in-memory computing）器件带来的计算成本下降，共同为上述挑战提供了新的解决机会；文章进一步论证（反）铁电材料在静电储能与低功耗内存内神经形态计算两方面均可超越现有瓶颈。

Conclusion: 核心观点为：能量收集、储能与计算三者的协同创造（co-creation）是使分布式智能成为集中式AI可靠且更可持续替代方案的必要条件。

Abstract: The exponential, pervasive rise of artificial intelligence (AI), hosted in large data centres, will test the limits of the underpinning infrastructure, including electricity and water supplies, which are becoming increasingly precious resources. Rather than performing all AI computations in centralized servers, for many functionalities, these can be performed locally across a network of billions of small, autonomous nodes, saving substantial energy costs associated with communication. This alternative paradigm is known as edge computing, or distributed intelligence (DI), but has been held back by the lack of availability of reliable local energy supplies matching the energy requirements of the computational infrastructure. Opportunities to address this challenge are emerging with rapid advances in high-performance indoor photovoltaics (IPVs) for local energy harvesting, as well as reductions in computational cost through neuromorphic or in-memory computing devices. In this perspective, we discuss the requirements of IPVs for DI, the extent to which emerging materials fulfil these requirements, and how current gaps could be addressed. We make the case that (anti)ferroelectric materials can surpass bottlenecks for both electrostatic energy storage and low-power in-memory neuromorphic computing. The core theme of this perspective is that co-creation between energy harvesting, storage and computing is essential for making DI a reliable and more sustainable alternative to centralized AI.

</details>


### [113] [Sliding and stacking thin homo-monolayers from energy landscapes and combinatorics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05363)
*Jose D. Mella,Jose Luis Cabellos,Bernardo S. Mendoza,Salvador Barraza-Lopez*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作以SnSe为范例，提出“分而治之”（divide and conquer）的组合方法，用于确定任意数量相同单层堆垛的最可能构型，并关联实验观测的二阶谐波光学响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构决定功能，掌握相同单层所有可能的堆垛与滑移方式，有助于设计层状材料中的非线性光学响应与铁电性等功能。同时，实验证据显示少层与体相样品的堆垛构型存在差异，亟需系统性的堆垛描述框架。

Method: 先用密度泛函理论（density functional theory）识别非莫尔（non-moiré）SnSe堆垛的基态与亚稳态构型，据此验证“分而治之”的组合方法，以确定任意数量相同单层材料中最可能的堆垛构型；并讨论有限温度效应。

Result: 少量点群（point groups）即可涵盖所有可能的堆垛，据此解释了实验观测到的二次谐波（second-harmonic）光学响应；有限温度效应亦在数据粒度受限条件下得到研究。

Conclusion: 该综合框架可推广至对任意亚稳态少层同质单层（few-homomonolayer）堆垛的预测性描述。

Abstract: Structure leads to function: Knowing all possible ways to stack and slide identical monolayers enables the design of functionalities such as nonlinear optical responses and ferroelectricity in layered materials. Besides, mounting experimental evidence points to differing stacking configurations in few-monolayer and bulk samples. To exemplify the core concepts for stacking arbitrary monolayers, ground-state and metastable non-moiré SnSe stacks were first identified using density functional theory and employed to validate a ``divide and conquer'' combinatoral approach for determining the most probable stacking configurations in materials with an arbitrary number of identical monolayers. A small number of point groups accounts for all possible stacks, which explains experimental second-harmonic optical responses. Finite-temperature effects are also studied despite the granularity of available data. This comprehensive framework readily generalizes to provide a predictive description of arbitrary metastable few-homomonolayer stacks.

</details>


### [114] [A Scale-Separated Full-Field Monopole Method for Precipitation Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05436)
*C. W. Sinclair*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出尺度分离的单极子（monopole）方法，用于在粒子尺度扩散场与更大尺度溶质变化共存时对析出（precipitation）进行全场预测。通过 Ewald 分解（Ewald decomposition）分离扩散场尺度，构建准静态粒子动力学与瞬态长程扩散的混合格式，并在 Al--Sc 体系中展示瞬态贫化场对后续形核与析出分布的持续影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当粒子尺度扩散场与远距离溶质变化共存时，传统全场预测方法难以兼顾粒子级析出动力学与介观尺度（mesoscale）溶质再分配。直接准静态表述在空间异质粒子群产生缓慢弛豫长波浓度场时会失效，因此需要一种能按空间尺度分离扩散场的方法。

Method: 以单极子格林函数（Green's-function）解表示析出物，实现基于粒子的相互作用生长与溶解描述。直接准静态表述经瞬态有限差分（finite-difference）计算与常规 Kampmann--Wagner 数值模型验证。随后采用 Ewald 分解按空间尺度分离扩散场：屏蔽的粒子尺度场作准静态处理，互补的长程场则以瞬态扩散演化，从而形成混合表述。

Result: 直接准静态表述在空间异质粒子群导致缓慢弛豫长波浓度场时不再适用。混合表述保留了显式的粒子级析出动力学，同时允许介观尺度溶质再分配按其物理时间尺度演化。在 Al--Sc 体系中，局部析出产生的瞬态贫化场可抑制后续形核，并在析出物数密度与体积分数上产生持久空间变化。

Conclusion: 尺度分离的单极子方法与 Ewald 分解相结合，可在多尺度溶质场下实现粒子级析出动力学的全场预测。该方法适用于析出动力学与介观溶质再分配耦合的场景，Al--Sc 实例表明瞬态贫化场对后续形核抑制与空间不均匀性具有关键作用。

Abstract: A scale-separated monopole method is developed for full-field prediction of precipitation under conditions where particle-scale diffusion fields coexist with solute variations over much larger distances. Precipitates are represented by monopole Green's-function solutions, providing a particle-based description of interacting particle growth and dissolution. The direct quasistatic formulation is validated against transient finite-difference calculations and a conventional Kampmann--Wagner numerical model, but becomes inappropriate when spatially heterogeneous particle populations generate slowly relaxing long-wavelength concentration fields. An Ewald decomposition is therefore used to separate the diffusion field by spatial scale. Screened particle-scale fields are treated quasistatically, while the complementary long-range field evolves by transient diffusion. The resulting hybrid formulation retains explicit particle-level precipitation kinetics while allowing mesoscale solute redistribution to evolve on its physical timescale. Application to Al--Sc demonstrates that a transient depletion field generated by localized precipitation can suppress subsequent nucleation and produce persistent spatial variations in precipitate number density and volume fraction.

</details>


### [115] [Deconvoluting Mechanisms of Radiation-Induced Grain Boundary Segregation in Dilute Ni-X (X = Fe, Cr, Mn) Alloys via Ion Irradiation and Phase-field Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05849)
*Sohail Shah,Sourabh Bhagwan Kadambi,Anshul Kamboj,Benjamin Diaz,Xinchang Zhang,Jana Howard,Michael Moorehead,Boopathy Kombaiah,Lin Shao,Mukesh Bachhav*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究结合 Ni2+ 离子辐照实验（STEM-EDS 表征）与速率理论相场模拟，系统揭示 Ni-Fe、Ni-Cr、Ni-Mn 二元合金晶界（grain boundary, GB）成分再分布机制：Fe 因空位-溶质交换偏好而耗竭，Cr 呈现 SIA 与空位输运的温度竞争，Mn 则由热力学偏聚主导富集。结果表明晶界化学演化需同时考虑非平衡动力学输运、热力学驱动力、体汇效应与晶界迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辐照诱导的晶界成分再分布对 Ni 基结构合金在先进核反应堆中的长期服役构成关键挑战，而溶质特异性偏聚（solute-specific segregation）机制尚不清晰，因为缺陷介导输运、热力学驱动力与竞争性微观结构汇（microstructural sinks）可能同时起作用。

Method: 在 500、800、1000 K 下对稀 Ni-Fe、Ni-Cr、Ni-Mn 二元合金进行 2 dpa 的 Ni2+ 离子辐照，用 STEM-EDS 表征晶界化学；并采用由原子尺度输运数据约束的速率理论相场建模（rate-theory phase-field modeling）解释偏聚行为，同时利用同一晶界的深度依赖表征区分辐照诱导与热力学贡献。

Result: Fe 在所有温度下经优先空位-溶质交换（inverse Kirkendall effect，逆柯肯达尔效应）在晶界耗竭；Cr 偏聚反映自间隙原子（self-interstitial atom, SIA）向晶界输运与空位向外输运之间的温度依赖竞争，500 K 净偏聚近零、更高温度下耗竭；Mn 则由热力学偏聚主导，在晶界局部富集。800 K 的空洞形成与 800、1000 K 的辐照诱导晶界迁移进一步抑制与再分布溶质。

Conclusion: 单一机制无法解释晶界化学演化；须同时处理非平衡动力学输运、热力学驱动力、体汇效应与晶界迁移，未辐照区可作为内部热力学参考。

Abstract: Radiation-induced composition redistribution at grain boundaries (GBs) in Ni-based structural alloys poses a critical challenge for the long-term deployment of advanced nuclear reactors. The mechanisms governing solute-specific segregation remain poorly understood because defect-mediated transport, thermodynamic driving forces, and competing microstructural sinks can all contribute under irradiation. We perform systematic Ni2+ ion irradiations on dilute Ni-Fe, Ni-Cr, and Ni-Mn binary alloys at 500, 800, and 1000 K to 2 dpa and characterize GB chemistry using STEM-EDS. The segregation behavior is interpreted using rate-theory phase-field modeling informed by atomistic transport data. We find that Fe depletes at GBs across all temperatures via preferential vacancy-solute exchange (inverse Kirkendall effect). Cr segregation reflects a temperature-dependent competition between self-interstitial atom (SIA)-mediated transport toward GBs and vacancy-mediated transport away from GBs, yielding near-zero net segregation at 500 K and depletion at higher temperatures. In contrast, Mn exhibits distinct behavior dominated by thermodynamic segregation that drives local enrichment at the GB. We leverage depth-dependent characterization along the same GB, spanning irradiated and unirradiated regions, to separate radiation-induced and thermodynamic contributions and validate the unirradiated region as an internal thermodynamic reference. Void formation at 800 K and irradiation-induced GB migration at 800 and 1000 K further add mechanistic complexity by suppressing and redistributing the solute. Our combined experimental and computational assessments show that interpreting GB chemistry evolution under irradiation requires concurrent treatment of non-equilibrium kinetic transport, thermodynamic driving forces, bulk sink effects, and GB migration.

</details>


### [116] [Out-of-Plane Oscillating Electric Fields Unlock Low-Temperature Nonequilibrium Superionicity in Quasi-Two-Dimensional AgCrSe2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05941)
*Jia-Wen Li,Kun Yang,Sheng Meng,Xinghua Shi,Wei-Hai Fang,Jin Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究利用电场响应型机器学习分子动力学（machine-learning molecular dynamics）模拟，揭示准二维 AgCrSe2 中通过面外振荡电场可在 300 K 实现超离子输运（superionic transport），离子电导率达约 1.7 S/cm，其机制为电场驱动的 Ag+ 动态无序而非热致无序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超离子导体（superionic conductors）虽具快速离子输运潜力，但其高导态通常仅存在于可动离子亚晶格的有序-无序转变温度以上，限制了低温工作。因此，作者寻求在远低于转变温度下实现超离子输运的非平衡途径。

Method: 采用电场响应型机器学习分子动力学（electric-field-responsive machine-learning molecular dynamics），对准二维 AgCrSe2 施加面外振荡电场，研究其非平衡离子输运行为。

Result: 在 300 K（远低于约 475 K 的转变温度）下，该电场激活了面内快速 Ag+ 输运，离子电导率达约 1.7 S/cm。随频率升高，Ag 占据状态由完全切换演变为高导的动态无序态，最终变为弱场响应的有序态；这些区间随温度或场幅增大而向高频移动。

Conclusion: 强场下时间尺度与场辅助热激活（field-assisted thermal activation）一致，弱场偏离暗示存在单离子激活之外的额外动力学。结果表明，时间尺度匹配的周期驱动（timescale-matched periodic driving）可作为在平衡超离子转变温度以下获得输运活跃离子无序的途径。

Abstract: Superionic conductors enable exceptionally fast ion transport in solids and offer broad technological potential. However, their highly conductive states are typically accessed only above the order-disorder transition of the mobile-ion sublattice, which limits low-temperature operation. Here, electric-field-responsive machine-learning molecular dynamics reveals a nonequilibrium route to superionic transport in quasi-two-dimensional AgCrSe2 driven by an out-of-plane oscillating electric field. At 300 K, far below the transition temperature near 475 K, the field activates fast in-plane Ag+ transport with an ionic conductivity reaching ~1.7 S/cm. This originates from field-driven dynamic disorder of Ag+ across two equivalent sublattices rather than thermally induced disordering. With increasing frequency, the Ag occupation evolves from complete switching to a highly conductive dynamically disordered state and finally to an ordered state with weak field response. These regimes shift to higher frequencies with increasing temperature or field amplitude, consistent with competition between the driving period and Ag-sublattice response time. At strong fields, this timescale is consistent with field-assisted thermal activation, while weak-field deviations suggest additional dynamics beyond single-ion activation. These results suggest timescale-matched periodic driving as a route to transport-active ionic disorder far below an equilibrium superionic transition.

</details>


### [117] [Geometrical Confinement of Magnetic Domains and Domain Walls on Two-Dimensional Cylindrical-Shaped Ferromagnetic Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05970)
*Gabriel Martínez-Carracedo,Aurelio Hierro-Rodríguez,Amador García-Fuente,Jaime Ferrer*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作通过解析海森堡模型（Heisenberg model）与数值模拟，揭示二维范德华（van der Waals, vdW）磁性单层卷曲成纳米管后，横向曲率会压缩磁畴壁（domain wall, DW）宽度，并在CrSBr纳米管中实现两个畴壁的束缚态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将二维vdW磁性单层卷成圆柱结构可无边缘钉扎（edge-pinning）地约束磁畴壁，为研究受限磁畴壁及其相互作用提供理想平台，但相关机制尚待阐明。

Method: 采用二维矩形晶格上的解析海森堡模型（Heisenberg model）分析横向曲率效应，并以原子级Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert（LLG）自旋动力学模拟进行验证；进一步对真实CrSBr（CSB）纳米管进行微磁学（micromagnetic）模拟。

Result: 横向曲率引入方位角交换能量代价（azimuthal exchange energy penalty），本质上压缩了DW宽度，解析模型与LLG模拟定量吻合；在CSB纳米管中，长程偶极吸引（dipolar attraction）与短程交换排斥（exchange repulsion）平衡形成两个相互作用DW的束缚态，且壁间距随管周长呈非单调演化。

Conclusion: vdW磁性纳米管可作为准一维（quasi-one-dimensional）自旋电子学（spintronic）架构中稳定磁畴约束的可靠平台。

Abstract: The rolling of two-dimensional (2D) van der Waals (vdW) magnetic monolayers into cylindrical architectures provides an ideal platform for confining magnetic domain walls (DWs) without edge-pinning effects. Here, we present a combined analytical and numerical investigation of DW confinement and interaction in ferromagnetic (FM) nanotubes. Using an analytical Heisenberg model on a 2D rectangular lattice, we show that transversal curvature introduces an azimuthal exchange energy penalty that intrinsically compresses the DW width. This model demonstrates excellent quantitative agreement with atomistic Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert (LLG) spin dynamics simulations. By extending our study to realistic CrSBr (CSB) nanotubes, we model a bound state of two interacting DWs arising from a balance between long-range dipolar attraction and short-range exchange repulsion. Micromagnetic simulations reveal a non-monotonic evolution of the inter-wall distance with the tube perimeter. These findings highlight vdW magnetic nanotubes as robust platforms for stable domain confinement in quasi-one-dimensional spintronic architectures.

</details>


### [118] [Compressed magnetic Moment Tensor Potentials via low-rank matrix and tensor factorizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06158)
*Alexey S. Kotykhov,Alexander V. Shapeev,Maxim Rakhuba,Ivan S. Novikov*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出基于矩阵与张量分解的降参磁矩张量势（mMTP），在保持验证集预测性能的同时将参数量减少约1.5–3倍，并在Fe-Al与CrN体系中验证了其与原始模型、密度泛函理论及实验数据的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始磁矩张量势（magnetic Moment Tensor Potential, mMTP）参数较多，压缩参数规模有助于降低计算与存储开销，同时需保持预测精度。

Method: 采用多种矩阵分解（matrix decomposition）与张量分解（tensor decomposition）方法，构建参数缩减版磁矩张量势（compressed mMTP）。

Result: 压缩后参数量减少约1.5–3倍，验证集预测性能未受损；对Fe-Al与CrN的磁性、结构、振动性质及分子动力学（molecular dynamics）模拟结果与未压缩模型数值一致，且与密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）计算和实验数据吻合。

Conclusion: 矩阵与张量分解可在不牺牲精度的前提下有效压缩mMTP参数，所得压缩势在多种性质与模拟场景中保持可靠性。

Abstract: We propose a parameter-reduced version of magnetic Moment Tensor Potential (mMTP) based on various matrix and tensor decompositions. The resulting compressed magnetic machine-learning potential reduces the number of parameters by a factor of approximately 1.5-3 without compromising predictive performance on the validation set. We evaluate the performance of the compressed potentials for magnetic, structural, and vibrational properties, as well as in molecular dynamics simulations of Fe-Al and CrN systems. We demonstrate that the simulation results obtained with the compressed mMTP are numerically consistent with those of the original uncompressed model and are in agreement with density functional theory calculations and experimental data.

</details>


### [119] [State aligned graph wavelet fingerprints of electron trapping in amorphous Si$_3$N$_4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06173)
*Jonathon Cottom,Emilia Olsson*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出“状态对齐图小波指纹（state-aligned graph-wavelet fingerprints）”，用于在非晶氮化硅（a-Si$_3$N$_4$）中联合解析电子态与网络响应，从而对电荷陷阱（charge traps）进行分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非晶材料中电荷陷阱的分类要求同时解析电子态与网络的结构响应。该体系内本征极化子型陷阱、配位驱动型陷阱与电荷驱动转化的陷阱在同一系综中共存，单一视角难以区分。

Method: 构建状态对齐的图小波指纹，并应用于 a-Si$_3$N$_4$ 中的电子俘获；在 415 对弛豫后的中性（neutral）与带负电（negatively charged）晶胞上，将局域化前后的陷阱态进行对齐，从而把电子身份（electronic identity）与多尺度结构指纹关联起来。

Result: 电荷驱动转化（charge-driven conversion）在多个图尺度上引起显著重组并提升指纹振幅；指纹变化可识别每个位点经历的具体转变，占据导致的能级偏移（occupation-induced level shift）追踪局域化增益，图形变则追踪稳定化。电荷诱导缺陷形成主导最深光谱陷阱十分位：69% 的位点在俘获时发生 Si–N 键断裂，而该路径仅占已分类陷阱的 44.2%。

Conclusion: 该指纹框架将电子身份、多尺度网络重组与能量稳定化统一于一个定量描述中，为解析非晶材料中的电荷俘获提供了可推广的方案。

Abstract: Classifying charge traps in amorphous materials requires the electronic state and the network response to be resolved together. We develop state-aligned graph-wavelet fingerprints and apply them to electron trapping in a-Si$_3$N$_4$, where intrinsic-polaronic trapping, coordination-driven trapping, and charge-driven conversion coexist within one ensemble. Across 415 pairs of relaxed neutral and negatively charged cells, alignment of the trap state before and after localisation connects electronic identity to multiscale structural fingerprints. Charge-driven conversion produces substantial reorganisation across graph scales and increases the fingerprint amplitude. The fingerprint change identifies the transformation each site undergoes, while the occupation-induced level shift tracks localisation gain and graph deformation tracks stabilisation. Charge-induced defect formation dominates the deepest spectral-trap decile: 69\% form at sites whose Si--N bond breaks on capture, although this route accounts for 44.2\% of the classified traps. The fingerprints unify electronic identity, multiscale network reorganisation and energetic stabilisation in a quantitative framework for resolving charge trapping in amorphous materials.

</details>


### [120] [Influence of Electrode Thickness and Electrolyte Composition on SEI Formation and Cycle Stability in Binder-Free Hard Carbon Anodes for Na-Ion Batteries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06253)
*Tien-Nguyen-Khoi Trinh,Adrian Beda,Samar Hajjar-Garreau,Cyril Vaulot,Cam{é}lia Matei Ghimbeu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究考察硬碳自支撑电极（HC SSEs）的厚度、大孔性及电解液添加剂对钠离子电池（SIBs）SEI 形成与电化学性能的影响，发现结构优化可达 93% 首次库仑效率，而 FEC 添加剂可显著改善厚电极的循环稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬碳自支撑电极（HC SSEs）可免去黏结剂、溶剂、导电碳与集流体，是钠离子电池（SIBs）传统阳极的替代方案，但其厚度、孔结构与电解液添加剂对固态电解质界面（SEI）及性能的影响尚需厘清。

Method: 以三种不同厚度的纤维素基滤纸（FP）为前驱体，经 1500 °C 热解制备 HC SSEs，并与商业碳布制得的 HC SSE 对比；系统评估厚度、大孔性及含/不含氟代碳酸乙烯酯（FEC）添加剂条件下的 SEI 形成与电化学表现。

Result: 合适的结构、形貌、孔隙率与表面化学性质可实现高达 93% 的首次库仑效率（initial Coulombic efficiency）及 328 mAh g⁻¹（37.2 mA g⁻¹）的可逆容量；在不含 FEC 的 NaPF₆ 中，最薄电极（79 μm）循环最多（50 次），厚度 >185 μm 者约 1–5 次即快速衰减；加入 FEC 后衰减缓解，除最厚的商业电极（1042 μm，5 次）外均可循环 100 次。

Conclusion: 循环稳定性受电极厚度/大孔性及电解液添加剂强烈影响；FEC 存在下形成薄而均匀、富无机组分（含更多 NaF）的 SEI，更稳定、具保护性且电子导电，从而提升稳定性。

Abstract: Hard carbon self-supported electrodes (HC SSEs) have gained significant interest as an alternative to classical binder-based anode materials for sodium-ion batteries (SIBs), eliminating the need for binders, solvents, conductive carbon, and current collectors. In this work, we investigate the impact of HC thickness and macroporosity, as well as the presence/absence of an electrolyte additive, on the formation of the solid electrolyte interphase (SEI) and electrochemical performance. Three cellulose-based filter paper (FP) precursors with different thicknesses were used to prepare HC SSEs through a pyrolysis process at 1500 {\textdegree}C, and then these were compared with an HC SSE obtained from commercial carbon fabric. The results highlight the importance of appropriate structural, morphological, porosity, and surface chemical properties of the materials to achieve high initial Coulombic efficiency (up to 93%) and high reversible capacity (up to 328 mAh g -1 at 37.2 mA g -1 ). However, the cycle stability was greatly impacted by the electrode thickness/macroporosity and the electrolyte additive. In NaPF6 without fluoroethylene carbonate (FEC) additive, the maximum number of cycles (50) was obtained for the thinnest electrode (79 $μ$m), while rapid fading (after $\approx$1-5 cycles) was observed for electrode thicknesses >185 $μ$m. By using the FEC additive, the fading is mitigated and cycling stability (100 cycles) is reached for all the electrodes, except the commercial one (5 cycles), due to its highest thickness (1042 $μ$m). The improvement in stability could be explained by the buildup of a thin homogeneous inorganic-rich SEI containing more NaF in the presence of FEC, which is more stable, protective, and electronically conductive.

</details>


### [121] [First-Principles Investigation of Multimodal Toxic Gas Sensing in Carbon-Tuned hBN-Graphene Alloys: Chemiresistive, Work-Function, and Optical Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06283)
*Tanjuma Shikder Jhumu,Ahmed Zubair*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 采用密度泛函理论（DFT）研究 hBN-石墨烯合金对 Cl2、CO、CO2、NO、NO2、HCN 的吸附行为，评估其作为化学电阻式、功函数式与光学气体传感器的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗、化工、食品、农业与工业安全领域对紧凑、可靠且低成本的气体传感器需求迫切，需对有害气体排放进行快速、高灵敏的监测与控制。因此需探索新型合金材料作为气敏传感介质。

Method: 基于第一性原理（first-principles）的密度泛函理论（DFT）计算，考察六方氮化硼（hBN）与石墨烯（graphene, Gr）构成合金对 Cl2、CO、CO2、NO、NO2、HCN 六种气体分子的吸附行为，并分析吸附能、电荷转移、带隙变化、化学电阻灵敏度、功函数变化、吸收光谱及温度影响。

Result: 含 22% 碳的合金（BNGr-2）对 Cl2、CO、CO2、HCN 的传感能力最优；含 33% 碳的合金（BNGr-3）对 NO 与 NO2 表现出更强吸附作用。NOx 类气体的化学电阻灵敏度最高，其他气体也具显著灵敏度与选择性。功函数变化约 20%。Cl2 与 NO2 为强物理吸附，其余为弱至中等物理吸附，恢复时间极短（10^-1~10^-6 秒）。各气体体系呈现独特吸收光谱；温度升高至 425 K 时气体可从 BNGr-2 脱附。

Conclusion: hBN-Gr 合金在化学电阻式、功函数式及光学气体传感方面均具潜力，可作为污染审计（pollution auditing）用的有前景的气体传感器材料。

Abstract: Compact, reliable, and cost-effective gas sensors have become a highly demanding subject for safety management in the medical sector, chemical manufacturing, food quality monitoring, agriculture, and industrial safety. Hazardous gas emissions need to be controlled and monitored with fast-responsive and highly sensitive sensing devices. A first-principles study employing density functional theory (DFT) was used to investigate the adsorption behavior of Cl2, CO, CO2, NO, NO2, and HCN gas molecules with our proposed alloys, which consisted of hexagonal boron nitride (hBN) and graphene (Gr). The alloy consisting of 22% carbon (BNGr-2) was found to be most competent for sensing Cl2, CO, CO2, and HCN with sufficient adsorption energy, charge transfer, and bandgap alteration. However, NO and NO2 gas molecules showed more engagement with 33% carbon-proportioned alloy (BNGr-3) in terms of adequate gas sensing properties. NOx gases exhibited the most chemiresistive sensitivity towards the adsorbents. Other gases also showed significant chemiresistive sensitivity and distinct selectivity ratios, which would facilitate these alloys as chemiresistive sensors. Besides, noticeable work function variation (~20%) of these systems manifested potential as work function based sensors. Cl2 and NO2 showed strong physical adsorption, while the rest of the gases were weakly to moderately physisorbed, resulting in very short recovery times (10-1 ~ 10-6 seconds). Additionally, the distinctive absorption spectra observed for the gas analyte systems highlighted the potential of the proposed alloys as optical gas sensors. Temperature variation revealed that all gas molecules can be freed from the adsorbent BNGr-2 at 425 K. These findings imply hBN-Gr alloys as promising gas sensors for pollution auditing.

</details>


### [122] [The phonomagnet: Spontaneous order of phonon angular momentum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06395)
*Maike Fahrensohn,R. Matthias Geilhufe*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出声子（phonon）可通过其角动量（angular momentum）的自发有序产生一种新型磁性——声子磁性（phonomagnetism），并预测其在铁磁临界点附近出现，指出 VI$_3$ 为候选材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁性通常被认为源自电子自旋与轨道角动量的集体有序，其他准粒子（quasiparticle）虽与磁性相互作用，却未被视作磁性的主要来源。本文旨在探讨声子能否作为磁性的独立起源。

Method: 基于自旋-声子角动量耦合（spin-phonon angular-momentum coupling）建立理论，推导局域离子角动量间的有效交换相互作用（exchange interaction），并将第一性原理声子计算（first-principles phonon calculations）与已有拉曼光谱（Raman spectroscopy）数据相结合进行验证。

Result: 理论表明该相互作用强度依赖自旋磁化率（spin susceptibility），在铁磁临界点附近可预测声子磁相；同时揭示声子海森堡模型（phonon Heisenberg）、声子 $Γ$-模型（phonon $Γ$-model）与声子 Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya 相互作用等自旋模型的声子类比；并识别 VI$_3$ 为候选声子磁体。

Conclusion: 该研究为解释 VI$_3$ 在体相铁磁转变温度之上所报道的弱磁异常提供了微观框架，并为皮秒（picosecond）时间尺度上的信息存储与操控开辟了新平台。

Abstract: Magnetism is a fundamental property of matter, typically arising from collective ordering of electronic spin and orbital angular momentum. Other quasiparticles interact with magnetism. However, they have not been considered its primary source. Here we show that phonons can generate a distinct form of magnetism through spontaneous ordering of their angular momentum. This phonomagnetism emerges from spin-phonon angular-momentum coupling, producing an effective exchange interaction between local ionic angular momenta. We show that the interaction strength depends on spin susceptibility, with a phonomagnetic phase predicted near a ferromagnetic critical point. Our theory also reveals phononic analogues of established spin models, including phonon Heisenberg, phonon $Γ$-model, and phonon Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interactions. Combining first-principles phonon calculations with recent data from Raman spectroscopy, we identify VI$_3$ as a candidate phonomagnet, providing a microscopic framework for interpreting the weak magnetic anomaly reported above its bulk ferromagnetic transition. These findings open avenues for new platforms for information storage and manipulation on picosecond timescales.

</details>


### [123] [Quantum spectral thermodynamics and active learning enable million-scale exploration of high-entropy ceramics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06467)
*Jie Sun,Yiheng Shen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出量子谱热力学框架，将相互作用诱导的声子谱展宽与自由能相联系，并结合不确定性引导的主动学习，在密度泛函理论精度下探索770万种高熵陶瓷构型，实现10^5倍加速，揭示了化学无序对相稳定性和力学/热学性质的内在贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多组分固体的相稳定性理解与庞大组分空间（compositional space）的探索是固态化学的核心挑战，现有方法难以在高精度下高效遍历海量构型。

Method: 构建量子谱热力学（quantum spectral thermodynamic）框架，将相互作用诱导的声子谱展宽（phonon spectral broadening）与自由能关联；采用不确定性引导的主动学习（uncertainty-guided active learning）工作流，在密度泛函理论（density-functional theory, DFT）精度下探索770万种高熵陶瓷（high-entropy ceramic）构型。

Result: 实现10^5倍加速；化学无序引起的声子自能（phonon self-energy）效应提供超越理想构型熵（configurational entropy）的内在振动热力学稳定贡献，补偿不利混合焓并抑制相分离；发现约12 at.%溶质阈值区分强化与软化区间，与金属-碳反键态（metal-carbon antibonding states）填充相关；化学无序带来高温机械刚度与低热导率的组合，并呈现异常的温度依赖晶格热输运。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了连接多体相互作用与热力学及相稳定性的量子谱基础，并提供可扩展框架以探索此前难以触及的多组分化学空间。

Abstract: Understanding phase stability and navigating vast compositional spaces in multicomponent solids remain central challenges in solid-state chemistry. Here, we develop a quantum spectral thermodynamic framework connecting interaction-induced phonon spectral broadening to free energy, alongside an uncertainty-guided active-learning workflow that explores 7.7 million high-entropy ceramic configurations at density-functional-theory fidelity, achieving a $10^5$-fold acceleration. We show that phonon self-energy effects arising from chemical disorder provide an intrinsic vibrational contribution to thermodynamic stabilization beyond ideal configurational entropy, compensating unfavorable mixing enthalpies and suppressing phase separation. Across the chemical space, we uncover a robust ~12 at.% solute threshold separating strengthening and softening regimes, associated with the filling of metal-carbon antibonding states. Chemical disorder further enables an unusual combination of high-temperature mechanical stiffness and low thermal conductivity, together with anomalous temperature-dependent lattice heat transport. This work establishes a quantum spectral foundation for connecting many-body interactions to thermodynamics and phase stability, while providing a scalable framework for exploring previously inaccessible multicomponent chemical spaces.

</details>


### [124] [Structural, electronic, and magnetic properties of HoCo$_2$ thin films grown by pulsed laser deposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06655)
*Ajay Kumar,Christopher Burgio,Anis Biswas,Yaroslav Mudryk*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 采用脉冲激光沉积（pulsed laser deposition, PLD）在 Si 衬底上制备 HoCo$_2$ 薄膜，薄膜表面与界面质量优良，并保持块体的亚铁磁有序，但相变性质与矫顽力显著区别于块体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探究 HoCo$_2$ 由块体（bulk）缩减至薄膜形态后，其结构质量、磁性相变特征与电子结构能否保持，从而为薄膜化 HoCo$_2$ 的物理性质提供实验依据。

Method: 使用脉冲激光沉积（pulsed laser deposition）在 Si 衬底上生长 HoCo$_2$ 薄膜，并通过 X 射线反射（x-ray reflectivity）、原子力显微镜（atomic force microscopy, AFM）、磁测量与 X 射线光电子能谱（x-ray photoelectron spectroscopy, XPS）进行表征。

Result: X 射线反射的 Kiessig 条纹给出膜厚 $35\pm1$~nm 并表明表面与界面轮廓平滑；AFM 显示亚纳米级表面粗糙度约 170~pm。两个独立制备薄膜均在 $T_{\mathrm{C}}=77\pm3$~K 呈现可重复的亚铁磁转变，但无可分辨的热滞，提示以连续的二阶型相变为主，与块体的第一阶相变不同。5~K 下薄膜具有清晰磁滞，矫顽场约 600~Oe，远大于块体的可忽略矫顽力。XPS 显示块体与薄膜的 Ho $4d$、Co $2p$ 芯能级线形相当，价带谱大体相似。

Conclusion: HoCo$_2$ 薄膜保留了块体的特征磁有序温度及基本局域电子环境与占据电子结构，但其相变类型与矫顽力因薄膜化而明显改变。

Abstract: We report the growth and physical properties of HoCo$_2$ thin films on Si substrate by pulsed laser deposition. Well-defined Kiessig fringes in the x-ray reflectivity data yield a film thickness of $35 \pm 1$~nm and demonstrate smooth film surface and interface profiles. Atomic force microscopy confirms the high morphological quality of the films, revealing subnanometer surface roughness of $\sim$170~pm. Two independently prepared films exhibit a reproducible ferrimagnetic transition at $T_{\mathrm{C}}=77\pm3$~K, demonstrating retention of the characteristic magnetic ordering of bulk HoCo$_2$. However, the absence of resolvable thermal hysteresis indicates a predominantly continuous, second-order like transition in the films, in contrast to the first-order transition of bulk HoCo$_2$. At 5~K, the films exhibit clear magnetic hysteresis with a coercive field of $\sim$600~Oe, substantially larger than the negligible coercivity of bulk HoCo$_2$. X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy reveals comparable Ho $4d$ and Co $2p$ core-level line shapes and broadly similar valence-band spectra for the bulk and film, indicating preservation of the essential local electronic environment and occupied electronic structure.

</details>


### [125] [Strain tunability of electron and hole g-factors in CsPb$X_3$: bulk and nanocrystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06696)
*Aleksander Cząstkiewicz,Paweł Scharoch,Krzysztof Gawarecki*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文在理论上研究了无机铅卤钙钛矿（lead-halide perovskites）参考立方相中的电子与空穴 g 因子（g-factor），采用 18 带 k·p 模型与线性响应理论，发现体材料 g 因子可通过外部应变大幅调控甚至调至零，而 CsPbX₃ 纳米晶中的载流子 g 因子因量子限域（quantum confinement）对应变显著不敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无机铅卤钙钛矿的电子与空穴 g 因子是理解其自旋相关性质的关键参数，但其对应变等外部条件的依赖关系尚需系统的理论刻画。论文旨在建立可描述该体系 g 因子及其应变调控行为的理论框架，并考察体材料与纳米晶之间的差异。

Method: 采用 18 带 k·p 模型（18-band k·p model），其参数由密度泛函理论（DFT）计算得到的目标能带结构确定；随后运用线性响应理论（linear response theory）计算 g 因子并研究其对外部应变的依赖；最后对钙钛矿纳米晶（nanocrystals）中的电子与空穴 g 因子进行实空间（real-space）计算。

Result: 体材料 g 因子可通过外部应变在较大范围内调控，在部分情形下应变可将其调至过零（tune through zero）。相比之下，CsPbX₃ 纳米晶中的载流子 g 因子对应变明显不敏感，其原因归结为量子限域效应。

Conclusion: 应变是调控无机铅卤钙钛矿体材料 g 因子的有效手段，可实现宽范围调节乃至过零；但该调控能力在 CsPbX₃ 纳米晶中因量子限域而大幅减弱，表明体系维度对 g 因子的应变响应具有决定性影响。

Abstract: In this paper, we theoretically study the electron and hole $g$-factors in inorganic lead-halide perovskites for the reference cubic phase. We employ an 18-band $k \cdot p$ model and determine its parameters using target band structures obtained from DFT calculations. We use the linear response theory to calculate the $g$-factors and investigate their dependence on external strain. We then perform real-space calculations of the electron and hole $g$-factors in perovskite nanocrystals. We show that the bulk $g$-factors can be tuned over a wide range by external strain. In some cases, strain can be used to tune the $g$-factor through zero. By contrast, the carrier $g$-factors in CsPb$X_3$ nanocrystals are considerably less sensitive to strain due to quantum confinement.

</details>

### [126] [Sharp inequalities for the Newtonian and logarithmic capacities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04811)
*Mengwei Su,Qiang Sun,Ge Xiong*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 该文在 $\mathbb{R}^n$ 中建立了 Riesz 容量（Riesz capacity）与对数容量（logarithmic capacity）之间的尖锐不等式，并完整刻画了等号成立条件，从而推广了 Pólya 与 Szegő 于 20 世纪 40 年代提出的关于牛顿容量（Newtonian capacity）与对数容量之比的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pólya 与 Szegő 在 1940 年代针对 $\mathbb{R}^2$ 中牛顿容量与对数容量之比提出猜想，此前缺乏一般维数下的相应刻画。本文旨在建立更一般的 Riesz 容量—对数容量尖锐不等式，以自然推广该经典猜想。

Method: 研究对象为 $\mathbb{R}^n$ 中的紧集（compact sets），分析工具为 Riesz 容量与对数容量这两种位势论（potential theory）容量。具体证明技术未在摘要中给出。

Result: 在 $\mathbb{R}^n$ 中建立了 Riesz 容量与对数容量之间的尖锐不等式（sharp inequalities），并对等号成立条件（equality conditions）进行了完整刻画。

Conclusion: 所得结果为 Pólya–Szegő 猜想在 $\mathbb{R}^2$ 情形下的自然推广，将该猜想的适用范围由牛顿容量与对数容量之比拓展至更一般维数下的 Riesz 容量框架。

Abstract: Sharp inequalities between the Riesz and logarithmic capacities of compact sets in $\mathbb{R}^n$ are established, and the equality conditions are fully characterized. These results naturally extend Pólya and Szegő's conjecture on the ratio of Newtonian to logarithmic capacities in $\mathbb{R}^2$, which was posed in the 1940s.

</details>

# hep-th [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [Supplementary Notes on VOA and 2D SCFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03725)
*Taizan Watari*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本笔记以顶点算子代数（vertex operator algebra）语言整理二维非全纯共形场论（conformal field theory, CFT）材料，并在第4节将 Type 0 CFT 表述于顶点算子超代数（vertex operator superalgebra, SVOA）框架下，通过两个辅助参数给出交叉对称性与交错超算子表述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第2--3节主要为作者私人整理二维（一般非全纯）CFT 材料与收集细节，意在补充该主题的入门物理教材。第4节则针对 Type 0 CFT 与 Ramond 相关扇区的表述需求，寻求可系统推广的框架。

Method: 采用顶点算子代数语言组织第2--3节内容；第4节用顶点算子超代数语言处理一般非全纯 Type 0 CFT，借助两个辅助参数（auxiliary parameters）表述 Ramond（SVOA-twisted）扇区中态的交叉对称性（crossing symmetry）与交错超算子。

Result: 给出了 Ramond（SVOA-twisted）扇区及 Ramond--Ramond 扇区中态的交叉对称性与交错超算子（intertwining superoperators）、CFT 顶点超算子（CFT vertex superoperators）的表述；该表述可系统推广至由四个条件刻画的简单流扩展（simple current extensions）类。

Conclusion: 所提出的表述允许向特定简单流扩展类作系统推广，该类由四条件界定：（1）第三上同调消失（vanishing third cohomology），（2）反对称双乘形式（skew-symmetric bimultiplicative form），（3）简单流的无不动点作用（fixed-point-free action of simple currents），以及（4）正文所述的一个附加条件。

Abstract: Sections 2--3 of this note were prepared primarily for the author's private use, as a means of organizing the materials in (generally non-holomorphic) conformal field theory (CFT) on 2-dimensions in the language of the vertex operator algebra, and also of collecting subtle details. The choice of topics is intended to supplement introductory physics textbooks on the subject.
  Section 4 deals with (generally non-holomorphic) Type 0 CFTs in the language of vertex operator superalgebra (SVOA). Formulations of crossing symmetry and intertwining superoperators [resp. CFT vertex superoperators] of states in the Ramond (SVOA-twisted) sector [resp. the Ramond--Ramond sector] are presented by using two auxiliary parameters. The formulation allows systematic generalization to the class of simple current extensions characterized by four conditions: (1) the vanishing third cohomology, (2) skew-symmetric bimultiplicative form, (3) fixed-point-free action of simple currents, and (4) one additional condition described in the main text.

</details>


### [128] [Comment on ''Chiral electromagnetic surface waves on Chern-Simons interfaces'' Phys.Rev.D114(2026)L051903 and on arXiv:2606.12513 by N. Kaloper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03843)
*Igor Bandos,Bohdan Zvorskyi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该工作指出，麦克斯韦电动力学（Maxwell electrodynamics）与平面 Chern-Simons 界面耦合时不存在局域在界面上的平面波解，并纠正了先前文献中遗漏项导致的错误结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对 Kaloper 在 Phys.Rev.D114(2026)L051903 中给出的界面局域平面波解，重新审视其推导过程，检验该结论的正确性。

Method: 通过重走 Kaloper 的推导步骤，定位其中间步骤中某方程遗漏的若干项；并考察 arXiv:2606.12513 [hep-th] 中复现同一结果所使用的方法的一致性。

Result: 计入被遗漏的项后，原文献的最终结论发生改变；同时表明另一文献用于复现该结果的方法并不自洽。

Conclusion: Maxwell 电动力学与平面 Chern-Simons 界面相互作用时不存在局域于界面的平面波解，先前两篇文献的相关结论应予修正。

Abstract: We demonstrate that Maxwell electrodynamics interacting with a planar Chern-Simons interface does not have plane wave solutions localized on the interface. We re-examine the derivation of such waves presented by N. Kaloper in [Phys.Rev.D114(2026)L051903] and show that at an intermediate step certain terms were missed in an equation therein. Taking these terms into account changes the final conclusion of [Phys.Rev.D114(2026)L051903]. We also show that the method used in [arXiv:2606.12513 [hep-th]] to reproduce the same result is not consistent.

</details>


### [129] [Complex Worldline Instantons in (Anti-)de Sitter Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03862)
*Alberto Castellano,Jinwei Chu,Eliseu Kloster,Jamie A. P. Law-Smith*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在世界线形式（worldline formalism）下研究二维最大对称背景加均匀电场中带电大质量粒子的复鞍点（complex saddle points）。研究表明半经典鞍点需要同时复化时空与固有时（proper-time），并给出拓扑不等价瞬子（instanton）扇区的构造与分类方案；世界线作用量仅依赖于绕紧致方向与穿刺点的拓扑数据。AdS 情形由无量纲比 $R|eE|/m$ 区分亚极端、极端与超极端区域，而 dS 与 Minkowski 时空无此区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是理解世界线路径积分中复鞍点的结构及其对非微扰效应与量子干涉的贡献。作者希望在二维最大对称背景（含均匀电场）中，为瞬子扇区的构造与分类提供系统性方案。

Method: 方法基于世界线路径积分（worldline path integral）的半经典近似，对目标时空与固有时参数进行复化，得到由单一复族的两片分支构成的解。作者将作用量改写为相关（非守恒）共轭动量（conjugate momentum）的留数求和，并讨论局域片（local patches）到全局 (A)dS 时空的拼接问题。

Result: 主要结果是瞬子世界线作用量仅依赖其拓扑数据，即绕紧致方向的绕数（winding numbers）和若干孤立穿刺点。在 AdS 中，解的定性结构由无量纲比 $R|eE|/m$（$R$ 为曲率半径）控制，可区分亚极端、极端与超极端区域；dS 中瞬子对该比值不敏感，Minkowski 时空则因缺乏曲率标度而无此区分。

Conclusion: 结论是复鞍点族应作为整体处理：虚固有时分支控制非微扰指数压低，实固有时分支可能携带量子干涉相位的复相位。局域片解提升到全局 (A)dS 时会带来额外约束，进一步限制可对单圈路径积分（one-loop path integral）有贡献的鞍点。

Abstract: We study complex saddle points of the worldline path integral for massive charged particles in two-dimensional maximally symmetric backgrounds threaded by homogeneous electric fields. Within the worldline formalism, we show that the semiclassical saddles generically require complexifying both the target spacetime and the proper-time parameter. The resulting solutions comprise two slices of a single complex family, distinguished by the direction of proper-time evolution.
