The one evolving in imaginary proper time carries an action that typically controls the exponential suppression of non-perturbative effects, while that evolving in real proper time may encode additional complex phases responsible for quantum interference between saddles. Treating this family as a whole, we give a prescription for constructing and classifying all topologically inequivalent instanton sectors, and we find that the worldline action depends only on their topological data, namely winding numbers around the compact directions and certain isolated punctures. Moreover, in Anti-de Sitter spacetime, the qualitative structure of the solutions is governed by the dimensionless ratio $R |eE|/m$ ($R$ being the curvature radius), which distinguishes sub-extremal, extremal, and super-extremal regimes. By contrast, no analogous distinction occurs in de Sitter---where the instantons are insensitive to this ratio---or in Minkowski spacetime, which lacks a curvature scale altogether. We also recast the action as a sum over residues of the relevant (non-conserved) conjugate momentum, making transparent which analytic data control each contribution. Finally, we discuss the subtleties that arise when solutions built in various local patches are lifted to global (Anti-)de Sitter space. These impose further necessary conditions on which saddles can in principle contribute to the one-loop path integral.

</details>


### [130] [Magnetic Higher-Form Symmetries as Dual Fundamental $\infty$-Groupoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03865)
*Alonso Perez-Lona*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文为预量子场论（pre-quantum field theory）的磁高形式对称性（magnetic higher-form symmetries）构建了一个高阶几何（higher-geometric）框架，将磁拓扑荷编码为栈型靶空间（stacky target）的形状，并通过与拓扑场论模栈的对偶获得磁对称背景，进而证明纤维化定理与分解定理，并以纯 Pin±(2) 规范理论作示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究对电对称性（electric symmetries）已有较成熟描述，但预量子场论中磁高形式对称性的几何刻画仍待建立。本文旨在以高阶几何与栈（stack）语言统一刻画磁拓扑荷及其背景，并推广 Pontryagin 对偶（Pontryagin duality）。

Method: 将带栈靶空间的 sigma 模型（sigma-model）的磁拓扑荷编码为其靶空间（等价于其场栈）的形状，该形状与支配电对称性的平坦自同构群（flat automorphism group）呈 op-对偶；把电荷对所选拓扑场论模栈（moduli stack of topological field theories）对偶化以得到磁对称背景，并通过堆叠（stacking）作用于理论；借助凝聚态模态（cohesive modalities）处理非有限、非离散数据。

Result: 当对偶化栈为群状 E∞-对象（grouplike E∞-object）时，证明纤维化定理（fibration theorem），用未规范理论及规范群的磁对称性表达电规范理论的磁对称性，并由同伦不动点谱序列（homotopy-fixed-point spectral sequence）计算且给出显式第一页；对带联络的高阶 U(1)-gerbe 模栈证明分解定理（factorization theorem）：连通靶空间的磁对称背景非典范地分解为整同调群（integral homology groups）的 Pontryagin 对偶，且因离散电荷与带联络靶空间对偶而必为平坦；以纯 Pin±(2) 规范理论示例，O(2) 在形阶 d-3、d-4、d-5 具可逆磁 Z2 对称性，而 Pin-(2) 缺失，前两者由谱序列的单个微分区分。

Conclusion: 该框架以高阶几何统一刻画了预量子场论中的磁高形式对称性，并通过纤维化与分解两个定理给出可计算的结构性结论，在 Pin±(2) 规范理论中得到具体验证。规范操作（gauging）与物理应用将在后续工作中展开。

Abstract: We develop a higher-geometric framework for magnetic higher-form symmetries of pre-quantum field theories. The magnetic (topological) charges of a sigma-model with stacky target are encoded by the shape of the target, equivalently of its stack of fields, which is op-dual to the flat automorphism group controlling electric symmetries. Magnetic symmetry backgrounds are obtained by dualizing these charges against a chosen moduli stack of topological field theories, generalizing Pontryagin duality, and act on the theory by stacking. Cohesive modalities allow for targets with non-finite and non-discrete data. When the dualizing stack is a grouplike $E_\infty$-object, we prove a fibration theorem expressing the magnetic symmetries of an electrically gauged theory in terms of those of the ungauged theory and of the gauge group, computed by a homotopy-fixed-point spectral sequence with an explicit first page. For moduli stacks of higher $U(1)$-gerbes with connection, encoding bulk topological local Lagrangians, we prove a factorization theorem: the magnetic symmetry backgrounds of any connected target split, non-canonically, into Pontryagin duals of its integral homology groups, and are necessarily flat, as a consequence of dualizing discrete charges against a target with connections. We illustrate both theorems with pure $\mathrm{Pin}^\pm(2)$ gauge theory: $O(2)$ carries invertible magnetic $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetries in form degrees $d-3$, $d-4$ and $d-5$ that are absent for $\mathrm{Pin}^-(2)$, the first two distinguished by a single differential of the spectral sequence. Gauging and physical applications are developed in upcoming work.

</details>


### [131] [SU($N$) QCD, four-manifolds and Segre numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03874)
*Elias Furrer,Ranveer Kumar Singh*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究紧致四维流形上拓扑扭曲的 $\mathcal N=2$ 超对称 QCD（gauge group $\mathrm{SU}(N)$）的关联函数，导出包含味道对称性背景通量耦合的 Coulomb 分支积分，并猜想其瞬子贡献等于秩 $N$ 虚拟 Segre 数的生成函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对任意 $N$ 的 $\mathrm{SU}(N)$ 拓扑扭曲 $\mathcal N=2$ QCD 关联函数，尤其是包含味道对称性背景通量耦合的一般情形，尚缺乏统一描述；且瞬子贡献与代数曲面上半稳定层模空间的相交数之间的联系有待建立与验证。

Method: 推导适用于所有 $N$ 的 Coulomb 分支积分（含味道对称性背景通量耦合）；在简单类型且 $b_2^+>1$ 的 $X$ 上，将关联函数化为有限个强耦合奇点上的求和，并用 Coulomb 分支耦合表示。计算这些耦合需引入新高阶技术：Siegel 模形式、精确 $\mathbb Z_N$ 对称性、电荷共轭对称性、Seiberg–Witten 曲线的因子化及爆破公式（blowup formula）。

Result: 对渐近自由的 $\mathrm{SU}(N)$ QCD（$N_f<2N$ 个等质量超多重态），猜想其瞬子对拓扑关联函数的贡献与秩 $N$ 虚拟 Segre 数的生成函数一致，并给出 Segre 数通用级数与 $\mathcal N=1$ 真空中 Coulomb 分支耦合之间的精确字典。纯 $\mathrm{SU}(N)$ 情形与 Mariño–Moore 结果一致，扭曲关联函数给出 $X$ 的 $\mathrm{SU}(N)$ Donaldson 不变量。$\mathrm{SU}(3)$ QCD 情形下确定每个 $N_f=1,\dots,5$ 的全部 13 个 Coulomb 分支耦合的闭式表达式，与 Göttsche–Kool 的秩 3 虚拟 Segre 数结果精确吻合。

Conclusion: 拓扑扭曲 $\mathcal N=2$ $\mathrm{SU}(N)$ QCD 的关联函数可由有限 Coulomb 分支耦合刻画，其瞬子分支贡献与代数曲面上半稳定层模空间的虚拟 Segre 数生成函数相符；该对应在纯 $\mathrm{SU}(N)$ 及 $\mathrm{SU}(3)$、$N_f=1,\dots,5$ 情形获得验证。

Abstract: We study correlation functions of topologically twisted $\mathcal N=2$ supersymmetric QCD with gauge group $\mathrm{SU}(N)$ on a compact four-manifold $X$. We derive the Coulomb branch integral for all $N$, including new couplings to background fluxes for the flavor symmetry. For $X$ of simple type with $b_2^+>1$, the correlators become sums over a finite number of strong-coupling singularities on the Coulomb branch, and can be expressed using a number of Coulomb branch couplings. The sum contains the $\mathcal N=1$ vacua, which become the supersymmetric vacua when the $\mathcal N=2$ theory is broken to $\mathcal N=1$ by an adjoint mass deformation, and whose contribution we attribute to the instanton branch. We focus on asymptotically free $\mathrm{SU}(N)$ QCD with $N_f<2N$ equal mass hypermultiplets, and conjecture that its instanton contribution to topological correlators agrees with the generating function of virtual Segre numbers of rank $N$, which are intersection numbers on the moduli space of semistable sheaves on an algebraic surface. We present a precise dictionary for all $N$ between universal series for Segre numbers and Coulomb branch couplings at the $\mathcal N=1$ vacua. The calculation of these couplings requires new higher-rank techniques, involving Siegel modular forms, an exact $\mathbb Z_N$ symmetry, charge conjugation symmetry, factorisation of the Seiberg--Witten curve, and the blowup formula. For pure $\mathrm{SU}(N)$, our result agrees with earlier work by Mariño and Moore, and the twisted correlators give the $\mathrm{SU}(N)$ Donaldson invariants of $X$. For $\mathrm{SU}(3)$ QCD, we determine closed-form expressions for all 13 Coulomb branch couplings for each $N_f=1,\dots, 5$, and find precise agreement with results of Göttsche and Kool for virtual rank 3 Segre numbers.

</details>


### [132] [Quantum covariant bit threads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03929)
*Matthew Headrick,Sreeman Reddy Kasireddy,Andrew Rolph*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将量子极值面（QES）公式重新表述为量子协变比特线程（quantum covariant bit threads），以体（bulk）中的最小或最大流量替代极值面，并可用于黑洞蒸发等场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠熵（entanglement entropy）在全息（holography）中具有核心地位，而量子极值面（QES）公式是同时处理动力学与体量子修正（如黑洞蒸发）的标准工具。作者希望提供与 QES 等价、但以流量而非极值面表述的替代方案。

Method: 推导并探讨 QES 等价 prescription，即量子协变比特线程（quantum covariant bit threads），用体（bulk）中的最小或最大流量来表述全息纠缠熵。方案分两类：通常为类空的 V-flows 与类时的 U-flows；为计入体量子修正，流线可在体内起止，其端点分布由体纠缠熵控制。

Result: 在比特线程语言中，QES 表现为流的量子瓶颈（quantum bottleneck）：部分线程穿过它（对应广义熵中的面积项），另一些线程跨越它（对应熵项）。

Conclusion: 该 prescription 为 QES 公式提供了互补视角，并为黑洞蒸发（black hole evaporation）、岛（islands）与婴儿宇宙（baby universes）等重要现象提供新见解。

Abstract: Entanglement entropy plays a central role in holography. The quantum extremal surface (QES) formula is the standard tool in settings where both dynamics and bulk quantum corrections are important, such as in black hole evaporation. We derive and explore QES-equivalent prescriptions --- quantum covariant bit threads --- that formulate holographic entanglement entropy in terms of minimal or maximal bulk flows rather than extremal surfaces. Like classical covariant bit threads, our prescriptions come in two types: (typically) spacelike V-flows, and timelike U-flows. To account for bulk quantum corrections, the flow lines can start and end at points in the bulk, with the distribution of endpoints controlled by bulk entanglement entropies. In our bit thread language, the QES is a quantum bottleneck to the flow, with some threads passing through it (accounting for the area term in the generalized entropy) and others jumping across it (accounting for the entropy term). Our prescriptions provide a complementary perspective to the QES formula and offer new insights on important phenomena such as black hole evaporation, islands, and baby universes.

</details>


### [133] [Exact effective central charges for a family of 3d superconformal field theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03950)
*Griffen Adams*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文针对与三维流形上 Chern-Simons 理论通过 3d-3d 对应（3d-3d correspondence）相关联的一类三维超共形场论（superconformal field theory），猜想其有效中心荷（effective central charge, c_eff）的精确公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有效中心荷 c_eff 控制共形场论中受保护态（protected states）子空间的增长，因此确定其精确取值对该类理论的结构刻画具有重要意义；摘要指出此前已有通过 resurgent continuation 得到的已知结果可作为对照。

Method: 作者提出一个猜想性公式，用某个特定模形式空间（space of modular forms）的维数来表达 c_eff；并通过与 resurgent continuation 计算所得已知结果进行比对来验证该方案。

Result: 摘要声称该公式可在模形式空间维数与 c_eff 之间建立对应关系，且该方案通过了与 resurgent continuation 已知结果的检验。

Conclusion: 在该类由 3d-3d 对应给出的三维超共形场论中，有效中心荷可由模形式空间维数精确刻画，此猜想得到了现有计算的部分支持。摘要未给出具体公式形式与数值细节。

Abstract: We conjecture an exact formula for the effective central charge, ceff, of a family of three- dimensional superconformal field theories related to Chern-Simons theory on a three-manifold by the 3d-3d correspondence. The formula determines ceff , which governs the growth of a protected sector of states in the conformal theory, in terms of the dimension of a particular space of modular forms. We confirm our approach by checking against known results computed through resurgent continuation.

</details>


### [134] [Wilson line defect indices for M2-brane SCFTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04065)
*Hirotaka Hayashi,Tomoki Nosaka,Tadashi Okazaki*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究三维 $\mathcal{N}\ge 4$ 超对称规范理论中的线缺陷库仑与希格斯指标，提出其 Witten 指标的一般公式，并发现 M2-膜理论中 Wilson 线缺陷指标的代数递推关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究线缺陷（line defect）在三维超对称规范理论中的指标结构，特别是库仑支（Coulomb branch）与希格斯支（Higgs branch）上的 Witten 指标及其物理意义。

Method: 通过将装饰缺陷指标（decorated defect indices）与普通指标（ordinary indices）之比定义为归一化线缺陷指标，并利用真空方程（vacuum equations）读取未破缺的剩余规范群（residual gauge group）。

Result: 归一化线缺陷指标给出良定义的整数值，即单位 R-荷逸度极限（unit R-charge fugacity limit）下的线缺陷 Witten 指标；两指标均计数量子态中在剩余规范群下不变的态。在无 Chern-Simons 项理论中给出库仑与希格斯极限下的一般公式，并在 ADHM 型理论与 ABJ(M) 理论中发现 Wilson 线缺陷库仑指标的代数递推关系。

Conclusion: 高阶线缺陷指标可由低阶数据递推确定，该结论适用于 $U(N)$、$USp(2N)$、$SO(2N)$ 与 $SO(2N+1)$ ADHM 型理论及 ABJ(M) 理论。

Abstract: We investigate line defect Coulomb and Higgs indices in 3d $\mathcal{N}\ge 4$ supersymmetric gauge theories. The normalized line defect indices defined by ratios of the decorated defect indices to the ordinary indices yield well-defined integer values, the line defect Witten indices in the unit R-charge fugacity limit. We propose general formulae for these Witten indices in both Coulomb and Higgs limits for the theories without Chern-Simons terms, and we show that both count the states invariant under the residual gauge group left unbroken at a generic point of the branch, which we read off from the vacuum equations. Furthermore, we find powerful algebraic recursion relations for Wilson line defect Coulomb indices in the 3d SCFTs describing M2-branes, including ADHM-type theories and ABJ(M) theories. These relations imply that higher rank line defect indices are recursively determined by lower rank data across $U(N)$, $USp(2N)$, $SO(2N)$ and $SO(2N+1)$ ADHM-type theories and ABJ(M) theories.

</details>


### [135] [Anomaly-free six-dimensional $Sp(1)_R$-gauged supergravities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04131)
*Katrin Becker,Ergin Sezgin,Qi You*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究六维 $N=(1,0)$ 超引力中规范 $Sp(1)_R$ 对称性的反常约束，结合弦电荷格嵌入条件，将矢量多重数限制为 $n_V=12$ 或 $96$，并系统枚举满足条件的谱与模型数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 六维超引力中局域引力、规范及混合反常（anomaly）的抵消对物质内容施加了强约束，而弦论嵌入要求反常系数落入幺模的秩二弦电荷格（rank-two string charge lattice）。本文旨在刻画这些约束对矢量多重（vector multiplet）数量的限制，并系统分类可行谱。

Method: 在六维 $N=(1,0)$ 超引力框架下，设定一个张量多重（tensor multiplet）、$n_V$ 个矢量多重与 $n_H$ 个超多重（hypermultiplet），施加局域引力、规范与混合反常抵消条件，并要求反常系数可嵌入幺模秩二弦电荷格，随后对半单（semisimple）非 $R$ 规范代数及可约（reductive）分支进行穷举式谱组装计算。

Result: 反常条件将矢量多重数强约束为正整数 $n_V=12$ 或 $96$。中性阿贝尔因子（drone）导致规范动能矩阵对应行列为零，无法作为普通传播矢量多重保留。$n_V=96$ 时，在不含 $A_1$ 与 $A_2$ 因子的半单非 $R$ 代数中不存在无 drone 谱；$n_V=12$ 时半单非 $R$ 分支必为 $A_1^3$。在双重态/伴随截断（doublet/adjoint truncation）下得到 $4{,}337{,}331$ 个一致模型，归为 $102$ 个反常矢量类（anomaly-vector class）。在可约分支 $A_2\times U(1)\times Sp(1)_R$ 中，限制 $A_2$ 表示为 $\{\mathbf 1,\mathbf 3,\overline{\mathbf 3},\mathbf 6,\overline{\mathbf 6},\mathbf 8\}$ 且 $U(1)$ 荷为原初赋值 $|q|\le 2$ 时，得到 $178{,}538$ 个解，归为 $21$ 组反常矢量。

Conclusion: 六维 $N=(1,0)$ 超引力的反常抵消与弦格嵌入条件高度限制矢量多重数目，仅 $n_V=12$ 与 $96$ 可行；drone 因子无法作为普通矢量多重存在。一致谱数量巨大但可按反常矢量类高度压缩，显示反常条件在模型分类中具有强组织作用。

Abstract: We study anomaly constraints in six-dimensional $N=(1,0)$ supergravity with gauged $Sp(1)_R$ symmetry, one tensor multiplet, $n_V$ vector multiplets and $n_H$ hypermultiplets. Imposing cancellation of local gravitational, gauge and mixed anomalies, together with the embedding of the anomaly coefficients in a unimodular rank-two string charge lattice, the number of vector multiplets is sharply constrained: the only allowed positive values are $n_V=12$ and $n_V=96$. For a neutral abelian factor, or drone, the anomaly equations force the corresponding row and column of the abelian gauge-kinetic matrix to vanish. Such a factor therefore cannot be retained as an ordinary propagating vector multiplet. For $n_V=96$, within the class of semisimple non-$R$ gauge algebras containing no $A_1$ or $A_2$ factor, the exhaustive spectra-assembly calculation yields no drone-free spectrum. For $n_V=12$, the semisimple non-$R$ branch is necessarily $A_1^3$. In the doublet/adjoint truncation described in the text, we find $4{,}337{,}331$ consistent models, classified by $102$ anomaly-vector classes. We also consider the reductive branch $A_2\times U(1)\times Sp(1)_R$. For $A_2$ representations in $\{\mathbf 1,\mathbf 3,\overline{\mathbf 3}, \mathbf 6,\overline{\mathbf 6},\mathbf 8\}$ and primitive $U(1)$ charge assignments with $|q|\le 2$, we find $178{,}538$ solutions, classified by $21$ sets of anomaly vectors.

</details>


### [136] [Higher-form Symmetries of Linearized Gravity in Maximally Symmetric Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04218)
*Natsuya Kido*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文采用基于嘉当几何（Cartan geometry）的 MacDowell-Mansouri 表述，将极大对称时空（maximally symmetric spacetimes）上的引力场表述为规范理论，并通过牛顿引力常数（Newton's gravitational constant）的级数展开研究线性化引力的高形式对称性（higher-form symmetries）。结果证明闵可夫斯基时空（Minkowski spacetime）上的线性化引力存在 1-形式对称性（1-form symmetry），并在小宇宙学常数（cosmological constant）下于德西特与反德西特时空（de Sitter and anti-de Sitter spacetimes）中证明存在近似 1-形式对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于将引力视为规范理论（gauge theory）的框架下，探究线性化引力场的高形式对称性（higher-form symmetries）。摘要未给出更具体的动机陈述。

Method: 方法上采用基于嘉当几何（Cartan geometry）的 MacDowell-Mansouri 表述，将极大对称时空（maximally symmetric spacetimes）上的引力场构建为规范理论，并对牛顿引力常数（Newton's gravitational constant）作级数展开以考察线性化引力。

Result: 结果表明闵可夫斯基时空（Minkowski spacetime）上的线性化引力存在 1-形式对称性（1-form symmetry）；对于小宇宙学常数（cosmological constant）情形下的其他极大对称时空（德西特与反德西特时空，de Sitter and anti-de Sitter spacetimes），相应线性化引力存在近似 1-形式对称性，并给出了该对称性对宇宙学常数的显式依赖关系。

Conclusion: 结论为：线性化引力在闵可夫斯基时空具有精确的 1-形式对称性，而在具有小宇宙学常数的德西特与反德西特时空中具有近似 1-形式对称性，其偏离程度可显式地由宇宙学常数刻画。

Abstract: We formulate the gravitational field on maximally symmetric spacetimes as a gauge theory, and investigate higher-form symmetries of the linearized gravitational field via a series expansion in Newton's gravitational constant. To treat gravity as a gauge theory, the MacDowell-Mansouri formulation based on Cartan geometry is utilized. We show the existence of a 1-form symmetry in linearized gravity on Minkowski spacetime. Furthermore, for other maximally symmetric spacetimes (de Sitter and anti-de Sitter spacetimes) with a small cosmological constant, we demonstrate the existence of an approximate 1-form symmetry in the corresponding linearized gravity, detailing its explicit dependence on the cosmological constant.

</details>


### [137] [Wald entropy and Wen-Zee type gauge-curvature coupling in 2+1-dimensional gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04476)
*Aryan Bhatia,Giandomenico Palumbo,Suraj S. Hegde*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将量子霍尔流体 Chern-Simons 描述中的 Wen-Zee 项（Wen-Zee term）推广为洛伦兹协变形式，基于 Maxwell 代数（Maxwell algebra）的三维 Chern-Simons 引力，计算其对黑洞视界熵的 Wald 熵贡献，并给出其作为视界推动荷（horizon boost charge）的环路积分表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wen-Zee 项原本描述量子霍尔流体中电荷与空间曲率的耦合，但其适用范围局限于非相对论背景。作者希望将该耦合扩展至洛伦兹协变情形及黑洞时空，从而把量子霍尔物理中的几何响应与引力视界热力学联系起来。

Method: 采用基于 Maxwell 代数（Maxwell algebra）的三维 Chern-Simons 引力框架，构建 Wen-Zee 耦合的洛伦兹协变推广；计算其 Wald 熵贡献，并给出沿分叉圆切向投影的附加规范场环路积分。进一步讨论该积分在投影方向协变常（covariantly constant）时的阿贝尔和乐（Abelian holonomy）解释，并在平直宇宙学解与 AdS-Lorentz 黑洞上评估该荷。

Result: 该耦合的 Wald 熵贡献表现为视界推动荷（horizon boost charge），由附加规范场沿分叉圆（bifurcation circle）切向投影的环路积分给出。即使混合项在壳（on shell）消失，该贡献仍可非零。在平直宇宙学解上完成荷的评估；在 AdS-Lorentz 黑洞上，结合光滑视界场与指定热系综完成评估。

Conclusion: Wen-Zee 型混合规范-曲率耦合可自然出现在基于 Maxwell 代数的三维 Chern-Simons 引力中，其对视界熵的贡献具有明确的荷解释与和乐结构。研究结果将量子霍尔物理中的几何响应与引力视界热力学相连接。

Abstract: The Wen-Zee term is a mixed gauge-curvature coupling in the topological Chern-Simons description of a quantum Hall fluid, encoding the electric charge bound to spatial curvature. We investigate a Lorentz-covariant generalisation of this coupling, expanding its scope to black-hole spacetimes and determine its contribution to horizon entropy. Such a coupling arises naturally in three-dimensional Chern-Simons gravity based on the Maxwell algebra. We show that its Wald entropy contribution is a horizon boost charge, given by a loop integral of the additional gauge field projected along the tangent to the bifurcation circle. This contribution can be nonzero even when the mixed term vanishes on shell. We show that the integral admits an Abelian holonomy interpretation when the projection direction is covariantly constant. We evaluate the charge on a flat cosmological solution and, using smooth horizon fields and a specified thermal ensemble, on an AdS-Lorentz black hole. These results connect the geometric response familiar from quantum Hall physics to gravitational horizon thermodynamics.

</details>


### [138] [Majorana bilinears, compact modular seeds and the approach to the Tsirelson bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04729)
*M. S. Guimaraes,I. Roditi,S. P. Sorella*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究 1+1 维自由大质量马约拉纳场（free massive Majorana field）真空中的贝尔-CHSH 不等式（Bell-CHSH inequality），以左右 Rindler 楔形（Rindler wedge）内涂抹场的偶双线性（even bilinear）作为二分观测量，并借助 Tomita-Takesaki 算子构造测试函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在精确刻画自由马约拉纳场偶扇区（even sector）如何实现 Summers-Werner 机制，即检验 Rindler 楔形真空是否违反经典贝尔界，以及 Tsirelson 界能否达到，从而为量子场论中的纠缠与定域性结构提供定量结论。

Method: 观测量取左右楔形内涂抹场的偶双线性；测试函数由模频率变量（modular-frequency variable）的种子出发，经右楔形的 Tomita-Takesaki 算子及其伴随乘以 i（对应扭曲对偶性，twisted duality）生成。对正模频率种子推导单粒子重叠与贝尔参数的闭式表达；当种子为单一紧支撑鼓包的相位旋转时，各楔形双线性生成一份 Pauli 代数（Pauli algebra），据此精确计算真空限制到两份代数上的关联矩阵（correlation matrix），并求最优测量设置。

Result: 正模频率种子的单粒子重叠与模权重无关，贝尔参数可得闭式解。最优设置违反经典界的谱宽（spectral width）可达平面设置的两倍以上。楔形真空中对任意二分观测量均无法达到 Tsirelson 界，且趋近该界要求贝尔向量的模谱权重集中到模算子谱点 λ=1。

Conclusion: 结果精确建立了自由马约拉纳场偶扇区实现 Summers-Werner 机制的方式，并给出楔形真空纠缠违背程度受模谱权重分布限制的定量刻画。

Abstract: In this work we study the Bell-CHSH inequality in the vacuum of the free massive Majorana field in $1+1$ dimensions, taking as the dichotomic observables of the two parties even bilinears of smeared fields localized in the right and in the left Rindler wedges. The test functions are generated from seeds in the modular-frequency variable through the Tomita-Takesaki operator of the right wedge and, on the side of the left wedge, through its adjoint multiplied by $i$, which accounts for twisted duality. For seeds supported at positive modular frequency the one-particle overlaps between the two parties do not depend on the modular weights, and we obtain the Bell parameter in closed form. When the seeds are phase rotations of a single compact bump, the bilinears generate a copy of the Pauli algebra in each wedge, and we compute exactly the correlation matrix of the vacuum restricted to the two copies. The optimal settings violate the classical bound up to spectral widths more than twice as large as those reached with planar settings. We also show that the Tsirelson bound is never attained in the wedge vacuum for any choice of dichotomic observables, and that approaching it forces the modular spectral weight of the Bell vectors to concentrate at the spectral point $λ=1$ of the modular operator. This establishes in a precise way how the even sector of the free Majorana field realizes the Summers-Werner mechanism.

</details>


### [139] [An Observer in de Sitter Space, and Rereading Everett](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04804)
*Edward Witten*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文重新审视 Hugh Everett 的经典论文，并从身处德西特空间（de Sitter space）内部的观察者视角讨论其熵（entropy），以此说明在宇宙学（cosmology）问题中，将观察者视为被观测宇宙的一部分具有重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究关注宇宙学中观察者地位这一基础性问题，即对某些特定问题而言，观察者本身属于被观测宇宙这一事实不容忽视。

Method: 方法为重新考察（re-examine）Hugh Everett 的一篇经典文章，并采用德西特空间内部观察者的立场（standpoint）来分析其熵。

Result: 摘要未给出具体计算或数值结果，仅指出该分析为“观察者内在于被观测宇宙”这一要点提供了一个良好的示例（nice example）。

Conclusion: 结论是：从内部观察者角度处理德西特空间熵，可具体体现宇宙学中严肃对待“观察者属于被观测宇宙”这一事实的重要性；本文发表于纪念 Gerard 't Hooft 的 International Journal of Modern Physics A 特刊。

Abstract: After re-examining a classic article by Hugh Everett, I will discuss the entropy of de Sitter space from the standpoint of an observer living in the space. This provides a nice example of the importance -- for certain questions -- of taking seriously the fact that in cosmology any observer is really part of the universe that is being observed. (This article has appeared in a special issue of International Journal of Modern Physics A in honor of Gerard 't Hooft.

</details>


### [140] [Scalar potential bounds on one-parameter Calabi-Yau threefolds with fluxes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04858)
*Nana Geraldine Cabo Bizet,José de J. Pérez Lona*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在单参数 Calabi–Yau 三流形紧化的 IIB 型弦论多场通量势中，检验精化 de Sitter 猜想（refined de Sitter conjecture, RdSC）、跨普朗克审查猜想（Trans-Planckian Censorship Conjecture, TCC）与物种尺度（species scale）Λ_s。利用精确周期（exact periods）扫描 conifold 区域，发现 no-scale 结构使 RdSC 第一条件普遍满足，而 TCC 给出紧化体积下界，并蕴含 V≲3Λ_s²，成为真正的约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弦论沼泽地（swampland）猜想对有效场论施加限制，但多数检验依赖大复结构展开，难以探测 conifold 区域。本文旨在用精确周期而非近似展开，在 h^{2,1}=1 的多场通量势中系统检验 RdSC、TCC 与物种尺度约束。

Method: 在单参数 Calabi–Yau 三流形上紧化 IIB 型弦论，标量势依赖 axio-dilaton 与单一复结构模量，沿 Kähler 方向为 no-scale。限制 h^{2,1}=1，用 Picard–Fuchs 方程得到的精确周期探测 conifold 区域。对镜像五次（mirror quintic）(h^{2,1},h^{1,1})=(1,101) 扫描 200 组通量配置，并做多场数值演化。

Result: 96% 的通量配置出现不稳定极值，c'=0.18。计入 Kähler runaway 后，no-scale 结构将每个临界点的对数斜率固定为普适值 √6，故 RdSC 第一条件处处满足。复结构为较重扇区，axio-dilaton 主导不稳定方向。TCC 斜率在大复结构处趋于 2，在 conifold 处发散，在 axio-dilaton 方向恰为 √2，此时领头势呈单一 SL(2) 协变形式。TCC 还给出紧化体积下界 v>v_0∼e^N。

Conclusion: 场位移满足物种尺度场程界；恢复 Λ_s 的体积依赖后，TCC 的体积下界蕴含 V≲3Λ_s²。因此 TCC 对这类模型构成真正的约束，而 RdSC 第一条件因 no-scale 结构而自动满足。

Abstract: We study the refined de Sitter conjecture (RdSC), the Trans-Planckian Censorship Conjecture (TCC) and the species scale $Λ_s$ in the multifield flux potential of type IIB string theory compactified on one-parameter Calabi--Yau threefolds. The scalar potential depends on the axio-dilaton and on the single complex-structure modulus, and is no-scale along the Kähler directions. Restricting to $h^{2,1}=1$ allows us to work with the exact periods obtained from the Picard--Fuchs equations rather than with their large-complex-structure expansion, and therefore to probe the conifold region. For the mirror quintic, $(h^{2,1},h^{1,1})=(1,101)$, we scan 200 flux configurations and find unstable extrema in $96\%$ of the cases, with $c'=0.18$. Once the Kähler runaway is taken into account, the no-scale structure fixes the logarithmic slope of the potential at every critical point to the universal value $\sqrt6$, so that the first RdSC condition is satisfied throughout. Statistically, the complex structure is the heavier sector and the axio-dilaton dominates the unstable directions. For the TCC we obtain analytic results valid for the whole class of one-parameter models: the slope approaches the universal value $2$ at large complex structure, diverges at the conifold, and equals exactly $\sqrt2$ in the axio-dilaton directions, where the leading potential takes a single $SL(2)$-covariant form, as the multifield evolutions confirm numerically. The TCC further translates into a lower bound on the compactification volume, $v>v_0\sim e^{N}$, controlled by the number of e-folds. Finally, the field displacements along our trajectories are compatible with the species-scale field-range bound, and restoring the volume dependence of $Λ_s$ shows that $V\lesssim3Λ_s^2$ is implied by the TCC volume bound, which is thus the genuine constraint on these models.

</details>


### [141] [The Phenomenology of the Dark Dimension and Non-Supersymmetric Strings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04909)
*Cumrun Vafa*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文综述“暗维度”（dark dimension）情景：基于沼地原理（Swampland principles）与暗能量 Λ 的微小性，预言一微米尺度额外维度，并系统梳理其粒子物理与宇宙学预言，随后转向非超对称弦真空（non-supersymmetric string vacua）的最新进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沼地原理结合暗能量 Λ 的极小值，自然导向含一微米级额外维度的暗维度情景，需要一个统一框架来解释暗扇区演化与层级问题，并为非超对称弦真空提供动机。

Method: 采用综述方式，整合暗维度情景的预言推导，并结合 DESI DR2（+CMB+超新星）数据拟合；同时回顾非超对称弦真空相关构造，包括非几何通量紧致化（non-geometric flux compactifications）与 M 理论 Scherk–Schwarz 紧致化中的对偶对称性保护临界点。

Result: 给出 KK 引力子暗物质（或引力子转化成的 quintessence 场 KK 塔）、通过体 B−L 规范对称性使中微子质量与 Λ^{1/4} 关联、QCD 轴子衰变常数 f_a∼Λ^{1/12}∼10^9–10^10 GeV（在 5d 普朗克尺度 M_5 处）、电弱层级与 Λ^{1/6} 关联，以及 GUT 与弱尺度以 M_5 为几何平均的关系；暗维度演化可关联暗能量与暗物质质量变化，符合 DESI DR2 数据并避免 phantom crossing。

Conclusion: 暗维度情景提供了演化暗扇区的具体实现，拟合结果暗示暗能量场位于模空间内部、靠近不稳定临界点；这推动了对非超对称弦真空（孤立临界点与新兴对偶网）的研究进展综述。

Abstract: Swampland principles, combined with the smallness of dark energy $Λ$, lead to the dark dimension scenario, with one extra dimension of micron size. We review this scenario and its predictions: KK graviton dark matter (or the KK tower of the quintessence field into which the gravitons convert), neutrino masses tied to $Λ^{1/4}$ via a bulk $B-L$ gauge symmetry, QCD axion decay constant $f_a\sim Λ^{1/12}\sim 10^9-10^{10}$ GeV at the 5d Planck scale $M_5$, connecting electroweak hierarchy to $Λ^{1/6}$, and a relation between the GUT and weak scales with $M_5$ as the geometric mean. The dark dimension also provides a concrete realization of an evolving dark sector: the evolution of its diameter correlates the variation of dark energy with that of the dark matter mass, fitting the recent DESI DR2 (+CMB+supernovae) data while avoiding the phantom crossing that the $w_0w_a$CDM parametrization would suggest---the apparent phantom behavior instead arises from the evolving dark matter mass. The fits suggest that the dark energy field sits in the interior of moduli space, near an unstable critical point. Motivated by this, we review recent progress on non-supersymmetric string vacua: isolated critical points protected by duality symmetries, in non-geometric flux compactifications and in Scherk--Schwarz compactifications of M-theory, and an emerging duality web for non-supersymmetric strings. This note is based on two talks given by the author at String Phenomenology 2026 (Paris) and Strings 2026 (Shanghai).

</details>


### [142] [$BF$ effective action topological implications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04962)
*Erika Amador-Ramos,Aldo Martínez-Merino,Roberto Santos-Silva*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 研究7维拓扑BF理论（topological BF theory）在4维约化（4-dimensional reduction）下的有效作用量，并给出Minkowski与Schwarzschild时空中的精确解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确说明研究动机，仅指出研究目标为考察7维拓扑BF理论经4维约化后的有效作用量及其拓扑含义。

Method: 采用维度约化（dimensional reduction）方法，将7维拓扑BF理论约化至4维，得到有效作用量（effective action）；随后推导相应运动方程（equations of motion），并在Minkowski与Schwarzschild两种时空中求解。

Result: 获得了约化后理论的有效作用量，讨论了其拓扑含义（topological implications），并推导出Minkowski与Schwarzschild时空中运动方程的精确解（exact solutions）。

Conclusion: 7维拓扑BF理论经4维约化后可得到具有拓扑意义且可精确求解的有效理论，其解在平直与Schwarzschild背景下均存在。

Abstract: We investigate the effective action of a 7-dimensional topological $BF$ theory under a 4-dimensional reduction. We discuss the topological implications of the resulting theory and analyze the corresponding field equations. In particular, we derive exact solutions to these equations of motion in both Minkowski and Schwarzschild spacetimes.

</details>


### [143] [From Critical Zero Modes to Interior Dynamics in Holographic Superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05208)
*Yan Liu,Hong-Da Lyu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究全息超导体（holographic superconductor）临界行为如何控制带电黑洞内部几何。临界零模在 Reissner–Nordström 柯西视界（Cauchy horizon）附近产生对数振荡，使内部临界极限非均匀，并决定 Einstein–Rosen 塌缩宽度与 Josephson 相位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探究全息超导体的临界行为与带电黑洞内部几何之间的对应关系，尤其是外域凝聚体在临界点消失后，临界零模如何影响视界内部结构。

Method: 方法为解析考察临界零模在 Reissner–Nordström 柯西视界附近的对数振荡行为，并结合透射临界模振幅与局部柯西视界量，推导 Einstein–Rosen 塌缩宽度 Δz_ER 与后续 Josephson 区间相位 Θ_J；同时以完全非线性解在普通与三临界（tricritical）情形下进行验证。

Result: 结果显示，若凝聚体满足 t≡1−T/T_c∝ε^{2r}，则 Δz_ER∝t^{1/r}、Θ_J∝t^{−1/r}；该 Josephson 相位传递至首个 Kasner 时期，产生振荡的 Kasner 指数及标量速度连续零点累积律 t_n∼n^{−r}。临界零模确定主导塌缩与相位系数，并获非线性解定量确认。

Conclusion: 结论为：近临界标度行为由临界模透射（critical-mode transmission）主导，而低温行为则由零温几何的红外完成（infrared completion）控制，两者由不同机制支配黑洞内部几何。

Abstract: We study how the critical behavior of holographic superconductors controls the interior geometry of charged black holes. Although the exterior condensate vanishes at criticality, the critical zero mode develops logarithmic oscillations near the Reissner--Nordström Cauchy horizon, rendering the interior critical limit nonuniform. We show that the transmitted critical-mode amplitude, together with local Cauchy-horizon quantities, fixes both the leading Einstein--Rosen collapse $Δz_{\rm ER}$ width and the phase $Θ_J$ in the subsequent Josephson regime. If the condensate obeys $t\equiv1-T/T_c\proptoε^{2r}$, then $Δz_{\rm ER}\propto t^{1/r}$ and $Θ_J\propto t^{-1/r}$. This Josephson phase is transferred to the first Kasner epoch, producing oscillatory Kasner exponents and an accumulation law $t_n\sim n^{-r}$ for successive zeros of the scalar velocity. The critical zero mode fixes the leading collapse and phase coefficients, which are quantitatively confirmed by fully nonlinear solutions in both the ordinary and tricritical cases. At low temperature, the interior instead depends on the infrared completion of the zero-temperature geometry. Thus near-critical scaling is governed by critical-mode transmission, whereas the low-temperature behavior is controlled by the infrared endpoint.

</details>


### [144] [Inflationary magnetogenesis in Proca electrodynamics with Schwinger particle production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05406)
*Mattia Dubbini,Orlando Luongo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究含大质量矢量场（massive vector fields）的暴胀磁生成（inflationary magnetogenesis），纳入施温格效应（Schwinger effect）导致的带电标量粒子产生及其对背景电场能量密度的反作用（backreaction）。结果表明，非零耦合常数下施温格粒子产生会在暴胀末期剧烈削减电场能量密度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探究暴胀时期磁生成过程中矢量场与带电粒子产生的相互作用，特别是施温格效应对背景电场能量密度的反作用，以理解电场与磁场能量密度的相对主导地位及其对磁生成的影响。

Method: 先忽略施温格效应对矢量场进行量子化，求解横模与纵模的运动方程并计算能量动量张量（energy momentum tensor）；随后引入电场能量密度的反作用，发现纵向能量密度为次主导。据此将背景电场与微扰贡献分离处理：背景电磁场与量子复标量场（quantum complex scalar field）最小耦合，磁性与纵向分量用量子场描述，施温格产生仅作用于占主导的电场能量密度。

Result: 纵向能量密度相对背景电场为次主导；电场能量密度主导于磁贡献；在零耦合常数（null coupling constant）下，所得电场能量密度与完全量子电磁描述一致，作为一致性检验；在非零耦合常数下，施温格粒子产生在暴胀末期剧烈降低电场能量密度。

Conclusion: 施温格效应可显著影响暴胀磁生成中的电场能量密度，尤其在其末期阶段。该框架在电场主导条件下自洽，并通过零耦合极限与全量子描述的一致性得到验证。

Abstract: We investigate inflationary magnetogenesis with massive vector fields, including charged scalar particle production via the Schwinger effect and its backreaction on the background electric energy density. Initially neglecting the Schwinger effect, we quantize the vector field and solve the equations of motion for transverse and longitudinal modes, computing the energy momentum tensor. We then incorporate the backreaction of the electric energy density and show that the longitudinal energy density is subleading compared to the electric background. Since the electric energy density dominates the magnetic contribution, we can treat the former separately from the perturbative contributions. We adopt this approach when studying particle production via the Schwinger effect, assuming that its backreaction acts only on the background electric energy density. The latter is computed using a classical background electromagnetic field minimally coupled to a quantum complex scalar field. The magnetic and longitudinal components are instead described through quantum fields. In this framework, the Schwinger production affects only the electric energy density, as long as it remains dominant compared to the other components. As a consistency check, we verify that for a null coupling constant we recover the same electric energy density as obtained within the fully quantum electromagnetic description. Finally, we show that for non-null coupling constants Schwinger particle production drastically reduces the electric energy density during the final stages of inflation.

</details>


### [145] [Extremal black hole throats from non extremal near horizon expansions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05460)
*Anirudhda Shinde,Mangesh Mandlik*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过非极端近地平展开（non-extremal near-horizon expansion）恢复极端近地平几何（extremal near-horizon geometry）的方法，适用于具有光滑极端极限的稳态黑洞族。通过表观引力（surface gravity）的近极端缩放、径向缩放与Killing时间重标度，可在缩放后地平上保留有限表观引力，将其取零即得极端近地平几何。方法统一复现了Reissner-Nordström、Kerr、Kerr-Newman与带电旋转BTZ黑洞的已知喉几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端黑洞近地平几何通常需单独求解，与同一黑洞族非极端情形下的近地平展开缺乏统一联系。本文旨在建立两者之间的系统性联系，从而用非极端展开构造极端极限。

Method: 在族内通用的Gaussian零坐标（Gaussian null coordinates）下，对表观引力作近极端缩放，并施加径向缩放与Killing时间重标度。分析时间度规分量中二次径向项在此缩放下的行为，采用至二阶的展开，并给出提取近地平系数的统一拉回（pullback）方案。

Result: 缩放后地平可保留有限表观引力，当该缩放表观引力取零时得到极端近地平几何；时间度规分量的二次径向项在缩放与时间重标度下可存活，而更高径向阶消失，故二阶展开既充分又必要（当所选坐标图中二次项极限系数非零）。该方法复现了Reissner-Nordström、Kerr、Kerr-Newman及带电旋转BTZ黑洞的已知喉（throat）几何；中性旋转BTZ则可直接由弦Carroll展开（string Carroll expansion）处理。

Conclusion: 非极端近地平展开经适当的表观引力缩放、径向缩放与时间重标度后，可统一给出极端近地平几何，且二阶展开在满足条件时是充分且必要的；该框架为不同黑洞族的极端喉几何提供了共通的提取处方。

Abstract: We show how extremal near horizon geometries can be recovered from the non extremal near horizon expansion for smooth stationary black hole families admitting a smooth extremal limit. In Gaussian null coordinates common to the family, we apply the near extremal scaling of the surface gravity together with a radial scaling and a rescaling of Killing time. Although the physical surface gravity vanishes, the scaled horizon can retain a finite surface gravity. The extremal near horizon geometry is obtained when this scaled surface gravity is taken to zero. Under the near extremal scaling and the time rescaling, the quadratic radial term in the temporal metric component can survive, while higher radial orders vanish. Expansion through second order is therefore sufficient and is necessary whenever the quadratic term has a nonzero limiting coefficient in the chosen chart. We derive a common pullback prescription for extracting the near horizon coefficients and recover the known throats of Reissner-Nordström, Kerr, Kerr-Newman, and charged rotating Bañados-Teitelboim-Zanelli (BTZ) black holes. Neutral rotating BTZ provides a case in which the string Carroll expansion already suffices.

</details>


### [146] [More on the Celestial Soft Symmetry Algebra in the ${\cal N}=8$ Supergravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05760)
*Changhyun Ahn*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在 ${\cal N}=8$ 超引力中构造 ${\rm w}_{1+\infty}$ 流与物质场的算子乘积展开（OPE），据此重现反全纯与全纯扇区的 ${\cal N}=8$ 天球软对称代数，并识别其中包含的扩展 BMS 代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是系统建立 ${\cal N}=8$ 天球软对称代数，并厘清这些流与物质场相互作用所对应的软定理及对称代数结构。

Method: 以反全纯光变换得到的 ${\cal N}=8$ ${\rm w}_{1+\infty}$ 流为起点，计算其与物质场的 OPE，通过双围道积分得到（反）对易子；再由电流-电流-物质三重（反）对易子（即固定末元的 Jacobi 恒等式）导出代数。对全纯光变换下的 ${\rm \bar{w}}_{1+\infty}$ 流重复同一流程，并借助 shadow 变换（全纯与反全纯光变换的连续复合）构造 shadow 流的 OPE。

Result: 在反全纯与全纯扇区分别重现并得到了两类 ${\cal N}=8$ 天球软对称代数，其中均包含 ${\cal N}=8$ 扩展 BMS 代数作为子代数；描述了引力子、引力微子与引力光子的非平凡软定理；在 shadow 流情形下识别出应力-能量张量、超流与超平移生成元。

Conclusion: 该工作表明 ${\cal N}=8$ 超引力中流-物质 OPE 可自洽地生成含扩展 BMS 代数的天球软对称结构，为理解软定理与渐近对称性的统一表述提供了代数框架。

Abstract: We construct the operator product expansions (OPEs) of the ${\cal N}=8$ ${\rm w}_{1+\infty}$ currents (obtained from the antiholomorphic light transform) with the matter fields in the ${\cal N}=8$ supergravity. The corresponding (anti)commutators are obtained from the double contour integrals using the above OPEs. By computing the triple (anti)commutators consisting of current-current-matter (i.e., the Jacobi identity for fixed last element), the previously known ${\cal N}=8$ celestial soft symmetry algebra between these currents (in the antiholomorphic sector) is reproduced. We repeat the above procedure by taking the holomorphic light transform and the OPEs of the ${\cal N}=8$ ${\rm \bar{w}}_{1+\infty}$ currents with the matter fields and the corresponding (anti)commutators are obtained. We also obtain another type of ${\cal N}=8$ celestial soft symmetry algebra between these currents (in the holomorphic sector). As a subalgebra in the antiholomorphic and holomorphic sectors, there exists the ${\cal N}=8$ extended BMS (Bondi, van der Burg, Metzner, and Sachs in 1962) algebra. We analyze the above results from the ${\cal N}=8$ conformally {\it soft} ${\rm w}_{1+\infty}$ (or ${\rm \bar{w}}_{1+\infty}$) currents in the antiholomorphic (or holomorphic) sector. The nontrivial soft theorems for the graviton, the gravitinos and the graviphotons are described. Finally, using the shadow transform (the consecutive composition of holomorphic and antiholomorphic light transforms), we obtain the OPEs of the ${\cal N}=8$ {\it shadow} ${\rm w}_{1+\infty}$ (or ${\rm \bar{w}}_{1+\infty}$) currents with the matter fields where the stress-energy tensor, the supercurrents and the supertranslation generator of the ${\cal N}=8$ extended BMS algebra are identified.

</details>


### [147] [Einstein--Maxwell Sum Rules and the Persistence of the Smooth-Brane No-Go Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05798)
*Paulo Michel Longo Tavares da Silva*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文检验最小耦合阿贝尔麦克斯韦（Abelian Maxwell）扇区能否规避五维翘曲膜世界（warped braneworlds）中的 Gibbons–Kallosh–Linde（GKL）不可行定理，结论为在标准假设下无法实现规避。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GKL 障碍（obstruction）限制了光滑五维翘曲膜世界的构造，本文探究引入体麦克斯韦场（bulk Maxwell field）这一额外自由度是否能松弛该限制。

Method: 对与体麦克斯韦场耦合的规范标量场（canonical scalar field），推导单参数族的全局一致性条件，并分别处理切向 F_{μν} 与混合 F_{μ5} 扇区；该分解因麦克斯韦应力张量（stress tensor）在五维中非无迹而具有必要性。

Result: 电磁项在形式上修正了积分形式的 GKL 约束，但在标准假设下并未提供一致的规避途径。精确的四维庞加莱不变性（Poincaré invariance）要求背景中确定性经典麦克斯韦场为零，因其场强会选取特定的四维方向，故对称背景中麦克斯韦扇区消失，原有光滑膜障碍重新出现。

Conclusion: 在标准假设下 GKL 不可行定理仍然成立；本文进一步指出需要放松哪些假设才能获得非平凡的电磁贡献，并将该背景结果与体规范场局域化（gauge-field localization）的局域一致性条件加以区分。

Abstract: We investigate whether a minimally coupled Abelian Maxwell sector can relax the Gibbons--Kallosh--Linde (GKL) obstruction to smooth five-dimensional warped braneworlds. For a canonical scalar field coupled to a bulk Maxwell field, we derive the one-parameter family of global consistency conditions while keeping the tangential F_{μν} and mixed F_{μ5} sectors separate. This decomposition is essential because the Maxwell stress tensor is not traceless in five dimensions. We show that electromagnetic terms formally modify the integrated GKL constraints, but this modification does not provide a consistent evasion of the no-go theorem under the standard assumptions. Exact four-dimensional Poincaré invariance excludes a nonzero deterministic classical Maxwell field from the background, since its field strength would select preferred four-dimensional directions. The Maxwell sector therefore vanishes in the symmetric background and the usual smooth-brane obstruction is recovered. We also identify which assumptions must be relaxed for a nontrivial electromagnetic contribution and distinguish this background result from local consistency conditions for bulk gauge-field localization.

</details>


### [148] [Global Consistency of Cosmological Braneworlds in Palatini and Metric-Affine f(R) Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05821)
*Paulo Michel L. T da Silva*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在 Palatini 与度规仿射引力（metric-affine gravity）框架下，研究五维翘曲膜世界（warped braneworlds）的全局一致性条件，推导了积分求和规则并考察独立联络自由度对标准不可行性结论的修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 Einstein 引力中，光滑紧致膜世界（compact braneworlds）常受不可行定理（no-go result）的阻碍；本文旨在考察当引力理论允许独立仿射结构（independent affine structure）时，该障碍是否以及如何被改变。

Method: 以广义 Gibbons-Kallosh-Linde 框架为出发点，针对宇宙学翘曲几何（cosmological warped geometries）推导积分求和规则（integrated sum rules），并将几何、物质与非黎曼（non-Riemannian）贡献加以分离，分析各独立联络自由度的影响。

Result: 广义一致性关系给出了对可容许构型的必要全局约束（necessary global constraints），并识别出导致偏离黎曼情形（Riemannian case）的具体项；这些关系明确了标准不可行结果可被修正的条件。

Conclusion: 研究为在具有独立仿射结构的引力理论中检验光滑五维膜世界的全局一致性建立了系统框架。

Abstract: We investigate global consistency conditions for five-dimensional warped braneworlds in Palatini and metric-affine gravity. Starting from the generalized Gibbons-Kallosh-Linde framework, we derive integrated sum rules for cosmological warped geometries and examine how independent connection degrees of freedom modify the standard Einstein-gravity obstruction to smooth compact braneworlds. The analysis separates geometric, matter, and non-Riemannian contributions and clarifies the conditions under which the usual no-go result can be modified. We show that the generalized consistency relations provide necessary global constraints on admissible configurations and identify the terms responsible for possible departures from the Riemannian case. The results establish a systematic framework for testing the global consistency of smooth five-dimensional braneworlds in gravitational theories with independent affine structure.

</details>


### [149] [Black Hole Entropy and Holographic Entanglement Entropy in DGP Brane Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05946)
*Takamasa Kanai*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究膜世界引力（braneworld gravity）中引力熵与全息纠缠熵的关系，在DGP模型及膜上高阶曲率理论（Lovelock、f(R)）中导出两者相等的条件，并指出该条件对静态位形自动成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在澄清引力熵（gravitational entropy）与全息纠缠熵（holographic entanglement entropy）在引力动力学局域于膜上的膜世界引力中的关系，以深化对膜世界情境下全息原理（holography）的理解。

Method: 首先在包含膜作用量中Einstein-Hilbert项的DGP膜模型中，推导膜世界黑洞的引力熵与全息纠缠熵一致所需的条件；随后将该膜局域Einstein-Hilbert项推广到高阶曲率引力理论，对膜上的Lovelock引力与f(R)引力导出相应的相等条件。

Result: 在DGP模型中，当涉及膜外曲率（extrinsic curvature）的特定条件满足时两种熵一致，且该条件对静态位形自动成立；对Lovelock引力与f(R)引力，所得条件与Einstein引力情形形式相同，虽对一般稳态位形不保证成立，但对静态膜世界黑洞满足，从而使两种熵一致。

Conclusion: 研究结果明确了膜世界引力中引力熵与全息纠缠熵之间的关系，并有助于更深入理解膜世界设定下的全息原理。

Abstract: We investigate the relation between gravitational entropy and holographic entanglement entropy in braneworld gravity with gravitational dynamics localized on the brane. We first consider the DGP brane model, in which an Einstein-Hilbert term is included in the brane action, and derive the condition under which the gravitational entropy of a braneworld black hole agrees with the holographic entanglement entropy. We show that the two entropies coincide when a specific condition involving the extrinsic curvature of the brane is satisfied. In particular, this condition is automatically satisfied for static configurations.
  We then generalize the brane-localized Einstein-Hilbert term to higher-curvature gravitational theories. For Lovelock gravity and $f(R)$ gravity on the brane, we derive the corresponding conditions for the equality between gravitational entropy and holographic entanglement entropy and show that they take the same form as that obtained for Einstein gravity. Although the equality is not guaranteed for general stationary configurations, the required condition is satisfied for static braneworld black holes, leading to an agreement between the two entropies.
  These results clarify the relation between gravitational entropy and holographic entanglement entropy in braneworld gravity and contribute to a deeper understanding of holography in braneworld settings.

</details>


### [150] [IR flow of stochastic power spectra for interacting spectator scalars in the inflationary de Sitter spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05957)
*Vishal Nath,Sourav Bhattacharya*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究相互作用旁观者标量场理论（interacting spectator scalar field theories）在暴胀德西特时空（inflationary de Sitter spacetime）中，宇宙学关联函数随红外尺度（infrared scale）流动的行为，通过一圈有效势与重整化群流分析对称性恢复及功率谱变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探究早期暴胀时期宇宙学关联函数在红外尺度流动下的行为，特别是相互作用旁观者标量场理论在德西特背景中的有效势与相关函数演化。

Method: 首先用局域 Schwinger-DeWitt 展开传播子（local Schwinger-DeWitt expansion propagator）与精确德西特传播子（exact de Sitter propagator）推导 φ⁴、φ⁴-Yukawa 及破缺相 O(N) 模型的一圈有效势；将其作为紫外边界条件（ultraviolet boundary conditions）代入 Wetterich 方程（Wetterich equation）生成红外流；再据此计算随机功率谱（stochastic power spectra）与谱指数（spectral indices）。

Result: 有效势显示自发对称性破缺（spontaneous symmetry breaking），而红外流驱使理论趋向对称性恢复（symmetry restoration）。两种传播子给出的结果在定性上大致一致，但定量上可能显著不同。φ⁴ 与 φ⁴-Yukawa 在深红外（deep IR）谱指数增大，并强调了费米子相互作用（fermionic interaction）的影响；对 O(N) 标量，N 越大有效势形状对流动越不敏感。

Conclusion: 红外流导致对称性恢复，并显著影响旁观者标量两点关联函数（two point correlation function）的功率谱与谱指数；两种传播子结果定性一致但定量有别，费米子相互作用与大 N 行为对关联函数具有可讨论的物理含义。

Abstract: In this work we investigate the behaviour of early inflationary cosmic correlation functions under the flow of the infrared scale for interacting spectator scalar field theories. We start with the derivation of the one-loop effective potentials for $φ^4$, $φ^4$-Yukawa and broken phase $O(N)$ model in the inflationary de Sitter spacetime, first using the local Schwinger-DeWitt expansion propagator, and next using the exact de Sitter propagator. The Schwinger-DeWitt expansion being valid in a sub-Hubble length scale, furnishes suitable framework to determine the leading curvature corrections to high energy phenomena having sub-Hubble Compton wavelengths. These effective potentials show spontaneous symmetry breaking pattern, and are next used as ultraviolet boundary conditions in the Wetterich equation generating the desired IR flow. The flow is shown to drive the theory towards symmetry restoration. Using these IR-improved effective potentials, we next compute the stochastic power spectra and spectral indices for the temporally coincident two point correlation function of the spectator scalar. We show that the results corresponding to the aforementioned two kind of propagators are roughly in qualitative agreement with each other, but may differ quantitatively significantly. Both of them show increase in the spectral index in the deep IR for $φ^4$ and $φ^4$-Yukawa. The effect of fermionic interaction has been emphasised. Also for the case of the $O(N)$ scalar, we demonstrate that the effective potential's shape becomes more and more insensitive to the flow as $N$ gets large. Implication of this on the correlation function has been discussed.

</details>


### [151] [Momentum Flux for Small-Amplitude Oscillons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06224)
*N. S. Manton,Azadeh Mohammadi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在(1+1)维标量场论中，振荡子（oscillon）为缓慢辐射并衰减的局域准周期解。本文计算其单周期积分动量通量，并给出小振幅展开下的幂次标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确呼吸子（breather）的周期积分动量通量为零，而振荡子（oscillon）缓慢辐射并衰减至真空解，因此其残余动量通量值得量化。该量可刻画振荡子偏离精确周期解的程度。

Method: 在(1+1)维标量场论（scalar field theory）框架下，对振幅为 \(\eps\) 的振荡子在其小振幅展开中截断至 \(\eps^N\) 阶，计算一个周期内积分后的动量通量（integrated momentum flux）。

Result: 截断至 \(\eps^N\) 阶时，积分动量通量的领头阶为：\(N\) 为偶数时是 \(\eps^{N+4}\)，\(N\) 为奇数时是 \(\eps^{N+5}\)；唯 \(N=1\) 例外，其阶数为 \(\eps^{4}\)。

Conclusion: 该积分动量通量在所给展开下极其微小，说明振荡子在截断阶数范围内接近精确呼吸子（breather）的无通量行为；仅 \(N=1\) 情形偏离上述奇偶模式。

Abstract: An oscillon in scalar field theory in $(1+1)$-dimensions is an oscillating, localised, almost periodic classical solution that slowly radiates and decays to the vacuum solution. We calculate its momentum flux integrated over a period, which would be zero for an exact breather. For an oscillon of small amplitude $\eps$, truncated at order $\eps^N$, we show that the integrated momentum flux is of leading order $\eps^{N+4}$ for even $N$ and $\eps^{N+5}$ for odd $N$, except for $N=1$, where it is of order $\eps^4$. It is therefore very small.

</details>


### [152] [Nonlocal Dirac theory in curved spacetime and the Bunch--Davies vacuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06390)
*Paulo José Porfírio,Ramires Nascimento da Silva*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了弯曲时空（curved spacetime）中的非局域自旋-1/2场论，其中形状因子是协变狄拉克算符（covariant Dirac operator）的非多项式整函数。将该理论应用于德西特时空（de Sitter spacetime）的开图表（open chart），构造了非局域Bunch-Davies模式，并给出玻戈留波夫系数（Bogoliubov coefficients）等物理量的显式非局域修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在将非局域场论（nonlocal field theory）框架从标量场推广到弯曲时空中的自旋-1/2场，以考察非局域性对费米子动力学及其在德西特宇宙学背景下量子效应的影响。

Method: 提出形状因子为协变狄拉克算符非多项式整函数的非局域自旋-1/2场论，推导相应的非局域狄拉克方程，并分析其与引力及U(1)规范场的耦合。在德西特时空开图表中构造非局域Bunch-Davies模式，按分支将非局域动力学映射为具有有效质量（effective mass）M_n的局域狄拉克方程，对指数型形状因子用Lambert-W函数的分支表示有效质量。

Result: Bunch-Davies构造的欧几里得解析结构（Euclidean analyticity structure）得以保持；在物理正残数分支（positive-residue branch）上，玻戈留波夫系数、压缩参数（squeezing parameter）、占据数（occupation number）与纠缠熵（entanglement entropy）均获得显式非局域修正，同时不存在物理费米子超曲率模式（fermionic supercurvature modes）的性质仍被保持。

Conclusion: 非局域自旋-1/2场论在德西特开图表中可逐分支映射为带有效质量的局域理论，并保持关键解析与模式结构；当Λ→∞时局域理论被平滑恢复。该结果给出非局域性对费米子量子效应的可控修正。

Abstract: We formulate a nonlocal spin-$1/2$ field theory in curved spacetime in which the form factor is a nonpolynomial entire function of the covariant Dirac operator. We derive the corresponding nonlocal Dirac equation and analyze its coupling to gravity and to a $\rm U(1)$ gauge field. As an application, we study the theory in the open chart of de Sitter spacetime and construct the associated nonlocal Bunch--Davies modes. The nonlocal dynamics can be mapped, branch by branch, onto the local Dirac equation with an effective mass $M_n$. For the exponential form factors considered here, the effective masses are expressed in terms of branches of the Lambert-$W$ function. We show that the Euclidean analyticity structure of the Bunch--Davies construction is preserved. On the physical positive-residue branch, the Bogoliubov coefficients, squeezing parameter, occupation number, and entanglement entropy acquire explicit nonlocal corrections, while the absence of physical fermionic supercurvature modes is preserved. The local theory is smoothly recovered in the limit $Λ\to\infty$.

</details>


### [153] [A local second law for black hole dynamics in the charged large-$D$ membrane paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06470)
*Supratim Halder,Mangesh Mandlik*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在大维度（large-$D$）膜范式（membrane paradigm）框架下，针对平坦背景中的带电黑洞，计算了膜速度散度的次领头阶修正，并据此构建膜熵流，证明了其散度在该阶内非负，从而为带电大维度膜范式确立了局域第二定律（local second law）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大维度膜范式提供了一种微扰、非引力的黑洞动力学描述，中性膜的局域热力学第二定律已建立，但带电大维度膜框架中局域熵产生方程（local entropy production equation）的显式确定尚未解决。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 计算平坦背景带电黑洞膜速度散度的次领头阶（subleading-order）修正；通过非仿射零测地线 Raychaudhuri 方程（non-affine null Raychaudhuri equation）在物理事件视界（event horizon）上求值标量 Einstein-Maxwell 约束方程，并将物理视界表达式与辅助膜运动学（membrane kinematics）相关联。

Result: 膜速度在膜世界体（membrane worldvolume）中首个非零阶产生非负散度，由两个二次型（quadratic forms）驱动：膜剪切（membrane shear）以及纯粹由 Maxwell 场产生的新贡献。

Conclusion: 利用该结果构建膜熵流（membrane entropy current），并证明其散度在该阶内非负，从而确立了带电大维度膜范式的局域第二定律。

Abstract: The large-$D$ membrane paradigm provides a perturbative, non-gravitational description of black hole dynamics. While the local second law of thermodynamics has been established for neutral membranes, the explicit determination of the local entropy production equation within the charged large-$D$ membrane framework remains unsolved. In this paper, we compute the subleading-order correction to the divergence of the membrane velocity for a charged black hole in a flat background. We evaluate the corresponding scalar Einstein-Maxwell constraint equation at the physical event horizon via the non-affine null Raychaudhuri equation, and relate the physical horizon expressions to the auxiliary membrane kinematics, demonstrating that the membrane velocity produces a non-negative divergence at the first non-vanishing order in the membrane worldvolume, which is driven by two quadratic forms: the membrane shear and a novel contribution generated purely by the Maxwell field. Using this result, we construct the membrane entropy current and prove that its divergence is non-negative through this order, thereby establishing the local second law for the charged large-$D$ membrane paradigm.

</details>


### [154] [Quantum Gravity in the Landau background gauge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06564)
*J. Frenkel,S. Martins-Filho*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在朗道背景规范（Landau background gauge）下考察引力与物质场量子理论的有效作用量（effective action），分析其BRST不变性，并显式计算单圈阶（one-loop order）的抵消项拉格朗日量（counterterm Lagrangian）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究背景规范下引力与物质场量子理论的有效作用量及其对称性，需要明确其BRST不变性（BRST invariance）及相应的Ward恒等式（Ward identities），以刻画背景有效作用量的对称结构。

Method: 在朗道背景规范下分析该理论的BRST不变性，研究实现背景有效作用量对称性的Ward恒等式，并在该规范下、单圈阶显式计算出离壳（off-shell）与在壳（on-shell）的抵消项拉格朗日量。

Result: 在朗道背景规范下、单圈阶，离壳与在壳抵消项拉格朗日量均被显式给出；该规范下的物质自能（matter self-energy）在此阶为有限。

Conclusion: 作者论证：在朗道背景规范下、于单圈阶有限的物质自能，可能在高阶圈图（higher loops）下同样保持有限。

Abstract: We examine, in the Landau background gauge, the effective action of a quantum theory of gravitational and matter fields. We analyse the BRST invariance of this theory and study the Ward identities which implement the symmetry of the background effective action. The off-shell and on-shell counterterm Lagrangians are explicitly calculated in this gauge at one-loop order. We argue that the matter self-energy in the Landau background gauge, which is finite to this order, may be finite also at higher loops.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.soft'></div>

# cond-mat.soft [[Back]](#toc)

### [155] [Efficient, Geometry-only Prediction of Advection-dominated Particle Transport through Saturated Soils](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03767)
*Hao Liu,Rizwan Khaleel,Markus Rolf,Martin G. J. Löder,Christina Bogner,Stephan Gekle*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究提出高效的孔隙网络模型（pore-network model）minD-PNM，仅以X射线显微计算机断层扫描（X-ray micro-computed tomography, μCT）获得的孔隙几何为输入，即可预测水饱和土壤中微塑料（microplastic）颗粒的穿透曲线（breakthrough curves）。模型在小系统与宏观石英沉积物实验中均与参考数据吻合良好，且可扩展至宏观样本体积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微塑料在土壤中的环境迁移引发生态与人类健康担忧，但土壤作为高度复杂且非透明的多孔介质（porous medium），使实验观测受限，相关迁移机制认识不足。直接粒子分辨（particle-resolved）模拟在真实土壤宏观尺度上仍具计算挑战，因此需要一种兼顾效率与真实结构的建模方法。

Method: 提出高效孔隙网络模型 minD-PNM，以实验μCT孔隙几何为唯一输入。模型将近似 t^{-3} 的孔隙尺度通过时间分布（pore-scale transit time distribution）与多入口-多出口孔隙的最小耗散分配（minimum-dissipation partition）相结合，并在孔隙网络中传播这些分布以预测穿透曲线。

Result: 模型在小系统上与格子玻尔兹曼（lattice Boltzmann）及浸没边界（immersed boundary）模拟进行基准对比，在宏观样本尺寸上与石英沉积物中的微塑料柱实验进行对比；两种情形下预测的穿透曲线均与相应参考数据吻合良好。

Conclusion: 由于仅需几何信息，minD-PNM 可扩展至宏观样本体积，为颗粒污染物（particulate pollutants）的污染物水文学（contaminant-hydrology）研究提供实用工具。

Abstract: The environmental transport of microplastic particles has raised concerns because of their potential ecological and human-health impacts. A particular challenge for experiments on microplastic transport in soils is the fact that soil is a highly complex and non-transparent porous medium. Thus, our knowledge of transport mechanisms of microplastics in soils and related ecological implications is still limited and computational simulations offer an additional possibility to obtain important insights into the physical transport behaviour of microplastics. Yet, direct particle-resolved simulations in realistic soil structures for macroscopic sample sizes remain a computational challenge. We introduce an efficient pore-network model, minD-PNM, that predicts particle breakthrough curves in water-saturated soils using experimental X-ray micro-computed tomography ($μ$CT) pore geometries as the only input. The model combines an approximately $t^{-3}$ pore-scale transit time distribution with a minimum-dissipation partition of multi-inlet-multi-outlet pores, and propagates these distributions through the pore network to predict breakthrough curves. We benchmark minD-PNM against lattice Boltzmann and immersed boundary simulations for small systems, and against microplastic column experiments in quartz sediments for macroscopic sample sizes. In both cases, the predicted breakthrough curves agree well with the corresponding reference data. Because it requires only geometric information, minD-PNM scales to macroscopic sample volumes offering a practical tool for contaminant-hydrology studies of particulate pollutants.

</details>


### [156] [Tactile Perception through Fluid-Solid Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03773)
*Arman Goshtasbi,Minke Berghuis,Aida Parvaresh,Saravana Prashanth Murali Babu,Robert W. Style,Ahmad Rafsanjani*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 提出一类基于流体-固体相互作用（fluid-solid interaction）的柔性触觉传感器（soft tactile sensor），感知部位完全无需电子元件，仅通过两个外部压力传感器读取流体压力模式，并结合机器学习实现触觉定位与力估计，可在水下及磁干扰环境中工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有柔性触觉传感器多依赖嵌入式电子器件，易受干扰并存在环境限制；因此需要一种在感知位点无电子元件、且在传统电子传感器易失效环境中仍有效的方案。

Method: 传感器由填充流体的弹性体通道（elastomeric channel）构成，仅连接两个外部压力传感器；触摸不同区域使黏性流体（viscous fluid）位移并产生可编码位置与力的压力模式。信号通过集成特征提取、软聚类（soft clustering）与自适应神经模糊推理（adaptive neuro-fuzzy inference）的机器学习框架进行解码。

Result: 在一维线性传感器中完成了单点触摸定位与力估计验证；并利用空间填充曲线（space-filling curve）将通道布线于表面，将同一传感原理扩展至二维触觉映射（2D tactile mapping），且保持相同的最小化硬件配置。

Conclusion: 该简化方案在水下或存在磁干扰等传统电子传感器常失效的环境中仍保持有效性，表明流体-固体相互作用路线在无电子感知位点的柔性触觉传感中具有可行性。

Abstract: Soft tactile sensors elevate robotic touch through enhanced flexibility and adaptability, yet most existing designs depend on embedded electronics that are susceptible to interference and environmental limitations. In this work, we leverage fluid-solid interactions to develop a class of soft tactile sensors that operate entirely without electronics at the sensing site. The sensor comprises a fluid-filled elastomeric channel connected to only two external pressure sensors. Touching different regions of the elastomeric surface displaces the viscous fluid, producing distinct pressure patterns that encode both touch position and force. These signals are decoded through a machine learning framework that integrates feature extraction, soft clustering, and adaptive neuro-fuzzy inference to achieve accurate localization and force estimation. We validate this concept through single-point touch localization and force estimation in a linear (1D) sensor and extend the same sensing principle to 2D tactile mapping by routing the channel across the surface using space-filling curves, while maintaining the same minimal hardware setup. This simple approach remains effective in environments where conventional electronic sensors often fail, such as underwater or in the presence of magnetic interference.

</details>


### [157] [Soap Films Amplify the Air-Water Interface but Neither Produce Detectable H2O2 Nor Enhance Reduction of Gold Ions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03774)
*Muzzamil Ahmad Eatoo,Yash K. Mohature,Francisco P. Tome,Lisa Oki Exposito,Sirisha Kamireddy,Najeh Kharbatia,Himanshu Mishra*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究通过多种分析手段重新检验了肥皂膜（soap film）气-水界面（air-water interface）具有氧化还原能力（redox capability）的报道，未发现生成 H2O2 或促进 Au3+ 还原的证据，并指出相关现象可能源自体相溶液化学（bulk-solution chemistry）而非界面效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既往报道称 Triton X 肥皂膜可自发氧化水生成 H2O2 并将 Au3+ 还原为金纳米颗粒，且无需外部能量或催化剂，这与气-水界面氧化还原性质这一争议性论点相关，因此有必要重新审视。

Method: 采用多种互补技术进行验证，包括核磁共振波谱（NMR spectroscopy）、荧光（fluorescence）、比色（colorimetric）分析、透射电子显微镜（TEM）、动态光散射（DLS）及电感耦合等离子体发射光谱（ICP-OES），并重复制备超过 300 次肥皂膜循环进行探测。

Result: NMR 等方法未检出 H2O2 生成的证据；TEM 与时间分辨 DLS 显示金纳米颗粒在体相溶液中已自发形成，制膜过程（300 次循环）对粒径或密度无可测影响，ICP-OES 予以佐证；此外，含或不含 Triton 肥皂的体相 HAuCl4 溶液中金纳米颗粒形成伴随 H2O2 产生，并给出机理层面的解释。

Conclusion: 结果表明，仅靠肥皂膜形成来放大气-水界面面积，不足以引发可检测的 H2O2 生成或增强 Au 离子还原；研究强调需区分由纯气-水界面效应驱动的反应与源自体相溶液化学及固-水界面（solid-water interface）的反应。

Abstract: Soap films afford gentle production of air-water interfaces and underlie numerous practical applications. Recently these interfaces have been shown to exhibit unexpected redox capability, wherein Triton X soap films formed with water or HAuCl4 solutions (i) oxidize water up to 1.67 mM H2O2 and (ii) reduce aqueous Au3+ ions into gold nanoparticles, without external energy or catalyst. These reports tend to align with the broader claims of the redox nature of the air-water interface, which has been intensely debated. Here, we re-examine these claims via complementary application of a broad range of techniques, including NMR spectroscopy, fluorescence, colorimetric, TEM, DLS, ICP-OES. Experimental results reveal no evidence for H2O2 formation despite repeated formation of over 300 cycles of soap films, were probed via NMR spectroscopy and other analytical methods. Next, we interrogated soap films for their ability to spontaneously reduce Au3+ ions. TEM and time-resolved DLS revealed that the formation of Au nanoparticles was already formed spontaneous in bulk solutions, and casting soap films (300 cycles) had no measurable effect on particle size or density. This observation was confirmed via complementary quantitative ICP-OES. Our investigations showed that Au nanoparticles formation in bulk HAuCl4 solution without or with triton soap was accompanied by H2O2 generation, and we provide mechanistic insights into these processes. Collectively, these results demonstrate that amplification of the air-water interfacial area through soap-film formation is insufficient, by itself, to induce detectable H2O2 generation or enhance Au-ion reduction. More broadly, our findings highlight the need to distinguish reactions that are driven by purely air-water interfacial effects from those that originate from bulk-solution chemistry and at the solid-water interface

</details>


### [158] [Predicting and extending the bubble-free operating limit of capillary-fed water electrolysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03782)
*Farkhondeh Khodabandeh,Zhengmao Lu*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究揭示毛细供液电解器（capillary-fed electrolyzer）中隔膜孔径通过两个相反机制决定无气泡运行的电流密度阈值，并证明该阈值可设计而非纯经验性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业级电流密度下水电解中气泡造成显著能量损失；毛细供液电解器虽可缓解气泡问题，但仅在低于某一阈值电流密度时有效，该阈值的决定机制此前不明确。

Method: 建立耦合电化学-输运模型（coupled electrochemical-transport model），以单一实测起始点为锚定，在0.1–8 μm孔径范围内量化两种机制；并开展实验，将孔径从8 μm减小至1.2 μm以验证。

Result: 孔径减小使气泡起始极限（bubble onset limit）从0.18提升至1.73 A cm⁻²，并在1 A cm⁻²下降低电池电压0.11 V，主要源于抑制气泡诱导的过电位（bubble-induced overpotential）；在0.45 μm及以下，补液限制（replenishment limit）占主导，电池在气泡形成前即干涸。

Conclusion: 无气泡运行极限（bubble-free operation limit）是可设计的，而非纯粹的经验结果。

Abstract: Gas bubbles impose large penalties in water electrolysis at industrially relevant rates. Capillary-fed electrolyzers greatly mitigate them by wicking electrolyte to electrodes and venting gas from the backside, but only below a threshold current density. We show that the separator pore size sets this threshold through two opposing effects: smaller pores raise the supersaturation required for nucleation but reduce the permeability for electrolyte replenishment. A coupled electrochemical-transport model, anchored to a single measured onset, quantifies both mechanisms across 0.1-8 $μ$m pores. Experimentally, reducing pore size from 8 to 1.2 $μ$m raises the bubble onset limit from 0.18 to 1.73 A cm$^{-2}$ and lowers cell voltage by 0.11 V at 1 A cm$^{-2}$, primarily by suppressing bubble-induced overpotential. At 0.45 $μ$m and below, the replenishment limit takes over and the cells dry out before bubbles form. Our findings establish the bubble-free operation limit as designable rather than purely empirical.

</details>


### [159] [Compression-controlled dynamic buckling in thin soft sheets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04416)
*Harsh Jain,Shankar Ghosh,Kirti Chandra Sahu*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该实验研究橡胶薄片从压缩相到屈曲相（buckled phase）的动态相变，发现高速压缩会使薄片进入高度压缩的亚稳态，其中高阶模态先增长后粗化，并伴随长度扩张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究关注薄片在压缩过程中的动态屈曲（dynamical buckling）行为，特别是由压缩相向屈曲相转变的动力学过程及其控制参数（如材料横截面、压缩速度）的影响。

Method: 采用橡胶薄片进行实验，测量薄片在压缩过程中的受力与长度，以追踪模态增长与模态粗化（mode coarsening）过程，并建立模态增长的机制，同时结合理论解释关键控制参数的作用。

Result: 高速压缩下橡胶条进入高度压缩的亚稳态（metastable state）；在压缩相中高阶模态增长，随后发生模态粗化；模态增长伴随长度扩张，且此时系统仍处于被压缩状态。

Conclusion: 研究提出了动态屈曲中模态增长与粗化的机制，并从理论上解释了材料横截面与压缩速度等控制参数对屈曲动力学的影响。

Abstract: We investigate experimentally the dynamic phase transition from compressed to buckled phases for thin sheets of rubber. We find that the rubber strips enter a highly compressed, metastable state when compression speed is high. During the compressed phase, higher modes grow, followed by mode coarsening. Mode growth is accompanied by an expansion of length while the system is still being compressed. We measure the forces and length of the sheet to confirm this, and we develop a mechanism for how modes grow and coarsen during dynamical buckling. The influence of crucial control parameters in the experiments, such as the material cross section and compression speed, on the buckling dynamics, are explained theoretically.

</details>


### [160] [Effect of Temperature and Added Salt on a Model Polyzwitterion Polymer in Dilute Solution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04932)
*Soumik Ghosh,Vivek M. Prabhu,Jack F. Douglas*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 采用显式溶剂分子动力学（molecular dynamics, MD）模拟研究模型聚两性电解质（polyzwitterion, PZ）链在无盐与加盐条件下的构象。无盐时链呈自缔合态且对温度弱敏感；加入NaCl后链内缔合减弱、链发生扩张，类似球状蛋白变性。其流体力学穿透函数更符合无规支化聚合物或单链纳米颗粒，而非无规线团。该溶胀趋势即反聚电解质效应（anti-polyelectrolyte effect）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚两性电解质（polyzwitterion, PZ）单体中正负电荷基团邻近所产生的竞争性相互作用，使其性质在某些方面与内在无序蛋白（intrinsically disordered proteins）相似。为获得对这一类重要水溶性聚合物的定性认识，需要理解其在无盐与加盐条件下的构象状态，故开展本研究。

Method: 对模型PZ链进行含显式溶剂（explicit solvent）的分子动力学（molecular dynamics, MD）模拟，考察无盐与加入NaCl两种情形下的构象状态。分析内容包括流体力学穿透函数（hydrodynamic penetration function，即流体力学半径与回转半径之比）、动态水化层（dynamic hydration layer）的空间范围、温度与盐浓度对该层内水迁移率的影响，以及PZ侧链迁移率。

Result: 无盐条件下孤立PZ链采取自缔合（self-associated）态，对温度依赖性弱。加入可观量NaCl后，链内缔合减弱、链发生扩张，该转变类似球状蛋白的变性。无盐时PZ链的流体力学穿透函数更符合无规支化聚合物（randomly branched polymer）或单链纳米颗粒，而非无规线团（random coil），其中链内物理交联源于链内偶极。加盐后链不仅趋于变大，其平均尺寸对温度的依赖性也明显增强。此外还考察了动态水化层范围及其中水迁移率、侧链迁移率，后者动力学与周围水分子强耦合。

Conclusion: PZ分子随加盐而溶胀的趋势被称为反聚电解质效应（anti-polyelectrolyte effect），与通常随加盐而收缩的聚电解质（polyelectrolyte）行为截然不同。侧链动力学与周围水分子强耦合，且盐与温度共同调控链尺寸及水化层中水的迁移率。

Abstract: The competitive interactions arising from the proximity of positive and negatively charged groups in the monomers in polyzwitterions (PZ) makes these polymers have properties similar in some ways to intrinsically disordered proteins. To gain qualitative insights into this important class of water-soluble polymers, we performed molecular dynamics (MD) simulations of a model PZ chain with an explicit solvent to understand the conformational state in the absence and presence of added salt. We find that isolated PZ chains under salt-free conditions adopt a self-associated state having a weak dependence on temperature. The addition of an appreciable amount of NaCl leads to a weakening of the intrachain associations, resulting in chain expansion, a transition reminiscent of the denaturation of globular proteins. In particular, the hydrodynamic penetration function, the ratio of hydrodynamic radius to the radius of gyration, of the PZ chains under salt free conditions, is found to be more consistent with a randomly branched polymer or single chain nanoparticle than a random coil polymer where the physical cross-links within the chain arise from the dipoles within the chain. The chain conformation not only tends to become larger with the addition of salt but also acquires a more appreciable temperature dependence of its average size with temperature. This swelling trend of PZ molecules with added salt, sometimes referred to as the anti-polyelectrolyte effect, is distinct from the normal trend polyelectrolytes, which typically become more contracted with the addition of salt. We also examined the spatial extent of the dynamic hydration layer the PZ chain, both with and without added salt, and the effect of temperature and salt concentration on the water mobility within this layer, and PZ side-chain mobility, whose dynamics is apparently strongly coupled to the surrounding water molecules.

</details>


### [161] [Effect of Added Salts on the Interfacial Dynamics of Monovalent Metal Ions and Model Water-Soluble Polymers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04934)
*Soumik Ghosh,Jack F. Douglas,Francis W. Starr*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究通过分子动力学（MD）模拟，探讨电解质与聚电解质溶液中离子与水溶性聚合物的水合（hydration）问题。研究成功复现了单价盐对水扩散系数（diffusion coefficient）的离子特异性（ion-specific）趋势，并揭示了聚合物周围约1纳米尺度的动态水合层（dynamic hydration layer）及其近通用型迁移率梯度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电解质与聚电解质溶液理论忽略了离子与聚合物的水合作用，导致对溶液热力学与动力学性质的描述过度理想化。尤其是某些离子在低浓度下提高或降低水扩散系数的现象缺乏理解，且该现象与描述盐对合成及生物大分子溶解性和自组装影响的霍夫迈斯特序列（Hofmeister series）密切相关，具有广泛的生物学、医学与技术意义。

Method: 采用分子动力学（molecular dynamics, MD）模拟，以代表性水溶性聚合物聚丙烯酰胺（polyacrylamide, PAM）和聚环氧乙烷（polyethylene oxide, PEO）为对象，研究含盐水溶液中的离子与不带电水溶性聚合物水合问题。研究中特别关注模型能否复现所观察到的单价盐对水扩散系数的影响趋势。

Result: 模型成功复现了加入单价盐后水迁移率的离子特异性趋势，而这一趋势在此前基于物理真实水模型的MD模拟中一直是反复出现的挑战。聚合物模拟揭示了扩展的纳米尺度动态水合层，其尺度约为1纳米，层内迁移率偏离体相值，且该层内的迁移率梯度呈现近通用的函数形式。

Conclusion: 该工作对盐溶液的研究构成重要进展，并以此为基础构建了含盐的水性聚合物溶液模型。研究强调了水合作用在理解离子特异性效应中的核心地位，为后续相关模拟与理论建模提供了可靠基础。

Abstract: Traditional theories of electrolyte and polyelectrolyte solutions overlook ion and polymer hydration, yielding an overly idealized perspective of the thermodynamic and dynamic properties of these solutions. In particular, the propensity of certain ions to increase or decrease the water diffusion coefficient at low solution concentrations, is poorly understood, a phenomenon that correlates strongly with the Hofmeister series describing the influence of salts on the solubility and self-assembly of synthetic and biomacromolecules as well as colloidal particles. These ion-specific effects have profound ramifications in diverse biological, medical science and technological applications. We address the general problem of understanding ion and uncharged water-soluble polymer hydration through molecular dynamics (MD) simulations of representative water-soluble polymers, polyacrylamide (PAM) and polyethylene oxide (PEO), in aqueous salt solutions. Careful attention is given to the capacity of our model to reproduce observed trends of added monovalent salts on the diffusion coefficient of water. Reproducing these ion-specific trends in the mobility of water has been a recurrent challenge in prior MD simulations of aqueous solutions based on a physically realistic model of water. Our work on salt solutions represents an important advance on which we build our modeling of aqueous polymer solutions with added salts. Simulations of our model polymers reveal an extended nanoscale dynamic hydration layer having a scale on the order of one nanometer (nm) in which the mobility is perturbed from its bulk value, and the mobility gradient in this layer appears to obey a near universal functional form.

</details>


### [162] [Metastability and nucleation of bubbles in active chiral systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05145)
*Raphael Maire,Alessandro Petrini,Umberto Marini Bettolo Marconi,Lorenzo Caprini*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文建立了手性活性流体（chiral active fluid）中气泡形成的成核理论（nucleation theory），通过将流体力学方程约化为单个集体坐标——气泡半径，导出有效非线性势，统一描述了均匀态的稳定、亚稳与不稳定区域，并用事件驱动分子动力学（event-driven molecular dynamics）模拟验证了临界半径、成核势垒与气泡尺寸的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密手性活性流体中常出现气泡，但其起源尚不明确：是均匀态的不稳定性（instability）所致，还是亚稳相（metastable phase）中的稀有成核（rare nucleation）事件。该研究旨在为这一争议提供统一的理论判据。

Method: 将流体力学方程（hydrodynamic equations）约化为单一集体坐标（collective coordinate），即气泡半径，从而获得描述径向动力学的有效非线性势（effective nonlinear potential）；据此分析亚稳区中的临界气泡半径与有效机械功势垒（effective mechanical-work barrier），并考察大半径极限行为及圆形气泡对非圆扰动的稳定性（线性稳定性分析）。理论预测通过与手性硬盘（chiral hard disks）的事件驱动分子动力学模拟对照检验。

Result: 有效非线性势刻画了均匀态的稳定、亚稳与不稳定三种区域；在亚稳区，理论给出临界气泡半径及其对应的有效机械功势垒，大半径行为决定气泡生长是饱和于有限尺寸还是无界增长。线性稳定性分析发现存在导致圆形气泡伸长（elongation）与破裂（breakup）的增长模式。事件驱动分子动力学模拟复现了临界半径、成核势垒与所选气泡尺寸的理论趋势。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了手性活性流体中稳定性与成核的统一框架（unified framework），并为在旋转胶体（spinning colloids）与手性旋转体（chiral spinners）实验中控制气泡形成提供了可能途径。

Abstract: Bubbles frequently emerge in dense chiral active fluids, yet it remains unclear whether they result from an instability of the homogeneous state or from rare nucleation events within a metastable phase. Here we develop a nucleation theory for chiral bubble formation by reducing the hydrodynamic equations to a single collective coordinate, the bubble radius. This reduction yields an effective nonlinear potential that captures the radial dynamics and predicts distinct stable, metastable, and unstable regimes of the homogeneous state. In the metastable regime, the theory identifies a critical bubble radius and an associated effective mechanical-work barrier, while its large-radius behavior determines whether bubble growth saturates at a finite size or becomes unbounded. We further analyze the stability of circular bubbles against noncircular perturbations, finding growing modes that can lead to elongation and breakup. The theoretical predictions are tested against event-driven molecular-dynamics simulations of chiral hard disks, which reproduce the predicted trends in the critical radius, nucleation barrier, and selected bubble size. Our results establish a unified framework for stability and nucleation in chiral active fluids and suggest routes to control bubble formation in experiments with spinning colloids and chiral spinners.

</details>


### [163] [Shape Transformations and Rupture of a Vesicle Driven by an Encapsulated Active Particle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05836)
*Gaurav Kumar,Abhishek Chaudhuri,Rajeev Kapri,Anil Kumar Dasanna*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本研究采用脂质分辨的粗粒化模型（lipid-resolved coarse-grained model），探究被限制在脂质囊泡（lipid vesicle）内的单个自驱动活性粒子如何驱动膜变形，揭示膜形态对粒子活性与弯曲刚度的非单调依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物膜（biological membrane）在多种细胞过程中发生形变，通常由分子马达（molecular motors）、细胞骨架机器（cytoskeletal machinery）及运动微生物等活性因子驱动，这些过程消耗能量并使系统远离平衡态（far from equilibrium）。论文旨在理解单个活性粒子驱动膜变形的机制。

Method: 使用脂质分辨的粗粒化模型，考察被包裹的自驱动粒子（self-propelled particle）的活性与形状如何影响囊泡动力学与形态；采用表面积相同但形状不同的粒子，系统研究膜形态随活性与弯曲刚度（bending rigidity）的变化，并以非球度（asphericity）和过量弯曲能（excess bending energy）量化膜变形。

Result: 活性粒子产生持续推进力并对膜施加机械应力（mechanical stresses），从而驱动变形；非球度与过量弯曲能对活性呈非单调依赖，在中等活性处达到峰值。当活性足够高时，膜在形成显著大尺度变形前即发生破裂（membrane rupture），限制了进一步重塑；膜表面张力（surface tension）在调控活性诱导的变形动力学中起重要作用。

Conclusion: 单个被限制的活性粒子可通过持续推进驱动脂质囊泡的形态转变，其变形程度对活性呈非单调响应并在高活性下受膜破裂限制；膜表面张力是调控该活性诱导变形行为的关键因素。

Abstract: Biological membranes are highly deformable structures that undergo shape transformations during numerous cellular processes. These transformations are often driven by active agents, including molecular motors, cytoskeletal machinery, and motile microorganisms, which consume energy and maintain biological systems far from equilibrium. Here, we investigate how a single active particle confined within a lipid vesicle drives membrane deformation. Using a lipid-resolved coarse-grained model, we examine how the activity and shape of an encapsulated self-propelled particle influence vesicle dynamics and morphology. Active particles generate persistent propulsion that exerts mechanical stresses on the enclosing membrane, thereby driving its deformation. Using particles of different shapes but identical surface area, we systematically investigate membrane morphologies as functions of activity and bending rigidity. We quantify membrane deformation through asphericity and excess bending energy relative to the equilibrium spherical state. Both measures exhibit a non-monotonic dependence on activity, peaking at intermediate activity. At sufficiently high activity, membrane rupture occurs before significant large-scale deformation can develop, limiting further remodeling. We further demonstrate that membrane surface tension plays a significant role in regulating activity-induced deformation dynamics.

</details>


### [164] [Translational and rotational irreversibility in cyclically sheared colloidal suspensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06172)
*Austin H. Walker,Jeffrey S. Urbach,Daniel L. Blair*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究利用定制共聚焦流变仪（confocal-rheometer）与光滑/瓣状胶体颗粒，在宽浓度范围内考察循环剪切悬浮液的平动与转动动力学，揭示剪切诱导各向异性（shear-induced anisotropy）依赖于颗粒浓度、形貌、颗粒间摩擦与互锁机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究聚焦于循环剪切悬浮液从可逆到不可逆状态的转变，以及有效扩散率（effective diffusivities）所表现出的流动诱导各向异性问题，尤关注颗粒浓度与颗粒形貌的作用。

Method: 采用定制共聚焦流变仪，配合化学性质相同但表面分别光滑与呈瓣状（lobed）的胶体颗粒，在三维空间追踪颗粒的平动轨迹与转动。

Result: 颗粒轨迹与转动追踪显示有效扩散率被增强，并呈现流动诱导各向异性，该各向异性随颗粒浓度和颗粒形貌（morphology）变化。

Conclusion: 在不可逆状态（irreversible state）内，剪切诱导各向异性由颗粒间摩擦（interparticle friction）与互锁机制（interlocking mechanisms）决定，这些机制促进混合并强制实现转动各向同性（rotational isotropy）。

Abstract: We investigate the translational and rotational dynamics of cyclically sheared suspensions over a broad range of particle concentrations. The transition to an irreversible state is studied using a custom confocal-rheometer and colloidal particles synthesized with chemically identical smooth and lobed surfaces. The particle trajectories and rotations are tracked in three dimensions to reveal enhanced effective diffusivities that exhibit flow induced anisotropy that depends on particle concentration and particle morphology. We show that within the irreversible state, the shear induced anisotropy is determined by interparticle friction and interlocking mechanisms that promote mixing and enforce rotational isotropy.

</details>


### [165] [Hydrodynamics of perceptual matter: from neural representations to collective motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06249)
*Mohammad Salahshour*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文中，谐波理论（Harmonic Theory, HT）的粒子法则被提升为非局域动理学与流体力学理论，无需引入朝向对齐（heading alignment）即可导出感知通道到集体物质行为的映射。理论预测由扭矩驱动的密度不稳定性（torque-driven density instability），并揭示感知通道阶数与空间阶k^2n的对应关系及图案波长选择机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是神经层面的邻居表征如何转化为集体物质行为。作者希望在不引入朝向对齐的前提下，从HT的粒子法则出发建立连续介质理论，同时保留每个系数可追溯至感觉锐度（sensory acuity）、决策动力学与交互距离的神经与感觉起源。

Method: 由HT粒子法则推导非局域动理学（nonlocal kinetic）与流体力学（hydrodynamic）理论，不添加朝向对齐项。所得场保留HT的序分辨感知谱（order-resolved perceptual spectrum），并显式保留系数与神经、感觉来源的联系；进一步对径向变换与两粒子群、双流响应进行分析，并在纯排斥（pure-repulsion）区进行单粒子计算。
