Result: 理论预测扭矩驱动的密度不稳定性：HT的直接接近通道（direct-approach channel）使个体转向密度过剩处，而自推进（self-propulsion）将其带向内部。对各向同性空间交互与正旋转扩散，感知通道n通常首次在空间阶k^2n影响守恒密度，完整径向变换选择有限图案波长。两个种群可共享相同的长波不稳定阈值却因高阶感知通道不同而选择不同的初始图案间距。越过阈值后，单粒子连续体维持双向向列柱（bidirectional nematic column），规定的、对称性允许的双流响应将方向性不平衡放大为持续极流（polar flow）。在纯排斥区，单粒子计算显示极输运与平移横向带（translating transverse bands），晚期图案选择对数值输运敏感。

Conclusion: 该框架将神经与感觉机制连接到连续介质系数、不稳定性以及无需对齐的集体运动（alignment-free collective motion），实现了从感知表征到集体行为的可追溯理论桥梁。

Abstract: How do neural representations of neighbors become collective material behavior? Harmonic Theory (HT) reduces bearing representations to signed perceptual channels traceable to sensory acuity, decision dynamics, and interaction distance. Here we derive a nonlocal kinetic and hydrodynamic theory from the HT particle law without introducing heading alignment. The resulting fields retain HT's order-resolved perceptual spectrum and keep the neural and sensory origin of each coefficient visible. The theory predicts a torque-driven density instability: HT's direct-approach channel turns agents toward density excesses, while self-propulsion carries them inward. For isotropic spatial interactions and positive rotational diffusion, perceptual channel $n$ generically first affects conserved density at spatial order $k^{2n}$, and the full radial transforms select finite pattern wavelengths. Two populations can share the same long-wave instability threshold yet select different initial pattern spacings because their higher perceptual channels differ. Beyond onset, the one-particle continuum sustains a bidirectional nematic column; a prescribed, symmetry-allowed two-stream response amplifies a directional imbalance into sustained polar flow. In a distinct pure-repulsion regime, one-particle calculations show polar transport and translating transverse bands, with late pattern selection sensitive to numerical transport. The framework connects neural and sensory mechanisms to continuum coefficients, instabilities, and alignment-free collective motion.

</details>


### [166] [Self-assembly Monte Carlo reveals localized entanglement in giant polymer melts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06498)
*Enrico Fornasa,Francesco Slongo,Cristian Micheletti*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种基于自组装系综（self-assembly ensemble）的蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）方案，绕过固定组分下的计算瓶颈，实现近线性标度的平衡采样，并据此刻画了超大尺度聚合物熔体（polymer melts）中局部化的链内结与链间链环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑缠结（topological entanglements）对理解与预测聚合物熔体性质至关重要，但其导致去相关时间（decorrelation time）随链长迅速增长，使平衡采样在计算上极为困难。

Method: 在自组装系综而非固定组分下进行蒙特卡洛模拟，采用严格局域的移动（local moves）在保持线性链数目守恒的同时跨尺度传播主链重连（backbone reconnections）；方法针对完全填充分子格（fully-packed lattice）构建。

Result: 去相关时间随体系尺寸呈近线性标度，$τ_{\rm eq}\sim V^{1.0}$；可平衡处理总计约 $1.1\times10^9$ 个单体的周期体系，进入对格点细节不敏感的普适熔体区；对链长至 $N\simeq 5\times10^5$ 单体的链分析表明，缠结表现为局域化的结（knots）与链环（links），而非全局缠结；相邻链间高斯环绕积分（Gauss linking integral）的量值仅按 $N^{1/4}$ 增长。

Conclusion: 所提方案可在超大体系与超长链尺度上实现平衡采样，并揭示聚合物熔体中的拓扑缠结本质上是局域化的结与链环结构，其链间环绕强度随链长仅以 $N^{1/4}$ 缓慢增长。

Abstract: Topological entanglements are central to understanding and predicting the properties of polymer melts. Yet, they make equilibrium sampling computationally challenging, as decorrelation times grow rapidly with chain length. Here, we introduce a Monte Carlo scheme that bypasses typical computational bottlenecks by working in a self-assembly ensemble rather than at fixed composition. Strictly local moves efficiently propagate backbone reconnections across scales while conserving the number of linear chains, achieving near-linear scaling of decorrelation time with system size, $τ_{\rm eq}\sim V^{1.0}$. With this method, formulated for a fully-packed lattice, we equilibrate periodic systems totalling up to $\simeq 1.1\times 10^9$ monomers, accessing a universal melt regime insensitive to lattice details. We analyze intra- and inter-chain entanglements for chains of up to $N\simeq 5 \times 10^5$ monomers, revealing that they manifest as localized knots and links rather than as global tangles. Finally, we show that the magnitude of the Gauss linking integral between neighbouring chains grows only as $N^{1/4}$.

</details>


<div id='math.NT'></div>

# math.NT [[Back]](#toc)

### [167] [Sequential exponential-motivic periods in spatially cut-off $φ^4_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04148)
*Juan Carlos Sampedro*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该文将空间截断（spatial cutoff）二维 φ⁴ 模型（φ⁴_2）的构造性积分（constructive integrals）表示为指数-动机周期（exponential-motivic periods）序列，并借助伽罗瓦变换（Galois transformation）分析其发散性与可观测极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究目的在于刻画构造性量子场论中积分序列在伽罗瓦变换下的解析行为，特别是空间截断非零时周期模长的发散性质及其对固定可观测极限的影响。

Method: 方法上，用指数-动机周期序列表示空间截断 φ⁴_2 的构造性积分；通过伽罗瓦变换考察分割函数周期（partition periods）沿满足构造性估计的逼近序列的模长行为；并刻画在自由场 L¹ 空间上具有有界实现（bounded realization）的变换子群；最后以单变量四次（quartic）例子进行考察。

Result: 结果表明：非零空间截断下，单一伽罗瓦变换即可使分割函数周期沿每个满足构造性估计的逼近序列在模长上发散。对于固定截断表述，刻画了在自由场 L¹ 空间上允许有界实现的变换子群，并证明该子群上所有固定可观测极限均为其物理值的公共标量倍。单变量四次例子显示，所有变换后的固定可观测序列可在无有界实现的情况下收敛，且其极限不与物理值成比例。

Conclusion: 结论指出，有界实现的存在性与固定可观测极限同物理值的比例关系密切相关；缺乏有界实现时，序列仍可收敛但极限偏离物理值，说明该子群刻画对极限性质具有关键区分作用。

Abstract: We represent constructive integrals in spatially cut-off $φ^4_2$ by sequences of exponential-motivic periods. For a nonzero spatial cutoff, a single Galois transformation makes the partition periods diverge in modulus along every sequence of approximations satisfying the constructive estimates. For a fixed cutoff presentation, we characterize the subgroup of transformations admitting a bounded realization on the free field's $L^1$ space and prove that all fixed-observable limits on this subgroup are a common scalar multiple of their physical values. A one-variable quartic example shows that all transformed fixed-observable sequences can converge without such a bounded realization, with limits that are not proportional to the physical values.

</details>


<div id='astro-ph.HE'></div>

# astro-ph.HE [[Back]](#toc)

### [168] [Polarization Transport and Polarized Images of a Rotating Braneworld Black Hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03738)
*Wen-Hao Deng,Qing-Quan Jiang,Kai Lin,Pei Wang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本研究通过求解偏振四矢量的零测地线与平行移动方程，考察旋转膜世界黑洞（braneworld black hole）薄光学吸积流中的偏振同步辐射（polarized synchrotron emission），并比较其与 Kerr 及 Kerr–Newman 时空的偏振特征差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维引力（higher-dimensional gravity）如何修改光子传播与偏振输运（polarization transport）尚待厘清。作者旨在利用偏振观测量区分时空、观测几何与吸积流效应，并探索以视界尺度偏振测量（horizon-scale polarimetry）约束膜世界潮汐电荷（tidal charge）的可能性。

Method: 在光学薄（optically thin）吸积流与统一的发射率、磁场设定下，积分偏振四矢量的零测地线与平行移动方程；扫描潮汐电荷、黑洞自旋（black-hole spin）、观测倾角（observer inclination）以及顺行/逆行轨道运动（prograde or retrograde orbital motion）等参数，并分析线偏振强度、电场矢量位置角（EVPA）、斯托克斯参数（Stokes parameters）、Q–U 偏振态空间与累积净线偏振度。

Result: 在所考虑参数范围内，潮汐电荷与自旋未产生定性上新的大尺度偏振形态，主要重新组织阴影边界（shadow boundary）与光子环（photon ring）附近强场区域的偏振强度、取向与偏振态分布。观测倾角主导投影形态，高倾角增强投影与多普勒不对称（Doppler asymmetries），并使顺行与逆行流的区分更显著。斯托克斯分量及其联合态空间揭示出强度图像中不明显的局部弯曲、拉伸与轨迹聚集。累积净线偏振度随累积半径增大而下降，较大潮汐电荷或自旋在代表性模型中降低其振幅。

Conclusion: 这些互补的偏振观测量为分离时空、观测几何与吸积流效应提供了潜在途径，并可能借助视界尺度偏振测量约束膜世界潮汐电荷。

Abstract: We investigate polarized synchrotron emission from an optically thin accretion flow around a rotating braneworld black hole, focusing on how higher-dimensional gravity modifies photon propagation and polarization transport. We integrate the null-geodesic and parallel-transport equations for the polarization four-vector and, within a common emissivity and magnetic-field prescription, examine the effects of tidal charge, black-hole spin, observer inclination, and prograde or retrograde orbital motion. We analyze the linearly polarized intensity, electric-vector position angle (EVPA), Stokes parameters, the joint $Q$--$U$ polarization-state space, and the cumulative net linear polarization degree, and compare representative braneworld configurations with Kerr and Kerr--Newman spacetimes. Within the parameter range considered, tidal charge and spin do not generate a qualitatively new large-scale polarization morphology. Instead, they primarily reorganize the polarized intensity, orientation, and polarization-state distribution in the strong-field region near the shadow boundary and photon ring. Viewing inclination controls the dominant projected morphology, while high inclinations enhance projection and Doppler asymmetries and make the distinction between prograde and retrograde flows more pronounced. The Stokes components and their joint state space expose local bending, stretching, and trajectory concentration that are less apparent in intensity images. The cumulative net linear polarization degree decreases with increasing cumulative radius, and larger tidal charge or spin lowers its amplitude for the representative models considered. Together, these complementary polarization observables provide a potential route for separating spacetime, viewing-geometry, and accretion-flow effects and for constraining braneworld tidal charge with horizon-scale polarimetry.

</details>


### [169] [Jets with Streaks: the Global Dynamics and Structure of Striped Poynting flux dominated Jets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04796)
*Ho-Sang Chan,Anna Chashkina,Omer Bromberg,Gregory R. Werner,Hayk Hakobyan*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文首次开展全局轴对称广义相对论磁流体力学（GRMHD）模拟，追踪条纹喷流（striped jets）从黑洞吸积发射到远距离能量耗散的完整演化，揭示迷你喷流（mini-jet）加速与电流片（current sheet）耗散机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条纹喷流具有交替极性的磁主导区域，可在全局电流片中实现快速磁能耗散，被认为是耀变体（blazars）和伽马射线暴（gamma-ray burst）瞬时辐射高能辐射的可行来源。但此前缺乏从喷流发射到远距离耗散的全局自洽模拟。

Method: 采用首个全局轴对称 GRMHD 模拟，通过交替极性极向磁环（poloidal magnetic loops）吸积到旋转黑洞上，追踪条纹喷流从发射到远距离能量耗散的演化过程。

Result: 每个磁环吸积发射一个坡印廷通量主导（Poynting-flux-dominated）的迷你喷流，携带继承自磁环的螺旋磁场，其集合构成喷流核心；核心被非相对论鞘层包围。高效发射要求磁环在黑洞附近（r ≲ 50 r_g）形成，否则因湍流失去相干性。迷你喷流以电磁方式加速，洛伦兹因子（Lorentz factor）随喷流横截面半径线性增加。在模拟盒 10^4 r_g 内测得能量耗散率约为观测者系中喷流坡印廷功率的数十百分比。

Conclusion: 模拟结果可解释 BL Lac 型耀变体中观测到的明亮超光速特征（superluminal features），支持条纹喷流作为高能辐射来源的图景。

Abstract: Striped jets, characterized by magnetically dominated regions with alternating polarity, can enable fast magnetic energy dissipation in global current sheets formed in between the stripes, and are thus considered as a viable source for the high-energy emission observed in blazars and gamma-ray burst prompt emission. We perform the first global axisymmetric GRMHD simulations that follow the evolution of striped jets from their launching, by accretion of poloidal magnetic loops with alternating polarity onto a spinning black-hole, to their energy dissipation at large distances. The accretion of each loop launches a Poynting-flux-dominated mini-jet carrying a helical magnetic field whose polarity is inherited from the loop, and the collection of which forms the jet core. The relativistic core is surrounded by a non-relativistic sheath composed of returning fieldlines from previously launched mini-jets in a nested configuration. Efficient mini-jet launching requires poloidal loops in the accretion disk to form close to the black hole ($r \lesssim 50\,r_g$); otherwise, they lose coherency due to turbulence. Once launched, the mini-jets accelerate electromagnetically, similar to steady-state jets, with the Lorentz factor increasing linearly with the jet cross-sectional radius as long as lateral causal contact with the jet axis is maintained. Current sheets form when two trailing mini-jets carrying oppositely directed magnetic fields come into contact. Early sheet formation is facilitated by the longitudinal expansion of the mini-jets, driven by internal pressure before the flow becomes super-fast-magnetosonic. We measure an energy dissipation rate of a few tens of percent of the jet Poynting power in the observer frame, within our simulation box of $10^4\,r_g$. Our results can account for the bright superluminal features observed in BL Lac type blazars.

</details>


### [170] [A Vortex Avalanche Model for Pulsar Glitches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04481)
*M. Ali Alpar,Erbil Gügercinoğlu*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 提出一个二维中子星壳层超流模型，将涡旋钉扎景观划分为“陷阱”与无涡旋区域，并由此形成快速涡旋运动的方位角通道，从而在定性层面解释脉冲星自转突变（glitch）的参数、演化及幅度双峰分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲星自转突变（pulsar glitch）被认为涉及大量钉扎涡旋（pinned vortices）的突然脱钉与快速输运，可能由中子星壳层固体的破裂触发。此类涡旋雪崩（vortex avalanche）只有在存在快速涡旋运动通道时才能以快时标发生，因此需要明确快速通道的形成机制。

Method: 构建中子星壳层超流（superfluid）的简单二维模型：涡旋钉扎景观被划分为“陷阱”（trap，即钉扎涡旋密度高于平均的区域）及其周围的无涡旋区域，后者可连通形成快速涡旋运动的方位角通道。陷阱与瓦片尺寸由壳层固体与涡旋钉扎的物理量估算，即壳层固体的临界应变角（critical strain angle）与涡旋脱钉的临界速度（critical velocity for vortex unpinning）。脱钉涡旋随超流沿方位角方向流动，同时因互摩擦（mutual friction）以一小角度向外径向散射。

Result: 该模型在定性上解释了年轻脉冲星中自转突变参数及其演化，并为自转突变幅度分布的双峰性（bi-modality）提供了解释。

Conclusion: 针对具有空间非均匀涡旋钉扎景观的涡旋系统的实验室实验与数值模拟，可能构成更贴近实际的脉冲星自转突变模型；打破空间标度不变性（scale-invariance）并监测其结果，也可能有助于更一般地理解钉扎涡旋系统中的集体行为（collective behaviour）。

Abstract: Pulsar glitches are thought to involve sudden unpinning and fast transport of large numbers of pinned vortices, possibly triggered by breaking of the neutron star crust solid. Such a vortex avalanche can take place on a fast timescale only if there are pathways for fast vortex motion. Formation of local vortex ``traps'' (regions containing larger than average density of pinned vortices) will provide vortex-free regions surrounding the traps, which can connect to form pathways for fast vortex motion. We propose a simple two-dimensional model for the neutron star crust superfluid, with the vortex pinning landscape tiled into traps and surrounding vortex-free regions which can connect to azimuthal pathways for fast vortex motion. The trap and tile sizes are estimated from the physics of the crust solid and vortex pinning, namely the critical strain angle of the crust solid and the critical velocity for vortex unpinning. Unpinned vortices move with the superfluid flow in the azimuthal direction while also scattering radially outward, by a small angle, due to mutual friction. The model qualitatively explains the glitch parameters and their evolution in young pulsars and provides an explanation for the bi-modality in the distribution of glitch magnitudes. Laboratory experiments and simulations on vortex systems with spatially inhomogeneous vortex pinning landscapes are likely to be more realistic models for pulsar glitches. Breaking the spatial scale-invariance and monitoring the results may also lead to better understanding of collective behaviour in pinned vortex systems in general.

</details>

# math.DG [[Back]](#toc)

### [171] [On the causality of gravitational pp-wave spacetimes: A null version of Anderson's rigidity theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03749)
*Hannah Cairo*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明平面波（plane wave）是唯一具有区分性的引力 pp-波时空（gravitational pp-wave spacetime），并据此建立 Anderson 刚性定理（Anderson's rigidity theorem）的零版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Costa e Silva 与 Flores 曾猜想：特定条件下的 Ricci-平坦 Brinkmann 时空应同构于平面波时空的商空间；该猜想需要一个关于 pp-波时空区分性的判据作为支撑。

Method: 通过刻画引力 pp-波时空的区分性（distinguishing）与强因果性（strongly causal），证明二者仅由平面波时空满足；进而将这一结果用于推导零方向的刚性结论。

Result: 平面波是唯一具有区分性的引力 pp-波时空，也是唯一强因果的此类时空；由此得到：任意测地完备（geodesically complete）、横截完备（transversally complete）、强因果、Ricci-平坦的 4 维 Brinkmann 时空均等距同构于某平面波时空的商空间。

Conclusion: 该工作证实了 Costa e Silva 与 Flores 的猜想，给出了 Anderson 刚性定理的零版本，表明平面波在 Brinkmann 时空刚性分类中具有唯一性地位。

Abstract: We prove that plane waves are the only gravitational pp-wave spacetimes which are distinguishing; in particular, they are the only strongly causal ones. This is used to prove a null version of Anderson's rigidity theorem, conjectured by Costa e Silva and Flores: every geodesically complete, transversally complete, strongly causal, Ricci-flat Brinkmann spacetime of dimension $4$ is isometric to a quotient of a plane wave spacetime.

</details>


### [172] [The pseudo-Riemannian spectral zeta function density on scattering manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05528)
*Ashkan Sadat Kyaee*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文研究混合号差、任意维数伪黎曼散射空间上主标量 Laplace 型算子复幂的谱 zeta 密度，证明对角迹密度存在亚纯延拓，并将极点与局部几何不变量（尤其伪黎曼标量曲率）相联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dang 与 Wrochna 此前仅对偶数维洛伦兹情形发展了 Feynman 复幂的局部分析；本文旨在将该分析推广至更一般号差的伪黎曼散射空间与任意维数。

Method: 对复幂 $(P\pm i\varepsilon)^{-\alpha}$（$\varepsilon>0$），结合一致的 Feynman 预解估计（uniform Feynman resolvent estimates）与伪黎曼号差下 Hadamard 拟基本解（Hadamard parametrix）的直接分析；在洛伦兹情形之外，以几何无返回论证（geometric no-return argument）替代因果凸性（causal convexity）。

Result: 证明对角迹密度（diagonal trace densities）允许亚纯延拓（meromorphic continuation），并将其极点与局部几何不变量、特别是伪黎曼标量曲率（pseudo-Riemannian scalar curvature）关联起来。

Conclusion: 结论为：Feynman 复幂的局部谱 zeta 分析可超越偶数维洛伦兹情形，推广至混合号差、任意维数的伪黎曼散射空间，且不需要因果凸性假设。

Abstract: We study spectral zeta densities associated with complex powers of principally scalar Laplace-type operators on pseudo-Riemannian scattering spaces of mixed signature and arbitrary dimension. The construction extends, beyond the even-dimensional Lorentzian case, the local analysis of Feynman complex powers developed by Dang and Wrochna. Namely, for $\varepsilon > 0$, we consider complex powers $(P\pm i\varepsilon)^{-α}$ and prove that the diagonal trace densities admit meromorphic continuation. We relate their poles to local geometric invariants, in particular to the pseudo-Riemannian scalar curvature. Our proof combines uniform Feynman resolvent estimates with a direct analysis of the Hadamard parametrix in pseudo-Riemannian signature. Beyond the Lorentzian case, the use of causal convexity is replaced by a geometric no-return argument.

</details>


### [173] [Manton's Vortex Equations and Anti-Self-Dual Connections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05962)
*Takashi Ono*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该研究在黎曼曲面（Riemann surface）X 上考察 Manton 五个涡旋方程（vortex equations）中的四个，并将其与反自对偶联络（anti-self-dual connections）建立对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于将 Manton 涡旋方程与反自对偶联络这一几何结构联系起来，以理解涡旋方程解与高维全纯丛之间的对应关系。

Method: 方法为：由每个涡旋方程的解构造 X×P^1、X×Δ 或 X×C 上的等变全纯丛（equivariant holomorphic bundle），并配以不变 Hermitian 或伪 Hermitian 度量（pseudo-Hermitian metric）；等变结构分别由 SU(2)、SU(1,1) 与 SE(2) 的作用给出。

Result: 结果表明，当相关联络为反自对偶时，由涡旋方程解可构造出满足条件的等变全纯丛；反向地，配备不变（伪）Hermitian 度量的等变全纯丛在联络反自对偶时也给出相应涡旋方程的解。

Conclusion: 结论是 Manton 的四个涡旋方程与反自对偶等变全纯丛之间存在双向对应关系。

Abstract: We study four of Manton's five vortex equations on a Riemann surface $X$ in relation to anti-self-dual connections. From a solution of each vortex equation, we construct an equivariant holomorphic bundle over $X$$\times$$\mathbb{P}^1$, $X$$\times$$Δ$, or $X$$\times$$\mathbb{C}$, together with an invariant Hermitian or pseudo-Hermitian metric whose associated connection is anti-self-dual. The equivariant structures are given by the actions of $SU(2)$, $SU(1,1)$, and $SE(2)$, respectively. Conversely, we show that an equivariant holomorphic bundle equipped with an invariant (pseudo-)Hermitian metric gives rise to a solution of the corresponding vortex equation when the associated connection is anti-self-dual.

</details>


<div id='astro-ph.IM'></div>

# astro-ph.IM [[Back]](#toc)

### [174] [Vector field dark matter search with Sagnac interferometer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04626)
*Yuki Inoue,Quynh Lan Nguyen,Ippei Obata*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 该文首次计算表明，材料非对称（material-asymmetric）Sagnac 速度计（speed meter）在低频段可将 $B-L$ 矢量暗物质（vector dark matter）的量子噪声极限灵敏度较 Fabry–Perot Michelson 位移计提升近两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有干涉测量多以位置计（position meter）构型搜索暗物质，在探测依赖材料成分的 $B-L$ 力时受低频量子噪声限制，因而需要评估更优的读出方案以拓展低质量端的搜索能力。

Method: 在双环干涉仪（dual-ring interferometer）中引入材料对比（material contrast）以探测成分依赖的 $B-L$ 力，并在相同入射功率（incident power）、输入镜透射率（input-mirror transmissivity）与腔往返长度（cavity round-trip length）条件下，对比材料非对称 Sagnac 速度计与 Fabry–Perot Michelson 位置计两种构型。

Result: 对蓝宝石镜（sapphire mirrors）与一周基准（one-week benchmark），仅考虑量子噪声时中位预期 $95\%$ CL 上限在 $m_A=4.1\times10^{-14}\,\mathrm{eV}/c^2$（$10\,\mathrm{Hz}$）达到 $1.2\times10^{-27}$，为 $57$ 倍改善；该频段边缘预测假设经典噪声可忽略，且 $10\,\mathrm{Hz}$ 灵敏度尚未在可实现的悬架与隔振系统中得到确证。两种构型中的材料诱导位移基本相同，增益源于低频量子反作用（quantum backaction）的降低。

Conclusion: 速度计读出（speed-meter readout）被识别为通向更低质量矢量暗物质搜索的有前景路径。

Abstract: We present the first calculation showing that a material-asymmetric Sagnac speed meter can improve quantum-noise-limited sensitivity to $B-L$ vector dark matter by nearly two orders of magnitude over a Fabry--Perot Michelson position meter at the low-mass end of the band. We introduce material contrast into a dual-ring interferometer to probe composition-dependent $B-L$ forces and compare the two configurations at equal incident power, input-mirror transmissivity, and cavity round-trip length. For sapphire mirrors and a one-week benchmark, the quantum-noise-only median expected $95\%$ CL upper limit reaches $1.2\times10^{-27}$ at $m_A=4.1\times10^{-14}\,\mathrm{eV}/c^2$ ($10\,\mathrm{Hz}$), a factor-$57$ improvement. This band-edge forecast assumes negligible classical noise; sensitivity at $10\,\mathrm{Hz}$ is not established for a realizable suspension and isolation system. The material-induced displacement is essentially identical in both configurations, with the gain arising from reduced low-frequency quantum backaction. These results identify speed-meter readout as a promising route to lower-mass vector-dark-matter searches.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [175] [Alkaid: A Compiler Framework for Ultra-Low-Latency Kernels on Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04808)
*Chang Sun,Zhiqiang Que,Dimitrios Danopoulos,Maurizio Pierini,Wayne Luk,Maria Spiropulu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: Alkaid 是一个开源领域专用编译器，可将亚微秒级延迟数据流内核在数秒内转换为平台无关的 RTL 或 HLS 就绪代码，面向超低延迟机器学习流水线，在典型 ML 负载上实现 20-30% 的 LUT 减少与最高 40% 的延迟降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亚微秒级超低延迟机器学习与数据处理流水线常包含静态数据流内核，需要细粒度位宽控制、算术优化与快速硬件性能估计；现有 ML-to-hardware 流程（如 hls4ml）主要面向神经网络本身，难以兼顾前后处理与控制逻辑。

Method: Alkaid 采用以算术为中心的中间表示 ALIR（Alkaid Low-level IR），用以保留定点语义、异构位宽与操作级结构，支持优化与代码生成；同时提供全面的硬件感知优化 pass 和可解释的白盒分析性能模型，用于无需综合在环的快速设计空间探索。

Result: 在 MLP、GNN 与 transformer 等代表性 ML 负载上，对位精确等效设计，Alkaid 相比最佳先前流程降低 20-30% 的 LUT 使用，部分情况下延迟降低最高 40%；在提升决策树合成上达到与最先进方法相当的性能，并支持排序网络、直方图等非 ML 内核的实现与集成。

Conclusion: Alkaid 通过算术中心 IR 与硬件感知优化及分析性能模型，为超低延迟 ML 流水线及其周边逻辑提供统一、平台无关且开源的编译方案，在资源与延迟上优于既有流程并扩展至非 ML 内核。

Abstract: Ultra-low-latency machine learning and data processing pipelines operating on the sub-microsecond level often contain static dataflow kernels that require fine-grained bitwidth control, arithmetic optimization, and fast hardware performance estimation. This works introduce Alkaid, a free and open source domain specific compiler that translates sub-microsecond latency dataflow kernels into platform agnostic Register Transfer Level (RTL) or High-Level Synthesis (HLS)-ready code in seconds. Alkaid targets ultra-low-latency ML pipelines, especially those addressed by existing ML-to-hardware flows such as \texttt{hls4ml}, while also supporting the surrounding pre-processing, post-processing, and control logic. At its core, Alkaid uses an arithmetic centric intermediate representation, Alkaid Low-level IR (ALIR), to preserve fixed-point semantics, heterogeneous bitwidths, and operation level structure for optimization and code generation. Alkaid further provides comprehensive hardware-aware optimization passes and an interpretable white-box analytical performance model for rapid design space exploration without synthesis in the loop. Across representative ML workloads, including MLPs, GNNs, and transformers, Alkaid achieves 20-30\% lower LUT usage than the best prior flows for bit-exact equivalent designs, with up to 40\% lower latency in some cases. Beyond typical neural networks, Alkaid achieves comparable performance to the state-of-the-art method for synthesizing boosted decision trees, while also enabling the implementation and integration of non-ML kernels such as sorting networks and histograms.

</details>

### [176] [The Euler characteristic of $Pin^{\pm}$ manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06747)
*Renee S. Hoekzema,Luuk Stehouwer,Simona Veselá*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文完全确定了给定维数下闭 $Pin^{\pm}$-流形（$Pin^{\pm}$-manifolds）欧拉示性数（Euler characteristic）的可能奇偶性，并据此部分计算了此前未确定的受控切割粘贴群（controlled cut-and-paste groups）$SKK_{2k+1}^{Pin^{\pm}}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确阐述研究动机，仅指出所计算的受控切割粘贴群此前处于未确定（previously undetermined）状态。

Method: 以确定闭 $Pin^{\pm}$-流形欧拉示性数可能奇偶性为主要手段，并将其作为工具应用于受控切割粘贴群的计算。

Result: 完全确定了给定维数下闭 $Pin^{\pm}$-流形欧拉示性数的可能奇偶性；作为应用，部分计算出 $SKK_{2k+1}^{Pin^{\pm}}$。

Conclusion: 欧拉示性数奇偶性的完整刻画可用于推进受控切割粘贴群的计算，摘要显示该应用仅获得部分结果。

Abstract: We completely determine the possible parities of the Euler characteristic of closed $Pin^{\pm}$-manifolds of a given dimension. As an application we partially calculate the controlled cut-and-paste groups $SKK_{2k+1}^{Pin^{\pm}}$, which were previously undetermined.

</details>

### [177] [Irreducibility of Verma modules for symmetrizable Kac-Moody superalgebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06109)
*Maria Gorelik*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文利用 Weyl-Kac 分母恒等式（Weyl-Kac denominator identity），为可对称化 Kac-Moody 超代数（symmetrizable Kac-Moody superalgebras）上的 Verma 模（Verma modules）建立了一个不可约性判据（criterion for irreducibility）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Verma 模的不可约性是表示论中的基础问题，而在可对称化 Kac-Moody 超代数这一推广框架下需要相应的判定标准。摘要未进一步说明具体动机背景。

Method: 主要工具为 Weyl-Kac 分母恒等式（Weyl-Kac denominator identity），以此推导可对称化 Kac-Moody 超代数上 Verma 模不可约性的判据。

Result: 给出了可对称化 Kac-Moody 超代数上 Verma 模不可约性的一个判据。摘要未提供判据的具体形式或证明细节。

Conclusion: Weyl-Kac 分母恒等式可有效用于刻画可对称化 Kac-Moody 超代数上 Verma 模的不可约性。摘要未给出进一步推论或应用。

Abstract: We establish a criterion for the irreducibility of Verma modules over symmetrizable Kac--Moody superalgebras. Our main tool is the Weyl-Kac denominator identity.

</details>

### [178] [Realizing Braided Fusion 2-Categories as (3+1)D Mixed-State Topological Orders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03864)
*Ryohei Kobayashi,Abhinav Prem,Matthew Yu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在(3+1)维构造了大类混合态拓扑序（mixed-state topological order），包含内禀混合态拓扑序（intrinsically mixed-state topological order）。作者将辫状融合2-范畴（braided fusion 2-category）的参数数据与刻画混合态拓扑序的强高形式对称性（strong higher-form symmetry）及其反常（anomaly）相联系，并在格点（lattice）上给出具体模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于将混合态拓扑序的分类与刻画推广到(3+1)维，并厘清其与辫状融合2-范畴数据及高形式对称性反常之间的关系。作者特别关注是否存在无法由任何(3+1)维有能隙基态（gapped ground state）实现的内禀混合态拓扑序。

Method: 方法上，作者把参数化辫状融合2-范畴的数据与强高形式对称性及其反常相关联，聚焦玻色型理论（bosonic theory，含线算符谱中出现涌现费米子的情形）。随后在格点上构造具体模型，识别辫状融合2-范畴数据的物理编码，并将其形式化，证明每个辫状融合2-范畴都可由“退相干（decoherence）后接强与弱规范化（strong and weak gauging）”的一般构造得到。

Result: 结果显示，每个辫状融合2-范畴均可作为某个(3+1)维混合态的拓扑算符范畴（category of topological operators）出现，内禀混合态拓扑序以强高形式对称性反常不可在有能隙基态中实现为特征。此外，(3+1)维存在一种无(2+1)维对应物的新机制：拓扑算符非退化辫合（braid non-degenerately）却携带引力反常（gravitational anomaly）的内禀混合态序。

Conclusion: 结论是(3+1)维混合态拓扑序可由辫状融合2-范畴统一刻画，并通过退相干与强、弱规范化的一般构造实现。其内禀类型由无法在有能隙基态中实现的强高形式对称性反常所界定，且(3+1)维独有地允许存在非退化辫合但带引力反常的内禀混合态序。

Abstract: We construct large families of mixed-state topological orders in (3+1)D, including intrinsically mixed-state topological orders. We realize these theories by relating the data parameterizing braided fusion 2-categories to the strong higher-form symmetries and their anomalies that characterize mixed-state topological order. We focus here on bosonic theories, which include theories which admit an emergent fermion in the spectrum of line operators. Our general framework is exemplified on the lattice, where we present concrete models and identify how the data of braided fusion 2-categories are physically encoded. We then formalize this data and show that every braided fusion 2-category arises from a general construction (decoherence followed by strong and weak gauging) as the category of topological operators of a (3+1)D mixed state, with intrinsically mixed-state topological orders distinguished by the presence of strong higher-form symmetries whose anomalies cannot be realized in any (3+1)D gapped ground state. Notably, we show that (3+1)D admits a new mechanism with no (2+1)D counterpart: intrinsically mixed-state order whose topological operators braid non-degenerately, but which carries a gravitational anomaly.

</details>


### [179] [Multi-Indexed Schatten Quasi- and Anti-Norms and Additivity of Conditional Rényi Entropies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04023)
*Jan Kochanowski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入并研究多指标 Schatten 空间（multi-indexed Schatten spaces），将交换的 $\ell_{q_1}[\ell_{q_2}[\cdots]]$ 空间提升至量子框架，推广 Pisier 的算子值 Schatten 范数及双指标 Schatten 拟范数，并应用于量子信息论中的熵可加性与链式法则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究工作（如 Pisier 的算子值 Schatten 范数与近期双指标 Schatten 拟范数）局限于单指标或双指标情形，缺乏统一的多指标框架来刻画高维张量积算子空间上的范数结构，且非交换 Minkowski 不等式与超压缩性张量化等核心工具在拟范数与负指标情形尚未建立。

Method: 对非零指标有序元组 $\mathcal{Q}=(q_1,\dots,q_n)$，在同号且满足兼容条件 $|1/q_i-1/q_j|\leq1$ 时定义 Hilbert 空间 $n$ 重张量积上算子的拟范数（正指标）或反范数（负指标），并通过结合算子空间与矩阵分析技术的因子分解公式（factorization formulas）建立其性质。

Result: 建立了所定义拟范数与反范数的若干理想性质；将 Devetak–Junge–King–Ruskai（CMP 2006）的核心工具提升至多指标拟范数框架，包括关联交换系统的非交换 Minkowski 不等式与两个去恒等（identity-removal）等式；由此证明完全超压缩性与完全反向超压缩性对所有兼容指标（含负指标）张量化。

Conclusion: 在量子信息论方面，证明完全有界最小与最大输出条件熵在所有 $\alpha\geq\frac12$ 下对量子信道的张量积可加，推广了 Fawzi–Kochanowski–Rouzé–Van Himbeeck（CMP 2026）中 $\alpha>1$ 的情形与 Devetak 等的无条件下结果；并建立 $\tilde H^\uparrow_\alpha$（$\alpha\in[\frac12,1)$）对可能具信号传递（signaling）信道的链式法则，将设备无关纠缠验证中使用的光滑最大熵链式法则扩展至整个 $\alpha\in[1/2,1)$ 区间。

Abstract: We introduce and study multi-indexed Schatten spaces which lift the commutative $\ell_{q_1}\![\ell_{q_2}\![\ell_{q_3}\![\dots]]]$ -spaces to the quantum toolbox. They extend Pisier's operator-valued Schatten norms and the recently introduced two-indexed Schatten quasi-norms. For every ordered tuple $\mathcal{Q}=(q_1,\dots,q_n)$ of non-zero indices we define a quasi-norm (for positive indices) or an anti-norm (for negative indices) on operators on an $n$-fold tensor product of Hilbert spaces. We do this for indices of the same sign satisfying the mutual compatibility condition $|\frac{1}{q_i}-\frac{1}{q_j}|\leq1$. Beyond establishing desirable properties of these quasi- and anti-norms via factorization formulas that combine operator space and matrix-analytic techniques, we lift several central tools and results of the seminal [Devetak, Junge, King, Ruskai, CMP 2006] to the multi-indexed and quasi-normed setting. These include a general non-commutative Minkowski inequality relating swapped systems and two identity-removal equalities. As a consequence, complete hypercontractivity and complete reverse hypercontractivity tensorize for all compatible indices, including negative ones.
  Concerning quantum information theory we prove that the completely bounded minimal and the maximal output conditional entropies are additive under tensor products of quantum channels for all $α\geq\frac12$. This extends the case $α>1$ of [Fawzi, Kochanowski, Rouzé, Van Himbeeck, CMP 2026] and the unconditional results of [Devetak, Junge, King, Ruskai, CMP 2006]. Beyond tensor products, we establish chain rules for $\tilde H^\uparrow_α$, $α\in[\frac12,1)$, for compositions of possibly signaling channels, extending upon the smooth max-entropy chain rule used in device-independent entanglement certification to the whole interval $α\in[1/2,1)$.

</details>


### [180] [Exact Finite-Channel Schur Moments of the Wigner-Smith Matrix with a Tunnel Barrier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04170)
*Israel F. Araujo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作研究破缺时间反演对称性的混沌腔中非理想耦合 Wigner–Smith 时延矩阵（Wigner–Smith time-delay matrix）的 Schur 矩，证明其可分解为理想耦合的逆拉盖尔值与非理想势垒因子，并借助 CUE 特征多项式比值的经典理论给出有限通道的精确时延矩公式及半经典展开所遗漏的指数小修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非理想耦合下 Wigner–Smith 矩阵的联合矩阵分布已有精确随机矩阵结果，但 Schur 矩（Schur moments）的显式有限通道公式尚不充分；本文旨在填补这一空白，并厘清半经典展开所遗漏的贡献来源。

Method: 将每个 Schur 矩分解为理想耦合的逆拉盖尔（inverse-Laguerre）值与一个由 Schur 多项式在圆酉系综（circular unitary ensemble, CUE）下的平均构成的势垒因子；引入对偶 Cauchy 生成函数，将该因子化为具有有理符号的 Toeplitz 行列式，从而纳入 CUE 特征多项式比值的经典理论框架。

Result: 对单行分拆给出涵盖所有势垒系数与有限时延矩（n≤M）的闭式生成函数，并证明精确比值公式可分解为半经典可见扇区与正比于 R^{M+1} 的额外贡献；后者对 1/M 的任意代数阶均不可见，且对二阶矩精确重现已知随机矩阵表达式中指数小项。此外给出单列分拆的精确有限求和及 M×M 方内 Schur 平均的互补对称性。

Conclusion: 本文的新意不在经典 CUE 比值恒等式本身，而在于将其与精确的非理想 Wigner–Smith 映射相结合，导出有限通道时延公式，并给出对半经典 Schur 矩猜想（semiclassical Schur-moment conjectures）的修正。

Abstract: We consider the Wigner--Smith time-delay matrix of a chaotic cavity with broken time-reversal symmetry, a finite number $M$ of equivalent open channels, and a uniform tunnel barrier of reflection probability $R$. Exact random-matrix results for nonideal coupling are known at the level of the joint matrix distribution, whereas explicit finite-channel formulas for Schur moments are much less developed. We show that every Schur moment of the nonideal Wigner--Smith matrix factorizes into its ideal-coupling inverse-Laguerre value and a barrier factor given by a circular unitary ensemble (CUE) average of a Schur polynomial. A dual-Cauchy generating function reduces the latter to a Toeplitz determinant with a rational symbol, placing the barrier problem within the classical theory of exact CUE characteristic-polynomial ratios.
  For one-row partitions, specializing the exact CUE ratio formula gives a closed generating function for all barrier coefficients and therefore all finite delay-time moments in the allowed range $n\le M$. The exact ratio formula decomposes into a sector visible to the semiclassical expansion and an additional contribution proportional to $R^{M+1}$, which is invisible to every algebraic order in $1/M$. For the second moment, this contribution exactly reproduces the exponentially small term in the known random-matrix expression for $\langleτ_W^2\rangle$ that is absent from the semiclassical expansion. We also derive an exact finite sum for all one-column partitions and an exact complement symmetry for Schur averages inside the $M\times M$ square. The new contribution therefore lies not in the underlying CUE ratio identities, which are classical, but in combining them with the exact nonideal Wigner--Smith mapping and extracting the resulting finite-channel time-delay formulas and corrections to semiclassical Schur-moment conjectures.

</details>


### [181] [The quantum-classical bipartite universe and the logical emergence of $\hbar$ from classical temporality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05083)
*Vincenzo Chilla*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文基于标准冯·诺依曼（von Neumann）形式体系，提出经典观测环境与量子被观测系统之间的结构性逻辑二分，论证两者须分别服从布尔（Boolean）与非布尔（non-Boolean）逻辑，并由理想经典时钟的耗散动力学导出普朗克常数 $\hbar$ 及与 't Hooft 元胞自动机（cellular automaton）形式的精确映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对普遍主义（universalist）框架——即认为单一逻辑或单一量子描述可覆盖全部物理实在——该文主张理论自洽性要求区分不同命题逻辑。其动机在于为测量问题（measurement problem）提供一种无需隐变量（hidden variables）与超决定论（superdeterminism）假设的替代本体论解释。

Method: 在冯·诺依曼形式体系内设定经典环境与量子系统的逻辑二分：环境由布尔逻辑支配，被观测系统由非布尔逻辑支配。将理想经典时钟建模为开放系统，其能谱均匀离散；动力学由 Gorini-Kossakowski-Sudarshan-Lindblad（GKSL）主方程给出，并施加哈密顿量保持可观测的约束。

Result: 时钟的时间演化虽由与测量装置的耗散耦合驱动，却存在稳定的确定性解，对应保信息的循环动力学（cyclic dynamics），并作为逻辑吸引子（logical attractor）由测量相互作用的布尔结构所稳定。$\hbar$ 由经典时钟的作用量-角变量对易关系（action-angle commutation relation）导出为基本标度参数；该框架与 't Hooft 的元胞自动机形式建立精确映射。

Conclusion: 量子动力学被视为潜在再语境化（potential recontextualization），经典动力学被视为时间实现化（temporal actualization）。二者的区分为测量问题提供了一条新的逻辑进路，并给出不依赖隐变量与超决定论假设的本体论诠释。

Abstract: We present a foundational reinterpretation of quantum dynamics based on the standard von Neumann formalism, positing a structural logical bipartition between a classical observational environment and a quantum observed system. Against universalist frameworks, we show that theoretical consistency requires distinct propositional logics: Boolean logic governs the environment, while non-Boolean logic applies to the observed system. Within this framework, an ideal classical clock is represented as an open system whose energy spectrum is uniformly discretized. The dynamics follow from the Gorini-Kossakowski-Sudarshan-Lindblad (GKSL) master equation under the constraint that the Hamiltonian remains observable. Although the clock's temporal evolution is driven by dissipative coupling to the measurement apparatus, it admits a stable, deterministic solution corresponding to information-preserving cyclic dynamics. This deterministic regime emerges as a logical attractor, stabilized by the Boolean structure inherent to the measurement interaction. Ultimately, $\hbar$ is derived as a fundamental scaling parameter from the classical clock's action-angle commutation relation, revealing that the quantum of action reflects the discrete logical structure underlying classical temporality. Furthermore, this framework establishes an exact mapping with 't Hooft's cellular automaton formalism, offering an alternative ontological interpretation that not require hidden variables and superdeterministic assumptions. The resulting distinction between quantum dynamics (as potential recontextualization) and classical dynamics (as temporal actualization) provides a novel logical approach to the measurement problem.

</details>


### [182] [Error-Transparent Code Switching Between Permutation-Invariant Codes with Transversal Clifford and Icosahedral Gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05189)
*Zachary P. Bradshaw*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究分析同一组量子比特上两类置换不变码（permutation-invariant codes）之间的切换问题：一类具横向 Clifford 门，另一类具来自非 Clifford 二元二十面体群（binary icosahedral group）的横向门，二者共同构成通用门集。作者证明，在满足与量子比特置换及共享横向门对易、且能容忍未知时刻单量子比特故障的条件下，存在误差透明（error-transparent）切换酉算子的充要条件是两个码可由一条距离为三的码连续路径连接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 码切换（code switching）可在两个横向门互补为通用的码之间移动编码量子比特，但切换操作本身通常非横向，可能将单个故障扩散为不可纠正错误。因此需要构造一种误差透明、可容忍单量子比特故障的切换方案，以保留横向门的容错优势。

Method: 研究同一组量子比特上的两类置换不变码（一类横向 Clifford 门、一类非 Clifford 二元二十面体群横向门），二者均兼容横向 X、Z 与 F = HS†；分析具备该性质的编码器所构成的线性空间，证明距离至少为三的码至多由两个二次方程刻画，且所有此类码具有相同的误差几何（单量子比特误差态内积相同）。对实数编码器，距离三的码由单个二次方程给出；在 13 个量子比特上构造沿圆的集体算子（collective operator）形式的切换酉算子。

Result: 13 量子比特上的距离三码构成一个显式圆，经过两个 Clifford 码与一个二十面体码（icosahedral code）。构造出的沿该圆的切换酉算子每故障的恢复不忠实度（recovery infidelity）达到机器精度，而两种自然替代方案的平均不忠实度分别为 0.13 和 0.85。此外，对不超过 29 的奇数量子比特数进行普查，列出了此类切换存在的情形。

Conclusion: 误差透明切换酉算子的存在性等价于两类码之间存在距离三码的连续路径，该条件可由至多两个二次方程刻画，并保证单个故障在任意时刻均以同一单量子比特误差形式传递至目标码。该构造的高恢复精度验证了方案的有效性；其向物理控制层面的综合实现（synthesis）仍为开放问题。

Abstract: Code switching moves an encoded qubit between two codes whose transversal gates together are universal, but the switching operation itself is usually not transversal and can spread a single fault into an uncorrectable error. We study switching between two permutation-invariant codes on the same qubits, one with transversal Clifford gates and one with transversal gates from the non-Clifford binary icosahedral group. Both are compatible with transversal $X$, $Z$, and $F = HS^\dagger$, and the encoders with this property form a small linear space. We show that its codes of distance at least three are cut out by at most two quadratic equations, and that all of them have the same error geometry in the sense that their single-qubit error states have the same inner products. It follows that a switching unitary commuting with qubit permutations and with the shared transversal gates at every instant, and tolerating one single-qubit fault at an unknown time, exists if and only if the two codes are joined by a continuous path of distance-three codes. Such a switch can be made error transparent, so that a fault at any moment reaches the target code as the same single-qubit error. For real encoders, the distance-three codes are the solutions of a single quadratic equation, and on thirteen qubits, they form an explicit circle through two Clifford codes and an icosahedral code. We construct the switching unitary along this circle as a collective operator, leaving its synthesis into physical control open, and verify that its recovery infidelity per fault is at machine precision, whereas two natural alternatives leave average infidelities of $0.13$ and $0.85$. A survey over odd qubit numbers up to $29$ lists where such switches exist.

</details>


### [183] [Cups and Gates II: Operads, twists, and addressable intra-code gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05214)
*Nikolas P. Breuckmann,Margarita Davydova,Jens Niklas Eberhardt,Lucas Ferdinand Hallwirth,Nathanan Tantivasadakarn*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文扩展了基于上同调不变量（cohomology invariants）构造量子 CSS 码逻辑门（logical gates）的框架，引入 twists（结合码自同构）、高阶扭曲上同调操作与相对上同调中的 folding 操作，实现从 $\mathbb{Z}/2$ 到 $\mathbb{Z}/2^k$ 的系数提升，从而支持所有对角逻辑门（如 $\operatorname{R}(2\pi/2^k)$）以及可寻址逻辑门（addressable logical gates）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Part I 的框架仅聚焦 cup products 及其推广，主要产生 $C^{k-1}Z$ 型 copy-cup 门。本文意在大幅扩展该框架，以覆盖更丰富的逻辑门类型（包括对角门与可寻址门），并突破拓扑码的限制，适用于 qLDPC 码。

Method: 引入 twists 以融合码自同构（code automorphisms）、高阶扭曲上同调操作（higher twisted cohomology operations）与相对上同调（relative cohomology）中的 folding 操作；通过 $E_\infty$-结构（$E_\infty$-structures）在上链（cochains）上构造操作，并借助循环消解（cyclic resolutions）与 surjection operad 给出显式构造；将操作与选择上同调类的映射复合以获得可寻址门。

Result: 框架实现了系数从 $\mathbb{Z}/2$ 到 $\mathbb{Z}/2^k$ 的提升，可生成单量子比特旋转 $\operatorname{R}(2\pi/2^k)$ 等所有对角逻辑门；给出三环面与四环面上的可寻址 $T$ 门，以及超出拓扑码范围的 qLDPC 码门；提出高阶扭曲 Pontryagin powers 等此前未见于数学文献的上同调操作。

Conclusion: 该形式化方法可推广至携带适当 operadic 结构的链复形（chain complexes），预期这一 operadic 视角将揭示同伦论（homotopy theory）与容错量子门（fault-tolerant quantum gates）之间更多联系；所构造的上同调操作本身在同伦论中亦具独立价值。

Abstract: In Part I, we introduced a general framework for constructing logical gates in quantum CSS codes from cohomology invariants, focusing on cup products and their generalizations from topological to qLDPC codes. In particular, we produced a family of $C^{k-1}Z$ logical gates that we called copy-cup on $k$ copies of the same quantum code implemented by finite-depth circuits. Here, we vastly extend this framework by introducing twists incorporating code automorphisms, higher twisted cohomology operations, and folding operations in relative cohomology. The framework includes operations lifting coefficients from $\mathbb{Z}/2$ to $\mathbb{Z}/2^k$, enabling all diagonal logical gates such as single-qubit rotations $\operatorname{R}(2π/2^k)$. Composing these operations with maps that select cohomology classes yields addressable logical gates. Examples include addressable $T$ gates on three- and four-tori and gates in qLDPC codes beyond topological ones. We introduce cohomology operations, such as the higher twisted Pontryagin powers, that to our knowledge have not previously appeared in the mathematics literature. These operations arise from $E_\infty$-structures on cochains, with explicit constructions using cyclic resolutions and the surjection operad. We expect these results to be of independent interest in homotopy theory. Our formalism applies much more broadly to chain complexes carrying suitable operadic structures, and we expect this operadic perspective to uncover further connections between homotopy theory and fault-tolerant quantum gates.

</details>


### [184] [Optimal Interferometer Geometry for Gravitationally Induced Quantum Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04471)
*Alireza Maleki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文指出干涉仪的几何构型对引力诱导纠缠（gravitationally induced entanglement）的产生具有决定性作用，几何优化可显著增强引力诱导相移，从而提升探测引力介导纠缠的实验可行性，并警示既有方案中的标准近似可能损害预测精度与实验可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找量子引力理论（quantum theory of gravity）是现代物理的核心挑战，但普朗克尺度（Planck scale）难以直接实验触及。近期提案提出，通过先进量子干涉技术（quantum interferometric techniques）实现两个大质量粒子间的引力诱导量子纠缠，可为引力的量子本性提供经验证据，因此需要评估该方案的实际可探测性。

Method: 基于对干涉仪（interferometer）几何构型的分析，考察其对纠缠产生的决定作用；并通过几何优化研究引力诱导相移（phase shift）的增强效果，同时检验既有提案所采用标准近似（standard approximations）的适用性。

Result: 干涉仪的几何构型对纠缠产生产生决定性影响；几何优化能够大幅增强引力诱导相移，从而改善引力介导纠缠实验探测的前景；此外，既有提案中的标准近似可能严重限制预测精度并损害实验可行性。

Conclusion: 几何优化是提升引力诱导纠缠可探测性的关键途径，而重新审视标准近似对于保证预测精度与实验可行性至关重要，这为基于纠缠的量子引力经验检验提供了改进方向。

Abstract: The quest for a quantum theory of gravity remains a central challenge in modern physics, driven by the goal of unifying general relativity and quantum mechanics. Direct experimental access to quantum gravitational effects is notoriously difficult due to the inaccessibility of the Planck scale. Recent proposals, however, suggest that gravitationally induced quantum entanglement between two massive particles, realized through advanced quantum interferometric techniques, could provide empirical evidence for the quantum nature of gravity. We demonstrate that the geometrical configuration of an interferometer plays a decisive role in entanglement generation. Our analysis reveals that geometric optimization can substantially enhance the gravitationally induced phase shift, thereby improving the prospects for experimental detection of gravity-mediated entanglement. Moreover, we show that standard approximations employed in prior proposals may severely limit predictive accuracy and compromise experimental feasibility.

</details>


### [185] [On Information Propagation in Krylov Subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03883)
*Pablo Martinez-Azcona,Maximilian Meyer-Moelleringhof,Adolfo del Campo,Apollonas S. Matsoukas-Roubeas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文基于 Krylov 子空间方法，将量子初值问题映射为一维链，通过时间有序路径表述推导动力学子空间内的速度极限，发现幂律增长的 Lanczos 系数导致超弹道光锥，并证明因果性要求系数至多线性增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Krylov 子空间方法可高效建模量子初值问题，但其动力学子空间内的速度极限与因果性约束尚未得到系统刻画，本文旨在建立该类子空间中的速度极限及 Lanczos 系数增长的上界。

Method: 采用时间有序路径表述（time ordered path formulation），使动力学生成元逐次在路径间分配概率，从而在 Krylov 链上导出速度极限；针对幂律增长的 Lanczos 系数 b_n ∼ n^δ 分析光锥行为，并在 SL(2,R) 与 Heisenberg Weyl 代数及多体 skew field Ising 模型中验证。

Result: 对 b_n ∼ n^δ 得到超弹道光锥（super ballistic lightcones），在最大增长 δ=1 时光锥呈指数扩散；Krylov 链中的因果性（causality）将 Lanczos 系数增长限制为至多线性；该界可推广至 Q 复杂度（Q complexities）族。

Conclusion: Krylov 链中的因果性给出 Lanczos 系数线性增长上界，映射到自旋链后得到 Lieb-Robinson 界（Lieb Robinson bound）的类比，从而将该问题开放给标准凝聚态（condensed matter）方法处理。

Abstract: Krylov subspace methods provide an efficient tool for modeling any quantum initial value problem as a one dimensional chain. A time ordered path formulation, in which the dynamical generator successively splits probability between paths, yields a speed limit within such dynamical subspaces. For power law growing Lanczos coefficients $b_n \sim n^δ$ we find super ballistic lightcones, with an exponentially spreading lightcone for maximal growth $δ= 1$. Causality in the Krylov chain constrains the Lanczos coefficients to grow at most linearly. We illustrate the validity of the above in the $SL(2, R)$ and Heisenberg Weyl algebras, and in a many body skew field Ising model. Moreover, we extend the above bound to the family of $Q$ complexities. Lastly, mapping to a spin chain gives a Lieb Robinson bound analogue and opens the problem to standard condensed matter methods.

</details>


### [186] [Foundational puzzles on quantum universality: From Wigner's friend to black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03979)
*Laurens Walleghem*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文围绕量子理论中的两大基础悖论——测量问题（measurement problem）与黑洞信息悖论（black hole information paradox）展开：第一部分扩展“维格纳的朋友”（Wigner's friend）情景并建立其与非经典性（nonclassicality）的关联；第二部分将扩展维格纳朋友论证与黑洞悖论统一，并用量子参考系（quantum reference frames）分析叠加黑洞附近的探测器响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子理论虽经验成功显著，却存在概念谜题：测量时叠加态为何坍缩为单值结果，即测量问题，维格纳的朋友情景即其体现，近期扩展更导出关于实在本质的强 no-go 结果。另一谜题出现在量子理论与广义相对论（general relativity）交界处，即黑洞信息悖论。论文旨在进一步探究这两个基础悖论。

Method: 第一部分提出若干新的扩展维格纳朋友论证，并将其与非经典性概念建立联系，具体包括对 Frauchiger–Renner 悖论的精细化版本、基于情境性（contextuality）的新扩展维格纳朋友情景，以及基于 Pusey–Barrett–Rudolph 定理反对自由选择绝对性的论证。第二部分构造一个统一扩展维格纳朋友论证与黑洞悖论的悖论，讨论其中的微妙之处，并结合量子理论与引力要素，用量子参考系分析叠加黑洞附近的探测器响应。

Result: 第一部分得到若干关键结果：Frauchiger–Renner 悖论的精细化版本、基于情境性的新扩展维格纳朋友情景、以及基于 Pusey–Barrett–Rudolph 定理反对自由选择绝对性的论证；后者补充了“局域友善性”（Local Friendliness）no-go 定理，该定理挑战了实验所得测量结果的绝对性。第二部分给出一个强化克隆（cloning）与防火墙（firewall）悖论的统一悖论，并完成叠加黑洞附近探测器响应的分析。

Conclusion: 论文通过扩展维格纳朋友情景深化了对量子非经典性的理解，并将此类论证与黑洞悖论相联系，强化了克隆与防火墙悖论；同时借助量子参考系对叠加黑洞附近的探测器响应给出分析，推进了对量子理论与引力交叉处基础问题的探讨。

Abstract: Despite its remarkable empirical success and predictive power, quantum theory is riddled with mysteries. One such conceptual puzzle is the measurement problem: why do quantum systems in superposition collapse to a single-valued outcome upon being measured? The scenario of Wigner's friend illustrates this tension, with recent extensions deriving strong no-go results for the nature of reality. Another fundamental puzzle arises at the interface of quantum theory and general relativity---the black hole information paradox. This thesis further explores these two foundational paradoxes. Part I of this thesis focuses on extensions of the Wigner's friend scenario. We introduce several novel extended Wigner's friend arguments, and establish connections with notions of nonclassicality, i.e. the study of quantum phenomena that evade classical explanation. Key results are a refined version of the Frauchiger--Renner paradox, new extended Wigner's friend scenarios based on contextuality, and an argument against the absoluteness of free choices based on the Pusey--Barrett--Rudolph theorem. The latter result complements the Local Friendliness no-go theorem, which challenged the absoluteness of experimentally obtained measurement outcomes. In Part II of this thesis, we turn to black hole puzzles and the interplay of quantum theory and gravity. We present a paradox that unifies extended Wigner's friend arguments with black hole puzzles, strengthening the cloning and firewall paradoxes. After discussing subtleties in these paradoxes, we next present an analysis combining elements of quantum theory with gravity, analyzing the detector response near a superposed black hole using elements of quantum reference frames.

</details>


### [187] [Spectral Analysis Beyond the Spectral Gap: Rapid Mixing of Quantum Gibbs Samplers in One Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05848)
*Hongrui Chen,Zhiyan Ding*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明了一维非对易局域自旋系统中量子 Gibbs 采样器（quantum Gibbs sampler）在任何固定正温度下均具有快速混合（rapid mixing）性质，收敛时间为 $O(\log^4(n)+\log(1/\epsilon))$，显著改进了此前 $O(n+\log(1/\epsilon))$ 的界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有量子 Gibbs 采样器的快速混合结果多局限于高温或弱相互作用（weak-interaction）区域，一般温度下仅能得到随系统尺度 $n$ 线性增长的收敛时间；本文旨在突破这些区域限制，并解释为何谱隙（spectral gap）可能严重低估平衡化速度。

Method: 核心洞察是远离平衡的态必然在低权重 Pauli 算子（low-weight Pauli operators）张成空间之外具有显著分量，该区域可适用更强的耗散估计。据此将弛豫分为两阶段：从坏初态出发的双指数（double-exponential）收敛，以及近平衡处的指数收敛，并由此构建了超越谱隙的谱分析框架。

Result: 对 $n$ 个格点的系统，Kubo–Martin–Schwinger（KMS）细致平衡（detailed-balanced）Gibbs 采样器从任意初态出发达到迹范数误差 $\epsilon$ 的时间为 $O(\log^4(n)+\log(1/\epsilon))$。该衰减估计等价于夹层 Rényi-2 散度（sandwiched Rényi-2 divergence）意义下的指数收敛，并结合谱隙给出速率为 $\Omega(\log^{-4}(n))$ 的修正对数 Sobolev 不等式（modified logarithmic Sobolev inequality）。

Conclusion: 一维非对易局域自旋系统在任意固定正温度下可实现快速混合，混合速度的多对数（polylogarithmic）标度优于线性界。结果表明最慢衰减率（谱隙）可大幅低估平衡化实际速度，所提出的超越谱隙的谱分析机制为快速混合证明提供了通用框架。

Abstract: We prove rapid mixing of a quantum Gibbs sampler for general one-dimensional non-commuting local spin systems at every fixed positive temperature. In particular, for a system of $n$ sites, we show that the Kubo--Martin--Schwinger (KMS) detailed-balanced Gibbs sampler of~\cite{CKG2023} converges from any initial state to trace-norm error $ε$ in time $O(\log^4(n)+\log(1/ε))$. This improves the previous $O(n+\log(1/ε))$ bound and establishes rapid mixing beyond high-temperature and weak-interaction regimes. Our proof explains why the slowest decay rate, captured by the spectral gap, can substantially underestimate the speed of equilibration. The key insight is that a state far from equilibrium must have a substantial component outside the span of low-weight Pauli operators, where stronger dissipation estimates apply. This yields two stages of relaxation: a double-exponential convergence from a bad initial state, followed by exponential convergence near equilibrium. We turn this mechanism into a framework for proving rapid mixing through spectral analysis beyond the gap. Our decay estimate is equivalent to exponential convergence in sandwiched Rényi-2 divergence. Together with the spectral gap, it also implies a modified logarithmic Sobolev inequality with rate $Ω(\log^{-4}(n))$.

</details>


### [188] [Adiabatic tracking and optimal schedules for classical and quantum Gibbs-state preparation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06268)
*Pedro C. S. Costa,Mauro E. S. Morales,Dong An,Kaur Kristjuhan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文为经典与量子优化算法中的吉布斯态（Gibbs-state）制备提出离散绝热（discrete adiabatic）框架，导出了有限时间非齐次可逆马尔可夫链（Markov chain）的追踪定理，并给出步复杂度界及自适应调度策略；在量子情形下，通过温度依赖的 Szegedy 量子游走（quantum walk）与离散量子绝热定理，证明方根谱放大仅对适配能隙的调度（gap-adapted schedules）带来二次加速，量子绝热代价为 $O(\delta_{\min}^{-1/2})$，优于经典的 $O(\delta_{\min}^{-1})$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于为经典与量子优化算法中的吉布斯态制备建立统一的离散绝热理论框架，并厘清量子游走中谱放大能否真正转化为计算复杂度上的加速。摘要未给出额外背景陈述。

Method: 对有限时间非齐次可逆马尔可夫链推导追踪定理（tracking theorem），将演化分布与瞬时平稳分布的距离分为已积累误差的收缩与新生成误差两部分；对吉布斯路径（Gibbs paths）导出显式步复杂度界，并构造由马尔可夫链谱隙（spectral gap）与能量涨落尺度 $V(\beta)=\sqrt{\operatorname{Var}_{\pi_\beta}(E)}$ 控制的自适应调度。量子方面，将离散量子绝热定理应用于温度依赖的 Szegedy 量子游走，并分析定理中的有限差分项。

Result: 在弱阻尼（weak-damping）区域，所得调度形式为 $\dot\beta\propto\delta(\beta)/V(\beta)$。量子游走的本征相位间隙（eigenphase gap）满足 $\Delta_w=\Theta(\sqrt{\delta})$，其中 $\delta$ 为底层马尔可夫链的谱隙；该方根谱放大约束下，仅在适配能隙的调度下才转化为对能隙依赖的二次改进，相应正则条件下量子绝热代价为 $O(\delta_{\min}^{-1/2})$，而经典弛豫尺度为 $O(\delta_{\min}^{-1})$。

Conclusion: 离散绝热框架为吉布斯态制备提供了误差追踪与复杂度分析工具，并可延伸至 Szegedy 量子游走。方根谱放大并不自动意味着量子加速，其二次改进需借助适配能隙的调度方案方能实现，从而在量子绝热代价上相对经典弛豫尺度获得优势。

Abstract: We develop a discrete adiabatic framework for Gibbs-state preparation in classical and quantum optimisation algorithms. For finite time-inhomogeneous reversible Markov chains, we derive a tracking theorem that bounds the distance from the evolving distribution to the instantaneous stationary state by combining two effects: contraction of previously accumulated error by the Markov dynamics and the new error generated as the stationary distribution changes from one step to the next. For Gibbs paths, this leads to explicit step-complexity bounds and to gap- and fluctuation-adapted schedules controlled by the Markov-chain spectral gap and the energy-fluctuation scale $V(β)=\sqrt{\operatorname{Var}_{π_β}(E)}$. In the weak-damping regime, the resulting schedule takes the form $\dotβ\proptoδ(β)/V(β)$. We then apply the discrete quantum adiabatic theorem to temperature-dependent Szegedy quantum walks. The quantum-walk eigenphase gap satisfies $Δ_w=Θ(\sqrtδ)$, where $δ$ is the spectral gap of the underlying Markov chain. By analysing the finite-difference terms entering the theorem, we show that this square-root spectral amplification translates into a quadratic improvement in the gap dependence only for suitably gap-adapted schedules. Under the corresponding regularity conditions, the quantum adiabatic cost scales as $O(δ_{\min}^{-1/2})$, compared with the classical $O(δ_{\min}^{-1})$ relaxation scale.

</details>


### [189] [An efficient Hamiltonian-based quantum algorithm for characters of the symmetric group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05752)
*Dikshant Rathore,Leo Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一种基于哈密顿量演化序列的对称群字符态（character state）制备算法，门复杂度依目标列选取不同，从 O(n) 到 Õ(n^{2.5}) 不等，并推广出量子字符变换（QCT）。其性能优于先前基于量子傅里叶变换（QFT）的 Õ(n^3) 方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作指出，从对称群（symmetric group, S_n）字符表中任一列所对应的量子态采样，在合理复杂度假设下是经典困难的，但已有需 Õ(n^3) 门的 QFT 制备方法（arXiv:2501.12579）。本文旨在给出更简单、门复杂度更低的替代制备算法。

Method: 采用一系列哈密顿量演化（Hamiltonian evolution）制备字符态，并在可重构量子比特（reconfigurable qubits）上实现仅用一维最近邻门（nearest-neighbor gates）的高效实现。门计数估计源于领头阶、依赖于态的 Trotter 误差分析；最坏情况界则基于量子奇异值变换（QSVT）的哈密顿量模拟方法，并辅以数值基准测试。

Result: 对固定目标态误差，充分总门数估计从 n-循环列的 O(n) 门，到猜想经典困难参数区间附近的 Õ(n^{1.75}) 门，再到最坏情况可证明的 Õ(n^{2.5}) 门。相比之下，先前方法的 QFT 子程序需 Õ(n^3) 门。Trotter 协议数值基准表明该估计偏保守。

Conclusion: 该协议被推广为对称群 S_n 上量子字符变换（quantum character transform, QCT）的显式算法，使用 Õ(n^{2.5}) 门与 O(n) 个量子比特；并讨论了其在对称轨道共形场论（symmetric orbifold conformal field theories）纠缠熵中的应用。

Abstract: Consider a quantum state vector proportional to any given column of the character table of the symmetric group ($S_n$), corresponding to a superposition over the irreps weighted by the character values. It was recently argued that sampling from this state is classically hard under reasonable complexity-theoretic assumptions, while there exists an efficient quantum algorithm for preparing this character state using the quantum Fourier transform (QFT) over $S_n$ [arXiv:2501.12579]. We give an alternative, simpler algorithm to prepare this character state with a sequence of Hamiltonian evolutions, as well as an efficient implementation with reconfigurable qubits that uses only nearest-neighbor gates in 1D. The gate complexity, accounting for Hamiltonian simulation errors, depends on the choice of the column: For fixed target state error, the estimated sufficient total gate count ranges from as few as $O(n)$ gates for the $n$-cycle column, to $\widetilde O(n^{1.75})$ gates near the conjecturally classically hard parameter regime, to provably $\widetilde O(n^{2.5})$ gates in the worst case. In contrast, the QFT subroutine in the prior approach uses $\widetilde O(n^3)$ gates. Our gate count estimates come from leading-order state-dependent Trotter error analysis, and the proven worst-case bound relies on a QSVT-based Hamiltonian simulation method; numerical benchmarks of the Trotter-based protocol suggest that the estimates are conservative. Furthermore, we generalize our protocol to give an explicit algorithm for the quantum character transform (QCT) over $S_n$ using $\widetilde O(n^{2.5})$ gates and $O(n)$ qubits. An application to entanglement entropy of symmetric orbifold conformal field theories is also discussed.

</details>


### [190] [Products of unitaries as continuous evolutions at finite precision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06541)
*Michael Jarret*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种将连续幺正演化离散化的方法，建立连续演化与有限维幺正乘积之间的双向对应，并借此把离散绝热定理归约为连续绝热定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散绝热定理与连续绝热定理之间存在论证割裂，若能把离散问题映射为已成熟的连续问题，则可直接复用连续绝热理论，避免为乘积形式单独建立离散绝热定理。

Method: 在合适有限分割上取受调控哈密顿量（Hamiltonian）的截断样本得到近似乘积；反之对时钟进行取整，把 T 个单步样本的乘积识别为连续演化（相差两个半步相位）。通过每个时间步内的抵消，将算子范数（operator-norm）距离界限制为 O(1/T)。假设条件为一阶、二阶导数有界，谱宽有界，且特征值差一致远离 2π 的非零整数倍。

Result: 对满足导数界的幺正行走（unitary walk），一个固定公共自由谱弧（free spectral arc）给出满足比较假设的 Hermitian 对数路径，从而使连续模拟保证可转移到相位修正后的乘积、连续绝热界可转移到乘积本身，误差为显式加性 O(1/T)，且各绝热定理的假设得以保留。对条件数（condition number）为 κ 的正定量子线性系统，得到泄漏为 δ 时 O(κ/δ) 步的重新标定量子化行走（qubitized walk），与既有的离散绝热定理所得标度一致。

Conclusion: 该对应关系表明此类乘积形式无需离散绝热定理即可获得绝热量化结论，连续绝热理论可直接迁移至离散乘积，并在量子线性系统求解上复现 O(κ/δ) 的步数标度。

Abstract: I present a short approach to discretizing continuous unitary evolution. It gives a correspondence in both directions between continuous unitary evolutions and products of unitaries in finite dimensions, and I use it to reduce discrete adiabatic theorems to continuous ones. At fine steps, holding truncated samples of a regulated Hamiltonian on a suitable finite partition gives an approximating product. Conversely, rounding the clock identifies a product of $T$ unit-step samples with a continuous evolution, up to two half-step phases. I bound its operator-norm distance from the original evolution by $O(1/T)$ through cancellation within each time step. This assumes bounded first and second derivatives, bounded spectral width, and eigenvalue differences bounded away from nonzero multiples of $2π$, uniformly in $T$. For a unitary walk with these derivative bounds, a fixed common free spectral arc supplies a Hermitian logarithm path satisfying the comparison hypotheses. Continuous simulation guarantees then transfer to the phase-corrected product, and continuous adiabatic bounds to the product itself, with an explicit additive $O(1/T)$ error and each adiabatic theorem's hypotheses retained. For positive definite quantum linear systems of condition number $κ$, this comparison and continuous adiabatic theory give a rescaled qubitized walk with $O(κ/δ)$ steps for leakage $δ$, matching the scaling previously obtained through a discrete adiabatic theorem. These products need no discrete adiabatic theorem.

</details>


### [191] [Quantum minimum description of density matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06742)
*Patrick Hayden,Alexander Maloney,Jinzhao Wang,Yuxiang Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究 Schumacher 压缩的一个变体问题：在已知谱、未知特征基且不要求保留纯化（purification）的条件下，压缩密度矩阵多份拷贝所需的最小存储代价。对固定维度与互异正特征值，作者通过其加性常数（additive constant）给出了该代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Schumacher 压缩假设特征基（eigenbasis）已知，而实际问题中特征基往往未知，且纯化未必需要保留。因此需要刻画这一更弱任务下存储代价的精确值。作者聚焦固定维度、互异正特征值的情形，试图确定压缩代价的加性常数，并厘清其与相关编码开销及自由熵（free entropy）的关系。

Method: 可达性（achievability）方面，将 Werner 克隆映射（Werner's cloning map）推广到 GL(d,C) 不可约表示（irreducible representations），并用最高权差（highest-weight differences）与行间隙（row gaps）控制有限迹距离界（trace-distance bounds）。逆命题（converse）基于不可约群轨道（irreducible group orbits）的 Koashi–Imoto 不可压缩性（incompressibility）的定量形式，并附加谱隙（spectral-gap）假设。作者还提供了证明的 Lean 形式化证书（Lean certificate）。

Result: 对固定维度与互异正特征值，通过加性常数确定了该压缩任务的代价，并给出可达性与匹配的逆命题。作者进一步识别出该量子最小描述长度（quantum minimum description length）与通用无损编码开销（universal lossless coding overhead）之间的关系；其与自由熵（free entropy）的联系在配套短文中说明。证明由 Lean 证书支持。

Conclusion: 该工作在谱已知、特征基未知且不保留纯化的 Schumacher 压缩变体下，精确刻画了固定维度互异正特征值情形的存储代价加性常数，并建立其与通用无损编码开销及自由熵的联系，同时以 Lean 形式化证书增强证明可靠性。

Abstract: We study a variant of Schumacher compression. The task asks for the minimal memory cost for compressing many copies of a density matrix with known spectrum and unknown eigenbasis, without preserving its purification. For fixed dimension and distinct positive eigenvalues, we obtain the cost through its additive constant. Achievability uses a generalization of Werner's cloning map to $\mathrm{GL}(d,\mathbb C)$ irreducible representations, with finite trace-distance bounds controlled by highest-weight differences and row gaps. The matching converse follows from a quantitative form of Koashi--Imoto incompressibility for irreducible group orbits under a spectral-gap assumption. We also identify the relation between this quantum minimum description length and universal lossless coding overhead, and its connection to free entropy is explained in a companion letter. We provide a Lean certificate for our proofs.

</details>


### [192] [Local universality of non-local quantum resources in rotationally invariant scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06791)
*Silas R. Beane,Roland C. Farrell*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本论文将弹性散射（elastic scattering）产生的纠缠（entanglement）与非局域魔法（non-local magic）统一为量子资源，推导了任意自旋 J 下 S 矩阵（S-matrix）的闭合形式解，并证明一大类非局域量子资源的领头阶生成能力仅由与最近非纠缠门的投影希尔伯特-施密特距离（projective Hilbert-Schmidt distance）平方决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弹性散射可作为生成自旋纠缠与非局域魔法的量子门，其生成能力编码于 S 矩阵中。此前工作仅对 J=1/2,1,3/2 的旋转不变（rotationally invariant）S 矩阵给出了基于散射相移（scattering phase shifts）的纠缠能力表达式，缺乏对任意 J 的一般性描述，也未揭示不同量子资源生成背后的共同结构。

Method: 论文推导了适用于所有自旋 J 的 S 矩阵纠缠能力的闭合形式解（closed form solution），并进一步分析非局域量子资源生成能力的领头阶（leading order）行为，将其与到最近非纠缠门的投影希尔伯特-施密特距离相联系。

Result: 得到适用于任意 J 的闭合形式解；证明包括纠缠能力（entanglement power）与非局域魔法能力（non-local magic power）在内的广泛非局域量子资源，其领头阶仅依赖于到最近非纠缠门的投影希尔伯特-施密特距离平方，资源类型差异仅改变整体归一化系数。

Conclusion: 该普适性（universality）表明，散射的领头阶资源生成能力具有共同的几何起源（geometric origin），与相互作用细节无关。

Abstract: Elastic scattering can generate entanglement and non-local magic between the spins of the two outgoing particles. The amount of entanglement and magic produced is encoded in the S-matrix; a unitary quantum gate that acts on the joint Hilbert space of the two spin-J particles. Previous work derived the entanglement power in terms of the scattering phase shifts for rotationally invariant S-matrices with J=1/2,1,3/2. In this paper, a closed form solution is derived for all J. We further prove that a broad class of non-local quantum resources depend to leading order only on the squared projective Hilbert-Schmidt distance from the nearest non-entangling gate. This holds for the entanglement power and non-local magic power, and the choice of resource simply changes the overall normalization. This universality establishes a common geometric origin for the leading resource-generating capacity of scattering, independent of the details of the interaction.

</details>


### [193] [Finite-bath projected ensembles in dual-unitary circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06754)
*Rahul Arvind,Nicholas Hunter-Jones*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作研究对偶幺正电路（dual-unitary circuits）经部分测量后所产生投影系综（projected ensemble）中的涌现随机性（emergent randomness），在三条逆多项式假设下证明了多项式规模的浴（bath）即可形成近似 $k$-设计（approximate $k$-designs），并给出微观输运解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有工作已表明，当浴尺寸趋于无穷时，投影系综可构成量子态设计（quantum state design），但有限浴尺寸下的收敛速率尚未被严格确立。本文旨在填补这一空白，厘清有限浴规模与设计收敛之间的关系。

Method: 考虑可解的对偶幺正砖墙电路（brickwork circuits）演化后再对部分系统测量，从而在未测量子系统上诱导出态系综；在三条逆多项式（inverse-polynomial）假设——局域置换混合（local permutation mixing）、对回归性反向运动的界，以及测量边界处的收缩——下进行严格证明。分析中发展出将偏离局域置换算符者视为缺陷的微观输运图景，并与量子行走（quantum walks）理论建立联系。

Result: 在上述假设下证明了多项式规模的浴足以形成近似 $k$-设计，即确立了有限浴尺寸下收敛速率的严格界。

Conclusion: 研究给出了投影系综涌现随机性的微观输运解释，并为有限浴尺寸下近似设计的形成提供了严格刻画，同时与量子行走理论建立了联系。

Abstract: We study the emergent randomness in projected ensembles generated by dual-unitary circuits. Specifically, we consider evolution by solvable dual-unitary brickwork circuits followed by measurements on part of the system, which induce an ensemble of states on the unmeasured subsystem. Previous work has established that this projected ensemble of states forms a quantum state design in the limit where the bath size is large, but the rate of convergence at finite bath size has not been rigorously established. Under three inverse-polynomial assumptions, local permutation mixing, a bound on recurrent backward motion, and contraction at the measured boundary, we fill this gap and prove that a bath of polynomial size suffices to form approximate $k$-designs. In establishing our results, we develop a microscopic transport interpretation of emergent randomness in the projected ensemble, in which deviations from local permutation operators appear as defects, and make connections with the theory of quantum walks.

</details>


### [194] [The Grothendieck Game and Self-Testing Continuous Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06755)
*Alexander Kulpe,Giulio Malavolta,Simon Schmidt,Michael Walter*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作建立了首个针对 Majorana 算符（Majorana operators）生成的完整连续测量族的鲁棒自检验（robust self-test），其核心工具是 Grothendieck 博弈（Grothendieck game）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自检验（self-testing）能够仅凭观测到的关联（correlations）认证量子态与测量，而无需对产生这些关联的器件作任何描述。论文旨在将这种器件无关（device-independent）认证拓展至连续参数化的测量族，此前尚缺此类结果。

Method: 利用 Grothendieck 博弈——一种双人非局域博弈（two-player nonlocal game），参与者接收球面上的方向并返回二元答案——证明该博弈构成 Majorana 可观测量（Majorana observables）的自检验；并将认证进一步推广至 Majorana 可观测量的乘积。

Result: 任何接近最优（near-optimal）的博弈表现均可认证整个连续参数化的量子测量族；对 Majorana 可观测量乘积的推广则给出连续 pin 群（continuous pin group）自旋表示（spin representation）的鲁棒自检验。

Conclusion: 该研究将鲁棒自检验从离散或有限情形扩展至连续族，为 Majorana 算符测量及 pin 群自旋表示提供了器件无关的认证方案。

Abstract: Self-testing certifies quantum states and measurements from observed correlations alone, without requiring a description of the devices that produce them. In this work we establish, to the best of our knowledge, the first robust self-test of the full continuous family of measurements generated by Majorana operators. We achieve this by showing that the Grothendieck game, a two-player nonlocal game in which the players receive directions on a sphere and return binary answers, is a self-test of Majorana observables. That is, any near-optimal performance certifies an entire continuously parameterized family of quantum measurements. We further extend this certification to products of Majorana observables, obtaining a robust self-test of the spin representation of the continuous pin group.

</details>


### [195] [Hypercontractivity and entropy decay for infinite-dimensional quantum Gibbs samplers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06764)
*Simon Becker,Cambyse Rouzé,Robert Salzmann*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了一大类无穷维量子吉布斯采样器（quantum Gibbs samplers）的指数相对熵（relative entropy）衰减速率，核心工具是超压缩性估计（hypercontractive estimates）与对数Sobolev不等式（logarithmic Sobolev inequalities）的微扰稳定性理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子吉布斯采样器向平衡态收敛的速率（即相对熵的指数衰减）是量子热化与采样复杂度的核心问题，但已有结果多局限于有限维或特定模型，对无穷维多模系统缺乏统一且具有普适性的衰减速率分析，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 技术核心是建立超压缩性估计与对数Sobolev不等式的微扰稳定性理论（perturbative stability theory）；在此基础上，针对适当选取的耗散耦合（dissipative couplings）分析无穷维系统的熵衰减行为，并对低能截断相互作用采用微扰论证。

Result: 对具有任意跃迁（hopping）与排斥密度相互作用（repulsive density interactions）的多模Bose-Hubbard采样器，证明了指数熵衰减；对谐振束缚的量子库仑气体（quantum Coulomb gases）与分子系统，证明了对相互作用每个固定有限秩低能截断（finite-rank low-energy truncation）的指数熵衰减。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在合适耗散耦合下，广泛的一类无穷维量子吉布斯采样器均具有指数熵衰减；同时通过一个具有正谱隙（positive spectral gap）却不存在任何p>2的有限时间L²到L^p界（L²-to-L^p bound）的周期充电模型，说明谱隙本身不足以保证此类超压缩型估计。

Abstract: We derive rates of exponential relative-entropy decay for a broad class of infinite-dimensional quantum Gibbs samplers. A key technical ingredient is a perturbative stability theory for hypercontractive estimates and logarithmic Sobolev inequalities. For suitably chosen dissipative couplings, we obtain exponential entropy decay for full multimode Bose-Hubbard samplers with arbitrary hopping and repulsive density interactions. For harmonically confined quantum Coulomb gases and molecular systems, we prove exponential entropy decay for every fixed finite-rank low-energy truncation of the interaction. Finally, we exhibit a periodic charging model with a positive spectral gap but no finite-time $L^2$-to-$L^p$ bound for any $p>2$.

</details>


### [196] [Fermions and the Tsirelson Problem in Real Quantum Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06767)
*Timothee Hoffreumon,Mischa P. Woods*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明实数量子理论（real quantum theory, RQT）与普通量子理论（ordinary quantum theory, QT）的不可区分性在两处推广下依然成立：一是允许分离测量由可交换可观测量（commuting observables）表示，二是引入超选择规则（superselection rules）。作者首先在 RQT 中证明 Tsirelson 问题的肯定答案，随后给出超选择理论的局部实数量子表述，并给出与费米子信息论（fermionic information theory）实验不可区分的费米子版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前期工作已表明 RQT 无法与 QT 在实验上区分，但该结论是否在更一般的物理设定下稳健尚不明确。本文因此考察两类自然推广——以可交换可观测量替代张量积可观测量，以及引入超选择规则——是否仍保持这种不可区分性。

Method: 先在 RQT 框架内研究可交换测量与张量积测量所生成关联的关系，回答 Tsirelson 问题；指出标准的复数证明无法直接迁移到实数情形，并将此证明障碍与超选择规则的存在联系起来。随后将 RQT 框架推广至超选择实数 RQT，构造超选择理论的局部实数量子表述。

Result: 第一项结果为 RQT 中 Tsirelson 问题的肯定答案：可交换测量与张量积测量生成相同的有限维关联（finite-dimensional correlations）。第二项结果为超选择理论的局部实数量子表述，该表述涵盖费米子版本，其预测与费米子信息论在实验上不可区分，并可推广至所有超选择量子理论。

Conclusion: RQT 与 QT 的不可区分性在两种自然推广下均得以保持：无论是在可交换测量设定中，还是在超选择（含费米子）设定中。作者由此给出统一的局部实数表述，将结果推广至所有超选择量子理论。

Abstract: In a previous work, we showed that real quantum theory (RQT) cannot be experimentally distinguished from ordinary quantum theory (QT). In this paper, we investigate whether this indistinguishability survives two natural extensions of the setting: allowing separated measurements to be represented by commuting rather than tensor-product observables, and imposing superselection rules, as in fermionic information theory. We show that it does in both cases.
  Our first result is a positive answer to the Tsirelson problem in RQT: commuting and tensor-product measurements generate the same finite-dimensional correlations. This is surprisingly not a trivial consequence of the standard complex proof, as it does not carry over to the reals. The proof obstruction becomes particularly relevant in the presence of superselection rules, which is why we first establish the equivalence in RQT before extending the framework to superselected RQT.
  Our second result is a local, real quantum formulation of superselected theories. We extend the RQT framework to accommodate a representation of superselection over the reals and show, in particular, that it admits a fermionic version whose predictions are experimentally indistinguishable from those of fermionic information theory. More generally, the construction extends to all superselected QTs.

</details>


### [197] [Polynomial-time classical algorithms for mean-field models up to the glass transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06807)
*Alexander Schmidhuber,Alexander Zlokapa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明经典算法可在多项式时间内估计Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev（SYK）模型在所有恒定温度下的局域热期望值，并发展出严格量子空腔方法（quantum cavity method），同时解决了经典自旋玻璃（spin glass）在相变前的热期望计算问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SYK模型是强相互作用费米子系统，其本质高度量子化：高斯态远离热态，且表示热态需大量子电路。近期虽有工作证明经典算法可在足够高温下以拟多项式时间估计局域热期望，但更低温度（恒定温度）下的经典可计算性仍是空白，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 核心技术是建立一套完全严格的量子空腔方法（quantum cavity method）。该方法借鉴经典空腔方法在优化、采样、推断与学习中的成功经验，并可直接推广至经典系统。

Result: 经典算法能在多项式时间内估计SYK模型在所有恒定温度下的局域可观测量；该技术亦解决经典自旋玻璃在直至相变点处计算热期望的开放问题；作为应用实例，给出可从任意恒定温度吉布斯态（Gibbs state）学习SYK哈密顿量的量子算法，具有多项式时间与样本复杂度。

Conclusion: 论文确立了SYK模型在恒定温度下的经典可计算性，并提出了具独立价值的量子空腔方法，预期将在优化、采样、推断与学习等领域获得进一步应用。

Abstract: The Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev model is a strongly interacting fermionic system that has been well-studied in condensed matter and high energy physics. It is highly quantum: Gaussian states are far from the thermal state (Hastings and O'Donnell, STOC'22) and representing the thermal state requires large polynomial-size quantum circuits (Anschuetz et al., QIP'25). Very recently, it was nonetheless proven that classical algorithms can estimate local thermal expectations at sufficiently high temperature in quasipolynomial time (Zlokapa, FOCS'26). We show that classical algorithms can in fact estimate local observables at all constant temperatures in polynomial time. Our techniques also extend straightforwardly to classical systems: we resolve an open question about computing thermal expectations of a classical spin glass up to its phase transition (Bencs et al., STOC'26). Our proof develops a fully rigorous quantum cavity method. Due to the success of the classical cavity method in optimization, sampling, inference and learning, we expect the quantum cavity method to find further applications of independent interest. As an example, we give a quantum algorithm that learns SYK Hamiltonians from the Gibbs state at any constant temperature with polynomial time and sample complexity.

</details>


### [198] [Finding Gaussian Structure in Bosonic States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06810)
*Alvan Arulandu,Sitan Chen,Ziyun Chen,Jerry Li,Eric Ma*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究纯玻色高斯态（pure bosonic Gaussian state）的不可知层析（agnostic tomography），给出在高保真与低保真两种情形下均高效的高斯态学习协议，并证明获得强不可知保证必须依赖非高斯测量（non-Gaussian measurements）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是在仅给定任意 n 模玻色态 ρ 的副本时，输出一个纯高斯态，使其与 ρ 的不保真度（infidelity）不超过 opt + ε，其中 opt 为任意纯高斯态可达的最小不保真度；作者希望在高保真与低保真两类区域都取得高效协议，并理解非高斯测量在此问题中的必要性。

Method: 协议共享同一范式：先迭代地结合一般高斯测量（general Gaussian measurements）与经典稳健统计（classical robust statistics）技术获得良好的热启动（warm start）估计，再利用非高斯测量，通过凸与非凸优化方法对该估计进行精化。

Result: 当 opt 低于某普适常数时，协议运行时间与副本复杂度（copy complexity）对 n、1/ε 及 log log E 强多项式（strongly polynomial），其中 E 为最接近的纯高斯态的能量；对任意 opt，协议使用 (n+1)^{poly(1/ε)} poly(1+log log E) 个副本与相同时长运行时间。由此首次得到真正容忍的高斯性检验（Gaussianity testing）协议，可区分 opt > c + ε 与 opt < c − ε（c ∈ (0,1) 任意阈值）。同时证明除非 NP ⊆ BQP，否则 poly(n, 1/ε) 的运行时间不可能实现。

Conclusion: 非高斯测量被证明是达到所获强不可知保证的必要条件，且这些保证可证明地优于经典高斯（classical Gaussians）稳健估计所能达到的水平；此外，多项式时间算法的不可行性由 NP ⊆ BQP 这一假设性结论所支撑。

Abstract: We study agnostic tomography of pure bosonic Gaussian states: given copies of an arbitrary $n$-mode bosonic state $ρ$, the goal is to output a pure Gaussian state whose infidelity with $ρ$ is at most $\mathrm{opt} + ε$, where $\mathrm{opt}$ is the minimum infidelity achievable by any pure Gaussian state.
  We give efficient protocols achieving this in both the high and low fidelity regimes. When $\mathrm{opt}$ is below some universal constant, our protocol has runtime and copy complexity which is strongly polynomial in $n, 1/ε$ and $\log \log E$, where $E$ is the energy of the closest pure Gaussian state. For arbitrary $\mathrm{opt}$, our protocol uses $(n+1)^{\mathrm{poly}(1/ε)} \mathrm{poly}\left(1+\log\log(E)\right)$ copies and runtime. As a corollary, we obtain the first truly tolerant Gaussianity testing protocol for distinguishing whether $\mathrm{opt} > c + ε$ or $\mathrm{opt} < c - ε$, for any threshold $c\in(0,1)$. We also prove $\mathrm{poly}(n,1/ε)$ runtime is impossible, unless $\mathrm{NP}\subseteq\mathrm{BQP}$.
  Our protocols follow a shared paradigm: first, we iteratively use general Gaussian measurements combined with techniques from classical robust statistics to obtain a good warm start estimate, then we leverage non-Gaussian measurements to refine this warm start using convex and non-convex optimization methods. Interestingly, we prove that non-Gaussian measurements are necessary to match the strong agnostic guarantees we obtain, and in fact these guarantees are provably superior to what is possible for robustly estimating classical Gaussians.

</details>


### [199] [Energy-constrained two-way capacities of the pure-loss bosonic channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06832)
*Stefano Pirandola*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文确定了纯损耗玻色信道（pure-loss bosonic channel）在无条件平均发射光子数约束下的双向量子、纠缠分发、私密与密钥容量，四者一致并由热模态熵（entropy of a thermal mode）表示为 g(N)−g((1−η)N)，从而解决了长期存在的有限能量容量问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯损耗玻色信道在有限能量条件下的容量问题长期未决，2009 年提出的反向相干信息速率（reverse-coherent-information rate）虽被猜想最优，但其最优性缺乏证明。该研究旨在建立有限能量约束下的统一容量框架。

Method: 核心工具为扇区隐形传态模拟（sector teleportation simulation），其作用是将输入能量约束转移至共享纠缠资源，从而在一般自适应协议（adaptive protocols）的逆向论证中始终保持该约束。方法覆盖无条件平均发射光子数约束，并分析并行协议（parallel protocols）下的硬总光子数截断情形及强逆阈值（strong-converse thresholds）。

Result: 对透射率 η 与平均光子数 N，四类容量重合且等于 g(N)−g((1−η)N)。对任意 N>0，相应强逆阈值与无约束值 −log₂(1−η) 一致；在硬总光子数截断的并行协议下，同一有限能量容量公式满足指数强逆（exponential strong converse）。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了自适应玻色通信（adaptive bosonic communication）的统有限能量框架，并揭示能量约束如何从根本上重塑可达速率与逆向界（converse bounds）。

Abstract: We determine the two-way quantum, entanglement-distribution, private, and secret-key capacities of the pure-loss bosonic channel under an unconditional mean transmitted-photon-number constraint. For transmissivity $η$ and mean photon number $N$, all four capacities coincide and are given by $g(N)-g((1-η)N)$, where $g$ is the entropy of a thermal mode. This resolves the longstanding finite-energy capacity problem by establishing the optimality of the reverse-coherent-information rate introduced in 2009. Our central tool is sector teleportation simulation, which we develop specifically to transfer the input-energy constraint to the shared entanglement resource, thereby preserving the constraint throughout converse arguments for general adaptive protocols. For every $N>0$, we further show that the corresponding strong-converse thresholds coincide with the unconstrained value $-\log_2(1-η)$. For parallel protocols with a hard total-photon-number cutoff, the same finite-energy capacity formula instead satisfies an exponential strong converse. Together, these results establish a unified finite-energy framework for adaptive bosonic communication and reveal how energy constraints fundamentally reshape both attainable rates and converse bounds.

</details>


### [200] [Quantum information processing under maximal distance measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06842)
*Bartosz Regula,Ludovico Lami,Mario Berta,Marco Tomamichel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究将量子信息中的误差度量（error metric）由经典距离的最小扩展改为最大扩展后，各类量子信息处理任务受到的影响。结果表明：信道编码（channel coding）与量子资源蒸馏（quantum resource distillation）不受影响，而信道模拟（channel simulation）与量子资源稀释（quantum resource dilution）的速率改由 Belavkin-Staszewski 相对熵（Belavkin-Staszewski relative entropy）刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信息中常用的迹距离（trace distance）或基于保真度的纯化距离（fidelity-based purified distance）是经典距离度量的最小扩展，但此类扩展并不唯一，存在一整个谱系的其他扩展。作者旨在考察当误差度量改为经典距离的最大扩展（即施加更严格的误差要求）时，量子信息处理任务的表现如何变化。

Method: 摘要未给出具体技术细节，仅说明研究策略是对比分析：将误差度量替换为经典距离度量的最大扩展，并在这一更严格的误差要求下，考察多种量子信息处理任务的性能（如速率）是否发生变化。

Result: 部分任务的性能完全不受影响，包括信道编码（channel coding）与量子资源蒸馏（quantum resource distillation）；另一些任务如信道模拟（channel simulation）与量子资源稀释（quantum resource dilution）则发生变化，其速率不再由标准的 Umegaki 量子相对熵（Umegaki quantum relative entropy）函数给出，而由基于 Belavkin-Staszewski 相对熵的函数给出。

Conclusion: 该结果将 Belavkin-Staszewski 相对熵与具体操作任务建立了联系，并揭示已知的可逆性结论——包括反向香农定理（reverse Shannon theorem）与量子资源变换的渐近可逆性（asymptotic reversibility）——在最大距离度量下不再成立。
