Abstract: The measures of distance frequently used in quantum information, such as trace distance or fidelity-based purified distance, are the smallest extensions of the corresponding distance measures in classical information theory. However, this way of extending classical distances is not unique, and a whole spectrum of other extensions can be defined. Here we study the question of how quantum information processing tasks are affected by a change of the error metrics to the maximal extensions of the classical distance measures, imposing more demanding error requirements on operational tasks. We find that the performance of some tasks, such as channel coding or quantum resource distillation, is not affected whatsoever by this change. However, for other tasks such as channel simulation and quantum resource dilution, we show that the rates are now given not by functions of the standard (Umegaki) quantum relative entropy, but ones based on the Belavkin-Staszewski relative entropy. Our results connect the Belavkin-Staszewski relative entropy with operational tasks and reveal that known reversibility results in quantum information, including the reverse Shannon theorem and the asymptotic reversibility of quantum resource transformations, no longer hold under maximal distance measures.

</details>


### [201] [Exact Temporal Criticalities of Entanglement and Negativity in Gaussian and Non-Gaussian Dissipative Kitaev Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03889)
*Shrey Chowdhary,Sanket Chirame,Stephan Haas,Rishabh Jha*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出"时间临界性（temporal criticality）"概念：当纠缠谱（entanglement spectrum）或负度谱（negativity spectrum）的最大两个本征值相遇时，相应纠缠/负度哈密顿量的能隙关闭而熵保持平滑。研究在开放费米子系统中此类临界性出现的条件、次序及其与几何退相干时间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者关注开放费米子系统中纠缠谱的能隙关闭现象及其时间次序问题，特别是临界性是否先于负度开始下降所对应的几何退相干时间（geometric decoherence time）。同时希望厘清高斯性（Gaussianity）与对称性（对称性）在决定临界性判据中的相对作用，并比较弱对称（weak symmetry）的损耗/增益与强对称（strong symmetry）的退相位（dephasing）两类动力学。

Method: 对比弱对称（仅在平均意义上遵守费米子宇称、保持态高斯的损耗与增益）与强对称（每次跳变均遵守宇称、使态非高斯的密度退相位）两类动力学。在高斯态中推导纠缠能隙与负度能隙关闭的判据，分析均匀平衡损耗/增益、开放 Kitaev 链中的弱非平衡损耗/增益，并对键项对易点处的非高斯动力学进行精确求解。

Result: 决定临界性判据的是高斯性而非对称性。高斯态下，子系统的纠缠能隙恰在其两个宇称扇区（parity sectors）等权时关闭；负度能隙恰在子系统或其补集出现该情形时关闭，故负度临界性从不晚于纠缠临界性。均匀平衡损耗与增益仅压缩两宇称扇区，对任意二次哈密顿量在任何速率下均保留两种临界性。开放 Kitaev 链中，弱非平衡损耗/增益使临界性按切割处占据数发生偏移；强速率下仅当奇数个相邻格点越过半填充时，临界性才可在弛豫时间附近关闭。弱退相位推迟两种临界性并在远离链端时使其提前于宇称平衡；退相位速率趋近临界阻尼时两种临界性退至无穷远时间；强退相位下负度在任一能隙开始收缩前很久即已开始下降。

Conclusion: 高斯性是确定纠缠与负度谱时间临界性判据的关键因素，而非对称性类型。两种临界性存在固定次序：负度临界性不晚于纠缠临界性。损耗/增益与退相位对临界性的时间和位置影响显著不同，且强退相位可使负度下降远离能隙关闭的时间尺度。

Abstract: When the two largest eigenvalues of an entanglement or negativity spectrum meet, the gap of the corresponding entanglement or negativity Hamiltonian closes while entropies stay smooth, an event we call a temporal criticality. We ask when such criticalities occur in open fermionic systems, in which order, and whether they precede the geometric decoherence time, at which the negativity starts to fall. We compare loss and gain, which respect fermion parity only on average (weak symmetry) and keep the state Gaussian, with density dephasing, which respects it in every jump (strong symmetry) and makes the state non-Gaussian. We find that Gaussianity, rather than the symmetry, fixes the criterion for a criticality. In a Gaussian state the entanglement gap of a subsystem closes exactly when its two parity sectors carry equal weight, and the negativity gap closes exactly when this happens for the subsystem or its complement, so the negativity criticality never comes after the entanglement one. Uniform balanced loss and gain only shrink the two parities and leave both criticalities in place at every rate, for any quadratic Hamiltonian. In an open Kitaev chain, weak imbalanced loss and gain shift the criticalities by amounts set by the occupations at the cut, and at strong rates they allow a closing near the relaxation time only when an odd number of neighboring sites pass half filling. For dephasing we solve the non-Gaussian dynamics exactly at the point where the bond terms of the chain commute. Weak dephasing delays both criticalities and, away from the chain ends, moves them ahead of the parity balance, where the parity rules of Gaussian states would place them. As the dephasing rate approaches critical damping, both criticalities recede to infinite time, and at strong dephasing the negativity starts to fall long before either gap begins to shrink.

</details>


### [202] [Efficient Quantum Monte Carlo through Cluster Expansions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03893)
*Jorge Sánchez-Segovia,Álvaro M. Alhambra*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种在“连接簇（connected clusters）”空间直接进行马尔可夫链采样的算法，用蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）采样替代簇展开（cluster expansion）中代价高昂的确定性枚举，从而在收敛区间内规避符号问题（sign problem），并将长程系统原本的准多项式时间降至多项式级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号问题（sign problem）是蒙特卡洛方法推广到一般量子多体系统的主要障碍，而簇展开（cluster expansion）虽能在收敛区间内给出自由能等的收敛表示及确定性多项式算法，却需穷举连接簇，产生 $\left(N\varepsilon^{-1}\right)^{\mathcal{O}(\log\Delta)}$ 的计算开销。该开销在长程系统（$\Delta\equiv N$）中退化为准多项式运行时间，构成亟待解决的瓶颈。

Method: 提出一种马尔可夫链（Markov chain）算法，直接在连接簇空间中采样簇展开，以蒙特卡洛采样取代确定性枚举。由于采样发生在抽象的簇空间而非物理构型空间，传统指数级符号问题在收敛区间内不再出现。方法针对一般聚合物模型（polymer models）及量子配分函数（quantum partition functions）给出分析。

Result: 对一般聚合物模型，建立了运行时间为 $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(N^4\varepsilon^{-3})$ 的 FPRAS（完全多项式随机近似方案）。对温度满足 $\beta\leq\beta_{\mathrm{sampling}}\equiv\mathcal{O}(1)$ 的量子配分函数，运行时间改进至 $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(N^{3+1/D}\varepsilon^{-3})$，其中 $D$ 为晶格维度。该框架可直接应用于相互作用为 $\propto 1/r^\alpha$ 的长程量子系统，包括 $\alpha>2D$ 的 $2$-局域（$2$-local）模型。

Conclusion: 通过在抽象簇空间采样替代确定性枚举，该算法在簇展开收敛区间内消除了传统符号问题，并为长程量子系统提供了多项式时间的近似方案，扩展了蒙特卡洛方法在多体系统中的应用范围。

Abstract: The sign problem remains a major obstacle to extending the success of Monte Carlo methods to generic quantum many-body systems. A powerful alternative relies on cluster expansions, which provide convergent representations of free energies and related quantities, with applications ranging from tensor network contractions to quantum dynamics and circuits. Within their convergence regime, these expansions often yield deterministic polynomial-time algorithms. However, such methods require exhaustive enumeration of connected clusters, leading to a computational overhead of $(N\varepsilon^{-1})^{\mathcal{O}(\logΔ)}$, where $N$ is the system size, $\varepsilon$ the desired precision, and $Δ$ the interaction-graph degree. This becomes particularly severe in long-range systems, where $Δ\equiv N$, resulting in quasi-polynomial runtimes. Here, we introduce a Markov chain algorithm that samples cluster expansions directly in the space of connected clusters, replacing deterministic enumeration by Monte Carlo sampling. Because sampling occurs in an abstract cluster space rather than a physical configuration space, the conventional exponential sign problem is absent within the convergence regime. For general polymer models, we establish an FPRAS with runtime $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(N^4\varepsilon^{-3})$. For quantum partition functions at temperatures $β\leqβ_{\mathrm{sampling}}\equiv\mathcal{O}(1)$, this improves to $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(N^{3+1/D}\varepsilon^{-3})$, where $D$ is the lattice dimension. The framework directly applies to long-range quantum systems with interactions $\propto1/r^α$, including $2$-local models with $α>2D$.

</details>


### [203] [Magic Quantum Code Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03920)
*Kathleen Chang,Anasuya Lyons,Yuanjie Ren,Harald Putterman,Nathanan Tantivasadakarn,Victor V. Albert,Benjamin J. Brown,Dominic J. Williamson*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个通用框架，用于在量子低密度奇偶校验（quantum low-density parity check, QLDPC）码中容错地测量横向 Clifford 逻辑算符（transversal Clifford logical operators），并证明变形码仍保持 LDPC 性质、距离在常数因子内保持，且测量过程具有随原码距离线性增长的故障距离（fault distance）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用量子计算所需的非 Clifford 操作（non-Clifford operations）可通过测量逻辑 Clifford 算符实现，因此在 QLDPC 码中实现容错的横向 Clifford 逻辑测量具有关键意义，而现有方法缺乏此类通用框架。

Method: 协议对任意 QLDPC 码进行变形：借助额外的辅助量子比特（ancillary qubits）使稳定子（stabilizers）的乘积测量目标逻辑 Clifford 算符，从而在保持 Clifford 稳定化的变形码上完成容错测量。

Result: 论文证明变形的 Clifford 稳定化码仍为 LDPC 码，其距离在独立于物理量子比特数的常数因子内得到保持；并证明该 Clifford 测量对数据量子比特误差与测量误差的故障距离随原码距离线性增长。

Conclusion: 该方法被应用于在算法相关子程序中实现逻辑非 Clifford 门，以及在量子稳定子码（quantum stabilizer codes）中制备魔术态（magic states）。

Abstract: Non-Clifford operations required for universal quantum computation can be implemented by measuring logical Clifford operators. We present a generalized framework to fault-tolerantly measure transversal Clifford logical operators in quantum low-density parity check (QLDPC) codes. Our protocol takes any QLDPC code and deforms the code across additional ancillary qubits such that a product of stabilizers measures the desired logical Clifford operator. We prove that the deformed, Clifford-stabilized code remains LDPC and that its distance is preserved up to a constant factor independent of the number of physical qubits. We further prove that the Clifford measurement has a fault distance against data qubit and measurement errors that grows linearly with the distance of the original code. We apply our methods to implement logical non-Clifford gates in algorithmically relevant subroutines and to prepare magic states in quantum stabilizer codes.

</details>


### [204] [Energy-filtered readout preserves gap-enhanced sensing in interacting quantum dots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04063)
*Shirwan Abdullah*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作以弱耦合稳态二能级模型研究连续读出下量子点电荷探测的激发间隙增强效应，指出其存活程度取决于探测器的能量结构而非仅噪声大小。量子点接触在高偏压强测量时会激发探针，而窄能级传感量子点可实现能量过滤读出，保留量子Fisher信息的平方反比间隙依赖，并使相互作用双电子获得约三倍于单电子的信息率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小激发间隙（excitation gap）使量子点成为探测弱电场的灵敏探针，但用于读出的电荷探测器（charge detector）本身也可能激发探针，从而削弱间隙带来的增强效应。论文旨在追问在连续读出（continuous readout）条件下，这种间隙增强有多少能够保留。

Method: 采用弱耦合、稳态的二能级模型（weak-coupling, stationary two-level model），研究门定义量子点（gate-defined quantum dot）中两个相互作用电子经由受限驱动交叉（confinement-driven crossover）的调控过程，并比较量子点接触与窄能级传感量子点两类探测器的读出性能。

Result: 量子点接触偏压越高测量越强，但一旦偏压能量超过间隙即会激发探针；当反作用（back-action）主导探针自身弛豫时，优化平均电流信息率仅随间隙倒数增长。窄能级传感量子点在低于间隙的偏压下达到满测量强度，对非相互作用探测器且在耦合二阶近似下零温时无法激发探针，其量子Fisher信息（quantum Fisher information）保持间隙平方反比依赖，下限由探测器能级宽度与电子温度设定；相互作用电子对的信息率约为同间隙、同探测器下单电子的三倍，而量子Fisher信息中该比值为1.6–1.7，接触型与库仑型相互作用均如此。

Conclusion: 两种探测器不同的间隙依赖关系及各自最优偏压，可通过在适当调谐的电荷感测双量子点（charge-sensed double dots）上进行偏压扫描与栅极扫描加以检验。

Abstract: A small excitation gap makes a quantum dot a sensitive probe of a weak electric field, but the charge detector that reads the probe can also excite it. We ask how much of this gap enhancement survives continuous readout, for two interacting electrons tuned through a confinement-driven crossover in a gate-defined quantum dot, using a weak-coupling, stationary two-level model. The answer depends on the energy structure of the detector, not only on its noise. A quantum point contact measures more strongly at higher bias, but once the bias energy exceeds the gap it also excites the probe. When this back-action dominates the probe's own relaxation, the optimised mean-current information rate grows only as the inverse of the gap. A sensor quantum dot with a narrow level instead reaches full measurement strength at a bias below the gap, where, for a non-interacting detector and to second order in the coupling, it cannot excite the probe at zero temperature. This energy-filtered readout keeps the inverse-square gap dependence of the quantum Fisher information, down to a gap set by the detector's level width and the electron temperature. Through such a detector the interacting pair gives about three times the information rate of a single electron with the same gap and detector, compared with 1.6-1.7 in the quantum Fisher information, for both contact and Coulomb-type interactions. These different gap dependences, and the different optimal biases of the two detectors, can be tested by bias and gate sweeps on suitably tuned charge-sensed double dots.

</details>


### [205] [How to distinguish chaos from integrability using OTOC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04197)
*Alexey Milekhin,Anders Wallberg*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文解析研究了算符纯度（operator purity）的晚期饱和值，发现其随系统尺寸的标度行为可稳健区分混沌与可积多体系统，方法基于Mazur界（Mazur bound），并用一维自旋链数值模拟加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乱序关联函数（out-of-time-ordered correlators, OTOCs）衰减至小残余值这一行为在混沌与相互作用可积多体系统中均普遍出现，故其本身无法区分二者，需要寻找新的可区分观测量。

Method: 以Mazur界（Mazur bound）为理论框架，解析研究算符纯度（operator purity）的晚期饱和值及其随系统尺寸的标度估计，并明确可积守恒荷（integrable charges） locality 的作用。

Result: 算符纯度的标度行为随系统尺寸变化，能够稳健地区分混沌系统与可积系统；该结论通过一维自旋链（one-dimensional spin chains）的数值模拟得到支持。

Conclusion: 算符纯度的晚期饱和标度可作为区分混沌与可积多体系统的判据，Mazur界为其提供了具体的标度估计，并凸显了可积守恒荷局域性的重要性。

Abstract: The decay of out-of-time-ordered correlators (OTOCs) to a small residual value signals the scrambling of quantum information. This behavior is generic: it occurs in both chaotic and interacting integrable many-body systems, and therefore cannot by itself distinguish between them. In this paper we analytically study the late-time saturation value of a closely related observable, operator purity, and show that its scaling with system size robustly distinguishes chaotic from integrable systems. Our approach is based on the Mazur bound, which provides concrete scaling estimates and makes explicit why the locality of integrable charges matters. We support our findings with numerical simulations of one-dimensional spin chains.

</details>


### [206] [Sidewall spacer passivated epitaxial NbN/AlN/NbN Josephson junctions for superconducting qubit applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04820)
*Koki Honda,Daiki Kurihara,Hiroki Kutsuma,Hirotaka Terai,Taro Yamashita*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出侧壁间隔层钝化（sidewall spacer passivation）的外延 NbN/AlN/NbN 约瑟夫森结（Josephson junction）制备工艺，降低非晶介质体积与工艺复杂度，实现高能隙电压、低亚能隙漏电流及宽临界电流密度范围的器件，并验证湿法刻蚀未损伤电学特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外延 NbN/AlN/NbN 结因其晶态隧道势垒与 NbN 高转变温度，有望替代传统 Al/AlO$_x$/Al 结用于超导量子比特；但其集成通常依赖含非晶层间介质（amorphous interlayer dielectric）的复杂工艺，可能劣化量子比特相干时间（coherence time）。

Method: 采用侧壁间隔层钝化的外延 NbN/AlN/NbN 结制备工艺，减少非晶介电材料体积并简化工艺流程；通过改变 AlN 隧道势垒厚度调控结特性，并对比缓冲氢氟酸（buffered hydrofluoric acid）处理前后的电流-电压特性。

Result: 制备的结展现出约 5.6 mV 的大能隙电压（gap voltage）与低亚能隙漏电流（subgap leakage current）；通过调节 AlN 势垒厚度，在 0.3 至 10$^4$ A/cm$^2$ 的宽临界电流密度（critical current density, ${\it J}_c$）范围内获得高质量结；湿法刻蚀前后电学特性对比表明该工艺未造成退化。

Conclusion: 结果确立了外延约瑟夫森结的可规模化（scalable）制备工艺，并为基于氮化物（nitride）的高相干超导量子比特提供了有前景的平台。

Abstract: Epitaxial NbN/AlN/NbN Josephson junctions are promising alternatives to conventional Al/AlO$_x$/Al junctions for superconducting qubits due to their crystalline tunnel barriers and the high transition temperature of NbN. However, their integration into quantum circuits typically requires complex fabrication processes involving amorphous interlayer dielectrics, which can degrade qubit coherence times. To address these issues, we developed sidewall spacer passivated epitaxial NbN/AlN/NbN junctions using a fabrication process that reduces both the volume of amorphous dielectric material and fabrication complexity. The fabricated junctions exhibited a large gap voltage of approximately 5.6 mV and low subgap leakage currents. By varying the thickness of the AlN tunnel barrier, high-quality junctions were fabricated over a wide range of critical current densities ${\it J}_c$, from 0.3 to 10$^4$ A/cm$^2$. In addition, the comparison of the current-voltage characteristics before and after buffered hydrofluoric acid treatment indicated that the wet etching process did not degrade the electrical characteristics of the junctions. These results establish a scalable fabrication process for epitaxial Josephson junctions and provide a promising platform for high-coherence nitride-based superconducting qubits.

</details>


### [207] [Epitaxial NbN-junction-based self-shunted superconducting flux qubit with high anharmonicity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04821)
*Tokunoshin Uchida,Shingo Nozaki,Daiki Kurihara,Hiroki Kutsuma,Shotaro Shirai,Atsushi Noguchi,Hirotaka Terai,Taro Yamashita*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究基于全外延生长的 NbN/AlN/NbN 约瑟夫森结（Josephson junction），实现了自分流超导磁通量子比特（self-shunted superconducting flux qubit, SSFQ），其兼具超紧凑、强非谐性与长寿命特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电容分流（capacitively shunted, C-shunt）磁通量子比特需大尺寸分流电容，占用较大面积，不利于可扩展性。研究旨在利用大尺寸外延 NbN 基约瑟夫森结实现无需外加分流电容的紧凑量子比特。

Method: 采用全外延生长的 NbN/AlN/NbN 约瑟夫森结构建 SSFQ，并测量其非谐性、器件尺寸与寿命，与 NbN 基 C-shunt 磁通量子比特及 Al 基磁通量子比特进行对比。

Result: SSFQ 实现 797 MHz 的高非谐性，且占位面积相较带大分流电容的 C-shunt 磁通量子比特大幅减小。其寿命达 13–25 μs，与 NbN 基 C-shunt 磁通量子比特及结面积小 10 倍以上的 Al 基磁通量子比特相当或更高。

Conclusion: 大尺寸外延 NbN 基约瑟夫森结既适用于 SSFQ，也适用于具有较大结的 merged-element transmon，为可扩展量子比特及面向大规模超导量子计算机的材料平台提供了新选择。

Abstract: We report a demonstration of a self-shunted superconducting flux qubit (SSFQ) as an ultracompact, strongly anharmonic, and long-lifetime qubit by using the full epitaxially grown NbN/AlN/NbN Josephson junctions. The NbN-junction-based SSFQ achieved a high anharmonicity of 797 MHz as well as drastically reduced footprint of the qubit compared to capacitively shunted (C-shunt) flux qubits with a large shunt capacitor. The lifetime of the SSFQ reached 13 - 25 $μ$s, which is comparable to or relatively higher than that of NbN-based C-shunt flux qubits and Al-based flux qubits with more than 10 times smaller junctions. This indicates that the large epitaxial NbN-based junction can be adopted for SSFQ as well as merged-element transmon with the relatively large junctions. The results presented in this work will offer a new choice of the scalable qubits as well as the material platform toward the realization of large-scale superconducting quantum computers.

</details>


### [208] [Onset of a quantum Fisher-Selke sequence in the transverse-field ANNNI model and its bilayer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05057)
*Tuan-Vu Truong,Vu-Quoc-Minh Nguyen,Hoang-Long Nguyen,Thanh-Tien Nguyen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过微扰论（perturbation theory）与量子蒙特卡洛（quantum Monte Carlo）研究正方晶格及双层体系上的横场轴次近邻伊辛（ANNNI）模型，揭示量子涨落如何在经典多相交点处按 Fisher-Selke 序列逐级选出调制结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 ANNNI 模型在多点 $\\kappa=J_2/J_0=1/2$ 处存在高度简并的基态，横场引入的量子涨落（quantum fluctuations）如何解除该简并、并可能再现经典三维模型的 Fisher-Selke 序列，是本文的核心问题；同时考察双层结构对该序列的影响。

Method: 利用经典多点处界壁（domain wall）的刚性，对每个直界壁构型独立开展横场 $g=\\Gamma/J_0$ 的微扰展开至 $g^8$；对最宽的两个相采用随机级数展开量子蒙特卡洛（stochastic series expansion quantum Monte Carlo），并以热力学积分（thermodynamic integration）比较竞争有序态的自由能，以规避退火冻结问题。

Result: 量子涨落在 $g^2$ 阶选出 $\\langle 3\\rangle$ 相（因紧邻界壁的自旋从虚翻转中获得最多能量）；随后 $\\langle 23\\rangle$、$\\langle 223\\rangle$、$\\langle 2223\\rangle$ 分别在 $g^4$、$g^6$、$g^8$ 阶出现于 $\\langle 3\\rangle$ 与反相 $\\langle 2\\rangle$ 之间，次序与经典三维模型的 Fisher-Selke 序列一致。第二层会更强地压制各相继相，其作用超出对局域场的刚性增强。模拟复现 $\\langle 3\\rangle$ 楔形区约至 1% 精度；在预注册检验中 $\\langle 23\\rangle$ 于七个点中的六个稳定，第七个点由事后补充运行解析。

Conclusion: 横场量子涨落可在经典简并点按 Fisher-Selke 次序逐级解除简并，双层耦合对高阶相的抑制强于局域场刚性增强所能解释的程度；微扰论与量子蒙特卡洛结果在相图边界上定量一致，支持上述相序列的存在。

Abstract: We study the transverse-field axial next-nearest-neighbor Ising (ANNNI) model on the square lattice and on a bilayer. At the classical multiphase point $κ=J_2/J_0=1/2$, every configuration of straight domain walls that separates domains of at least two sites is a ground state. The walls are rigid, so perturbation theory in the transverse field $g=Γ/J_0$ can be carried out for each configuration separately, and we take it to order $g^8$. Quantum fluctuations select $\langle 3\rangle$ ($\uparrow\uparrow\uparrow\downarrow\downarrow\downarrow$) at order $g^2$, because spins next to a wall gain the most energy from virtual flips. The structures $\langle 23\rangle$, $\langle 223\rangle$ and $\langle 2223\rangle$ then open between $\langle 3\rangle$ and the antiphase $\langle 2\rangle$ at orders $g^4$, $g^6$ and $g^8$, in the order of the Fisher-Selke sequence of the classical three-dimensional model. A second layer suppresses each successive phase more strongly, beyond the stiffening of the local fields. We test the two widest phases with stochastic series expansion quantum Monte Carlo, comparing the free energies of the competing ordered states by thermodynamic integration, since annealing freezes in the modulation that forms at the ordering transition. The simulations reproduce the $\langle 3\rangle$ wedge to about 1%, and in a pre-registered test $\langle 23\rangle$ is stable at six of seven points, with the seventh resolved by post-hoc runs.

</details>


### [209] [Nonlinear quantum multi-spin dynamics without entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05521)
*Ivan P. Miranda,Erik Sjöqvist*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种变分乘积态（variational product-state）ansatz，将经典原子尺度自旋动力学（atomistic spin dynamics）扩展至任意自旋 $s$ 的量子区间，在排除位点间纠缠（intersite entanglement）的同时保留局域量子效应，并给出 $\mathcal{O}(N)$ 量级的可实现模拟方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多真实材料中位点间纠缠在极短时间尺度内预计仍可忽略，但局域量子效应不可忽略，因此需要一种既排除纠缠又保留局域非经典性的量子自旋动力学框架。该框架亦旨在厘清量子 Landau-Lifshitz 与 Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert 动力学之间不等价性的来源。

Method: 采用包含纯态与混合局域态的变分乘积态 ansatz，通过 $s\geq 1$ 密度算符（density operators）的非经典结构保留局域量子效应；非线性动力学源自局域态的均场（mean-field）相互依赖以及状态依赖的耗散项（state-dependent dissipative terms）。

Result: 该框架阐明了量子 Landau-Lifshitz 与 Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert 动力学不等价性的变分与几何起源，给出二者在共同时间重标度（time rescaling）下等价性的判据，并通过两个性质不同的极限（$s\to\infty$ 与大量子自旋数 $N$）建立与经典理论的联系，同时支持材料中量子自旋动力学的 $\mathcal{O}(N)$ 实际模拟。

Conclusion: 乘积态 ansatz 构成一条将经典原子尺度自旋动力学延伸至量子区间的独立路径，在可控计算代价下兼顾局域量子效应与经典极限的一致性，并为量子自旋动力学模拟提供实用方案。

Abstract: We introduce a distinct route for extending classical atomistic spin dynamics into the quantum regime for arbitrary spin $s$, based on a variational product-state \textit{ansatz} that includes both pure and mixed local states. By construction, intersite entanglement -- expected to remain negligible even on very short time scales in many real materials -- is excluded, while local quantum effects are retained through the nonclassical structure of $s\geq 1$ density operators, and nonlinear dynamics arise from both the mean-field interdependence of the local states and the state-dependent dissipative terms. This framework clarifies the variational and geometric origin of the inequivalence between quantum Landau-Lifshitz and Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert dynamics, provides a criterion for their equivalence under a common time rescaling, establishes connections with the classical theory through two qualitatively distinct limits ($s\to\infty$ and a large number of quantum spins $N$), and allows realistic $\mathcal{O}(N)$ simulations of quantum spin dynamics in materials.

</details>


### [210] [Momentum-Space Path Integral Approach to Non-Hermitian Symmetry Breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05702)
*Zhuo-Ting Cai,Wei Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文在动量空间构建非厄米对称性破缺的量子-经典对应，通过迹公式与量子化条件证明本征值的实/复性质由半经典轨道的对称性决定，并揭示轨道拓扑（可缩与非可缩）决定量子化条件的自然表述形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前基于坐标空间路径积分的量子-经典对应已建立，但缺乏动量空间的对偶表述；为完成该框架并拓展其适用性，需要构建动量空间的迹公式与量子化条件。

Method: 构造动量空间的迹公式（trace formula）与量子化条件（quantization condition），与坐标空间路径积分表述对照，并分析半经典轨道对称性及相位空间中可缩（contractible）与非可缩（noncontractible）轨道的拓扑差异。

Result: 单个本征值的实或复性质由相应半经典轨道的对称性决定；可缩周期轨道的坐标与动量表述等价，非可缩轨道沿绕转方向（winding direction）的量子化条件仍有效但需边界项修正；实空间绕转与布里渊区（Brillouin zone）绕转轨道分别自然选择坐标与动量空间量子化。在有限非厄米晶格中，穿越布里渊区的布洛赫振荡（Bloch oscillations）轨道保持相关对称性并给出实能级，而边界反射轨道形成对称相关对并产生复共轭本征值。

Conclusion: 该工作完善了非厄米对称性破缺的量子-经典对应框架，将其适用性拓展至更广泛的非厄米问题。

Abstract: A quantum-classical correspondence for non-Hermitian symmetry breaking has recently been established using coordinate-space path integrals, providing a semiclassical understanding of spectral transitions at the level of individual eigenstates. Here we develop its dual formulation in momentum space by constructing the corresponding trace formula and quantization condition. We show that the real or complex nature of individual eigenvalues is determined by the symmetry properties of the associated semiclassical orbits, as in the coordinate-space path-integral approach. Moreover, we demonstrate that the topology of semiclassical orbits determines the natural formulation of the quantization condition: the coordinate- and momentum-space formulations are equivalent for contractible periodic orbits in phase space, whereas for noncontractible orbits, the quantization condition along the winding direction remains valid, but its dual form must be corrected by a boundary term. In particular, real-space-winding and Brillouin-zone-winding orbits naturally select coordinate- and momentum-space quantization, respectively. As a nontrivial application, we investigate the boundary-induced spectral transition of Bloch oscillations in a finite non-Hermitian lattice, where Bloch-oscillation orbits winding across the Brillouin zone preserve the relevant symmetry and yield real energy levels, whereas boundary-reflected orbits form symmetry-related pairs and give rise to complex-conjugate eigenvalues. Our work completes the quantum-classical correspondence framework for non-Hermitian symmetry breaking, extending its applicability to a broader class of non-Hermitian problems.

</details>


### [211] [Fast mixing of the SYK model at high temperatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05953)
*Yiyi Cai,Yongtao Zhan,Alexander Zlokapa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明SYK模型在高常数温度下、以高概率存在具有与系统尺寸无关谱隙的量子吉布斯采样器，从而可在量子计算机上多项式时间制备SYK吉布斯态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SYK模型是全对全无序相互作用的强关联费米子系统，其动力学难以用Lieb-Robinson界限和集团展开分析。此前的非严格计算虽暗示SYK热化，但仅限于研究局域关联函数，作者希望给出更严格且更强的结果。

Method: 证明中引入了面向费米子的伪Lindblad方法（pseudo-Lindbladian approach），并采用一种基于与经典动力学（classical dynamics）比较的、特别简化的SYK分析。

Result: 在足够高的常数温度下，对无序以高概率成立，SYK模型具有谱隙（spectral gap）与系统尺寸无关的量子吉布斯采样器（quantum Gibbs sampler），并可在量子计算机上多项式时间（polynomial-time）制备SYK吉布斯态；且该结果在任意逆多项式迹距离（inverse polynomial trace distance）范围内成立。

Conclusion: 将伪Lindblad方法扩展至费米子系统并结合经典动力学比较，可克服SYK模型中Lieb-Robinson界限与集团展开的困难。相对于此前仅限局域关联函数的非严格热化证据，该结果在全态迹距离层面严格保证了SYK吉布斯态的高效量子制备。

Abstract: The Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev (SYK) model is a strongly interacting fermionic system. Its dynamics are difficult to analyze with Lieb-Robinson bounds and cluster expansions due to its all-to-all disordered interactions. We prove that, with high probability over the disorder, the SYK model admits a quantum Gibbs sampler with a system-size-independent spectral gap at sufficiently high constant temperatures. This yields polynomial-time preparation of SYK Gibbs states on a quantum computer. While prior non-rigorous computations that suggested SYK thermalization were limited to studying local correlators, we emphasize that our result holds up to arbitrary inverse polynomial trace distance. Our proof introduces the pseudo-Lindbladian approach to fermions and uses an especially simple SYK analysis based on a comparison to classical dynamics.

</details>


### [212] [Casimir force screening by a quantum Boltzmann plasma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06202)
*Petr Brandyshev,Maksim Koniushenko,Yury Budkov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文构建了有限温度场论框架，描述被双组分量子玻尔兹曼等离子体（quantum Boltzmann plasma）填充的完美导体边界间的卡西米尔相互作用（Casimir interaction），并揭示其量子修正与经典屏蔽图像的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于：在均一介电背景中嵌入可移动带电粒子的情形下，如何超越经典卡西米尔屏蔽（Casimir screening）图像，刻画有限热德布罗意波长（thermal de Broglie wavelength）带来的量子修正。

Method: 用量子粒子的虚时世界线（imaginary-time worldlines）表示电荷运动，电磁场采用高斯近似（Gaussian approximation），由此构造包含纵向与横向响应贡献以及无经典对应的量子诱导源项（quantum-induced source term）的配分函数（partition function）。

Result: 所得理论在经典极限下退化为通常的卡西米尔屏蔽图像：静态纵向模受德拜屏蔽（Debye screening），而横向电磁部分不受经典可动电荷影响。有限热德布罗意波长修正非零松原响应（Matsubara response），给出力的主导量子修正；静态长波极限下纵向介电函数的展开结构与简并电子气林哈德响应（Lindhard response）一致（对应关系 k_TF↔κ_D、k_F↔λ_dB^{-1}），静态横向响应则重现高温朗道轨道抗磁磁化率（Landau orbital diamagnetic susceptibility）。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了包含量子修正的有限温度卡西米尔相互作用场论描述，统一了经典屏蔽图像与量子响应，并揭示其与林哈德响应及朗道抗磁性的对应结构。

Abstract: We develop a finite-temperature field-theoretical description of the Casimir interaction between two perfectly conducting boundaries with the intervening gap filled by a two-component quantum Boltzmann plasma embedded in a homogeneous dielectric background. The quantum motion of the charged particles is represented by imaginary-time worldlines, while the electromagnetic field is treated in the Gaussian approximation. The resulting partition function contains distinct longitudinal and transverse response contributions and an additional quantum-induced source term with no classical counterpart. In the classical limit the theory reduces to the usual Casimir-screening picture: the static longitudinal mode is Debye screened, whereas the transverse electromagnetic sector is unaffected by classical mobile charges. Finite thermal de Broglie wavelengths modify the nonzero Matsubara response and produce the leading quantum correction to the force. In the static long-wavelength limit, the longitudinal dielectric function has, through the leading quantum correction, the same expansion structure as the Lindhard response of a degenerate electron gas under the correspondence $k_{TF}\leftrightarrowκ_D$ and $k_F\leftrightarrowλ_{dB}^{-1}$, while the static transverse response reproduces the leading high-temperature Landau orbital diamagnetic susceptibility.

</details>


### [213] [Interferometric braiding of non-Abelian anyons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06203)
*Francesco Debortoli,Fabian Grusdt*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一种基于量子杂质（quantum impurity）的方案，利用具有可寻址内禀自旋态的移动杂质，实现非阿贝尔任意子（non-Abelian anyon）的编织（braiding），并用以测量刻画任意子统计的 R 矩阵与 F 矩阵元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非阿贝尔任意子的操纵是拓扑量子计算（topological quantum computing）的基础，也是拓扑保护量子存储的前提，因此需要可行的实验协议来探测与利用其非阿贝尔统计特性。

Method: 采用量子杂质方案，利用具有可寻址内禀自旋态的移动杂质对非阿贝尔任意子进行编织（braiding）；重点考察 Ising 任意子与 Fibonacci 任意子两类情形，并通过所提出的 Ramsey 干涉仪（Ramsey interferometer）使任意子特性直接显现，从而测量 R 矩阵与 F 矩阵的矩阵元。

Result: 在该框架下，任意子的非阿贝尔本质可直接在所提出的 Ramsey 干涉仪中体现，并可实验测量表征非阿贝尔统计的 R、F 矩阵元；此外，作者提出一种常规—拓扑量子比特编码器（conventional-to-topological qubit encoder），可将量子信息从常规飞行量子比特（flying qubit）转移至拓扑保护的量子存储中。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了基于量子杂质的非阿贝尔任意子编织与统计测量的具体实验协议，并进一步给出将常规量子信息编码进拓扑保护量子存储的方案，为拓扑量子计算与量子存储的实现提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The manipulation of non-Abelian anyons is a foundational ingredient for topological quantum computing and underlies topologically protected quantum memories. Here we analyze a quantum-impurity-based protocol for braiding non-Abelian anyons. Exploiting mobile impurities with addressable internal spin states, we provide a concrete protocol to experimentally measure the matrix elements of the $R$ and $F$ matrices that characterize the non-Abelian statistics of anyons. In particular, focusing on the case of Ising and Fibonacci anyons, we illustrate how the anyonic nature of the excitations emerges directly in the Ramsey interferometer we propose. Building on this framework, we further introduce a conventional-to-topological qubit encoder to transfer quantum information from conventional flying qubits into a topologically protected quantum memory.

</details>


### [214] [Probing Quantum Anomalous Hall Transport Under Microwave Irradiation Using a Topological Circulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06245)
*Athul Ashok,Frank Jin,Nick Du,Luis A. Martinez,Jenny Zhou,Sean O'Kelley,Zachary J. -R. Espley,Gang Qiu,Kang L. Wang,Dong-Xia Qu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究利用量子反常霍尔（quantum anomalous Hall）拓扑环行器，通过泵浦-探测（pump-probe）微波测量研究了边缘磁等离激元（edge magnetoplasmons, EMPs）输运受到的光子诱导扰动，并借助非厄米（non-Hermitian）模式杂化在奇异点（exceptional point, EP）附近实现了增强的微波响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EMPs 可用于探测拓扑量子材料中的手性电荷动力学（chiral charge dynamics）与非互易微波输运（nonreciprocal microwave transport），但 EMP 传播的微小扰动量在常规微波散射测量中信号往往较弱，难以被检测。

Method: 采用量子反常霍尔拓扑环行器，结合泵浦-探测测量，考察微波辐照对 EMP 透射的扰动；并利用耦合 EMP-谐振器系统的非厄米模式杂化，在奇异点附近探测响应。

Result: 响应呈现强频率选择性：在若干泵浦频率下微波辐照显著改变 EMP 透射，而在特定频率（包括 4 GHz 与 7 GHz）透射几乎不变；在奇异点附近，泵浦引起的透射幅度变化约为远离奇异点时的两倍，表明扰动响应被增强。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了手性 EMP 输运、微波光子诱导扰动与非厄米动力学之间的相互作用，并表明拓扑环行器可作为增强微波光谱学（microspectroscopy）与传感（sensing）的潜在平台。

Abstract: Edge magnetoplasmons (EMPs) provide a platform for probing chiral charge dynamics and nonreciprocal microwave transport in topological quantum materials. However, detecting small perturbations to EMP propagation remains challenging because their signatures in conventional microwave scattering measurements can be weak. Here, we investigate microwave-photon-induced perturbations of EMP transport using a quantum anomalous Hall topological circulator. Pump--probe measurements reveal a strongly frequency-selective response: while microwave irradiation substantially modifies the EMP transmission at several pump frequencies, the transmission remains nearly unchanged at selected frequencies, including 4 and 7 GHz. We further exploit the non-Hermitian mode hybridization of the coupled EMP-resonator system to probe the response near an exceptional point (EP). The pump-induced change in transmission magnitude near the EP is approximately twice that observed away from the EP, demonstrating an enhanced microwave response to perturbations. These results reveal the interplay among chiral EMP transport, microwave-photon-induced perturbations, and non-Hermitian dynamics, and demonstrate the potential of topological circulators as a promising platform for enhanced microwave spectroscopy and sensing.

</details>


### [215] [Lindblad equation for the quantum Rabi model in the deep strong coupling regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06417)
*Daniel Holst,Patrick Potts,Peter Samuelsson*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在微扰深强耦合（deep strong coupling）区域，通过结合 secular 近似与 singular-coupling 近似，并在 Schrieffer-Wolff 变换后的参照系中，为开放量子 Rabi 模型推导出 Lindblad 形式的量子主方程，并与 Bloch-Redfield 方程进行基准对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究针对开放量子 Rabi 模型在深强耦合微扰区域的处理需求，该区域能谱接近简并且呈谐波特征，需要适用的主方程描述。

Method: 结合 secular 近似（secular approximation）与 singular-coupling 近似（singular-coupling approximation），在 Schrieffer-Wolff 变换（Schrieffer-Wolff transformed）参照系中推导出 Lindblad 形式（Lindblad form）的量子主方程（quantum master equation）。

Result: 将该方程与 Bloch-Redfield 方程进行基准对比，分析若干可观测量的时间演化与稳态性质，二者表现出极好的一致性；并在极限情形下获得了解析结果。

Conclusion: 所导出的 Lindblad 形式主方程在深强耦合微扰区与 Bloch-Redfield 描述高度吻合，且可在极限情形下给出解析结果，为该区域开放量子 Rabi 模型提供了有效描述。

Abstract: We derive a quantum master equation in Lindblad form for the open quantum Rabi model in the perturbative deep strong coupling regime, where the spectrum is close to degenerate and harmonic. The Lindblad form is obtained by combining secular and singular-coupling approximations, working in a Schrieffer-Wolff transformed frame. We benchmark our equation against a Bloch-Redfield one, analyzing the time-evolution and steady state properties of several observables and find excellent agreement. Analytical results are obtained in limiting cases.

</details>


### [216] [A Practical Introduction to VQE: Methods, Challenges, and Applications in Physics and Chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06448)
*Jannis Ehrlich,Felix Rupprecht,Konstantin Lamp,Elias Walter,Alejandro D. Somoza,Manuel Enns,Christian Elsässer,Birger Horstmann,Daniel F. Urban*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对变分量子本征求解器（Variational Quantum Eigensolver, VQE）进行结构化综述，梳理问题编码、拟设构建、经典优化与测量策略等算法组件，并讨论噪声、错误缓解与硬件约束等实际影响因素，为初学者提供入门指引。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VQE 已成为近期量子硬件上处理电子结构与材料科学问题的核心混合量子-经典方法，物理、化学与量子信息领域的研究者面临大量算法选择，亟需一个可访问的入门性梳理以支持知情决策。

Method: 采用结构化综述方式，系统梳理 VQE 的主要组件（问题编码、拟设构造、经典优化、测量策略），并结合部分早期实践经验，讨论噪声特性、错误缓解技术与硬件相关电路考量等实际因素。

Result: 归纳出对新手尤具相关性的关键要点，并总结实际工作流程中常见的挑战，辅以物理学与量子化学中的示例加以说明。

Conclusion: 旨在为采用 VQE 进行量子模拟的研究者提供易于进入的切入点，并在方法与底层硬件持续演进的背景下支持合理决策。

Abstract: The Variational Quantum Eigensolver (VQE) has become a central hybrid quantum-classical method for addressing electronic structure and materials science problems on near-term quantum hardware. As researchers across physics, chemistry, and quantum information engage with VQE, they encounter a wide range of algorithmic choices, including problem encoding, ansatz construction, classical optimization, and measurement strategies to choose from. This article provides a structured overview of these components and discusses practical factors that influence VQE performance in real settings, such as noise characteristics, available error-mitigation techniques, and hardware-dependent circuit considerations. Drawing partly on our early experiences, we highlight aspects that may be especially relevant for newcomers and outline common challenges observed in practical workflows. Our aim is to offer an accessible entry point for researchers approaching VQE-based quantum simulation and to support informed decision-making as the method and underlying hardware continue to evolve, which we support by discussion of examples from physics and quantum chemistry.

</details>


### [217] [Causal memory and structural complexity of a quantum space series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06519)
*Thomas J. Elliott*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作为量子空间序列（quantum space series）发展计算力学（computational mechanics）的全量子推广，证明在有限维、本原因果模型下存在等距生成的记忆最小模型，其规范矩阵乘积态表征同时确定最小记忆维度与最小记忆熵，并揭示纠缠熵（entanglement entropy）作为因果生成的不可约记忆的 operational 解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文追问“为何一个量子过程比另一个更复杂”，采用受经典复杂性科学启发的 operational 路径，以因果生成所需的记忆来刻画结构。其目标是为量子空间序列建立可量化的内在结构框架。

Method: 将计算力学扩展至全量子情形，研究对象为不与生成器返回交互的量子输出序列（quantum space series）。针对平稳、有记忆且允许有限维本原因果模型的量子空间序列，构造可等距生成输出的记忆最小模型，并给出规范的矩阵乘积态（matrix product state）表征。

Result: 该规范矩阵乘积态表征同时确定最小记忆维度与最小记忆熵；后者恰为过去-未来二分（past-future bipartition）上的纠缠熵，从而赋予纠缠以“因果生成的不可约记忆”的 operational 解释。在该类模型内，进一步证明基本熵率为零、不存在因果不对称性（causal asymmetry），且量子马尔可夫阶（quantum Markov order）为无穷。

Conclusion: 结果建立了一个量化内在量子结构的 operational 框架，揭示因果生成、复杂性、记忆、熵与纠缠之间的深层联系。

Abstract: What makes one quantum process more complex than another? We adopt an operational approach inspired by classical complexity science, which characterises structure through the memory required for causal generation. We develop a fully-quantum extension of computational mechanics for quantum space series: Sequences of quantum outputs that do not return to interact with their generator. For stationary, memoryful quantum space series admitting finite-dimensional, primitive causal models, we show that a memory-minimal model can be chosen to generate the outputs isometrically, without a discarded environment. The resulting canonical matrix product state representation determines both the minimum memory dimension and the minimum memory entropy. The latter is precisely the entanglement entropy across the past-future bipartition, giving entanglement an operational interpretation as irreducible memory for causal generation. Within this class, we further establish a vanishing fundamental entropy rate, the absence of causal asymmetry, and infinite quantum Markov order. These results establish an operational framework for quantifying intrinsic quantum structure, revealing deep connections between causal generation, complexity, memory, entropy, and entanglement.

</details>


### [218] [Climbing the Fusion Tree: Center Ordering and Decoding Hierarchy in Non-Abelian Mixed States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06692)
*Pablo Sala,Vlad Temkin,Cenke Xu,Daniel Podolsky,Ehud Altman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种操作性框架研究非阿贝尔对称群（non-Abelian symmetry group）G 的强-弱自发对称破缺（strong-to-weak spontaneous symmetry breaking, sw-SSB），将其表述为在强对称退相干下区分两个强对称态的译码任务，并揭示其与量子双重模型 D(G) 及统计力学层级模型的对偶关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有 sw-SSB 研究多集中于阿贝尔情形，非阿贝尔对称群缺乏系统性框架。本文旨在为研究非阿贝尔 sw-SSB 以及构造非阿贝尔拓扑码（non-Abelian topological codes）的译码器提供统一方法。

Method: 将 sw-SSB 形式化为译码任务：区分两个强对称态，二者相差一对插入的不可约表示（irreps），并均被强对称退相干破坏。对有限群 G，该任务对偶于纯电荷噪声下区分量子双重 D(G) 的逻辑态；由此导出描述最优译码的统计力学模型层级，其中测得的 irreps 作为淬火无序（quenched disorder）并满足类西森条件（Nishimori-like condition）。

Result: 由于局域 irreps 无法决定其全局融合方式，强 G×G 对称仅在非局域意义上实现，且对 Z(G) 平凡带电的 irreps 可隐藏在噪声产生的 irreps 之中；强对称自发破缺至 Z(G) 与弱对称之积，非零错误率下唯一相变为中心的序化，其阈值随层级升高不下降，对非幂零群（non-nilpotent groups）在任意有限层级成立。张量网络计算证实：S₃ 第一层级（中心平凡）无相变，所有 irreps 的保真度关联衰减至非零值；SU(2) 呈现与西森-伊辛普适类（Nishimori-Ising universality class）一致的中心 Z₂ 相变，自旋-1/2 irreps 的保真度关联变为长程，阈值从第一层级到第二层级上升 16%。

Conclusion: 结果提供了一个研究非阿贝尔 sw-SSB 的系统框架，并为构造非阿贝尔拓扑码的译码器奠定基础。

Abstract: We develop an operational approach to strong-to-weak spontaneous symmetry breaking (sw-SSB) of a non-Abelian symmetry group G. We formulate it as the decoding task of distinguishing two strongly symmetric states that differ by a pair of inserted irreps, after both are corrupted by strongly symmetric decoherence. For finite G, this task is dual to distinguishing logical states of the quantum double D(G) under pure-charge noise. We derive a hierarchy of statistical mechanics models describing optimal decoding from the fusion outcomes measured on successively larger nested regions. As for maximum-likelihood decoders of Abelian codes the measured irreps act as quenched disorder obeying a Nishimori-like condition. Strikingly, because local irreps do not determine how they fuse globally, the strong $G\times G$ symmetry is realized only nonlocally, and irreps trivially charged under Z(G) (the center of G) can hide inside noise-generated ones. As a result, the strong symmetry spontaneously breaks down to Z(G), times the weak symmetry. The only transition at nonzero error rate is then an ordering of the center, whose threshold cannot decrease as the hierarchy is ascended. This conclusion holds at any finite level of the hierarchy for non-nilpotent groups. We confirm these predictions with tensor-network calculations for $S_3$ and SU(2). At the first level, the $S_3$ model, whose center is trivial, shows no transition, and the fidelity correlations of all irreps provably decay to non-zero values. The SU(2) model exhibits a $Z_2$ transition consistent with the Nishimori-Ising universality class, where the fidelity correlations of spin-$1/2$ irreps become long-range. The threshold rises from the first to the second level of the hierarchy by $16\%$. Our results provide a systematic framework for studying non-Abelian sw-SSB and for constructing decoders for non Abelian topological codes.

</details>


### [219] [Engineering logical gates with irrep surface codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06775)
*Yabo Li,Zijian Song,Vieri Mattei,Naren Manjunath,Apoorv Tiwari,Tyler D. Ellison*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出“不可约表示曲面码（irrep surface codes, ISCs）”，一类可横向（transversally）实现指定逻辑门集合的二维拓扑量子纠错码。其设计仅要求目标门集生成有限群且不可约。作者同时给出格点手术（lattice surgery）协议，实现曲面码与 ISC 间信息转移，从而将该方案整合进常规计算流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算（fault-tolerant quantum computation）通常依赖固定的逻辑原语（logical primitives），实现其他期望操作往往需要大量资源。因此，需要一种能够针对指定逻辑门量身定制拓扑码的系统性方法，并使其可嵌入现有计算架构。

Method: 构造 ISC 这类二维拓扑量子纠错码（topological quantum error-correcting code），使其横向实现给定逻辑门集。所设计门集需满足两个条件：(i) 生成有限群（由 Eastin-Knill 定理在全局相位意义下强制）；(ii) 不可约，即与该门集对易的算符必正比于恒等算符。此外发展格点手术协议，在曲面码与满足两种充分条件之一的 ISC 之间转移编码信息。

Result: 该框架可容纳任意有限的对角门群（diagonal gates）与任意可逆经典门（reversible classical gates），因为二者均可嵌入更大的不可约有限门群。它还能实现位于 Clifford 层级（Clifford hierarchy）任一有限层之外的逻辑门。格点手术协议使这些操作得以进入曲面码计算。

Conclusion: 结果共同建立了一套系统性方法：为期望的逻辑操作量身定制拓扑码，并将其整合到常规（曲面码）计算中。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computation typically relies on a fixed set of logical primitives, while realizing other desired operations can require substantial resources. We introduce irrep surface codes (ISCs), two-dimensional topological quantum error-correcting codes engineered to transversally implement a prescribed set of logical gates. The engineered gates only need to satisfy two conditions: (i) they generate a finite group, which, up to global phases, is forced by the Eastin-Knill theorem, and (ii) they are irreducible, meaning that any operator commuting with the set of gates must be proportional to the identity. This accommodates arbitrary finite groups of diagonal gates and arbitrary reversible classical gates, since each can be embedded into a larger irreducible finite group of gates. It also enables logical gates outside every finite level of the Clifford hierarchy. To incorporate these operations into surface-code computations, we further develop lattice-surgery protocols to transfer the encoded information between surface codes and ISCs obeying either of two sufficient conditions. Together, these results establish a systematic method for tailoring topological codes to desired logical operations and integrating them into conventional computations.

</details>


### [220] [Rewire and reset are all you need: Minimal resources for manipulating non-Abelions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06845)
*Chiu Fan Bowen Lo,Rohith Sajith,Anasuya Lyons,Ashvin Vishwanath,Ruben Verresen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作确定在一般有限群的量子双模型（quantum double models）中执行任意子编织（braiding）与融合（fusion）所需的最小电路资源：编织可由无辅助比特（ancilla）的序贯幺正电路实现，而输入无关的融合则可由量子比特重置（qudit reset）实现，证明非阿贝尔任意子操作并不需要辅助资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编织与融合任意子是拓扑量子计算（topological quantum computation）的基础操作，但现有电路协议通常依赖辅助比特与测量。

Method: 通过变形任意子所依附的底层图，构造无辅助比特的序贯幺正编织电路；并引入图重连（graph rewiring）这一图解演算，为单个双量子比特门（two-qudit gates）赋予几何解释并追踪其对稳定子（stabilizers）的作用。

Result: 对融合操作，证明了排除无辅助比特纯幺正协议的不可行定理（no-go theorem）；同时表明仅使用量子比特重置即可实现此类“输入无关（input-agnostic）”融合。

Conclusion: 与既有观念相反，编织或融合非阿贝尔任意子（non-Abelions）无需辅助资源；该融合协议凸显重置作为纠错中尚未充分利用的原语，可避免辅助比特与测量带来的时空开销。

Abstract: Braiding and fusing anyons are fundamental to topological quantum computation, yet existing circuit protocols often employ ancillas and measurements. We determine the minimal circuit resources for performing such anyon operations in quantum double models of generic finite groups. Our approach braids anyons through a sequential unitary circuit without ancillas. Intuitively, this is obtained by deforming the underlying graph on which the anyons live. More generally, we introduce graph rewiring, a diagrammatic calculus that assigns a geometric interpretation to individual two-qudit gates and tracks their action on stabilizers. For fusion, we prove a general no-go theorem excluding purely unitary protocols for fusing unknown anyon types without introducing ancillas. Remarkably, we show such "input-agnostic" fusion is enabled by simply using a qudit reset. Hence, contrary to folklore, ancilla resources are not needed to braid or fuse non-Abelian anyons (non-Abelions). This work has implications in experimental efforts in error correction: our fusion protocol highlights reset as an underutilized primitive for error correction without the spacetime-overhead of ancillas and measurements.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.str-el'></div>

# cond-mat.str-el [[Back]](#toc)

### [221] [The ALPS project release 3.0: open source software for strongly correlated systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03884)
*F. Alet,T. Chen,A. Feiguin,E. Gull,S. Iskakov,J. P. F. LeBlanc,F. Lin,A. Mirmira,G. Möller,L. Pollet,M. Rosales,V. W. Scarola,H. Shinaoka,H. Terletska,S. Todo,M. Troyer,M. Wallerberger,P. Werner,T. M. R. Wolf*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: ALPS 3.0 是面向强关联量子晶格模型的开源模拟软件项目的新版本，核心变动包括分发渠道扩展、开发流程迁移、许可证更换、文档重建、C++ 代码现代化与 DMRG 更新，并移除部分遗留组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该项目旨在为量子磁体、晶格玻色子及强关联费米子系统等强关联量子晶格模型（strongly correlated quantum lattice models）提供模拟所需的库与应用软件。其开发以通用数据格式、简化并加速代码开发的库，以及面向非专家的全功能模拟程序为中心。

Method: 软件集成的主要算法包括：采用非局域更新的经典与量子蒙特卡罗（quantum Monte Carlo, QMC）、扩展系综模拟、精确与完全对角化（exact and full diagonalization, ED）、密度矩阵重整化群（density matrix renormalization group, DMRG），以及用于动力学平均场理论（dynamical mean-field theory, DMFT）的连续时间 QMC 求解器；支持串行或并行数值模拟。

Result: 3.0 版的主要变化包括：通过 Python Package Index（pip install pyalps）与 Spack 分发 pyalps 二进制包以支持 HPC 系统；开发迁移至 GitHub 并引入持续集成与自动化测试；改用宽松的 MIT 许可证；重建文档与教程网站，含 Jupyter notebook 教程及本地化内容；C++ 代码库广泛现代化（符合 C++17、兼容 Boost 与 NumPy 2.0、清理警告与死代码），并伴随重大 DMRG 更新及可靠性、构建兼容性修复；移除遗留组件（VisTrails 溯源集成与 TEBD、MPS、directed-worm-algorithm 应用代码）；3.0.0 版本以 Zenodo DOI（10.5281/zenodo.22775899）归档。

Conclusion: 项目在美国国家科学基金会（US National Science Foundation, NSF）POSE 计划下建立了正式的治理与可持续性模型，软件发布于 https://alps.comp-phys.org。

Abstract: We present release 3.0 of the ALPS (Algorithms and Libraries for Physics Simulations) project, an open-source software project to develop libraries and application programs for the simulation of strongly correlated quantum lattice models such as quantum magnets, lattice bosons, and strongly correlated fermion systems. As in previous releases, development is centered on common data formats, on libraries to simplify and speed up code development, and on full-featured simulation programs that let non-experts carry out serial or parallel numerical simulations using the important algorithms for quantum lattice models: classical and quantum Monte Carlo (QMC) using non-local updates, extended-ensemble simulations, exact and full diagonalization (ED), the density matrix renormalization group (DMRG), and continuous-time QMC solvers for dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT). Major changes in release 3.0 include distribution of the pyalps binary through the Python Package Index (pip install pyalps) and through Spack for HPC systems; migration of development to GitHub with continuous integration and automated testing; relicensing of the package under the permissive MIT license; a rebuilt documentation and tutorial website, including a set of Jupyter-notebook tutorials and localized content; a broad modernization of the C++ codebase (C++17 compliance, Boost and NumPy 2.0 compatibility, and warning and dead-code cleanup) together with a major DMRG update and associated reliability and build-compatibility fixes; the removal of legacy components (the VisTrails provenance integration and the TEBD, MPS, and directed-worm-algorithm application codes); archival of release 3.0.0 with a Zenodo DOI (10.5281/zenodo.22775899); and a formal governance and sustainability model developed under the US National Science Foundation (NSF) POSE program. The software is available at https://alps.comp-phys.org.

</details>


### [222] [ToF-PAX-RIXS: A Time-of-Flight Spectrometer for combined ARPES and RIXS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03993)
*Chafic Fawaz,Tom Lacmannn,Yaryna Lytvynenko,Sergii Chernov,Olena Tkach,Siegmar Roth,Jan O. Schunck,Amon Lanz,Jakob Dilling,Lukas Bruckmeier,Markus Scholz,Frank Scholz,Arne Meyer,Dirk Fuchs,Lara Schmieder,Yiran Liu,Matteo Minola,Bernhard Keimer,Dmytro Kutnyakhov,Kai Rossnagel,Olena Fedchenko,Angela Wittmann,Moritz Hoesch,Gerd Schönhense,Matthieu Le Tacon,Hans-Joachim Elmers,Martin Beye*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出并实现了一台紧凑型仪器 ToF-PAX-RIXS，将飞行时间动量显微角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）与基于光电子能谱分析 X 射线的共振非弹性 X 射线散射（PAX-RIXS）集成于单一多模式电子光学柱中，可在相同实验条件下对同一样品顺序测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于在同一紧凑平台上关联电子结构与集体激发（collective excitations），从而规避不同仪器、不同实验条件带来的可比性问题。

Method: 方法上，仪器采用单一多模式电子光学柱实现两种模式：ARPES 模式记录光电子强度随动能与两个面内动量分量的分布；PAX-RIXS 模式将散射软 X 射线光子吸收于薄金属转换器（converter），并对所生成光电子的能量与发射位置进行分析。

Result: 结果方面，在 Cu $L_3$ 吸收边用 CaCuO$_2$ 及 Au、Ag、Pt 转换器进行基准测试，测得谱重现了常规光栅 RIXS 光谱仪的主要特征，去卷积后有效重构能量分辨率优于 $120$~meV；同一 Ca 掺杂 YBa$_2$Cu$_3$O$_{6.45}$ 样品上的 ARPES 与 PAX-RIXS 联合测量验证了关联测量能力。

Conclusion: 结论为：该仪器可在单一紧凑平台上实现电子结构与集体激发的关联测量，其 PAX-RIXS 性能经基准测试得到验证。

Abstract: We present ToF-PAX-RIXS, a compact instrument that combines time-of-flight momentum-microscopy angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES) with resonant inelastic X-ray scattering (RIXS). The latter is based on photoelectron spectrometry for analysis of X-rays (PAX). The two techniques are implemented with a single multimode electron-optical column, allowing measurements to be performed sequentially on the same sample under identical experimental conditions. In ARPES mode, the instrument records the photoemission intensity as a function of kinetic energy and two in-plane momentum components. In PAX-RIXS mode, scattered soft X-ray photons are absorbed in a thin metallic converter, and the generated photoelectrons are analyzed in energy and position of emission on the converter. The electron kinetic energy retains the spectral information of the scattered photons, while the position is related to the scattering angle and therefore to the momentum transfer, enabling simultaneous access to a finite momentum-transfer interval. We describe the mechanical design, converter assembly, scattering geometry, and multimode electron optics of the instrument. Its PAX-RIXS performance is benchmarked at the Cu $L_3$ edge using CaCuO$_2$, with Au, Ag, and Pt converters. The measured spectra reproduce the principal features obtained with a conventional grating RIXS spectrometer, and deconvolution yields an effective reconstructed energy resolution better than $120$~meV under the present conditions. Finally, combined ARPES and PAX-RIXS measurements on the same Ca-doped YBa$_2$Cu$_3$O$_{6.45}$ sample demonstrate the capability of the instrument to correlate electronic structure and collective excitations within a common compact platform.

</details>


### [223] [Magneto-transport and electronic structure studies of ternary antimonides, La$T$Sb$_2$ ($T$ = Cu, Ag)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04290)
*Himanshu Pant,Neeraj Bhatt,Deepali Sharma,Asif Ali,A. Thamizhavel,Saurabh Singh,Kentaro Kuga,Tsunehiro Takeuchi,Ravi Shankar Singh,R. Bindu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 基于Sb方网（square net）的Dirac半金属（Dirac semimetal）三元锑化物LaTSb2（T = Cu, Ag）在低温下偏离费米液体（Fermi liquid）行为、呈线性磁阻（magnetoresistance, MR），其中仅T = Ag样品显示电荷密度波（charge density wave, CDW）特征；Sb方网被认为与CDW现象相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探究Sb方网基Dirac半金属三元锑化物中CDW形成与局域电子环境的关联，并厘清CDW与非CDW体系在输运与谱学行为上的差异。

Method: 方法包括磁输运（magnetotransport）测量与光电子谱（photoelectron spectroscopy）研究，并结合基于密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）的从头算（ab initio）计算，采用DFT+U并考虑自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling, SOC）。

Result: 两化合物均表现出电阻率正温度系数、线性磁阻及2-30 K低温下偏离费米液体行为；仅T = Ag样品显示CDW特征，其温度依赖MR表明单带半经典输运（single-band semiclassical transport）不足；DFT+U表明需对Cu 3d与Ag 4d引入在位库仑相互作用（on-site Coulomb interaction, U）并含SOC才能重现实验价带谱；两者均存在不等价Sb位点；T = Ag在CDW相中呈异常低温展宽，而T = Cu遵循预期的温度依赖核心能级锐化；T = Ag的Sb 3d谱暗示Sb方网与CDW相关，且CDW转变温度以下ε_F处谱DOS小幅下降。

Conclusion: 核心能级研究为比较CDW与非CDW体系、并阐明局域电子环境在CDW形成中的作用提供了有价值的谱学基准（spectroscopic benchmark）。

Abstract: We investigate Sb square net-based Dirac semimetallic ternary antimonides La$T$Sb$_2$ ($T$ = Cu, Ag) using magnetotransport and photoelectron spectroscopic studies, complemented by density functional theory (DFT) based ab initio calculations. Our results show that both compounds exhibit a positive temperature coefficient of resistivity, linear magnetoresistance (MR), and a deviation from Fermi liquid behaviour at low temperatures (2-30 K). But only $T$ = Ag compound exhibit signature of charge density wave (CDW) phenomena and the results of its temperature dependent MR data suggest that single-band semiclassical transport is not sufficient to understand the transport behaviour. $\text{DFT}+U$ studies show that in both samples, an on-site Coulomb interaction ($U$) for Cu 3$d$ and Ag 4$d$ electrons, in the presence of spin-orbit coupling (SOC) is required to reproduce experimental valence band spectra. Sb 3$d$, 4$d$ core-level spectra combined with $\text{DFT}+U$ calculations and La 3$d$ spectra confirm the presence and effects of inequivalent Sb sites for both the samples. In contrast to the $T$ = Cu compound, which follows expected temperature-dependent core-level sharpening, the $T$ = Ag compound exhibits an anomalous low-temperature broadening in the CDW phase. The analysis of Sb 3$d$ spectra suggests Sb square nets are linked to CDW phenomena in the $T$ = Ag compound. Temperature-dependent ultraviolet photoemission spectroscopic studies on $T$ = Ag show a small decrement in spectral DOS at the $ε_F$ for the temperature below the CDW transition temperature. These core-level studies provide a valuable spectroscopic benchmark for comparing CDW and non-CDW systems and clarifying the role of local electronic environments in CDW formation.

</details>


### [224] [Determining the j-Specific Unoccupied 5f Electronic Structure in Actinides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04654)
*J. G. Tobin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究通过实验对比了基于高能分辨荧光检测（HERFD）的 M4,5 高分辨 X 射线吸收谱（XAS）与轫致辐射等色谱（BIS），对象为多种锕系材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在厘清高分辨 XAS-HERFD 与作为逆光电子谱（IPES）高能变体的 BIS 在探测锕系材料未占据态方面的关系与差异。

Method: 对多种锕系材料同时开展 M4,5 边 XAS-HERFD 与 BIS 的实证比较实验。

Result: 结果表明 XAS-HERFD 本质上是 BIS 的 j 特异（j-specific）版本。

Conclusion: XAS-HERFD 可直接分别测定 5f5/2 与 5f7/2 的未占据态密度（UDOS）。

Abstract: An empirical comparison of high resolution M4,5 X-ray Absorption Spectroscopy (XAS), based upon High Energy Resolution Fluorescence Detection (HERFD), with Bremsstrahlung Isochromat Spectroscopy (BIS), the high energy variant of Inverse Photoelectron Spectroscopy (IPES), of various actinide materials is presented here. It will be shown that XAS-HERFD is essentially the j-specific version of BIS, providing a direct measure of the separate 5f5/2 and 5f7/2 Unoccupied Density of States (UDOS).

</details>


### [225] [Does a pole in the self-energy imply a violation of the Luttinger theorem?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04663)
*Artem Abanov,Andrey Chubukov*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文证明：自能存在极点时，Luttinger 定理（Luttinger theorem）并不必然失效。以一个含两条能隙带（gapped bands）与一条窄残余准粒子带（residual quasiparticle band，小残余 $Z\ll 1$）的费米子系统为例，在 Model I 中 Luttinger-Ward 分析对所有 $Z>0$ 完全适用，结果与能隙带不存在时相同；Model II 中当 $Z$ 超过依赖费米子密度的临界值时 Luttinger-Ward 分析恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Luttinger 定理将费米面体积与每自旋电子密度 $n$ 相联系，在任意费米液体（Fermi liquid）中成立，但学界普遍认为当费米子自能出现极点时会失效。该文旨在检验这一普遍看法是否成立。

Method: 构造含自能 $Σ(ω, \mathbf{k})$（具极点）的费米子系统，其谱结构包含两条能隙带与一条在 $ω=0$ 处交叉、残余 $Z \ll 1$ 的窄残余准粒子带；相应格林函数（Green's function）$G(ω, \mathbf{k})$ 含两个零点与三个极点（一个来自准粒子带，两个来自能隙带）。区分 Model I（$Z$ 同时将准粒子带宽 $W$ 重整化为 $ZW$）与 Model II（$W$ 不被重整化），并分别施加 Luttinger-Ward 分析。

Result: 在 Model I 中，Luttinger-Ward 分析对任意 $Z > 0$ 均完全适用，且结果与能隙带不存在时完全一致。在 Model II 中，当 $Z$ 超过某个依赖费米子密度的临界值时，Luttinger-Ward 分析得以恢复。

Conclusion: 费米子自能出现极点并不必然导致 Luttinger 定理失效；定理是否成立取决于带宽重整化方式（Model I 与 Model II）及残余 $Z$ 相对临界值的位置。

Abstract: Luttinger theorem relates Fermi surface volume to electron density per spin $n$. It holds in an arbitrary Fermi liquid but is widely believed to break when a fermionic self-energy has a pole.
  We show that this is not necessarily the case.
  As an example, we consider a fermionic system consisting of two gapped bands, originating from a self-energy $Σ(ω, \mathbf{k})$ with a pole,
  and a narrow residual quasiparticle band crossing $ω=0$, with a small residue $Z \ll 1$
  (a residual Fermi liquid). We consider Model I, in which $Z$ also renormalizes quasiparticle bandwidth $W$ to $ZW$ and Model II, in which $W$ remains unrenormalized.
  The Green's function for such a system $G(ω, \mathbf{k})$ contains two zeros and three poles --- one from the quasiparticle band and two from the gapped bands.
  Yet, we show that in Model I, the Luttinger-Ward analysis is fully applicable
  for any $Z >0$,
  and the results are exactly the same as if the gapped bands did not exist. For Model II, the Luttinger-Ward analysis recovers once $Z$ exceeds some critical value, which depends on fermionic density.

</details>


### [226] [Floquet dressing of bulk metallic bands in 2H-NbSe2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04958)
*Cong Li,Qirui Cui,Maciej Dendzik,Magnus H. Berntsen,Wanyu Chen,Zhilin Li,Anna Delin,Wentao Zhang,Oscar Tjernberg*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究利用时间与角度分辨光电子能谱（time- and angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy, tr-ARPES）在体金属 2H-NbSe₂ 中识别出对体衍生金属能带的 Floquet 修饰（Floquet dressing），其光子位移复制带保留母带色散，且权重随泵浦光偏振旋转在动量空间分支间重新分布，可由 Floquet–Volkov 相干干涉较好地描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Floquet 修饰是实现周期光场调控电子态的途径，但既往实现多集中于拓扑绝缘体表面态、半导体与二维狄拉克半金属；将其扩展至体金属可调控金属输运与集体序所依赖的巡游态。然而体金属中的强屏蔽与快速散射长期令人质疑其可行性，且光电子修饰（photoelectron dressing）会干扰其识别。

Method: 采用时间与角度分辨光电子能谱（tr-ARPES）观测 2H-NbSe₂ 中相互重叠、体衍生的金属能带；通过改变泵浦光（pump）线性偏振并固定探测光（probe）偏振，考察复制带权重在动量空间分支间的分布；并构建包含固体内部与外部光场的模型进行拟合。

Result: 在稀疏的未占据态窗口中出现光子位移复制带，其保留母带色散且仅在泵浦-探测重叠期间出现；固定探测偏振、旋转泵浦线性偏振可重新分配复制带在动量空间分支间的权重。使用同一组参数时，考虑固体内外光场的模型通过相干 Floquet–Volkov 干涉比单一路径或其非相干强度叠加更贴近地刻画该权重再分布。

Conclusion: 研究将 Floquet 修饰扩展至多带金属的巡游体带，且不依赖对母带与光子修饰复制带间杂化开隙（energy gap）的观测，为探索相干光场修饰如何耦合金属输运与集体电子相提供了基础。

Abstract: Floquet dressing offers a route to controlling electronic states through periodic optical driving. Its realization in solids has centred primarily on topological-insulator surface states, semiconductors and two-dimensional Dirac semimetals. Extending this control to bulk metals would enable manipulation of the itinerant states underlying metallic transport and collective order. Yet strong screening and rapid scattering have long cast doubt on its feasibility in bulk metals, while photoelectron dressing complicates its identification. Here we identify Floquet dressing of the overlapping, bulk-derived metallic bands of 2H-NbSe2 using time- and angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy. Photon-shifted replicas in a sparse unoccupied-state window retain the parent-band dispersions and appear only during pump--probe overlap. Rotating the pump's linear polarization at fixed probe polarization redistributes replica weight between momentum-space branches. With a common parameter set, a model accounting for the optical fields inside and outside the solid captures this redistribution through coherent Floquet--Volkov interference more closely than either pathway alone or their incoherent intensity sum. These findings extend Floquet dressing to the itinerant bulk bands of a multiband metal without relying on the observation of energy gaps opened by hybridization between parent bands and their photon-dressed replicas, providing a basis for exploring how coherent optical dressing couples to metallic transport and collective electronic phases.

</details>


### [227] [Unveiling High-Energy Ultrafast Responses Driven by Fermi-Level Gap Opening in Charge-Density Waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05175)
*Qiong Wu,Tianchen Hu,Junhan Huang,Yongjie Liu,Fei Sun,Shuxiang Xu,Li Yue,Zijian Lin,Xinyu Zhou,Tao Dong,Nan-Lin Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作利用超快泵浦-探测光谱（ultrafast pump-probe spectroscopy）研究电荷密度波（charge-density-wave, CDW）材料 EuAl4 的载流子弛豫动力学，发现光激发下 CDW 能隙显著收缩，并由此调制带隙边缘相关的带间跃迁（interband transition），在对应探测能量处产生显著瞬态反射率变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超快泵浦-探测光谱是研究超导体与关联电子体系（correlated electron systems）中相互作用、集体激发（collective excitations）及非平衡动力学（nonequilibrium dynamics）的重要工具，但费米面（Fermi surface）附近小能隙打开如何影响高能带间跃迁区的光谱变化尚不明确。该文旨在厘清这一低能与高能光谱响应之间的关联问题。

Method: 针对上述问题，作者对典型电荷密度波材料 EuAl4 开展了系统的载流子弛豫动力学（carrier relaxation dynamics）研究，采用超快泵浦-探测光谱手段测量其在不同探测能量下的瞬态反射率变化。

Result: 结果显示，光泵浦下 CDW 能隙明显收缩（shrink），该能带重整化（band renormalization）强烈调制与能隙边缘相关的带间跃迁，从而在相应探测能量处诱导出显著的瞬态反射率变化。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了低能序参量动力学（order-parameter dynamics）与高能光谱响应之间的直接联系，为解读带隙量子材料（gapped quantum materials）中的瞬态信号提供了普适性框架。

Abstract: Ultrafast pump-probe spectroscopy is indispensable for investigating interactions, collective excitations, and nonequilibrium dynamics in superconductors and correlated electron systems. However, how a small gap opening near Fermi surface affects the spectral changes in the high-energy interband transition region remains elusive. To address this issue, we perform systematic studies of carrier relaxation dynamics in the prototype charge-density-wave (CDW) material EuAl4. We find that the CDW gap shrinks substantially under optical pumping. This band renormalization strongly modulates interband transitions associated with the gap edge, inducing a pronounced transient reflectivity change at the corresponding probe energies. These results establish a direct link between low-energy order-parameter dynamics and high-energy spectral responses, providing a general framework for interpreting transient signals in gapped quantum materials.

</details>


### [228] [Complete Entanglement Structure of the Kitaev Honeycomb Spin Liquid from Exact Tensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05395)
*Hidehiro Saito,Chisa Hotta*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作通过精确的二维张量网络（tensor network）构造，表明Kitaev蜂窝模型中拓扑纠缠熵（topological entanglement entropy）仅在对物理自旋希尔伯特空间施加Gutzwiller投影、从而由规范约束（gauge constraint）移除一半Schmidt态之后，才在所有本征态上出现，据此确立激发态同样具有拓扑序（topological order）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kitaev蜂窝模型的自旋液体（spin liquid）性质虽已由$\mathbb{Z}_2$ plaquette与Majorana费米子（Majorana fermion）图像阐明大部分特征，但仍有遗留问题待解决。作者认为，恢复精确的量子多体形式可为固定这些遗留问题提供线索。

Method: 从随机乘积态（random product state）出发，经序列量子数投影（quantum-number projection）与Gutzwiller投影，给出完整解集的精确二维张量网络描述，二者均表示为维度 $D=2$ 的局域矩阵乘积算符（matrix-product operator）。并在 $S=1/2$ kagome与三角Heisenberg模型上做同类论证。

Result: 基态与激发态共享相同键维度（bond dimension），但Schmidt谱不同，从而区分其面积律（area-law）与体积律（volume-law）纠缠。拓扑纠缠熵仅在对物理自旋希尔伯特空间做Gutzwiller投影之后对所有本征态出现，规范约束恰好移除一半Schmidt态。另发现Parton平均场态中存在投影诱导的波函数坍缩至单一规范扇区。

Conclusion: 激发态同样具有拓扑序；该视角可推广至更广泛的承载 $\mathbb{Z}_2$ 自旋液体的模型。研究结果以Wilson环（Wilson-loop）对拓扑序的刻画，可视化了体-边对应（bulk-boundary correspondence）。

Abstract: While most of the features of the Kitaev honeycomb model are already clarified from the combined ${\mathbb Z}_2$ plaquette and Majorana fermion picture, we show that retrieving the exact quantum many-body form gives a clue to fix the remaining issues on its spin liquid property. We provide an exact two-dimensional tensor network description of a full set of solutions from a random product state, by sequential quantum-number projection and Gutzwiller projection, both expressed as local matrix-product operators of dimension $D=2$. The ground and excited states share the same bond dimension yet exhibit distinct Schmidt spectra, distinguishing their area-law and volume-law entanglement. We demonstrate that the topological entanglement entropy emerges {\it for all eigenstates only after} the Gutzwiller projection onto the physical spin Hilbert space, where the gauge constraint removes exactly half of the Schmidt states. This establishes that excited states are likewise topologically ordered. We further show that there exists a projection-induced collapse of the wave function into a single gauge sector in the Parton mean-field states that is known to give a good description of the spin liquids. The demonstrations on the $S=1/2$ kagome and triangular Heisenberg models suggest that the present perspective is applied to a wider class of models hosting ${\mathbb Z}_2$ spin liquids. Our finding visualizes a bulk-boundary correspondence with the Wilson-loop characterization of topological order.

</details>


### [229] [Nonthermal nonlinear many-body excitonic dynamics in high-purity silicon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06016)
*Patrick Pilch,Siyu Duan,Yang Liu,Manfred Bayer,Marc Assmann,Zhe Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作利用时间分辨光泵浦-太赫兹三次谐波产生光谱（time-resolved optical pump-terahertz third-harmonic generation spectroscopy），在室温高纯硅中观测到皮秒尺度非热多体激子（nonthermal many-body excitonic）瞬态态的形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室温下硅中激子（exciton）结合能低于热能而不稳定，因此探索非平衡条件下能否存在瞬态激子态成为研究动机。

Method: 采用时间分辨光泵浦-太赫兹三次谐波产生光谱，对室温高纯硅进行高分辨率非线性光谱测量，并施加脉冲非共振光泵浦。

Result: 在皮秒时间尺度上识别出瞬态激子态的形成；与未束缚电子-空穴对（unbound electron-hole pairs）相比，非热多体激子动力学表现出特征性的时间依赖演化、明显的电荷密度依赖以及非微扰非线性太赫兹（terahertz）响应。

Conclusion: 结果表明，尽管热平衡下硅激子不稳定，非共振光泵浦后仍可在皮秒尺度形成具有独特多体非线性响应的瞬态激子态。

Abstract: We report time-resolved optical pump-terahertz third-harmonic generation spectroscopy of picosecond nonthermal many-body charge dynamics in high-purity silicon at room temperature. Although in thermal equilibrium excitons in silicon are unstable due to a lower binding energy than the thermal energy at room temperature, following pulsed non-resonant optical pumping we can identify the formation of transient excitonic states on the picosecond time scale in our high-resolution nonlinear spectroscopic measurements. In contrast to unbound electron-hole pairs, the nonthermal many-body excitonic dynamics feature a characteristic time-dependent evolution, a distinct dependence on charge density, and a nonperturbative nonlinear terahertz response.

</details>


### [230] [Lifshitz transition and magnetic superconductivity in flat band systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06140)
*Sudeshna Pal,K. B. Yogendra,Madhuparna Karmakar,Rajesh Narayanan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究多带超导体（multiband superconductor）中的 Lifshitz 型转变，以二维 Lieb 晶格上的吸引 Hubbard–Kondo 模型为对象，采用实空间 Bogoliubov–de Gennes 平均场理论与解析格林函数方法，识别出两种不等价的 Lifshitz 型转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lifshitz 转变通常与金属体系中费米面连通性的变化相关，而该现象学可自然推广至超导态，此时重构的对象是最小准粒子能量流形。作者希望探讨多带、含平带的超导体中此类转变的表现及其与磁序、配对竞争的关系。

Method: 采用二维 Lieb 晶格上的吸引 Hubbard–Kondo 模型，经典核心自旋与巡游电子耦合；使用实空间 Bogoliubov–de Gennes 平均场理论（real-space Bogoliubov–de Gennes mean-field theory）与解析格林函数（Green's function）计算。

Result: Kondo 通道与配对通道的竞争导致体系整体为磁性超导体，半填充附近为 (π,π) 序，随后依次转为 (0,π)，在更强的 Kondo 耦合下转为不可公度螺旋 (0,q)。识别出两种不等价 Lifshitz 型转变：Kondo 耦合为零时，最小能量等值线随化学势形变，M 点附近的空穴口袋在 μ=-2t 处张开为完全嵌套的正方形并重连为 Γ 附近的口袋，该嵌套决定了 (π,π) 序的出现位置；在有限 Kondo 耦合的某些化学势下，流形本身维度下降。

Conclusion: 对每种磁序解析求得最小能量流形，结果显示仅螺旋序使最小能量穿过零点，从而成核节点并留下无能隙磁性超导体，在态密度（density of states）与谱函数（spectral function）中具有清晰特征。

Abstract: A Lifshitz transition is a class of non-symmetry-breaking transitions typically associated with metallic systems wherein the Fermi surface changes its connectivity as the chemical potential sweeps through a band extremum or a saddle point. The phenomenology naturally adapts itself to the superconducting state, where the object that reconstructs is the manifold of minimum quasiparticle energy rather than the Fermi surface. This paper concerns Lifshitz-type transitions in multi-band superconductors. Thus, we focus on the attractive Hubbard--Kondo model on the two-dimensional Lieb lattice, with classical core spins coupled to the itinerant electrons, by laying recourse to a real-space Bogoliubov--de Gennes mean-field theory and an analytic Green's function calculation. The competition between the Kondo and pairing channels yields a magnetic superconductor throughout, with $(π,π)$ order near half filling giving way to $(0,π)$ and then, at stronger Kondo coupling, to an incommensurate spiral $(0,q)$. In such a multiband superconductor hosting a flat band, we identify two inequivalent Lifshitz-type transitions. With the core spins decoupled (the Kondo coupling set to zero), the minimum energy contour deforms with chemical potential, a hole pocket about $M$ opening into a perfectly nested square at $μ=-2t$ and reconnecting into pockets about $Γ$; this nesting fixes where $(π,π)$ order sets in. At finite Kondo coupling, for certain chemical potential, the dimension of the manifold itself drops. We obtain it analytically for each magnetic order and show that only the spiral carries the minimum energy through zero, nucleating nodes and leaving a gapless magnetic superconductor, with clear signatures in the density of states and the spectral function.

</details>


### [231] [Pairing order and condensate fraction across the BCS-BEC crossover in the two-dimensional attractive Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06319)
*Hao Du,Qian Sun,Zhuotao Xie,Yuan-Yao He*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作采用数值严格的辅助场量子蒙特卡洛方法，系统研究二维吸引哈伯德模型中BCS-BEC渡越区的超导基态，重点考察配对序与凝聚体分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 吸引哈伯德模型（attractive Hubbard model）中超导基态在BCS-BEC渡越区的行为尚需系统且无偏的数值刻画。摘要指出研究动机是为理解跨渡越区超导性、并验证未来理论与数值方法提供定量基准。

Method: 采用数值精确的辅助场量子蒙特卡洛方法（auxiliary-field quantum Monte Carlo），在相互作用强度U/t=2~12、费米子密度n=0.25~1.0的参数范围内进行计算，并做有限尺寸外推（finite-size extrapolation）以获得热力学极限值；同时研究次近邻跃迁（next-nearest-neighbor hopping）的影响。

Result: 系统刻画了配对序与凝聚体分数（condensate fraction）对相互作用强度和费米子密度的依赖关系，获得了热力学极限下的数值，并给出双占据数（double occupancy）与总能量的高精度结果，以及次近邻跃迁对超导性质的影响。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一套关于吸引哈伯德模型的全面无偏数值数据，为理解BCS-BEC渡越区超导性及验证后续理论与数值方法建立了定量基准。

Abstract: We report a systematic study of the superconducting ground state in the two-dimensional attractive Hubbard model, employing the {\it numerically exact} auxiliary-field quantum Monte Carlo method. We focus primarily on the pairing order and condensate fraction across the BCS-BEC crossover. Our numerical calculations cover a broad and physically relevant parameter regime, with the interaction strength $U/t=2\sim 12$ (where $t$ is the nearest-neighbor hopping amplitude) and fermion density $n=0.25\sim 1.0$. We systematically characterize the dependence of the pairing order and condensate fraction on both the interaction strength and fermion density, and perform finite-size extrapolations to obtain their thermodynamic-limit values. As complementary quantities, we also provide high-precision numerical results for the double occupancy and total energy. Furthermore, we investigate the effects of next-nearest-neighbor hopping on the superconducting properties of the model. Our results offer a comprehensive set of unbiased numerical data for the attractive Hubbard model, establishing quantitative benchmarks for understanding superconductivity across the BCS-BEC crossover and for validating future theoretical and numerical approaches.

</details>


### [232] [Dynamical Valence Fluctuations and Magnetic Anisotropy in Sm(Co,Cu)$_5$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06568)
*Alexander B. Shick,Alexander V. Andreev,Evgenia A. Tereshina-Chitrova,Institute of Physics,the Czech Academy of Sciences,Na Slovance 2,CZ-18221 Prague,Czech Republic*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 采用能显式处理 Sm 4f 壳层动态涨落的自洽 DFT+U(ED) 方法研究 SmCo5 与 SmCu5 的电子结构与磁各向异性，发现 SmCo5 存在显著 Sm2+–Sm3+ 价态涨落并由此近补偿自旋与轨道磁矩，而 Co 被 Cu 替代后 4f 构型向 f6 偏移，磁矩与磁各向异性显著降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sm 基金属间化合物（intermetallics）的磁性由局域 4f 态、交换相互作用与价态涨落（valence fluctuations）的微妙平衡决定，但动态 4f 关联（dynamical 4f correlations）的作用尚不清晰。该研究旨在厘清动态 4f 关联在 SmCo5 与 SmCu5 电子结构与磁各向异性中的作用。

Method: 使用自洽 DFT+U(ED)（self-consistent DFT+U with exact diagonalization）方法，显式处理 Sm 4f 壳层的动态涨落，计算 SmCo5 与 SmCu5 的电子结构与磁各向异性，并比较 Co 被 Cu 替代后 4f 构型与磁矩的变化。

Result: SmCo5 表现出显著的 Sm2+–Sm3+ 价态涨落，4f 基态由 f5 与 f6 构型主导；动态 4f 关联导致 Sm 自旋与轨道磁矩近补偿，并定量描述其电子结构、磁矩与大的单轴磁各向异性（uniaxial magnetic anisotropy）。以 Cu 替代 Co 后平衡向 f6 构型偏移，Sm 自旋与轨道磁矩出现显著变化；在 SmCu5 中该 4f 构型演化伴随总磁矩与磁各向异性明显降低。

Conclusion: 动态 4f 价态涨落是理解 SmCo5 与 SmCu5 磁性差异的关键，并为描述 Sm(Co,Cu)5 中 Co 到 Cu 替代引起的磁各向异性提供了微观框架（microscopic framework）。

Abstract: The magnetic properties of Sm-based intermetallics are governed by a delicate interplay between localized $4f$ states, exchange interactions, and valence fluctuations, yet the role of dynamical $4f$ correlations remains poorly understood. Here we investigate the electronic structure and magnetic anisotropy of SmCo$_5$ and SmCu$_5$ using self-consistent DFT+$U$(ED), which explicitly treats dynamical fluctuations of the Sm $4f$ shell. We find that SmCo$_5$ exhibits pronounced Sm$^{2+}$--Sm$^{3+}$ valence fluctuations, with the $4f$ ground state dominated by $f^5$ and $f^6$ configurations. The resulting dynamical $4f$ correlations yield near compensation of the Sm spin and orbital moments and provide a quantitative description of the electronic structure, magnetic moments, and large uniaxial magnetic anisotropy of SmCo$_5$. Upon replacing Co with Cu, the balance shifts toward the $f^6$ configuration, with substantial changes in the Sm spin and orbital moments. In SmCu$_5$, this evolution of the $4f$ configuration is accompanied by a pronounced reduction of both the total magnetic moment and magnetic anisotropy. These results demonstrate that dynamical $4f$ valence fluctuations are essential for understanding the contrasting magnetic properties of SmCo$_5$ and SmCu$_5$ and provide a microscopic framework for describing magnetic anisotropy upon Co to Cu substitution in Sm(Co,Cu)$_5$.

</details>


### [233] [Interacting quantum criticality with U(1) symmetry breaking in 1+1 dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06682)
*Mutsumi Shimomura,Kohei Kawabata*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文报告在一维（one-dimensional）自旋-1/2与自旋-1链中观测到U(1)自旋旋转对称性（spin-rotation symmetry）的自发破缺，并定位到一个相互作用量子临界点（interacting quantum critical point），关联长度临界指数ν=0.60±0.02、动力学临界指数z=1.986±0.005。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hohenberg-Mermin-Wagner定理（Hohenberg-Mermin-Wagner theorem）通常使人预期一维量子基态中连续对称性（continuous symmetry）不会被破缺。本文旨在检验并挑战这一预期，探寻一维量子基态中连续对称性自发破缺的可能性。

Method: 采用子系统Binder标度（subsystem Binder scaling）定位临界点并提取关联长度临界指数ν；同时对最低中性激发能隙（lowest neutral excitation gap）进行有限尺寸标度（finite-size scaling）分析以确定动力学临界指数z。

Result: 测得ν=0.60±0.02，明显偏离高斯值（Gaussian value）0.5，但与微扰重整化群（perturbative renormalization-group）预言一致；测得z=1.986±0.005，满足z<2，与无阻挫模型（frustration-free models）对该临界理论的微观实现不相容。

Conclusion: 结果表明一维量子基态中可实现连续对称性破缺，其机制不同于无阻挫性（frustration freeness），为超出Hohenberg-Mermin-Wagner预期的一维连续对称性破缺提供了机制性解释。

Abstract: The Hohenberg-Mermin-Wagner theorem motivates the expectation that continuous symmetries remain unbroken in one-dimensional quantum ground states. Here, contrary to this expectation, we demonstrate the spontaneous breaking of U(1) spin-rotation symmetry and the emergence of an interacting quantum critical point in lattice spin-$1/2$ and spin-$1$ chains. Using subsystem Binder scaling, we locate the critical point and determine the correlation-length critical exponent as $ν= 0.60 \pm 0.02$, distinct from the Gaussian value $0.5$ but consistent with the perturbative renormalization-group prediction. We further analyze the finite-size scaling of the lowest neutral excitation gap and obtain the dynamical critical exponent $z=1.986 \pm 0.005$. The result $z < 2$ is incompatible with microscopic realizations of this critical theory by frustration-free models, implying a mechanism for continuous symmetry breaking in one-dimensional quantum ground states distinct from frustration freeness.

</details>


### [234] [Strong metamagnetic switching and anisotropic magnetotransport in the distorted kagome magnet Tb6Ti4Al43](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06732)
*Lipika,Sneh,Shobha Singh,Ralf Koban,Walter Schnelle,Rie Umetsu,Kaustuv Manna*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究系统表征了单晶 Tb6Ti4Al43 的磁性、电输运与热力学性质，揭示其畸变 kagome 晶格中的反铁磁序、各向异性场诱导相变及显著的反常霍尔效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tb 原子在复杂 Ti-Al 框架中构成畸变 kagome 晶格（distorted kagome lattice），此类体系可能呈现自旋、电荷与晶格自由度的耦合，但其磁性与输运行为尚待实验阐明。

Method: 对单晶 Tb6Ti4Al43 开展综合性磁性、电学与热力学测量，包括不同场方向（c 轴与 ab 面）下的磁化与磁输运（magnetotransport）测量，并分析反常霍尔贡献。

Result: 样品在约 19 K 出现反铁磁序（antiferromagnetic ordering）并具有显著磁各向异性；沿 c 轴加场观察到强变磁开关（metamagnetic switching）与低温下明显的不对称磁滞，沿 ab 面加场则出现自旋翻转转变（spin-flop transition）；场诱导磁态在磁电阻中产生显著异常并带来反常霍尔贡献，且观测到反常霍尔电导（anomalous Hall conductivity）的显著本征贡献，表明其源于动量空间拓扑导致的 Berry 曲率（Berry curvature）效应。

Conclusion: 结果凸显 Tb6Ti4Al43 中自旋、电荷与晶格自由度的相互作用，并将该畸变 kagome 反铁磁体确立为可场调控的多功能平台，具备电学可读磁开关、自旋操控与磁-机械功能等潜力。

Abstract: We report a comprehensive investigation of the magnetic, electrical, and thermodynamic properties of single crystalline Tb6Ti4Al43, in which Tb atoms form a distorted kagome lattice embedded within a complex Ti-Al framework. Tb6Ti4Al43 exhibits pronounced magnetic anisotropy and antiferromagnetic ordering at approximately 19 K. For magnetic field applied along the c-axis, a strong metamagnetic switching is observed, accompanied by a clear asymmetric hysteresis at low temperatures, while for field applied along the ab plane, a spin-flop transition is observed. The effect of field induced magnetic states is strongly reflected in magnetotransport measurements, giving rise to pronounced anomalies in magnetoresistance and an anomalous Hall contribution. A significant intrinsic contribution to the anomalous Hall conductivity is observed, highlighting the Berry curvature effects arising from the system's momentum-space topology. Our results highlight the interplay of spin, charge, and lattice degrees of freedom in Tb6Ti4Al43 and establish this distorted kagome antiferromagnet as a promising platform for field-tunable multifunctional behaviour, including electrically readable magnetic switching, spin manipulation, and magneto-mechanical functionalities.

</details>


### [235] [Unraveling Fermi liquid charge and heat transport in RuO$_{2}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06763)
*Harrison LaBollita,Fabian B. Kugler,Antoine Georges,Cyrus E. Dreyer*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el
