TL;DR: 采用现实性动力学平均场（dynamical mean-field theory, DMFT）计算，将 RuO₂ 顺磁态识别为中等关联费米液体（Fermi liquid），其光学电导率、电阻率与热电势与实验达到（半）定量一致，但电子热阻率显著低于实验推断值，构成电荷与热流弛豫差异的重要谜题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RuO₂ 是多轨道费米液体（multi-orbital Fermi liquid），近期多种互补实验测量使其成为定量多体电子结构理论（many-body electronic structure theory）的严格检验平台。论文旨在以该材料为试金石，建立并批判性检验关联量子材料的定量描述。

Method: 使用现实性动力学平均场（realistic dynamical mean-field）计算，针对 RuO₂ 顺磁态（paramagnetic state）计算其光谱性质与输运性质，并与互补实验观测进行对照；同时探讨低温电子-声子散射（electron-phonon scattering）作为解释热阻率偏差的可能机制。

Result: 计算得到 RuO₂ 为中等关联费米液体，其光学电导率（optical conductivity）、电阻率（electrical resistivity）与热电势（thermopower）同实验呈（半）定量一致。然而电子热阻率（electronic thermal resistivity）计算结果显著低于实验推断的低温热阻率，形成关于费米液体区间内电荷流与热流相对弛豫（relative relaxation）的重要谜题。

Conclusion: 跨多个互补可观测量的一致性与残余偏差共同确立了 RuO₂ 的“精密”多体描述，并将偏差转化为对理论的有意义检验；低温电子-声子散射被探讨为可能机制，但完全定量的解释仍是未解问题。更广泛地，结果展示了将精密 DFT+DMFT 计算与互补实验探针结合，以构建并批判性检验关联量子材料定量描述的能力。

Abstract: RuO$_2$ is a multi-orbital Fermi liquid for which an array of complementary experimental measurements has recently become available, making it a stringent testbed for quantitative many-body electronic structure theory. Using realistic dynamical mean-field calculations, we compute the spectroscopic and transport properties of RuO$_2$ in the paramagnetic state. We identify RuO$_2$ as a moderately correlated Fermi liquid and obtain (semi-)quantitative agreement with experiment for its optical conductivity, electrical resistivity, and thermopower. This broad agreement across complementary observables establishes a ``precision'' many-body description of RuO$_2$ and allows remaining discrepancies to be identified as meaningful tests of the theory. In particular, the calculated electronic thermal resistivity significantly undershoots the experimentally inferred low-temperature thermal resistivity, raising an important puzzle concerning the relative relaxation of charge and heat currents in the Fermi liquid regime. We explore low-temperature electron-phonon scattering as a possible mechanism for resolving this discrepancy, while a fully quantitative description remains an open problem. More broadly, our results demonstrate the power of combining precision DFT+DMFT calculations with complementary experimental probes to build and critically test quantitative descriptions of correlated quantum materials.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.dis-nn'></div>

### [236] [Ground-state properties of the two-dimensional SWAP spin-glass ensemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05046)
*Alexander K. Hartmann,Leticia F. Cugliandolo,Marco Tarzia*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文研究近期提出的伊辛自旋玻璃（Ising spin-glass）SWAP 系综，其中自旋具有可变长度（长度尺度 Δ∈[0,2]），并可按 SWAP 算法交换长度。通过退火（annealing）与精确基态对比，验证了慢退火可获得对应哈密顿量基态的条件，并揭示 Δ 大小对体系铁磁（ferromagnetic）或自旋玻璃性质的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究动机在于理解新引入的 SWAP 系综中，自旋长度可变与长度交换机制如何影响伊辛自旋玻璃的基态求解能力及其零温（zero-temperature）物理性质。

Method: 采用类似玻璃态多分散硬球（poly-disperse hard-sphere）体系 SWAP 算法的思想交换自旋长度，并结合退火调度进行求解；通过与图匹配算法（graph matching algorithms）获得的精确基态比较，在二维系统（尺寸达 L²=1024² 自旋）中验证结果，并利用周期与反周期边界条件计算精确零温畴壁能（domain-wall energy）。

Result: 若退火足够慢，可高概率获得将自旋长度并入键的伊辛哈密顿量基态；对于中等或较大 Δ（如 Δ=1），慢退火下实现变为铁磁，即失去零温自旋玻璃性质；对于较小 Δ，实现仍保持玻璃态，但 SWAP 退火不易找到真正基态。

Conclusion: SWAP 退火在适当慢速条件下能高概率求解二维大尺寸体系基态，但所得无序实现的性质强烈依赖 Δ：较大 Δ 会导致自旋玻璃性质消失，较小 Δ 虽保持玻璃态却降低基态搜索成功率。

Abstract: We study the recently introduced SWAP ensemble for Ising spin-glasses, where the spins obtain varying lengths, with length scale $Δ\in [0, 2]$. The lengths can be exchanged in the spirit of the SWAP algorithm for glassy poly-disperse hard-sphere systems. Using an annealing schedule, if the annealing is slow enough, ground states of the corresponding Ising Hamiltonian, where the spin lengths are incorporated into the bonds, can be obtained with high probability. We prove this statement for two-dimensional systems with sizes of up to $L^2=1024^2$ spins by comparison with exact ground states obtained by using graph matching algorithms. In particular, we analyze the nature of the obtained realizations of the disorder by calculating exact zero-temperature domain-wall energies, from exact ground state calculations using periodic and anti-periodic boundary conditions. For medium or large values of $Δ$, e.g. $Δ=1$, the realizations turn ferromagnetic if the annealing is slow enough, i.e., they lose their zero-temperature spin-glass property. If $Δ$ is small, the realizations remain glassy, but the SWAP annealing does not easily find a true ground state.

</details>


### [237] [Disorder-driven loss of crystalline coherence in two-dimensional electron solids: Quantum Hartree-Fock and classical molecular dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06371)
*Sankar Das Sarma,Haining Pan*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文结合Hartree-Fock理论与经典分子动力学，研究淬火无序（quenched disorder）对二维电子固体（two-dimensional electron solids）的影响，发现无序削弱晶序、展宽结构因子并缩短相干长度，与Imry-Ma和Larkin图像一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究淬火无序如何影响二维电子固体的结构有序性，并检验其演化是否符合Imry-Ma与Larkin关于随机钉扎破坏平移序的理论预期。

Method: 采用Hartree-Fock理论（Hartree-Fock theory）与经典分子动力学（classical molecular dynamics）相结合的方法，通过调节无序强度考察晶序相关性、结构因子与相干长度的变化，并进行经典模拟。

Result: 无序增强使晶序相关性减弱、结构因子展宽、测量相干长度趋近于无杂质晶格间距；经典模拟显示，即便晶序消失后，更强钉扎仍提高了粒子重排的温度。

Conclusion: 无序在抑制结构相干性的同时，使非晶固体在热重排面前更加稳定，即削弱晶序却增强了非晶固体的热稳定性。

Abstract: We investigate the effects of quenched disorder on two-dimensional electron solids using Hartree-Fock theory and classical molecular dynamics. Increasing disorder weakens crystalline correlations, broadens the structure factor, and reduces the measured coherence length toward the pristine lattice spacing. This evolution is consistent with the Imry-Ma and Larkin picture: random pinning disrupts translational order on sufficiently long scales, and stronger disorder reduces the distance over which crystalline organization survives. Classical simulations further show that stronger pinning raises the temperature for particle rearrangement even after crystalline order has disappeared. Disorder thus suppresses structural coherence while stabilizing an amorphous solid against thermal rearrangement.

</details>

### [238] [Shifted quantum affine superalgebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05228)
*Piao-Juan Hu,Jian-Rong Li,Yan-Feng Luo*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文对具有固定 Drinfeld 当前表示的量子仿射超代数（quantum affine superalgebra）定义移位版本，并系统研究标准宇称型 A 类代数 \(\mathcal U_q^{\mu_+,\mu_-}(\widehat{\mathfrak{sl}}_{m|n})\) 的三角分解、PBW--Drinfeld 基、RTT 实现、Ding--Frenkel 同构及移位余积结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确陈述研究动机，仅指明研究对象为固定 Drinfeld 当前表示下的量子仿射超代数及其移位版本，并聚焦标准宇称型 A 类代数。故动机信息在摘要中未给出。

Method: 方法为：通过修改 Cartan--Drinfeld 当前的首项次数而保留其余当前关系与 Serre 关系来定义移位版本；随后证明三角分解、构造 PBW--Drinfeld 基与 Levendorskii 型有限表示、构造移位 RTT 实现与 Ding--Frenkel 同构，并构造移位形式 Drinfeld 余积及单侧/双侧非完备余积。

Result: 对任意移位及 \(m,n\ge1\)、\(m\ne n\) 证明三角分解；当 \(m,n>1\) 时对任意移位给出 PBW--Drinfeld 基，并对每个反支配 \(\mu\) 给出单侧代数 \(\mathcal U_q^{0,\mu}\) 的 Levendorskii 型有限表示；对反支配双侧移位构造 RTT 实现并证明 Ding--Frenkel 同构；构造在完备张量积中余结合的移位形式 Drinfeld 余积；其三元余结合性在中移位反支配（含边界情形）时成立；对双侧反支配移位 \(\mu_+,\mu_-\in\Lambda^-\) 给出移位全一般线性 Ding--Frenkel 同构。

Conclusion: 移位全一般线性 Ding--Frenkel 同构将特殊线性简单流代数（special-linear simple-current algebra）识别为共同标量重标度下的不动代数，并由此得到余结合的双侧非完备余积；单侧非完备余积在反支配区域可由有限表示在有限多个生成元上显式给出，并经单射移位同态延拓至任意单侧移位。

Abstract: For a quantum affine superalgebra with a fixed Drinfeld current presentation, we define shifted versions by modifying the leading degrees of the Cartan--Drinfeld currents while retaining the remaining current and Serre relations.
  We then study the standard-parity type A algebras $\mathcal U_q^{μ_+,μ_-}(\widehat{\mathfrak{sl}}_{m|n})$. For arbitrary shifts and $m,n\ge1$, $m\ne n$, we prove a triangular decomposition. When $m,n>1$, we obtain PBW--Drinfeld bases for arbitrary shifts and a Levendorskii-type finite presentation of the one-sided algebra $\mathcal U_q^{0,μ}$ for every antidominant $μ$. For antidominant two-sided shifts, we construct shifted RTT realizations and prove the corresponding Ding--Frenkel isomorphisms.
  We construct a shifted formal Drinfeld coproduct for arbitrary two-sided shifts, coassociative in a completed tensor product. Shifted RTT realizations yield non-completed one-sided coproducts in the antidominant region, made explicit on finitely many generators by the finite presentation; injective shift homomorphisms extend them to arbitrary one-sided shifts. Their ternary coassociativity holds whenever the middle shift is antidominant, including boundary cases. Finally, for two-sided antidominant shifts $μ_+,μ_-\inΛ^-$, a shifted full general-linear Ding--Frenkel isomorphism identifies the special-linear simple-current algebra as the fixed algebra under common scalar rescalings and yields coassociative two-sided non-completed coproducts.

</details>


### [239] [Quantum symmetries and nimreps for an exotic exceptional modular invariant of F4 at level 6](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05449)
*Robert Coquereaux*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文重新研究 1989 年发现的 \(F_4\) 第 6 层非对角模不变式（non-diagonal modular invariant），将其联系到融合范畴 \(\mathcal A=\mathcal C(F_4,6)\) 的模范畴 \(\mathcal M\)，并计算其量子对称代数与对偶 \(\mathcal O\)。核心发现是手征图不连通、各含两对同构分支，分别对应共享同一模不变式的两个模范畴 \(\mathcal M\) 与 \(\mathcal M^\prime\)，其中仅 \(\mathcal M\) 具有自融合（self-fusion）性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该 \(F_4\) 第 6 层非对角模不变式自发现起即无法由仿射李代数（affine Lie algebras）的共形嵌入（conformal embedding）得到，其后虽证明可由非李型的顶点算子代数（vertex operator algebra）扩张获得，但其完整共形场论（CFT）结构与量子对称性仍缺乏系统描述。本文旨在补齐这一缺口，刻画与之相关的模范畴及其量子对称代数。

Method: 将模不变式对应的完整 CFT 表述为融合范畴 \(\mathcal A=\mathcal C(F_4,6)\) 上的模范畴 \(\mathcal M\)，并利用 \(\mathcal M\) 与 étale 代数（étale algebra）的关联（即量子子群／type I／flat 性质）研究 \(\mathcal A\) 关于 \(\mathcal M\) 的对偶 \(\mathcal O\)。同时把 Verlinde 代数 \(A\) 的作用限制到相应阿贝尔群 \(M\) 与 \(M^\prime\) 上，构造非负整数矩阵（nimreps）及其邻接图。

Result: 得到量子对称代数，给出手征生成元（chiral generators）并展示 \(\mathcal A\) 基本不可约表示（irreps）的 Ocneanu 图；手征图不连通，每个含两对同构分支，其顶点集等同于两个共享同一模不变式的模范畴 \(\mathcal M\)、\(\mathcal M^\prime\) 的简单对象等价类。限制作用给出两族 nimreps 矩阵与邻接图。副产品包括缺陷线（defect lines）矩阵、附着于 \(A\) 及其模的整数与有理融合多项式，以及量子维数（quantum dimensions）、量子质量（quantum masses）与诱导-限制规则等数值不变量。

Conclusion: 该 \(F_4\) 第 6 层模不变式可通过非李型顶点算子代数扩张及 étale 代数机制统一理解，其完整 CFT 的量子对称结构由不连通的手征图刻画。\(\mathcal M\) 与 \(\mathcal M^\prime\) 共享模不变式但自融合性质不同，说明模不变式并不能唯一决定模范畴的融合性质；相关 nimreps、缺陷线与融合多项式为进一步构造与分类提供数据基础。

Abstract: We revisit an old non-diagonal modular invariant of $F_4$ at level $6$, discovered in 1989. At the time of its discovery, it was noticed that it could not be obtained from a conformal embedding of affine Lie algebras. Later, it was shown that it can be obtained from an extension of a vertex operator algebra which is not of Lie type. To this modular invariant, one can attach a full CFT defined (or formulated) as a module category $\mathcal M$ for the fusion category $\mathcal A = \mathcal C(F_4,6)$. More recently, it was recognized that $\mathcal M$ is associated with an étale algebra of $\mathcal A$. In other words, the resulting quantum module is a quantum subgroup (it is also said to have self-fusion, to be of type I, or to be flat). We study the dual $\mathcal O$ of $\mathcal A$ with respect to $\mathcal M$. In particular, we describe the algebra of quantum symmetries (we obtain the chiral generators and display the Ocneanu graphs for the fundamental irreps of $\mathcal A$). The chiral graphs are not connected, each one containing two pairs of isomorphic components. The vertex sets of these components are identified with the simple objects, up to equivalence, of two module categories $\mathcal M$ and $\mathcal M^\prime$ sharing the same modular invariant. $\mathcal M$ enjoys self-fusion, whereas $\mathcal M^\prime$ does not. The restriction of the action of the Verlinde algebra $A$ to the associated abelian groups $M$ and $M^\prime$ defines two families of matrices with non-negative integer entries (nimreps) and their adjacency graphs. As a by-product, we obtain the matrices describing the defect lines of the theory, the integer and rational fusion polynomials attached to $A$ and its modules, and the relevant numerical invariants, quantum dimensions, quantum masses, and induction-restriction rules.

</details>

### [240] [Fluctuation theorems and exact multi-allele fixation probabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04090)
*Emil A. Yuzbashyan,Alexander Feigel,Alexandre V. Morozov*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 该文利用非平衡统计力学中的涨落定理（fluctuation theorems）重新推导了Kimura的固定概率公式，并将该框架推广至多等位基因系统，给出多等位基因固定概率问题的递归解析解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限种群中突变基因最终或消失或被固定，确定其固定概率（fixation probability）是建模进化动力学的前提；而无性种群中克隆干扰（clonal interference）的定量描述需要同时存在多个等位基因时的固定概率，等价于求解多维后向Kolmogorov方程，数学上极为困难。

Method: 采用非平衡统计力学中的涨落定理（fluctuation theorems），首先重新推导Kimura（1962）的单等位基因固定概率公式；随后将该方法扩展至多等位基因系统，给出多等位基因固定概率问题的递归解析解（recursive analytical solution）。

Result: 涨落定理为多等位基因系统提供了任何有效固定概率集合都必须满足的约束条件；在克隆干扰情景下研究新有益突变的命运时，精确处理结果与将目标突变视为与单一有效基因型竞争的平均场近似（mean-field approximation）之间存在显著偏差。

Conclusion: 该形式化框架可替代无性进化模型中的显式模拟（explicit simulations）与近似解析结果，为多等位基因固定概率问题提供精确解析工具。

Abstract: A mutant gene that appears in a finite population will, in the long run, either disappear from it or become fixed, so that all members of the population will carry a copy of the mutant gene. Determining the fixation probability of a mutant gene is thus a prerequisite for modeling evolutionary dynamics in finite populations. Within the diffusion theory framework, the mutant's competition with the wild-type genes is described by the closed-form solution of a one-dimensional backward Kolmogorov equation for the mutant fixation probability, derived by Motoo Kimura in 1962. However, in asexual populations the rate of adaptation and the relative importance of alternative evolutionary pathways are shaped by clonal interference - the competition between several lineages arising from distinct beneficial or deleterious mutations. Quantitative descriptions of clonal interference require computing fixation probabilities in populations where multiple alleles are present simultaneously - a formidable mathematical problem which amounts to solving a multi-dimensional backward Kolmogorov equation. Here, we employ fluctuation theorems (a set of exact equalities in non-equilibrium statistical mechanics) in order to re-derive Kimura's fixation probability formula. When extended to multi-allele systems, fluctuation theorems provide a constraint which must be satisfied by any valid set of fixation probabilities. Moreover, we present a recursive analytical solution of the multi-allele fixation probability problem. We use our formalism to study the fate of a novel beneficial mutation in a clonal interference scenario, discovering substantial deviations between our exact treatment and the mean-field approximation in which the target mutation competes with a single effective genotype. Our formalism can be used to replace both explicit simulations and approximate analytical results in models of asexual evolution.

</details>

### [241] [Data-driven measurements of the beauty hadron production in p-Pb collisions at \sqrt{s_{\mathrm{NN}}} = 5.02 and 8.16 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05345)
*Liang Dong,Tianle Liu,Guangsheng Li,Xiaozhi Bai,Fabrizio Grosa,Yifei Zhang,Jianhui Zhu*

Main category: nucl-ex

TL;DR: 本文通过数据驱动的贝叶斯展开（Bayesian unfolding）方法，从 ALICE 与 LHCb 的非瞬时（non-prompt）$D^{0}$ 与 J/$\psi$ 介子测量中提取 p-Pb 碰撞中开美强子（open beauty hadron）的产生截面与核修正因子 $R_{pPb}$，首次给出覆盖 $-4.0<y<4.0$ 全快度区间的基于展开的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美强子（beauty hadron）产生是探测冷核物质（cold nuclear matter, CNM）效应的有力探针：美夸克质量大，产生于早期硬散射且可用微扰 QCD（perturbative QCD）计算，其核修正可约束核部分子分布函数（nuclear parton distribution functions, nPDFs）。精确量化这些效应是分离重离子碰撞中夸克-胶子等离子体（quark-gluon plasma）形成信号的前提。

Method: 对 $\sqrt{s_{\mathrm{NN}}}=5.02$ 与 $8.16$ TeV 的 p-Pb 碰撞，基于 ALICE 与 LHCb 对 non-prompt $D^{0}$ 与 J/$\psi$ 介子的测量，采用数据驱动的贝叶斯展开（Bayesian unfolding）方法获得美强子产生截面。

Result: 展开得到的 $p_{T}$ 与 $y$ 微分截面在不同衰变道间相互一致，并与直接重建的美强子测量相符。报告了 $R_{pPb}$ 随横动量与快度的变化，首次给出覆盖全快度区间 $-4.0<y<4.0$ 的基于展开的 $R_{pPb}$ 结果，精度与现有数据相当或更优。结果与基于 nPDFs 的计算进行比较，并给出 p-Pb 碰撞中总包容 B 强子产生截面。观察到美强子的 CNM 压低弱于粲强子，与冷核物质效应预期的质量层级一致。

Conclusion: 美强子的核修正可为未来全局 nPDF 拟合提供有价值的约束；其 CNM 压低弱于粲强子，符合冷核物质效应的质量层级预期。

Abstract: Beauty-hadron production in proton-nucleus collisions is a particularly powerful probe of cold nuclear matter (CNM) effects: thanks to the large beauty-quark mass, the production is initiated in early hard scatterings and is calculable in perturbative QCD, while the modifications observed in nuclear collisions directly constrain the nuclear parton distribution functions (nPDFs) in a kinematic region complementary to that accessed by charm. Precisely quantifying these effects is a prerequisite for isolating possible signatures of quark-gluon plasma formation in heavy-ion collisions. In this work, the production of open beauty hadrons in p-Pb collisions at $\sqrt{s_{\mathrm{NN}}} = 5.02$ and $8.16$~TeV is studied via a data-driven Bayesian unfolding of ALICE and LHCb measurements of non-prompt $D^{0}$ and J/$ψ$ mesons. The unfolded $p_{T}$- and $y$-differential cross sections are mutually consistent across decay channels and compatible with directly reconstructed beauty-hadron measurements. The nuclear modification factor $R_{pPb}$ is reported as a function of transverse momentum and rapidity, providing the first unfolding-based result spanning $-4.0 < y < 4.0$ of beauty-hadron $R_{pPb}$ covering the full rapidity range, with a precision comparable to or better than that of existing data. The results are compared with calculations based on nPDFs and can provide valuable constraints for future global nPDF fits. The total inclusive B hadron production cross section in p-Pb collisions is also reported. A weaker CNM suppression is observed for beauty hadrons than for charm hadrons, consistent with the expected mass hierarchy of cold-nuclear-matter effects.

</details>

### [242] [Advances in the Vacuum Magnetic Birefringence measurement using a pulsed Foil Coil](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06043)
*Enzo Rodriguez Alvarez,Jonathan Agil,Julien Billette,Jérôme Béard,Rémy Battesti,Carlo Rizzo*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 在BMV（真空磁双折射，Biréfringence Magnétique du Vide）实验框架下，详细描述现有装置并讨论其关键点，同时利用专门设计的脉冲线圈（Foil Coils）报道了真空线性磁双折射常数（vacuum linear magnetic birefringence constant）的新测量结果 $K_{VMB} < 1.7 \cdot 10^{-21}~\text{T}^{-2}$（68% 置信水平），较2014年测量有所改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在推进BMV实验框架下的真空磁双折射测量，通过改进装置与线圈设计提升对真空线性磁双折射常数的约束精度，相对于2014年的测量结果取得进一步改善。

Method: 论文详细介绍了BMV实验当前的装置并讨论其关键点，同时采用专门设计的脉冲线圈（Foil Coils）开展测量，用于确定真空线性磁双折射常数。

Result: 新测得的真空线性磁双折射常数为 $K_{VMB} < 1.7 \cdot 10^{-21}~\text{T}^{-2}$，置信水平为68%，该结果优于作者2014年的测量。

Conclusion: 基于BMV实验装置与Foil Coils脉冲线圈，研究实现了对真空线性磁双折射常数的改进测量，给出了更严格的上限约束，较2014年结果有所提升。

Abstract: In this paper, in the framework of the {\it Biréfringence Magnétique du Vide} (BMV) experiment, we present our current apparatus in detail and we discuss its critical points. We also report the results obtained using specially designed pulsed coils called Foil Coils. Our novel measurement of the vacuum linear magnetic birefringence constant is $K_{VMB} < 1.7 \cdot 10^{-21}~\text{T}^{-2}$ at a confidence level of 68$\%$, which is an improvement with respect to our previous 2014 measurement.

</details>


### [243] [Surface-pinned nonsingular topologically charged defects in nanoplasmonics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05433)
*Michael I. Tribelsky,Vladimir V. Ternovski*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文讨论等离激元（plasmonic）物体光散射中由表面钉扎、拓扑带电、非奇异缺陷（nonsingular defects, SD）构成的缺陷结构；其源于精确的麦克斯韦边界条件（Maxwell boundary conditions）且纯属拓扑性质，不受表面力影响。圆拄（circular cylinder）精确解揭示了保持拓扑荷的分岔（bifurcation）过程及损耗（loss）下缺陷区域的收缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于阐明光散射场中一类与表面力无关、由边界条件精确决定的拓扑缺陷，从而区分拓扑效应与力学效应。摘要指出这些缺陷在光学频段、（准）二维（quasi-2D）情形下于特定偏振中显现，构成对散射场拓扑结构的系统性刻画需求。

Method: 方法上以精确的麦克斯韦边界条件为出发点，推导表面钉扎的拓扑带电非奇异缺陷；并在圆拄几何下求取精确解，比较 $H^{(z)}$ 与 $E^{(z)}$ 两种偏振下场分布的差异。此外通过引入损耗考察缺陷区域的演化。

Result: 结果显示，在光学频段、（准）二维情形下，SD 出现于 $H^{(z)}$ 偏振的电场与坡印廷矢量（Poynting vector）场中，而在 $E^{(z)}$ 偏振中不出现。圆拄精确解给出保持拓扑荷守恒（charge-conserving）的分岔，涉及 SD 与体奇异（bulk singularities）共存或仅有 SD 的情形，并观察到损耗导致缺陷区域收缩。

Conclusion: 结论可概括为：该拓扑缺陷由精确边界条件决定且不受表面力影响，具有纯拓扑属性；其存在与偏振选择性强相关，且在损耗下呈现区域收缩。上述特征由圆拄精确解的拓扑荷守恒分岔所支持。

Abstract: We discuss surface-pinned, topologically charged, nonsingular defects (SD) in light scattering by a plasmonic object. They follow from exact Maxwell boundary conditions and are purely topological---unaffected by surface forces. At optical frequencies, in (quasi) 2D, they appear in the electric and Poynting vector fields for $H^{(z)}$ polarization, not for $E^{(z)}$. Exact solutions for a circular cylinder reveal topological charge-conserving bifurcations involving SDs with bulk singularities or SDs alone, and contraction of defect regions under loss.

</details>


### [244] [Spin-orbit-induced gauge field and spin Hall effects in quasi-paraxial fluids of light](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05493)
*Caroline Stier,Nicolas Cherroret*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该工作表明，非线性克尔介质（nonlinear Kerr media）中传播的准傍轴光流体（quasi-paraxial fluids of light）会自然产生有效规范场（effective gauge field），其源于麦克斯韦方程（Maxwell equations）内禀的光自旋-轨道相互作用（spin-orbit interaction of light）。该规范场由流体自身自洽产生，可改变流体动力学行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在揭示准傍轴光流体中非傍轴修正（nonparaxial corrections）所隐含的规范场效应，说明该有效规范场并非外部强加，而是源于光自身的内禀自旋-轨道相互作用。

Method: 通过推导控制光流体演化的非线性薛定谔方程（nonlinear Schrödinger equation）的最低阶非傍轴修正，分析其对圆偏振光束（circularly polarized beams）的影响，得到等效洛伦兹力（effective Lorentz force）。

Result: 该修正对圆偏振光束产生有效洛伦兹力，进而改变流体动力学；有效规范场的符号由光螺旋度（light helicity）控制，幅度由非线性折射率（nonlinear refractive index）的空间变化决定。物理后果在两种构型中得以展示：绕过障碍物的正常流与超流体（superfluid）的手性形变（chiral deformation）及横向自旋霍尔力（transverse spin Hall force）；以及被聚焦流体捕获的光波包（optical wave packet）的累积进动（cumulative precession）。

Conclusion: 准傍轴光流体中的有效规范场是自洽生成的，可由光螺旋度与非线性折射率空间变化调控，并在手性流动形变与波包进动等构型中产生可观测效应。

Abstract: We show that quasi-paraxial fluids of light propagating in nonlinear Kerr media naturally experience an effective gauge field arising from the intrinsic spin-orbit interaction of light encoded in Maxwell equations. By deriving the leading nonparaxial corrections to the nonlinear Schrödinger equation governing the fluid evolution, we find that, for circularly polarized beams, these corrections generate an effective Lorentz force that modifies the fluid dynamics. Remarkably, the corresponding gauge field is not externally imposed but self-consistently generated by the fluid itself, with its sign controlled by the light helicity and its magnitude governed by the spatial variations of the nonlinear refractive index. We illustrate the physical consequences of this gauge field in two configurations: the chiral deformation of normal and superfluid flows past an obstacle, accompanied by a transverse spin Hall force, and the cumulative precession of an optical wave packet trapped in a focusing fluid.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [245] [Time-dependent integrable quantum field theories with dynamical boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03870)
*Pradip Kattel*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种从自治可积模型的散射与反射数据出发，在有限空间区间上构造含时可积手征量子场论的方法；其核心是谱标签到特征坐标的仿射映射，使体驱动与边界驱动由同一仿射谱坐标统一决定，并以边界 qKZ 方程保证往返相容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是在有限空间区间上构造含时（time-dependent）可积手征量子场论（integrable chiral quantum field theory），并利用自治可积模型（autonomous integrable model）的散射（scattering）与反射（reflection）数据作为构造输入，从而将体驱动与边界驱动纳入统一框架。

Method: 要求固定时刻的右—左散射事件与其位置无关，迫使特征坐标（characteristic coordinates）到谱标签（spectral labels）的映射为仿射（affine）且两种手征性（chirality）斜率相同。由此，同一时刻的体散射事件采样同一谱参数，两次边界反射采样对应端点参数；通过反转单体与两体匹配问题重构局域哈密顿量（local Hamiltonian），体矩阵确定接触相互作用，反射矩阵确定边界耦合。

Result: 体驱动与边界驱动并非独立的含时形变，其时间依赖由同一仿射谱坐标固定。两端点的连续反射使谱标签平移有限量；追踪粒子完成一次完整往返产生差分输运（difference transport），其相容性给出边界量子 Knizhnik–Zamolodchikov（boundary qKZ）方程。Yang–Baxter 方程与反射方程保证局域相容性，BqKZ 平坦性保证完整往返的相容性。求解边界 qKZ 系统后，其特征回拉给出相应的含时多体波函数（many-body wavefunction）。

Conclusion: 以有理 SU(N) 模型与含动力学边界杂质（dynamical boundary impurities）的 O(3) 不变模型为例说明了该构造，并获得驱动 Kondo 相互作用（driven Kondo interactions），其耦合由同一仿射谱坐标确定。同一自治因子化数据同时决定边界 qKZ 输运问题与局域驱动哈密顿量。

Abstract: We present a method for constructing time-dependent integrable chiral quantum field theories on a finite spatial interval from the scattering and reflection data of an autonomous integrable model. Each particle carries a spectral label that is constant along its free trajectory. Requiring a right--left scattering event at a fixed time to be independent of its position forces the map from characteristic coordinates to spectral labels to be affine, with the same slope for both chiralities. As a result, all bulk scattering events at a given time sample one common spectral argument, and the two boundary reflections sample the corresponding endpoint arguments. The bulk and boundary drives are not independent time-dependent deformations: their time dependence is fixed by the same affine spectral coordinate. Successive reflections at the two endpoints translate the spectral label by a finite amount. Following a particle through a complete round trip then produces a difference transport whose consistency gives the boundary quantum Knizhnik--Zamolodchikov equations. The Yang--Baxter and reflection equations ensure local compatibility, while BqKZ flatness ensures compatibility of complete round trips. We then reconstruct the local Hamiltonian by inverting the one- and two-particle matching problems. The bulk matrix fixes the contact interaction, while the reflection matrix fixes the corresponding boundary coupling. The same autonomous factorized data determine the boundary qKZ transport problem and the local driven Hamiltonian. Once the boundary qKZ system is solved, its characteristic pullback gives the corresponding time-dependent many-body wavefunction. We illustrate the construction with rational $SU(N)$ models and an $O(3)$-invariant model with dynamical boundary impurities, obtaining driven Kondo interactions whose couplings are fixed by the same affine spectral coordinate.

</details>


### [246] [Quenched Growth of Normal Matrices and Harmonic Measure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03912)
*Oleg Alekseev*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究大阶正态随机矩阵（normal random matrix）特征值构成的二维库仑气体（two-dimensional Coulomb gas），在冻结（freeze）一个 N 特征值构型后，对新增 M 个特征值的条件分布进行刻画，证明其空间分布收敛于旧边界上的调和测度（harmonic measure）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大阶正态随机矩阵的特征值在固定权重下随矩阵阶数增长形成谱液滴（spectral droplet），其半经典密度增量由调和测度描述。作者关心在已冻结部分特征值的条件下，新增特征值如何分布，以揭示谱液滴增长的微观机制与条件结构。

Method: 冻结一个 N 特征值构型，利用投影到次数小于 N+M 且在冻结点取零的多项式（polynomials）空间，抽取 M 个新增特征值；对规则解析液滴（regular analytic droplet）情形，结合精确多项式系综（exact polynomial ensemble）与平衡过程（equilibrium process）分析其极限行为。

Result: 对规则解析液滴，证明当 M/log N→∞ 且 M/N→0 时，新特征值的空间分布条件收敛于旧边界上的调和测度；该收敛在冻结谱上成立，例外冻结构型的概率趋于零。由次数 N,…,N+M−1 的正交多项式（orthogonal polynomials）构成的平衡过程具有相同极限。

Conclusion: 条件均值的多项式矩（polynomial moments）与冻结谱涨落（fluctuations）之间存在精确有限秩恒等式（exact finite-rank identity），其余项来自次数 N+M 及以上的多项式模式。该结果刻画了谱液滴增长在条件意义下的调和测度极限。

Abstract: The eigenvalues of a large normal random matrix form a two-dimensional Coulomb gas. At fixed weight, the spectral droplet grows with the matrix rank, and its semiclassical density increment is harmonic measure. We freeze an $N$-eigenvalue configuration and sample $M$ further eigenvalues using the projection onto polynomials of degree less than $N+M$ that vanish at the frozen points. Their conditional law is an exact polynomial ensemble in the potential of the frozen charges. For a regular analytic droplet, we prove that the spatial distribution of the new eigenvalues converges to harmonic measure on the old boundary when $M/\log N\to\infty$ and $M/N\to0$. This convergence holds conditionally on the frozen spectrum, outside a set of frozen configurations whose probability tends to zero. The equilibrium process formed from orthogonal polynomials of degrees $N,\ldots,N+M-1$ has the same limit. An exact finite-rank identity relates polynomial moments of the conditional mean to fluctuations of the frozen spectrum, with a remainder from polynomial modes of degree $N+M$ and above.

</details>


### [247] [Branching Gaps, Coexistence, and Boundary Influence for the Ising Model on Spherically Symmetric Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03941)
*Farrukh Mukhamedov,Otabek Khakimov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究有根球对称树（rooted spherically symmetric trees）上最近邻铁磁 Ising 模型（nearest-neighbor ferromagnetic Ising model），揭示分支序列中长非分支段对唯一性的影响，并将有界间隙与共存现象建立等价关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究多关注规则或周期树上 Ising 模型的相变，但对分支层按任意给定序列变化时唯一性（uniqueness）与共存（coexistence）如何受非分支段结构影响，尚缺乏系统刻画。

Method: 采用有根球对称树模型，每顶点有 1 个或 q 个子节点，分支层由给定序列决定。通过定位足够长的非分支段、构造密度为一的树、并与周期树作最优一致比较，结合全周期回归映射（full-period return map）与负 Schwarz 导数（negative Schwarzian derivative）进行分析。

Result: 无界单子层段（unbounded runs of one-child levels）导致任意非零均匀场与有限逆温下唯一；对每个给定的趋于零序列，可构造边界影响沿子序列衰减更慢的密度为一的树；有界间隙（bounded gaps）与该族中某有限温度和（非零场的）共存等价；负 Schwarz 导数给出超临界情形的完全分类。

Conclusion: 在球对称树上，分支序列的非分支段结构决定唯一性与共存：有界间隙等价于共存，周期间隙下 Gibbs 唯一性等价于回归映射的不动点唯一性，非零端点自动为非退化折叠（nondegenerate folds），合并分支呈平方根分裂（square-root splitting）。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the ferromagnetic nearest-neighbor Ising model on rooted spherically symmetric trees with one or \(q\) children per vertex, according to a prescribed sequence of branching levels. Unbounded runs of one-child levels force uniqueness at every nonzero homogeneous field and every finite inverse temperature. We quantify this effect through the locations of sufficiently long nonbranching stretches. Conversely, uniqueness need not entail any uniform rate: for each prescribed vanishing sequence, we construct a density-one tree whose finite-volume boundary influence decays more slowly along a subsequence. The tree retains branching number \(q\), although its nonzero-field coexistence region disappears. For eventually bounded gaps, we prove an optimal uniform comparison with the periodic tree having the largest allowed gap and obtain an explicit interval of coexistence fields. Thus bounded gaps are equivalent, within this family, to coexistence at some finite temperature and nonzero field. For eventually periodic gaps, Gibbs uniqueness is equivalent to fixed-point uniqueness of the full-period return map. A negative Schwarzian derivative yields a complete classification: in the supercritical regime the map has three fixed points inside a closed coexistence interval, two at its endpoints, and one outside. The nonzero endpoints are automatically nondegenerate folds, with square-root splitting of the merging branches.

</details>


### [248] [A Self-Generated Local Structural Prior for Resolving Target Merging in Electrical Impedance Tomography (EIT)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04394)
*Junkang Liu,Yizhuang Song*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种自生成局部结构先验（self-generated local structural prior），用于抑制电阻抗断层成像（electrical impedance tomography, EIT）重建中相邻电导率目标被合并为单一连通区域的现象，并通过数值与水箱实验验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EIT 属于病态逆成像（ill-posed inverse imaging）技术，其分辨率有限且空间分布不均匀。位于空间灵敏度退化区域的目标易被重建为单一连通区域，导致相邻结构间空间可分性（spatial separability）丢失。

Method: 先用现有 EIT 重建方法由边界测量数据获得初始电导率图像，并将其作为参考图像，依据像素间强度相似度构建高斯相似矩阵（Gaussian similarity matrix）。随后自适应选取协方差矩阵的特征向量以提取参考图像的主要结构信息，构建局部结构掩模（localized structural mask），并将其引入后续 EIT 重建。

Result: 数值仿真与水箱实验（water tank experiment）结果表明，所提方法可有效分离被传统 EIT 重建方法合并的相邻电导率目标。

Conclusion: 自生成局部结构先验能够保持相邻目标的空间可分性，从而抑制 EIT 重建中的目标合并问题。

Abstract: Electrical impedance tomography (EIT) is an ill-posed inverse imaging technique with limited and spatially nonuniform resolution. Conductivity targets located in regions with degraded spatial sensitivity may be reconstructed as a single connected region, resulting in the loss of spatial separability between neighboring structures. In this work, we propose a self-generated local structural prior for suppressing target merging in EIT reconstruction. First, an initial conductivity image is obtained from the measured boundary data using an existing EIT reconstruction method. This reconstruction is subsequently used as a reference image to construct a Gaussian similarity matrix according to the intensity similarity between image pixels. The adaptively chosen eigenvector of the covariance matrix is then employed to extract the principal structural information of the reference image. A localized structural mask is constructed and incorporated into a subsequent EIT reconstruction to preserve the spatial separability of neighboring targets. Numerical and water tank experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method effectively separates neighboring conductivity targets that are merged by conventional EIT reconstruction methods.

</details>


### [249] [Supersymmetric semi--infinite cohomology and $N = 2$ superconformal structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04434)
*Thomas Creutzig,Uhi Rinn Suh,Sangwon Yoon*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过超仿射顶点代数的BRST约化研究超对称半无限上同调及其N=2超共形结构，并实现超陪集模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探讨源于Kazama--Suzuki N=2条件的超Manin对所关联的超仿射顶点代数（super-affine vertex algebra）上的N=3/2超共形结构，并借此理解超对称半无限上同调（supersymmetric semi-infinite cohomology）及其N=2超共形结构。

Method: 方法上，对超仿射顶点代数上的N=3/2超共形结构进行BRST约化（BRST reduction），并结合由Kazama--Suzuki N=2条件产生的超Manin对（super Manin pair）；此外利用相对超对称半无限上同调来构造超陪集模型。

Result: 通过BRST约化获得了超对称半无限上同调及其N=2超共形结构（N = 2 superconformal structure）的描述；并以相对超对称半无限上同调（relative supersymmetric semi-infinite cohomology）实现了超陪集模型（super coset models）。

Conclusion: 超对称半无限上同调及其N=2超共形结构可由超仿射顶点代数上N=3/2超共形结构的BRST约化导出，且相对超对称半无限上同调为超陪集模型提供了实现途径。

Abstract: We study supersymmetric semi-infinite cohomology and its $N = 2$ superconformal structure via the BRST reduction of an $N = {\frac{3}{2}}$ superconformal structure on the super-affine vertex algebra associated to a super Manin pair arising from the Kazama--Suzuki $N = 2$ condition. Moreover, we realize super coset models in terms of relative supersymmetric semi-infinite cohomology.

</details>


### [250] [Covariant Euler-Poincaré Reduction for Field Theories on Centered Semidirect Products](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04628)
*Miguel Ángel Berbel,Leonardo Colombo,Álvaro Rodríguez Abella*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文为配备左右交换线性表示的李群（Lie group）中心半直积 $G\Join V$ 上的协变场论建立拉格朗日（Lagrangian）与哈密顿（Hamiltonian）双视角约化方案，导出约化 Euler-Poincaré 与 Lie-Poisson 场方程及其 $\mathfrak g$、$V$ 分量分解，并给出约化多辛形式（multisymplectic form）与 Noether、能量-动量守恒律。该框架被应用于双侧矩阵微观结构模型与 $\mathbb R^r$ 上二阶射流群（jet group）$G_r^2$，后者得到半线性对称双曲系统与具精确能量-动量交换的行波转换前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于将约化理论从标准半直积结构群推广至具有左右交换表示的中心半直积 $G\Join V$，以刻画同时携带双侧几何数据的场论问题。

Method: 方法上，从拉格朗日与哈密顿两种视角对结构群为 $G\Join V$ 的主丛（principal bundle）协变场论实施约化，通过 heart 与 diamond 算子分离左右贡献，推导约化场方程、多辛形式公式及守恒律，并与标准半直积结构群的情形进行比较。

Result: 结果表明约化方程可分解为 $\mathfrak g$-分量与 $V$-分量；中心变量保留原有双侧几何数据。应用于 $\operatorname{Mat}(3)$ 矩阵模型时，共轭表示将其分解为标量、矢量与对称无迹扇区，厘清了与指向矢及取向张量变量的关系但不将其等同于标准向列动力学；对 $(1+1)$ 维 Minkowski 基上的一维射流群得到半线性对称双曲系统、精确行波转换前沿及线性-二次射流扇区间的明确能量-动量交换，其双曲目标解释给出线性化扰动的守恒正能量。

Conclusion: 结论方面，数值实验展示了约化演化与群值场的重构，并通过网格细化与独立的波映射（wave-map）计算评估了一致性，验证了所提约化框架的有效性。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a Lie group equipped with commuting left and right linear representations on a vector space $V$, and let $G\Join V$ be the associated centered semidirect product. We develop a reduction scheme for covariant field theories on principal bundles with structure group $G\Join V$ from both Lagrangian and Hamiltonian perspectives, obtaining the reduced Euler-Poincaré and Lie-Poisson field equations decomposed into their $\mathfrak g$- and $V$-components, where $\mathfrak g$ is the Lie algebra of $G$. We also derive the reduced multisymplectic form formula, as well as the Noether and energy-momentum balance laws. Our reduction results are compared with those for principal bundles with standard semidirect-product structure groups, and the role of the centered variables is investigated: they retain the original two-sided geometric data and separate the left and right contributions via the heart and diamond operators. The framework is then applied to a two-sided matrix microstructural model and to the second-order jet group on $\mathbb R^r$, $G_r^2$. For the matrix model, the conjugation representation decomposes $\operatorname{Mat}(3)$ into scalar, vector, and symmetric-traceless sectors, clarifying its precise relation to director and alignment-tensor variables without identifying it with standard nematodynamics. For the one-dimensional jet group on a $(1+1)$-dimensional Minkowski base, we obtain a semilinear symmetric-hyperbolic system and an exact traveling conversion front with explicit energy-momentum exchange between the linear- and quadratic-jet sectors. Its hyperbolic-target interpretation yields a conserved positive energy for the linearized perturbations. A nonlinear numerical experiment illustrates the reduced evolution and reconstruction of the group-valued field, with consistency assessed through mesh refinement and an independent wave-map computation.

</details>


### [251] [Dynamical quasi-symmetry of the classical Kepler-Coulomb problem and the conservation of the Laplace-Runge-Lenz vector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04650)
*Radosław Szmytkowski*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究在 Kepler-Coulomb 力（Kepler-Coulomb force）作用下经典非相对论粒子在 R^N 空间中的运动。作者证明一种包含矢径投影变换（projective transformation）与微分时间标度（differential time scaling）的扩展构型空间变换，构成“动力学准对称（dynamical quasi-symmetry）”，将牛顿运动方程映射为带符号因子的同类方程，并分析了圆锥轨迹与速度矢端图（velocity hodograph）的映射，以及能量守恒与 Laplace-Runge-Lenz 矢量（Laplace-Runge-Lenz vector）守恒之间的等价关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kepler-Coulomb 系统以其动力学对称（dynamical symmetry）著称（负能量为 SO(N+1)，正能量为 SO(N,1)），但摘要指出该准对称独立于且互补于已知的动力学对称，说明作者意在揭示一种尚未被纳入该对称框架的额外变换结构。

Method: 采用解析分析（analytical analysis）与图形分析（graphical analysis）：构造含实参数矢量 c（量纲为逆长度）的扩展构型空间变换 r→R=r/(1+c·r) 连同 dt→dT=dt/(1+c·r)^2，考察其对牛顿方程的映射效果，并研究原系统与变换后系统所对应的非退化圆锥曲线轨迹之间的诱导映射及速度矢端图映射。

Result: 该变换将 m(d²r/dt²)=αr/r³ 映射为 m(d²R/dT²)=(αR/R³)·sgn(1−c·R)，即保持 Kepler-Coulomb 形式但附加符号因子，构成动力学准对称。摘要给出了非退化圆锥曲线轨迹间诱导映射的详细解析与图形结果，并简要讨论了速度矢端图之间的映射。

Conclusion: 该准对称独立于且互补于众所周知的正能量 SO(N,1) 或负能量 SO(N+1)，并含退化为取反号的情形。此外证明了变换后运动中的能量守恒等价于原动力学中 Laplace-Runge-Lenz 矢量的守恒。

Abstract: The paper studies a classical non-relativistic particle moving in $\mathbb{R}^{N}$, $(N\geqslant2)$, in the presence of the Kepler-Coulomb force $\boldsymbol{F}(\boldsymbol{r})=α\boldsymbol{r}/r^{3}$, $(α\neq0)$. It is shown that the extended-configuration-space transformation involving the projective transformation of the radius vector $\boldsymbol{r}\to\boldsymbol{R}=\boldsymbol{r}/(1+\boldsymbol{c}\cdot\boldsymbol{r})$ and the differential time scaling $\mathrm{d}t\to\mathrm{d}T=\mathrm{d}t/(1+\boldsymbol{c}\cdot\boldsymbol{r})^{2}$, where $\boldsymbol{c}$ is a real vector-valued parameter with dimensions of inverse length, constitutes a dynamical quasi-symmetry in the sense that it maps Newton's equation of motion $m(\mathrm{d}^{2}\boldsymbol{r}/\mathrm{d}t^{2})=α\boldsymbol{r}/r^{3}$ into the equation $m(\mathrm{d}^{2}\boldsymbol{R}/\mathrm{d}T^{2})=(α\boldsymbol{R}/R^{3}) \operatorname{sgn}(1-\boldsymbol{c}\cdot\boldsymbol{R})$. This quasi-symmetry is independent of, yet complementary to, the well-known Kepler-Coulomb dynamical symmetry, which is SO($N+1$) for negative energies and SO($N,1$) for positive energies. A detailed analytical and graphical analysis of induced mappings between non-degenerate conic trajectories associated with the original and transformed systems is presented. Mappings between velocity hodographs are also briefly discussed. It is then proved that the conservation of energy in the transformed motion implies the conservation of the Laplace-Runge-Lenz vector in the original dynamics.

</details>


### [252] [Alternative Hamiltonian and Lagrangian Approaches for Constrained Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04692)
*M. A. de Andrade,C. Neves,E. V. Corrêa Silva*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于修正的 Barcelos-Wotzasek（BW）辛算法（symplectic algorithm）的替代方法，用于为受约束系统（constrained systems）构建广义哈密顿（Hamiltonian）与拉格朗日（Lagrangian）框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准哈密顿与拉格朗日方法对无约束经典系统（unconstrained classical systems）有效，但不直接适用于受约束系统（constrained systems），尽管文献中已有成熟处理方式。本文旨在提供一种替代推导路径。

Method: 采用修正的 Barcelos-Wotzasek（BW）辛算法（symplectic algorithm）来推导广义哈密顿与拉格朗日框架。

Result: 该方法能够一致且成功地构建受约束系统的标准拉格朗日描述与哈密顿描述。

Conclusion: 修正的 BW 辛算法为受约束系统的拉格朗日与哈密顿描述提供了一种自洽、可行的替代构造方案。

Abstract: While standard Hamiltonian and Lagrangian methods are powerful for unconstrained classical systems, they are not directly suited to constrained ones, despite the existence of well-established treatments in the literature. In this paper, we present an alternative approach to deriving a generalized Hamiltonian and Lagrangian framework, based on the revised Barcelos-Wotzasek (BW) symplectic algorithm. This method enables a consistent and successful construction of the standard Lagrangian and Hamiltonian descriptions for constrained systems.

</details>


### [253] [A bulk--edge correspondence for two-dimensional self-adjoint boundary and interface problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05616)
*Guillaume Bal*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究半平面二维拓扑绝缘体边界（或两绝缘体界面）上的量子化非对称输运（asymmetric transport），指出其量子化依赖于终止样本所需的自伴实现（self-adjoint realization）$H_L$ 的选择；通过椭圆实现与平移不变两种路径证明边界电流可良定义，并建立体—边对应（bulk–edge correspondence）公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 与欧几里得平面（Euclidean plane）上的问题不同，终止样本需要选择自伴实现 $H_L$，而这一选择是否影响量子化输运此前并不明确。本文旨在厘清该依赖性，并为边界/界面电流这一物理可观测量建立严格的数学基础与体—边对应。

Method: 以边缘/界面电流 $\sigma_I[H_L]=\Tr\,i[H_L,P]\varphi'(H_L)$ 作为建模非对称输运的可观测量，沿两条路径论证其良定义：对 Shapiro–Lopatinski 椭圆实现（Shapiro–Lopatinski elliptic realizations），借助 Boutet de Monvel 演算（calculus）中的估计；对沿边缘平移不变的算子，通过谱分析（spectral analysis）在边缘谱于大动量处逸出体隙（bulk gap）时定义 $\mathcal I$。此外将公式推广至带任意透射条件（transmission conditions）的界面。

Result: 证明该电流可观测量为良定义迹（trace），$\mathcal I=2\pi\sigma_I[H_L]$ 是 Fredholm 指标（Fredholm index），并在算子及其终止的形变与紧支集改变下保持稳定；平移不变情形下 $\mathcal I$ 同时是谱流（spectral flow）与 Maslov 指标（Maslov index）。主要定理给出体—边对应 $\mathcal I=\cR-\cR_L+\eta(L_\infty,\Lm^\Phi(+))-\eta(L_\infty,\Lm^\Phi(-))$，并确定了 $\mathcal I$ 的取值范围；在透明条件（transparent condition）下，该不变量等于欧几里得平面的体差不变（bulk-difference invariant）。八个例子用于说明理论结果。

Conclusion: 量子化边界输运依赖于终止样本的自伴实现选择，但在上述两类实现下 $\mathcal I$ 均为稳定不变量，并可表示为谱流与 Maslov 指标，满足体—边对应公式且取值范围已确定；该框架可推广至任意透射条件的界面，并在透明条件下退化为欧几里得平面的体差不变量。

Abstract: This paper analyzes the quantized asymmetric transport along the boundary of a two-dimensional topological insulator occupying a half plane, or along the interface separating two insulators. In contrast with problems posed on the Euclidean plane, terminating a sample requires a choice of self-adjointrealization, $H_L$, and we show that a quantized transport depends on that choice.
  The physical observable modeling the asymmetric transport is the edge, or interface, current $σ_I[H_L]=\Tr\,i[H_L,P]\varphi'(H_L)$. It is shown to be well defined along two routes. For Shapiro--Lopatinski elliptic realizations, we use estimates from Boutet de Monvel's calculus to show that the current observable is a well-defined trace, that $\mathcal I=2πσ_I[H_L]$ is a Fredholm index, and that it is stable under deformations and compactly supported changes of the operator and of its termination. For translation invariant operators along the edge, a spectral analysis defines $\mathcal I$ when the edge spectrum escapes a bulk gap at large momenta. $\mathcal I$ is then also a spectral flow and a Maslov index.
  Our main theorem is a bulk--edge correspondence \[
  \mathcal I=\cR-\cR_L+η\bigl(L_\infty,\Lm^Φ(+)\bigr)-η\bigl(L_\infty,\Lm^Φ(-)\bigr), \] We determine the range of $\mathcal I$. We extend the formula to interfaces with arbitrary transmission conditions. At the transparent condition, the invariant equals the bulk-difference invariant of the Euclidean plane. Eight examples illustrate the theoretical findings.

</details>


### [254] [On the signaling dimension of the cross-polytope](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05924)
*Tamás Bencze,Péter E. \ Frenkel*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究信息论一般概率模型中通信协议（即其状态空间）的“信令维数（signaling dimension）”，即在输入—输出关联上完美模拟该协议所需的经典通信协议最小复杂度。已有结果表明三维、四维和五维交叉多胞形（cross-polytopes）的信令维数为 3；本文针对更高维交叉多胞形给出信令维数的上界与下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于刻画一般概率模型中通信协议的经典可模拟复杂度，即用最小的经典通信复杂度完美复现给定协议的输入—输出关联。由于交叉多胞形的信令维数仅在 3、4、5 维情形已知为 3，更高维情形尚不明确，因此需要给出更高维交叉多胞形信令维数的界定。

Method: 方法为在信息论一般概率模型的框架内，围绕交叉多胞形（cross-polytopes）状态空间的信令维数（signaling dimension）进行界定分析，对更高维交叉多胞形推导其信令维数的上界与下界。摘要未提供具体技术细节。

Result: 结果为：对更高维交叉多胞形，给出了其信令维数的上界与下界；同时重申三维、四维、五维交叉多胞形的信令维数为 3 这一已知结论。摘要未给出具体数值形式的界。

Conclusion: 结论是：交叉多胞形的信令维数问题在高于五维的情形可通过上界与下界加以刻画，从而扩展了此前仅覆盖三维至五维（其值均为 3）的结果。摘要未给出进一步的结论性推断。

Abstract: In the general probabilistic model of information theory, the signaling dimension of a given communication protocol (or, equivalently, of its state space) is the smallest possible complexity of a classical communication protocol capable of perfectly simulating the given one in terms of input-output correlations.
  The signaling dimensions of three-, four-, and five-dimensional cross-polytopes have been known to be 3. We give an upper and a lower bound for signaling dimensions of higher dimensional cross-polytopes.

</details>


### [255] [Asymptotically free solutions of the scalar flow equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06165)
*Christoph Kopper*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文分析四维欧氏标量场论中重整化群（renormalization group）流方程的解，构造出满足非多项式边界条件的紫外渐近自由（UV asymptotically free）解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于考察标量场论（scalar field theory）在四维欧氏空间（four-dimensional Euclidean space）中，当边界条件为非多项式（nonpolynomial）时重整化群流方程的解是否存在及其性质。

Method: 方法为分析n点Schwinger函数（n-point Schwinger functions）所满足的重整化群流方程，并在非多项式边界条件下求解。

Result: 结果表明存在此类紫外渐近自由解，这些解具有Bose对称性（Bose symmetric）、在欧氏群（Euclidean group）下不变、对流动参数与动量解析，并满足Osterwalder-Schrader增长条件（Osterwalder-Schrader growth condition）。

Conclusion: 结论是非多项式边界条件下四维欧氏标量场论存在满足多项物理与数学性质要求的紫外渐近自由解，从而扩展了可容许解的类型。

Abstract: We analyse solutions of the flow equations of the renormalisation group for the n-point Schwinger functions of scalar field theory in four-dimensional Euclidean space with nonpolynomial boundary conditions. We show that there exist UV asymptotically free solutions of this type which are Bose symmetric, invariant under the Euclidean group, analytic in the flow parameter and the momenta, and satisfy the Osterwalder-Schrader growth condition.

</details>


### [256] [Entropy Contraction and Hypercontractivity for Gaussian Quantum Markov Semigroups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06201)
*Zhengwei Liu,Jincheng Wan,Jinsong Wu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究具有忠实不变高斯态（faithful invariant Gaussian state）的有限模高斯量子马尔可夫半群（finite-mode Gaussian quantum Markov semigroups），给出了相对于不动点代数（fixed-point algebra）的完全修正对数Sobolev不等式（complete modified logarithmic Sobolev inequality）的代数刻画，并在漂移演化（drift evolution）收敛假设下得到最优常数，同时建立了超收缩性（hypercontractivity）与p-对数Sobolev不等式的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子马尔可夫半群中修正对数Sobolev不等式与超收缩性的刻画是量子信息与算子代数交叉领域的核心问题。本文旨在将相关理论推广到有限模高斯情形，并厘清完全修正对数Sobolev不等式、超收缩性与反向超收缩性（reverse hypercontractivity）以及p-对数Sobolev不等式之间的联系。

Method: 对象为具有忠实不变高斯态的有限模高斯量子马尔可夫半群。方法上给出相对于不动点代数的完全修正对数Sobolev不等式的代数刻画，并在漂移演化 \(e^{t\mathbf{Z}}\) 当 \(t\to\infty\) 收敛的假设下求取相应最优常数；通过引入Hurwitz漂移条件分析超收缩性，并系统考察p-对数Sobolev不等式及其常数性质。

Result: 在漂移演化收敛假设下得到完全修正对数Sobolev不等式的最优常数。对具Hurwitz漂移的半群建立了超收缩性，即任意固定对 \(1<p<q<\infty\) 在充分大时间处被达到；同时发现通常从时刻零出发具有正速率的反向超收缩曲线一般可能失效，但在Hurwitz假设下任意固定对 \(1/2<p<q<1\) 在充分大时间处被达到。此外，p-对数Sobolev常数在 \(0<p<1/2\) 时可以为负。

Conclusion: 有限模高斯量子马尔可夫半群在Hurwitz漂移与漂移演化收敛条件下可具备最优的完全修正对数Sobolev常数与（渐近意义下的）超收缩及反向超收缩性质。反向超收缩性在正速率、从零时刻出发的经典形式下并非普遍成立，而p-对数Sobolev常数在 \(0<p<1/2\) 区间可能为负，揭示该类不等式的参数依赖性。

Abstract: In this paper, we study finite-mode Gaussian quantum Markov semigroups with a faithful invariant Gaussian state. We present an algebraic characterization of the complete modified logarithmic Sobolev inequality relative to the fixed-point algebra, and we obtain the corresponding optimal constant under the assumption that the drift evolution $e^{t\mathbf{Z}}$ converges as $t\to\infty$. We establish hypercontractivity for such quantum Markov semigroups with Hurwitz drift, in the sense that every fixed pair $1<p<q<\infty$ is attained at sufficiently large times. We find that the usual reverse hypercontractive curves with a positive rate from time zero can fail in general, but every fixed pair $1/2 < p < q < 1$ is attained at sufficiently large times under the Hurwitz assumption. We also systematically investigate the $p$-logarithmic Sobolev inequality and show that the $p$-logarithmic Sobolev constant can be negative for $0 < p < 1/2$.

</details>


### [257] [Quantum Fluctuations in Bose--Einstein Condensates:Microscopic Correlations and Moment Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06367)
*Mauris Chueng,Keheng Zhu,Hexiang Wang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究三维 Gross–Pitaevskii 区制下量子涨落的高阶矩与微观关联能，沿精确多体演化传播正关联能的三阶谱矩，并在尺度 N² 上识别激发能测度及其极限结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在刻画 Gross–Pitaevskii 区制中量子涨落的高阶矩（higher moments）与微观关联能（microscopic correlation energy），并建立与 N 无关的一致界及激发能测度的极限描述。

Method: 对光滑紧支非负径向相互作用（radial interaction）与 H⁶ 中 N 无关的归一化初始凝聚态波函数，沿精确多体演化传播正关联能的三阶谱矩；并以初始关联能矩的一致界构造允许的纯态与混合态族。

Result: 获得消耗数（depletion number）三阶矩与集体单粒子涨落六阶矩的与 N 无关的一致初始界；在尺度 N² 上识别加权激发能测度，其正能部分为相对散射算子 −2Δ+v 的谱测度乘以含时 dressed contact，并证明非零参考下 contact 的严格正性。对 1≤p<2 确定标度相互作用能矩，二阶矩受 O(N²) 控制；有限三次近似（cubic approximation）在能量形式域误差 O(N^{−3/16})，在更强生成元初始域上误差 O(N^{−5/4})。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在 Gross–Pitaevskii 区制中高阶涨落矩与激发能测度可被一致控制，其极限由相对散射算子谱测度与含时 dressed contact 共同刻画，且有限三次近似具有明确的范数误差阶。

Abstract: We study higher moments of quantum fluctuations and microscopic correlation energy in the three-dimensional Gross--Pitaevskii regime. For a smooth, compactly supported, nonnegative radial interaction and an $N$-independent normalized initial condensate wave function in $H^6$, we propagate the third spectral moment of a positive correlated energy along the exact many-body evolution. A uniform initial bound controls the third moment of the depletion number and sixth moments of collective one-particle fluctuations on every compact time interval, independently of $N$. For a nonzero interaction and a smooth, compactly supported initial condensate, we also identify the excitation-energy measure at scale $N^2$, weighted by the first energy. This limit holds for norm-convergent pure-state families with a uniform bound on the initial third correlated-energy moment. Its positive-energy part is a spectral measure of the relative scattering operator $-2Δ+v$, multiplied by a time-dependent dressed contact. We compute the radial density and prove strict positivity of the contact for nonzero references with finite kinetic energy and finite mean excitation number. The same evolving states have bounded physical fluctuation moments and excitation-energy moments of order $p>1$ bounded below by a positive multiple of $N^{2p-2}$, in the extended spectral sense. Inverse powers of the positive initial energy construct admissible pure and mixed families. For the norm-convergent pure-state class, we determine the scaled interaction-energy moments for $1\le p<2$ and bound the second moment by $O(N^2)$. A finite cubic approximation has norm error $O(N^{-3/16})$ on the energy form domain and $O(N^{-5/4})$ on a stronger initial domain of its generator.

</details>


### [258] [Electrostatic inequalities and stability of matter on asymptotically Euclidean manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06624)
*David Stuart*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文在渐近欧几里得（asymptotically Euclidean）黎曼流形上，证明了固定原子核背景下非相对论费米子物质（nonrelativistic fermionic matter）的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前关于物质稳定性的研究多基于平坦空间或特定曲率符号条件；本文旨在放宽几何假设，考察一般黎曼度量下物质是否仍保持稳定。

Method: 沿用 Lieb–Thirring 不等式与静电不等式（electrostatic inequalities）路线；其中静电不等式通过一种 Voronoi 分解（Voronoi decomposition）型论证证明，但该分解基于格林函数（Green functions）\(\{G(\cdot,R_j)\}\) 而非距离函数。

Result: 在仅假设截面曲率（sectional curvatures）有上下界、不施加任何曲率符号条件的前提下，得到非相对论费米子物质稳定性的证明。

Conclusion: 结果表明，物质稳定性可在不指定曲率符号的渐近欧几里得流形上成立，其关键在于以格林函数为基础的 Voronoi 型分解替代传统依赖距离函数的论证。

Abstract: We prove stability of nonrelativistic fermionic matter in the presence of fixed nuclei \(\{R_j\}\) on an
  asymptotically Euclidean Riemannian manifold \((M,g)\).
  Upper and lower bounds on sectional curvatures are assumed, but
  no specific sign conditions on the curvature are imposed.
  The proof follows the route of Lieb-Thirring and electrostatic inequalities, the latter being
  proved by means of a Voronoi decomposition type argument which is however based on the Green functions \(\{G(\,\cdot\,,R_j)\}\) rather than the distance function.

</details>


### [259] [A Cohomological Characterization of the Clifford Hierarchy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06699)
*Junaid Aftab*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文利用非阿贝尔 1-上闭链（non-abelian 1-cocycle）对 Clifford 层级（Clifford hierarchy）进行递归上同调刻画，并在第三层级分解为辛、仿射与相位分量，进而证明三量子比特（qudit）门的三层级半-Clifford 性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Clifford 层级在容错量子计算（fault-tolerant quantum computation）中居于核心地位，但其更高层级仍仅被部分理解。已有的量子高阶傅里叶分析（quantum higher order Fourier analysis）借助量子导数刻画层级归属，却未给出各层级的显式描述。

Method: 证明酉算子（unitary operator）的量子导数集合构成非阿贝尔 1-上闭链，且反之每个此类 1-上闭链均来自某个酉算子，由此得到 Clifford 层级的递归上同调（cohomological）刻画；再将框架特化到第三层级，把上闭链数据分解为辛（symplectic）、仿射（affine）与相位（phase）分量，并各自给出上同调解释。

Result: 得到 Clifford 层级的递归上同调刻画，以及第三层级的辛、仿射、相位分量分解，各分量均具上同调解释；据此给出第三层级中每个双量子比特门为半-Clifford（semi-Clifford）的新证明，并首次证明第三层级中每个三量子比特门均为半-Clifford。

Conclusion: 论文以非阿贝尔 1-上闭链为核心工具，建立了 Clifford 层级的上同调描述框架，并将第三层级的结构细化为可分别解释的分量；其应用推进了对第三层级半-Clifford 性质的认识，尤其首次覆盖三量子比特门情形。

Abstract: The Clifford hierarchy plays a central role in fault-tolerant quantum computation, but its higher levels remain only partially understood. Recent work on quantum higher order Fourier analysis characterizes membership in the Clifford hierarchy using quantum derivatives, but does not provide an explicit description of the individual levels. In this work, we address this problem by showing that the collection of quantum derivatives of a unitary operator forms a non-abelian \(1\)-cocycle, and conversely that every such \(1\)-cocycle arises from a unitary operator. This yields a recursive cohomological characterization of the Clifford hierarchy. We then specialize this framework to the third level and decompose the cocycle data into symplectic, affine, and phase components, each admitting a cohomological interpretation. As applications, we give a new proof that every two-qudit gate in the third level of the Clifford hierarchy is semi-Clifford, and prove for the first time that every three-qudit gate in the third level is semi-Clifford.

</details>


<div id='astro-ph.CO'></div>

# astro-ph.CO [[Back]](#toc)

### [260] [Stars Would Remember a Recent Gravitational Transition: Testing G-step Resolutions of the Hubble Tension with Red Dwarfs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04048)
*Jeremy Sakstein,Harry Desmond*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该论文检验了“牛顿引力常数（Newton's gravitational constant, G）在约 73–130 Myr 前发生快速跃变”这一用于缓解哈勃张力（Hubble tension）的假说，利用全对流红矮星（fully convective red dwarf）的质量-半径测量结果，将该类模型以 3.1σ 与 4.0σ 的显著性排除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分解决哈勃张力（Hubble tension）的方案提出引力常数 G 在约 73–130 Myr 前经历快速转变。质量低于约 0.35 M⊙ 的全对流红矮星，其热时标（thermal timescale）与所提转变的回溯时间（look-back time）相当，因此可能保留可观的现今记忆，为直接检验该假说提供了途径。

Method: 研究推导了 G 的快速阶跃（rapid step）所导致的红矮星半径的可测变化，并将这些理论预言与分离开的食双星（detached eclipsing binary）中八颗红矮星的质量-半径测量结果进行对比。

Result: 对应于回溯时间 73 Myr 处 G 变化 4%、以及 130 Myr 处变化 11% 的两类 G-阶跃模型，分别以 3.1σ 和 4.0σ 的显著性被排除。

Conclusion: 以红矮星质量-半径观测为探针，用于解释哈勃张力的 G 快速跃变模型与观测不符，因而受到显著约束乃至排除。

Abstract: We test the hypothesis that Newton's gravitational constant underwent a rapid transition approximately $73$-$130\,{\rm Myr}$ ago, as proposed in some resolutions of the Hubble tension. Fully convective red dwarf stars with masses below $\sim0.35\,M_\odot$ have thermal timescales comparable to the proposed transition look-back times, so an appreciable present-day memory can remain. We show that a rapid step in $G$ induces measurable changes in their radii and confront these predictions with mass-radius measurements of eight red dwarfs in detached eclipsing binaries. We find that the $G$-step models proposed to resolve the Hubble tension, corresponding to a $4\%$ change in $G$ at a look-back time of $73\,{\rm Myr}$ and an $11\%$ change at $130\,{\rm Myr}$, are excluded at significances of $3.1σ$ and $4.0σ$, respectively.

</details>


### [261] [Beyond dust in local cosmography: covariant kinetic theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04284)
*Chris Clarkson,Roy Maartens*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该文在无背景时空与几何对称性假设下发展协变动理学框架，以无碰撞气体替代尘埃模型，刻画流交叉（stream crossing）区域。通过速度权重组织Vlasov层次并在四极矩处自洽截断，得到关于能量密度、各向同性压强与各向异性压强的闭合动力学，并给出4-加速度与涡旋（vorticity）的精确非线性解。该框架将非测地物质流嵌入协变局域宇宙学拟合（cosmography），识别出可用局域距离—红移数据检验无碰撞物质的量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将冷暗物质（cold dark matter）建模为尘埃（dust）会使物质汇（matter congruence）成为测地线，从而消除局域哈勃流（local Hubble flow）中的内禀偶极。然而，当相空间出现流交叉时尘埃模型失效，而无碰撞气体可通过相空间中的速度弥散（velocity dispersion）容纳流交叉。因此需要建立不依赖背景时空或几何对称性的协变动力学描述。

Method: 从精确的有质量Vlasov层次（massive Vlasov hierarchy）出发，构造其能量积分矩并按速度权重组织。在四极矩处进行自洽截断，使动力学闭合于物质能量密度、各向同性压强与各向异性压强；其梯度生成物质参考系中的4-加速度（4-acceleration），并给出动量约束（momentum constraint）的精确非线性解，且该解在一般情形下无需截断即成立。此外推导涡旋演化的非线性表达式。

Result: 得到动量约束的精确非线性解，超越以往引入任意4-加速度的处理方式；速度弥散通过4-加速度的旋度（curl）作为涡旋源，并给出涡旋演化的非线性表达。将非测地物质流传播至协变局域宇宙学拟合，其中4-加速度即内禀哈勃偶极（intrinsic Hubble dipole），同时进入减速多极（deceleration multipoles）。

Conclusion: 该构造为尘埃宇宙学拟合提供了协变动理学完备化，并识别出可直接用局域距离—红移数据检验无碰撞物质所需的物理量。姊妹论文将该层次特化至扰动FLRW时空，并预测由此产生的宇宙学拟合信号量级。

Abstract: Modelling cold dark matter as dust makes the matter congruence geodesic and therefore removes any intrinsic dipole from the local Hubble flow. The dust model breaks down as stream crossing develops, whereas a collisionless gas accommodates stream crossing via velocity dispersion in phase space. We develop a covariant kinetic framework for this regime without assuming a background spacetime or any geometrical symmetry. Starting from the exact massive Vlasov hierarchy, we construct its energy-integrated moments and organise these by velocity weight. A self-consistent truncation at the quadrupole closes the dynamics on the matter energy density, isotropic pressure and anisotropic pressure. Their gradients generate a matter-frame 4-acceleration, for which we find an exact nonlinear solution of the momentum constraint, valid in the general case without truncation. This goes beyond previous work in which an arbitrary 4-acceleration is introduced. Velocity dispersion also sources vorticity through the curl of 4-acceleration, and we derive the nonlinear expression for vorticity evolution. We then propagate the non-geodesic matter flow into covariant local cosmography, where the 4-acceleration is the intrinsic Hubble dipole and also enters the deceleration multipoles. Our construction provides a covariant kinetic completion of dust cosmography and identifies the quantities required to test collisionless matter directly with local distance--redshift data. A companion paper specialises the hierarchy to perturbed FLRW spacetime and predicts the size of the resulting cosmographic signals.

</details>


### [262] [Kinetic-theory derivation and Bayesian analysis of the finite-temperature shifted CMB power spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05435)
*I. Y. Park*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 对有限温度量子引力修正引入的两个密度参数 Ω_{Λ_2}、Ω_{Λ_3}，分别用贝叶斯 MCMC 与自建 CMB 计算管线两种互补方法分析；两者均与零一致，因信号被 h 与谱整体归一化的简并吸收而无法被探测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限温度量子引力修正（quantum-gravitational corrections）给宇宙学常数（cosmological constant）带来两个额外密度参数 Ω_{Λ_2} 与 Ω_{Λ_3}，其对应项分别按 a^{-4} 与 a^{-2} 标度。前期工作（Papers I 和 II）已用机器学习（machine-learning）方法找到最佳拟合点，本文旨在通过互补分析补全图景，明确数据能施加何种约束并理解其背后的物理机制。

Method: 第一，基于修改版 \textsc{class} 对 Planck 2018 似然（likelihood）开展贝叶斯 MCMC 分析，以在边缘化（marginalize）全部参数不确定性后考察数据约束。第二，从零构建 CMB 计算管线（CMB pipeline），遵循 Weinberg 的动理学理论（kinetic-theory）表述，并以 \textsc{class} 进行验证，进而开展响应分析（response analysis）以研究底层物理。

Result: 两个参数均与零一致，其中 Ω_{Λ_3} 仅获得单侧约束。响应分析表明：Ω_{Λ_3} 几乎完全被 h 的移动所吸收，作用于测量良好的声学多极（acoustic multipoles）；无法被吸收的部分位于低 ℓ，那里由宇宙方差（cosmic variance）主导。Ω_{Λ_2} 的信号则与功率谱的整体振幅近乎简并（nearly degenerate）。两参数对 CMB 功率谱的影响均很小，因为它们只是宇宙学常数的高阶热修正（higher-order thermal corrections）。

Conclusion: 由此得到的约束相应较弱：所诱导的偏移在很大程度上被与 h 以及与谱整体归一化之间的简并性所吸收，这正是两个参数均未被探测到的原因。两种互补分析（贝叶斯约束与物理响应分析）共同完成了对该参数空间的完整刻画。

Abstract: Finite-temperature quantum-gravitational corrections to the cosmological constant introduce two additional density parameters, $Ω_{Λ_2}$ and $Ω_{Λ_3}$, the associated terms of which scale as $a^{-4}$ and $a^{-2}$, respectively. In previous work (Papers I and II), these parameters were studied using machine-learning methods that identified a best-fit point. Here we complete the picture with two complementary analyses. First, we perform a Bayesian MCMC analysis using a modified version of \textsc{class} against the Planck 2018 likelihoods. This tells us what the data can constrain once all parameter uncertainties have been marginalized over. Both parameters are consistent with zero, with $Ω_{Λ_3}$ constrained only from one side. Second, we build a CMB pipeline from scratch, following Weinberg's kinetic-theory formulation and validating it against \textsc{class}. This allows us to study the underlying physics. The response analysis carried out with it shows that $Ω_{Λ_3}$ is almost entirely absorbed by a shift in $h$ over the well-measured acoustic multipoles. The part of the signal that cannot be absorbed resides at low $\ell$, where cosmic variance dominates. Meanwhile, the $Ω_{Λ_2}$ signature is nearly degenerate with the overall amplitude of the spectrum. The effects of both parameters on the CMB power spectrum are small, since they represent higher-order thermal corrections to the cosmological constant. The resulting constraints are correspondingly weak: the induced shifts are largely absorbed by degeneracies with $h$ and with the overall normalization of the spectrum. This is the reason why neither parameter is detected.

</details>


### [263] [Gravitational-wave background from supermassive black holes without merger trees: Tidally regulated mergers and direct inference from the NANOGrav 15 yr data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06220)
*Shin'ichiro Ando*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 提出半解析框架，直接积分经 N 体再校准的扩展 Press-Schechter 吸积核，无需蒙特卡洛并合树即可快速计算超大质量黑洞双星产生的引力波背景，并直接对 NANOGrav 15 年数据采样五参数后验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统引力波背景计算依赖蒙特卡洛并合树，计算量大且分辨率受限，难以直接与脉冲星计时数据拟合。本文旨在构建无分辨率限制、可直接采样的快速半解析方法。

Method: 在半解析框架中直接积分 N 体再校准的扩展 Press-Schechter 吸积核；将卫星经模拟校准的潮汐质量损失与 Chandrasekhar 动力学摩擦连续耦合；结合每双星的恒星硬化转折与偏心谱，对 NANOGrav 15 年 Hellings-Downs 相关自由谱（排除 15.8 nHz 频段）直接采样五参数后验。

Result: 卫星在近乎不随红移变化的质比阈值 ξ≃0.04 以下被剥离快于下沉并停滞，其阻力坍塌至恒星核，至 z=0 时 38% 的被吸积卫星未并合；数据偏好黑洞质量归一化比本地校准的黑洞-核球质量关系高 +0.31^{+0.20}_{-0.23} dex；谱与偏心双星或致密恒星环境相容但并非必需。

Conclusion: 该框架无需模拟器或预计算模型库即可直接拟合脉冲星计时数据，结果与纳赫兹背景相对本地 SMBH 校准偏响的既有迹象一致，且对延迟模型、并合核、本地质量校准及其红移演化稳健。

Abstract: We present a semi-analytic framework that computes the gravitational-wave background (GWB) from supermassive black hole (SMBH) binaries by direct integration of an $N$-body-recalibrated extended Press-Schechter accretion kernel, bypassing Monte Carlo merger trees entirely. The calculation is resolution-free and fast enough to be sampled directly against pulsar-timing data with no emulator or precomputed model bank. Between halo accretion and black-hole binary formation, we couple the satellite's simulation-calibrated tidal mass loss continuously to Chandrasekhar dynamical friction. Satellites below a sharp, nearly redshift-independent mass-ratio threshold, $ξ\simeq 0.04$, are stripped faster than they sink and stall: their drag collapses onto the stellar core, and 38% of accreted satellites are unmerged by $z=0$. Combining this population model with per-binary stellar-hardening turnovers and eccentric spectra, we sample the five-parameter posterior directly against the NANOGrav 15 yr Hellings-Downs-correlated free spectrum, excluding the $15.8$ nHz bin, whose excess power a subsequent chromatic-noise reanalysis attributes to pulsar noise. The data prefer a black-hole mass normalization $+0.31^{+0.20}_{-0.23}$ dex above the locally calibrated black-hole-bulge mass relation, consistent with earlier indications that the nanohertz background is loud relative to local SMBH calibrations and robust to the delay model, merger kernel, local mass calibration, and its redshift evolution. The spectrum is compatible with, but does not require, eccentric binaries or dense stellar environments.

</details>

# cond-mat.supr-con [[Back]](#toc)

### [264] [Trapping magnetic flux quanta in superconducting 3D caps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04446)
*Igor Bogush,Vladimir M. Fomin,Oleksandr Dobrovolskiy*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 通过将超导薄膜塑造为三维曲面（如帽形膜），可在均匀外磁场下实现可控涡旋（vortex）捕获；曲率把均匀磁场转化为非均匀法向分量，形成可重构的不对称边缘钉扎势，从而实现磁场可编程的涡旋操控与超导二极管效应（superconducting diode effect）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: II 型超导体（type-II superconductor）中的磁通量子（magnetic flux quantum）对局域环境敏感，可作为电流密度、磁场与钉扎势（pinning landscape）的探针。但在平面薄膜中，单个涡旋难以操控，通常依赖复杂的纳米图案化或扫描探针设备，因此需要新的调控途径。

Method: 采用含时金兹堡-朗道方程（time-dependent Ginzburg-Landau equation）的共形（conformal）表述，对帽形超导膜（cap-shaped superconducting membrane）中的涡旋动力学进行数值模拟，并与平面参照体系进行对比。

Result: 曲率将均匀外磁场转化为非均匀的局域法向分量，产生可由磁场取向调控的可重构不对称边缘钉扎势；旋转磁场即可控制沿边缘被捕获涡旋的数量与位置。此外，不对称钉扎通过依赖电流方向的涡旋捕获产生超导二极管效应。

Conclusion: 三维曲率提供了工程化局域涡旋钉扎的途径，可用于实现具有磁场可编程涡旋捕获与输运功能的磁通器件（fluxonic devices）。

Abstract: Magnetic flux quanta in type-II superconductors are topological excitations of the order parameter whose sensitivity to the local environment makes them probes of current density, magnetic fields, and pinning landscapes. Yet, in planar thin films, individual vortices are difficult to manipulate, often requiring intricate nanopatterning or demanding scanning-probe instrumentation. Here, we show that shaping a superconducting thin film into a three-dimensional curved geometry enables controllable vortex trapping under a spatially uniform applied magnetic field. Employing a conformal formulation of the time-dependent Ginzburg-Landau equation, we simulate vortex dynamics in a cap-shaped superconducting membrane and compare it with a planar reference. Curvature converts the uniform applied magnetic field into a nonuniform local normal component, creating a reconfigurable asymmetric rim-pinning potential tunable by magnetic-field orientation. Rotating the magnetic field therefore controls the number and positions of vortices trapped along the rim. Moreover, the asymmetric pinning produces a superconducting diode effect through current-direction-dependent vortex capture. Three-dimensional curvature thus provides a route to engineer local vortex pinning and realize fluxonic devices with magnetic-field-programmable vortex trapping and transport.

</details>


### [265] [Superconducting landscape of the strongly correlated bilayer Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04855)
*Motoharu Kitatani,Yusuke Nomura,Shiro Sakai,Ryotaro Arita*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文利用动力学顶点近似（dynamical vertex approximation, DVA），在填充（filling）与层间跃迁（interlayer hopping）构成的扩展参数空间中绘制度量了强关联双层 Hubbard 模型（bilayer Hubbard model）的超导相图，识别出中间层间跃迁与中等掺杂的最优超导区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耦合两个强关联层为排斥驱动超导（repulsion-driven superconductivity）提供了新途径，但同一层间耦合也使超导优化变得高度复杂。因此需要在更大的参数空间中明确超导区域的调控规律。

Method: 采用动力学顶点近似（dynamical vertex approximation, DVA），在由填充（filling）和层间跃迁（interlayer hopping）张成的扩展参数空间中确定强关联双层 Hubbard 模型的超导相图。

Result: 在中间层间跃迁与偏离半填充的中等掺杂处存在最优区，体系呈现较高的超导转变温度（$T_{\rm c}$）及带间 $s_{\pm}$ 波能隙结构；趋近半填充时，强自能效应（self-energy effects）通过削减低能谱权重抑制超导倾向；更长程跃迁（longer-range hoppings）引起的费米面畸变（Fermi-surface distortion）可削弱该抑制并提升 $T_{\rm c}$。

Conclusion: 结果表明，填充、层间跃迁与费米面几何（Fermi-surface geometry）三者共同决定了强关联双层 Hubbard 模型中超导区的最优化条件。

Abstract: Coupling two strongly correlated layers opens a distinct route to repulsion-driven superconductivity. Yet the same interlayer coupling makes the optimization of superconductivity highly nontrivial. Using the dynamical vertex approximation, we determine the superconducting landscape in the enlarged parameter space spanned by filling and interlayer hopping. We identify an optimal regime at intermediate interlayer hopping and moderate doping away from half filling, where the system exhibits high superconducting transition temperatures ($T_{\rm c}$) with an interband $s_{\pm}$-wave gap structure. Toward half filling, however, strong self-energy effects can suppress the superconducting tendency by reducing the low-energy spectral weight. We further show that a Fermi-surface distortion induced by longer-range hoppings can weaken this suppression and enhance $T_{\rm c}$. Our results establish how filling, interlayer hopping, and Fermi-surface geometry jointly optimize the superconducting regime in the strongly correlated bilayer Hubbard model.

</details>


### [266] [Paramagnon softening upon doping in oxychloride cuprate superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05160)
*Chafic Fawaz,Léo Gaspard,Laura Chaix,Luca Laveder,Benjamin Bacq-Labreuil,Victor Porée,Flora Yakhou-Harris,Kurt Kummer,Nicholas B. Brookes,Alessandro Nicolaou,Hajime Yamamoto,Yuichi Okazaki,Ikuya Yamada,Masaki Azuma,David Santos-Cottin,Marie-Aude Méasson,Benjamin Lenz,Matteo d'Astuto*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 在氧氯化铜酸盐（oxychloride cuprate）Na$_{x}$Ca$_{2-x}$CuO$_2$Cl$_2$中，顺磁振子（paramagnon）色散（dispersion）随掺杂的演化被系统测定：区边界带宽基本不变，而节点方向出现显著软化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解磁激发（magnetic excitations）与交换相互作用（exchange interactions）随掺杂的演化，是解读高温铜氧化物超导体（high-temperature cuprate superconductors）相图物理的关键。

Method: 对 Na$_{x}$Ca$_{2-x}$CuO$_2$Cl$_2$ 在可达最高掺杂水平下测定顺磁振子色散，并采用包含至第三近邻跃迁项（$t^{\prime\prime}$）的单带 Hubbard 模型（one-band Hubbard model）计算动态自旋结构因子（dynamical spin structure factor）进行对比。

Result: 以 $X$ 区边界 $(0.5,0,0)$ 能量定义的顺磁振子带宽在实验不确定度内保持不变，但出现明显展宽（broadening）；沿节点方向 $Γ-M$（即 $(0,0,0)$ 至 $(0.5,0.5,0)$）能量随掺杂显著软化，最高掺杂时区中点位移可达 $ΔE\sim150$ meV，约为反铁磁相（antiferromagnetic phase）初始值的一半。Hubbard 模型计算复现了观测到的波矢依赖性与布里渊区（Brillouin zone）内的方向差异。

Conclusion: 顺磁振子色散随掺杂呈现各向异性演化：区边界带宽稳定而节点方向软化，该行为可由含 $t^{\prime\prime}$ 的单带 Hubbard 模型定量解释。

Abstract: Understanding how magnetic excitations and exchange interactions evolve with doping is crucial for deciphering the physics that shapes the phase diagram of high-temperature cuprate superconductors. Here, we clearly determine the behavior of the paramagnon dispersion in the oxychloride cuprate Na$_{x}$Ca$_{2-x}$CuO$_2$Cl$_2$ up to the highest doping level. We find that the paramagnon bandwidth, defined by the energy at the $X$ zone boundary $(0.5,0,0)$, remains unchanged within experimental uncertainty, even though it exhibits significant broadening. In contrast, along the nodal direction $Γ-M$ (\textit{i.e.}, from $(0,0,0)$ to $(0.5,0.5,0)$), the paramagnon energy shows a marked softening with doping, with a shift of up to $ΔE\sim150$ meV at the midpoint of the zone for the highest doping, which is about half of its initial value in the antiferromagnetic phase. Calculations of the dynamical spin structure factor for a one-band Hubbard model, including hopping terms up to the third-nearest neighbor ($t^{\prime\prime}$), explain this behavior and accurately reproduce the observed wave vector dependence and directional variation in the Brillouin zone.

</details>


### [267] [Solid-state reaction synthesis of superconducting refractory-metal germanides for Ge/SiGe quantum devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05210)
*Sebastiaan R. Roelofs,Jan Cornelis Wolff,Nick van Loo,Karina L. Hudson,Giordano Scappucci,Greg P. Mazur*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该工作研究了在365-390 °C低温下V、Ta、Nb与Ge及Ge/SiGe异质结构势垒层Si0.2Ge0.8的固相反应，发现Ge上可形成超导转变温度约0.14 K（V-Ge）、2.1 K（Ta-Ge）、2.8 K（Nb-Ge）的层；但异质结构中反应仅局限于界面5-12 nm，残留金属层主导超导响应，界面反应受限成为向埋置量子阱扩展的主要障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将超导体（superconductor）集成到Ge/SiGe异质结构中可推动相关器件应用，但在埋置Ge量子阱（Ge quantum well）附近形成高质量超导层存在困难，因此需要探索低温固相反应（solid-phase reaction）路径。

Method: 在365-390 °C处理温度下，使V、Ta、Nb分别与元素Ge以及Ge/SiGe异质结构的Si0.2Ge0.8势垒层（barrier）发生反应；采用截面电子显微镜（cross-sectional electron microscopy）与低温电学输运（low-temperature electrical transport）进行表征。Ge上沉积时以约2 nm金属增量分段沉积，并在增量间停止生长进行原位退火（in-situ annealing）。

Result: 在390 °C于Ge上沉积可使反应贯穿整个沉积膜，并出现超导转变：V-Ge约0.14 K、Ta-Ge约2.1 K、Nb-Ge约2.8 K；Nb-Ge层在2.1 K时面外临界场（out-of-plane critical field）约0.8 T。在异质结构样品中，互混（intermixing）仅局限于金属-SiGe界面附近约5-12 nm厚的区域，并保留大量残留金属层；显微与输运结果共同表明该残留膜主导了所测得的超导响应。

Conclusion: 研究确立了难熔金属-Ge（refractory-metal-Ge）超导层的低温形成方法，并指出界面反应受限是该方法向埋置量子阱扩展的主要障碍。

Abstract: Solid-phase reactions offer a route to integrate superconductors into Ge/SiGe heterostructures, but forming high-quality superconducting layers near the buried Ge quantum well remains challenging. We investigate reactions of V, Ta, and Nb with elemental Ge and with the Si$_{0.2}$Ge$_{0.8}$ barrier of Ge/SiGe heterostructures at processing temperatures of 365-390 $^\circ$C. Cross-sectional electron microscopy and low-temperature electrical transport show that deposition on Ge at 390 $^\circ$C, in which approximately 2 nm metal increments are separated by growth stops for in-situ annealing, produces reactions throughout the deposited films and superconducting transitions at approximately 0.14 K for V-Ge, 2.1 K for Ta-Ge, and 2.8 K for Nb-Ge. The Nb-Ge layer has an out-of-plane critical field of approximately 0.8 T at 2.1 K. In the heterostructure samples, intermixing is confined to regions approximately 5-12 nm thick near the metal-SiGe interface, leaving a substantial residual metal layer. Microscopy and transport together suggest that this residual film dominates the measured superconducting response. These results establish low-temperature formation of superconducting refractory-metal-Ge layers while identifying the limited interfacial reaction as the principal obstacle to extending this approach toward the buried quantum well.

</details>


### [268] [Competing charge density-wave and superconducting states in a quasi-one dimensional two-band electron-phonon model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05321)
*C. Bourbonnais,A. Ghosh,M. Haguier*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文用重整化群方法研究准一维双能带体系中电荷密度波与超导的竞争与叠加，并给出有限温度相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究电子-声子耦合下电荷密度波（charge density-wave, CDW）与超导（superconductivity）两相在准一维（quasi-one-dimensional）紧束缚能带中的相互影响。

Method: 采用重整化群（renormalization group）方法，在有限温度下建立双截断标度方案，将电子与声子能标嵌入能带内及能带间声子介导相互作用的流方程，并在单圈（one-loop）层次导出覆盖绝热（adiabatic）与反绝热（antiadiabatic）区域的重整化区间。

Result: 得到以电子谱嵌套阻挫（nesting frustration）为变量的有限温度相图；发现CDW与超导有序态可叠加存在，且CDW序涨落对库珀配对（Cooper pairing）的量子临界增强可产生超导穹顶（domes of superconductivity）。

Conclusion: 讨论电子结构的多能带（multiband）特征对超导有序温度、量子临界性（quantum criticality）及同位素效应（isotope effect）的影响。

Abstract: We apply the renormalization group method to examine the interplay between the charge density-wave and superconductivity phases in two quasi-one-dimensional tight-binding electronic bands coupled by the electron-phonon interaction. The electronic and phonon energy scales are embedded in a two-cutoff scaling scheme at finite temperature for the renormalization group flow equations of intra- and inter-band phonon-mediated interactions between electrons. At the one-loop level different regimes of renormalization are derived covering both the adiabatic and antiadiabatic domains for small and large phonon frequencies. Finite temperature phase diagrams as a function of nesting frustration of the electron spectrum are thus obtained. The possibilities for superimposed charge density-wave and superconducting ordered states are given. The emergence of domes of superconductivity resulting from quantum critical reinforcement of Cooper pairing by charge density-wave order fluctuations is found. The impact of the multiband character of the electronic structure on the ordering temperature of superconductivity, quantum criticality, and isotope effect is discussed.

</details>


### [269] [Field-free dual superconducting diode via photon-assisted interference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05536)
*Laura Borgongino,Rubén Seoane Souto,José Luis del Olmo,Ramón Aguado,Alessandro Crippa,Francesco Giazotto,Elia Strambini*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于常规 Al/AlOx/Al 隧道结（tunnel junction）的可动态重构双功能超导二极管（superconducting diode），通过微波双谐驱动（biharmonic driving）与多音光子辅助隧穿（multi-tone photon-assisted tunneling）在同一器件中独立调控无耗散与耗散输运通道，实现从超电流二极管到理想准粒子二极管（quasiparticle diode）的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统半导体二极管在亚开尔文（sub-Kelvin）温度下性能下降，而随低温电子学（cryogenic electronics）持续微缩，亟需兼容低温、低耗散条件的二极管工作方式。现有超导二极管多局限于库珀对（Cooper-pair）或准粒子（quasiparticle）单一输运通道，且常依赖复杂材料堆叠、非对称几何或外部磁场，尚无单一几何对称结能同时整合两类通道的整流功能。

Method: 采用常规 Al/AlOx/Al 隧道结构建几何对称结，利用微波双谐驱动激发多音光子辅助隧穿，从而在同一器件内独立控制无耗散与耗散输运通道；通过改变微波驱动幅度诱导器件在超电流二极管与理想准粒子二极管之间转变，并在电流偏置与电压偏置两种配置下验证交流信号整流。

Result: 调节微波驱动幅度可实现从超电流二极管到理想准粒子二极管的转变，其整流效率（rectification efficiency）超过传统肖特基二极管（Schottky diode）及当前超导二极管。此外，器件在电流偏置与电压偏置下均实现交流信号整流，并演示了兼具零正向电阻与强抑制反向导通的“绝对二极管（absolute diode）”概念验证。

Conclusion: 该可调谐方法为低功耗低温电子架构提供了通用的构建单元（building block），表明单一几何对称的隧道结可在两类输运通道上整合整流功能，并摆脱对复杂材料堆叠、非对称几何或外部磁场的依赖。

Abstract: Non-reciprocal transport underpins key functionalities in signal processing and logic; however, conventional semiconductor diodes exhibit reduced performance at sub-Kelvin temperatures. As cryogenic electronics continue to scale, there is increasing demand for diode operation compatible with low-temperature and low-dissipation conditions. This need has driven the development of alternatives to p-n junctions that rely on non-reciprocal electron transport mechanisms efficient in the deep-cryogenic regime. Superconducting devices constitute a natural low-loss platform, yet most existing superconducting diodes are restricted to either Cooper-pair or quasiparticle transport, often requiring complex material stacks, asymmetric geometries, or external magnetic fields. No single, geometrically symmetric junction has yet integrated rectification across both transport channels to achieve vanishing resistance under forward bias and strongly suppressed conduction under reverse bias. Here, we demonstrate a dynamically reconfigurable dual-function superconducting diode based on a conventional Al/AlOx/Al tunnel junction. Via microwave biharmonic driving, we exploit multi-tone photon-assisted tunneling to independently control dissipationless and dissipative transport channels within the same device. Varying the microwave drive amplitude induces a transition from a supercurrent diode to an ideal quasiparticle diode, achieving rectification efficiencies exceeding those of conventional Schottky diodes and contemporary superconducting diodes. We further demonstrate AC signal rectification under both current-bias and voltage-bias configurations. Finally, we implement a proof-of-concept "absolute diode" that combines zero forward resistance with strongly suppressed reverse conduction. This tunable approach provides a versatile building block for low-power cryogenic electronic architectures.

</details>


### [270] [Giant nonlinearity of kinetic inductance in superconducting hybrid strip](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05903)
*D. Yu. Vodolazov,Yu. P. Korneeva,M. A. Dryazgov,S. S. Ustavschikov,N. V. Porokhov,N. N. Osipov,I. Yu. Pashenkin,A. A. Korneev,M. A. Tarkhov*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 在超导 NbN/Al 混合条带中观测到动电感（kinetic inductance, $L_k$）的巨非线性，电流诱导的 $L_k$ 变化达 50 倍，远超普通超导体的数十百分比量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 普通超导体中电流诱导的 $L_k$ 变化上限仅为数十百分比，限制了基于动电感的器件灵敏度；本文旨在探索能否通过材料与参数设计实现数量级更强的非线性响应。

Method: 采用超导 NbN/Al 混合条带（hybrid strip）结构，利用 Al 层中由邻近效应（proximity effect）诱导的超导电性，并选择特定参数使超导电流 $I$ 对库珀对动量（Cooper pair momentum）$\hbar q$ 的依赖关系在远低于失配对电流（depairing current）的电流 $I^*$ 处出现平台（plateau）。
