Result: 观测到 $L_k$ 的 50 倍变化，形式上对应 $L_k \sim dq/dI \to \infty$（当 $I \to I^*$），即动电感随电流趋于发散式的巨大非线性。

Conclusion: 该效应为构建下一代高灵敏电磁辐射探测器、量热计（calorimeter）、参量放大器（parametric amplifier）与磁强计（magnetometer）提供了新路径。

Abstract: We demonstrate giant nonlinearity of the kinetic inductance $L_k$ in a superconducting NbN/Al hybrid strip. In contrast to ordinary superconductors, where the maximum observed current-induced variation of $L_k$ does not exceed tens of percent, we observe a 50-fold change in $L_k$. Physically, this effect is connected with the proximity-induced superconductivity in the Al layer and the specific choice of parameters for which the dependence of the superconducting current $I$ on the Cooper pair momentum $\hbar q$ exhibits a plateau at a current $I^*$ {\it well below} the depairing current. Formally, this leads to $L_k \sim dq/dI \to \infty$ as $I \to I^*$. We believe that our results open a new route for creating the next generation of highly sensitive detectors of electromagnetic radiation, calorimeters, parametric amplifiers, and magnetometers.

</details>


### [271] [Two superconducting pairing models with a Kitaev spin-liquid glue: microscopic kernel and parity alternation on odd-fold bond stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06157)
*Youping Dai*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文在掺杂 Kitaev 蜂窝晶格（Kitaev honeycomb）上构建了两个超导配对模型，其微观核（kernel）并非假设而是推导得出。二者由在奇偶宇称配对通道间中性的键局域胶（bond-local glue）驱动，手性—宇称关联（chirality–parity linkage）由键星几何（bond-star geometry）决定。奇数扇区模型给出酉三重态（unitary triplet）配对，偶数扇区模型给出 d+id 单重态，并总结出关于奇重键星的通用定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在超越对超导配对核（pairing kernel）的唯象假设，从掺杂 Kitaev 蜂窝模型的微观机制出发推导配对形式，以厘清手性（chirality）与宇称（parity）之间的关联来源及其几何约束。

Method: 模型1利用纯模型的磁通扇区选择定则（flux-sector selection rule）确定核的组元对角、最近邻可分、每键秩一形式，其中 w_α=J_K^2χ_α∝r_αm_α，m_α 由 Hellmann–Feynman 定理给出，r_α≃0.24 通过数值计算获得；模型2则依据区角手性声子（zone-corner chiral phonon）的 C_3 小群选择定则确定核，并显式给出库珀通道扩展的动量约束。

Result: 模型1的自旋顶点态为具有键锁定各向异性 d 矢量的酉三重态，其能隙为键正弦的 Gram 求和，节点方向由几何固定。模型2的电荷顶点配对为 d+id 单重态，宇称分解 f_±=g_±+p_± 投影到 g_±，手性受声子极化支配，C=±4 且 κ_xy/T=2κ_0；反转泵浦螺旋度（pump helicity）会反转陈数（Chern number）、克尔旋转与热霍尔符号。通用定理表明，在奇重键星上单星手性形状因子的角动量阶梯宇称交替，任何由此类键局域形状因子选出的手性态均为几何强制的单重态—三重态复合体，并具有普适振幅比（n=3：d⊕p；n=5：g⊕p 或 d⊕f）；相干双星组合可恢复宇称纯度。混合态的陈数在拓扑边界处服从主导的在壳分量。自旋胶给出 C=±2，电荷核给出 C=±4。

Conclusion: 掺杂 Kitaev 蜂窝模型中的手性超导态可由键局域胶与键星几何共同强制产生，手性—宇称关联源自几何而非微观胶本身；自旋通道与电荷通道分别对应 C=±2 与 C=±4 的拓扑量子态，且其拓扑与热输运响应可通过泵浦螺旋度进行调控。

Abstract: We construct two superconducting pairing models on doped Kitaev honeycomb, whose microscopic kernels are derived rather than assumed. Both are driven by a bond-local glue neutral between the even- and odd-parity pairing channels; the chirality--parity linkage is set by bond-star geometry, not by the microscopic glue. (i) Model~1(odd-parity sector): the kernel follows from the flux-sector selection rule of the pure model, fixing its component-diagonal, nearest-neighbor separable, rank-one-per-bond form, with $w_α=J_K^2χ_α\propto r_αm_α$, where $m_α$ follows from the Hellmann--Feynman theorem and $r_α\simeq0.24$ is computed numerically. The spin-vertex state is a unitary triplet with a bond-locked anisotropic $\mathbf d$ vector; its gap is a Gram sum of bond sines, so nodal directions are fixed by geometry. (ii) Model~2(even): the kernel is fixed by the $C_3$ little-group selection rule of a zone-corner chiral phonon, up to a Cooper-channel extension whose momentum constraint we state explicitly. The charge-vertex pairing is a $d+id$ singlet: the parity decomposition $f_\pm=g_\pm+p_\pm$ projects onto $g_\pm$, so chirality is slaved to the phonon polarization, with $C=\pm4$ and $κ_{xy}/T=2κ_0$. Reversing the pump helicity reverses the Chern number, Kerr rotation, and thermal Hall sign. (iii) General theorem: on odd-fold bond stars, the angular-momentum ladder of a single-star chiral form factor alternates in parity. Every chiral state selected by such a bond-local form factor is a geometrically enforced singlet--triplet composite with universal amplitude ratios ($n=3$: $d\oplus p$; $n=5$: $g\oplus p$ or $d\oplus f$); coherent doubled-star combinations can restore parity purity. The Chern number of the mixed state follows the dominant on-shell component across a topological boundary. The spin glue drives $C=\pm2$; the charge kernel $C=\pm4$.

</details>


### [272] [General boundary conditions for the quasiclassical kinetic equations in the presence of magnetism and unconventional superconductivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06486)
*Tim Kokkeler,Yukio Tanaka,F. Sebastian Bergeret*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该工作通过作用量形式（action formalism）推导了描述自旋活性界面（spin active interfaces）的准经典格林函数（quasiclassical Green's functions）边界条件，适用于至少一侧处于扩散区（diffusive regime）的体系，并给出其在反常邻近效应（anomalous proximity effect）与约瑟夫森耦合（Josephson coupling）中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋活性界面在扩散输运与超导混合结构中的准经典描述需要干净且易处理的边界条件，而既有文献中的表达式在普适性与可计算性上存在不足。该研究旨在以统一形式建立此类边界条件，并覆盖正常态与超导态、电荷/热/自旋多类电流。

Method: 采用作用量形式（action formalism）推导边界条件，并借助 Keldysh-Nambu-spin 形式体系（Keldysh-Nambu-spin formalism）使其在正常态与超导态下均适用。由该构造进一步导出电荷流、热流与自旋流的全计数统计（full counting statistics），并与已有文献的极限情形相联系。

Result: 导出的边界条件可描述正常输运（normal transport）、磁电阻输运（magneto-resistance transport）与自旋进动（spin-precession）。在 p 波超导体与自旋滤波器（spin-filter）结中给出零偏压电导（zero bias conductance）的解析表达式，并揭示其依赖交换场（exchange field）与 d-vector 的相对取向。对约瑟夫森结，发现反常约瑟夫森电流（anomalous Josephson currents）出现的自旋相对取向条件在两自旋劈裂超导体（spin-split superconductors）情形与自旋劈裂超导体-三重态超导体情形显著不同，且与时间反演对称性（time-reversal symmetry）一致。

Conclusion: 作用量形式提供了自旋活性扩散界面的统一、易处理准经典边界条件框架，兼容正常态与超导态并涵盖全计数统计。反常约瑟夫森电流仅在自旋上下透明度均非零且不相等时才伴随二极管效应（diode effect）。

Abstract: We present boundary conditions for the quasiclassical Green's functions describing spin active interfaces between two materials, at least one of which is in the diffusive regime. These boundary conditions are developed with the help of the action formalism, and provide clean and easily tractable expressions. We connect our expressions to previous literature in limiting cases and describe normal transport, magneto-resistance transport and spin-precession through the bound ary. By using the Keldysh-Nambu-spin formalism, our equations are valid both in the normal and superconducting state. The construction through the action formalism also allows us to derive the corresponding full counting statistics for charge currents, thermal currents and spin currents. We use this boundary condition to consider the anomalous proximity effect, and to study the Josephson coupling between triplet and spin-split superconductors through a magnetic interface. We find an analytic expression for the zero bias conductance in junctions with a p-wave superconductor and a spin-filter, and show how it depends on the relative orientation of the exchange field and d-vector. For Josephson junctions, we show that, in agreement with time-reversal symmetry, the conditions on the relative orientations of the spins in the materials for the appearance of anomalous Josephson currents, are distinctly different for two spin-split superconductors and a spin-split superconductor with a triplet superconductor. Moreover, we show that the anomalous Josephson currents are only accompanied by a diode effect if both transparencies for spin up and down are nonzero and distinct.

</details>


<div id='nlin.SI'></div>

# nlin.SI [[Back]](#toc)

### [273] [The Riemann theta function near soliton limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05716)
*Yuji Kodama*

Main category: nlin.SI

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了高亏格（higher genus）情形下 Riemann θ 函数（Riemann θ-function）与 Hirota 型多孤子解（Hirota-type multi-soliton solution）之间的结构相似性，并据此构造 KdV 与 KP 方程的准周期解（quasi-periodic solutions）及准周期背景上的孤子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有结果已知 Jacobi 椭圆函数（Jacobi elliptic function）的平方可表示为 sech² 型单孤子（single soliton）的无穷和，本文旨在探究该结构在高亏格情形下是否存在对应推广，并为其寻找基于 Riemann θ 函数准周期性（quasi-periodicity）的解释。

Method: 从实 Riemann θ 函数出发，考察其对数二阶导数在孤子极限（soliton limit）附近的行为，证明其可被 g 维实空间 R^g 中实且正则的 g-孤子解之和良好近似；并借助 Schottky 群（Schottky group）对相应 Riemann 曲面进行单值化（uniformization）以构造准周期解。

Result: 得到 log θ 的二阶导数在孤子极限下由实正则 g-孤子解之和逼近；该结构诱导出 R^g 的一种镶嵌（tessellation），其瓷砖为由 θ 函数主指数（dominant exponents）超平面分割为 2^g 部分的斜棱柱。据此构造了 KdV 与 KP 方程的准周期解，并讨论了通过收缩部分同调闭链（homological cycles）得到的准周期背景上的孤子。

Conclusion: 高亏格情形下 Jacobi 椭圆函数—单孤子和结构的推广成立，其根源在于 Riemann θ 函数的准周期性；该框架为 KdV、KP 方程的准周期解及准周期背景孤子提供了统一构造途径。

Abstract: It has been known that (the square of) Jacobi elliptic function can be expressed by an infinite sum of single solitons of $\sech^2$ shape. In this paper, we show that there exists a similar structure for higher genus cases. It turns out that this is just a consequence of the quasi-periodicity of the Riemann $θ$-function. More precisely, we show that the second derivative of $\log θ$ with the \emph{real} Riemann $θ$-function of genus $g$ near soliton limit can be well approximated by the \emph{sum} of \emph{real} and \emph{regular} $g$-soliton solutions of Hirota-type in $g$-dimensional real space $\R^g$. This leads to a tessellation of $\R^g$, whose tile is an oblique prism divided into $2^g$ sections by hyper-planes of dominant exponents in the theta function. We apply the results to study quasi-periodic solutions to the KdV and KP equations. We construct the quasi-periodic solutions using the Schottky group, which uniformizes the corresponding Riemann surfaces. We also discuss solitons on quasi-periodic background by pinching some of the homological cycles

</details>


<div id='math.AP'></div>

# math.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [274] [The $L^1$-realization of an elliptic operator with unbounded coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06659)
*Sallah Eddine Boutiah*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文为无界系数（unbounded coefficients）椭圆算子（elliptic operators）在端点情形 $p=1$ 建立 $L^1$-理论，构造出的 $L^1$-实现生成正解析 $C_0$-半群（positive analytic $C_0$-semigroup），且在每个正时刻为紧算子（compact operator），并刻画了生成元定义域（generator domain）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有关于无界系数椭圆算子的半群生成结果（如 Boutiah 等 2018、Canale 等 2016）未覆盖端点情形 $p=1$。本文旨在将该理论拓展至 $L^1$ 端点，从而补全 $L^p$ 框架的完整谱系。

Method: 采用 $L^1$-实现（$L^1$-realization）的途径处理无界系数椭圆算子，并通过加权估计（weighted estimates）来刻画生成元的定义域。

Result: 所构造的 $L^1$-实现生成正解析 $C_0$-半群，该半群在每个正时刻均为紧算子；同时给出生成元定义域的加权估计刻画，并证明其与 $1<p<\infty$ 情形的相应 $L^p$-半群具有一致性。

Conclusion: 该工作将无界系数椭圆算子的半群生成理论延伸至 $p=1$ 端点，建立了 $L^1$-理论并保证与已有 $L^p$（$1<p<\infty$）结果相容，完善了该问题的理论框架。

Abstract: We establish an $L^1$-theory for a class of elliptic operators with unbounded coefficients, extending the semigroup generation results of Boutiah et al. (J. Differential Equations 264 (2018), 2184--2204) and Canale et al. (Ann. Sc. Norm. Super. Pisa Cl. Sci. (5) 16 (2016), 581--601) to the endpoint $p=1$. The $L^1$-realization generates a positive analytic $C_0$-semigroup that is compact for every positive time. We characterize the generator domain through weighted estimates and prove consistency with the corresponding $L^p$-semigroups for $1<p<\infty$.

</details>


### [275] [Complete classification of solutions for spin-1 Bose-Einstein condensates in R](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04411)
*Xiao Luo,Cunxin Wei,Juncheng Wei,Maoding Zhen*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文延续作者此前工作，运用非标准广田双线性方法（non-standard Hirota's bilinear method）、行列式公式与一般左唯一性策略（general left-uniqueness strategy），对 $\mathbb{R}$ 上自旋-1 玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚（spin-1 Bose-Einstein condensate）的解进行完整分类，并给出若干新显式解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋-1 玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚是研究量子磁性（quantum magnetism）与相干自旋动力学的基础平台，其三个塞曼分量（Zeeman components）在粒子数与磁化守恒下由自旋交换相互作用耦合，产生丰富磁相与非线性自旋结构。因此对定态解进行严格分类，有助于厘清可能的自旋构型及其物理来源。

Method: 在 $\mathbb{R}$ 上研究自旋-1 玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚解的分类，采用非标准广田双线性方法、行列式公式（determinant formula）以及一般左唯一性策略。与经典广田方法相比，非标准方法需引入辅助函数（auxiliary functions）构造双线性形式，从而对波函数附加额外约束，以处理更复杂的方程。

Result: 给出了若干新颖显式解，并实现了对该自旋-1 玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚系统解的完整分类。据作者所述，这是首次对该系统解进行系统性完整分类的研究。

Conclusion: 分类结果可给出关于自旋布居（spin populations）、相对相位（relative phases）与磁结构（magnetic textures）的可实验检验预言，并为理解自旋-1 玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚的定态构型提供理论依据。

Abstract: Spin-1 Bose-Einstein condensates provide a fundamental platform for exploring quantum magnetism and coherent spin dynamics. Their three Zeeman components are coupled by spin-exchange interactions under conserved particle number and magnetization, giving rise to rich magnetic phases and nonlinear spin structures; a rigorous classification of stationary states therefore clarifies the possible spin configurations and their physical origins. In this paper, as a continuation of our previous work \cite{LLWZ23,LLWZ24,LLWZ26}, we study the classification of solution for the spin-1 Bose-Einstein condensates in $\mathbb{R}$, by applying the non-standard Hirota's bilinear method, the determinant formula and the general left-uniqueness strategy, we present some novel explicit solution and give the complete classification of the solutions. In comparison with the classical Hirota's method, the non-standard Hirota's method needs to introduce auxiliary functions to construct Hirota's bilinear form, which essentially adds additional constraint on the wave functions, so that it possible to deal with more complex equations. As far as we know, this is the first systematic study the complete classification of solutions to the spin-1 Bose-Einstein condensates. Our classification results yields experimentally testable predictions for spin populations, relative phases, and magnetic textures

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mes-hall'></div>

# cond-mat.mes-hall [[Back]](#toc)

### [276] [Magnon chiral damping beyond the Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03766)
*Darpa Narayan Basu,Subhadip Ghosh,Peter M. Oppeneer,Alexander Mook,Ritwik Mondal*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作基于线性自旋波理论（linear spin-wave theory）与Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert（LLG）方程中的反对称Gilbert阻尼，提出磁振子手性阻尼（magnon chiral damping）的替代理论解释：无需依赖Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya（DM）相互作用，反对称Gilbert阻尼本身即可产生非互易磁振子阻尼。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁振子手性阻尼指阻尼相对于磁振子波矢不对称的非互易现象，此前普遍归因于反对称的Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya自旋相互作用。本文旨在提供一种不依赖该机制、仅由反对称Gilbert阻尼驱动的替代理论解释。

Method: 采用线性自旋波理论（linear spin-wave theory），在Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert（LLG）运动方程框架内引入反对称Gilbert阻尼。首先构建矩形晶格上单子晶格铁磁体（single sublattice ferromagnet）的最小模型，分析标量非局域Gilbert阻尼与反对称阻尼张量的效应；随后将分析扩展至双子晶格（two-sublattice）系统，考察跨子晶格（cross-sublattice）Gilbert阻尼的作用。

Result: 单子晶格情形下，有效磁振子阻尼与系统能量无关（含DM相互作用贡献）；标量非局域Gilbert阻尼使有效阻尼参数α_eff依赖于波矢，在长波极限下呈k的二次依赖，而阻尼张量的反对称分量产生非互易阻尼且其不对称性在长波极限下线性于k，与近期实验一致。双子晶格情形下，仅含标量阻尼时非互易性源于DM相互作用；而跨子晶格反对称阻尼分量即便将DM相互作用参数化设为零，仍可产生非互易磁振子阻尼。值得注意的是，反对称Gilbert阻尼诱导的非互易性比DM相互作用所导致的大约高一个数量级。

Conclusion: 反对称Gilbert阻尼可作为不对称自旋波弛豫（spin-wave relaxation）的独立机制，为磁振子手性阻尼提供超越DM相互作用解释的替代途径，且其非互易效应在量级上显著强于DM相互作用机制。

Abstract: Magnon chiral damping denotes damping that is asymmetric with respect to the magnon wave vector. Such nonreciprocity has been attributed to antisymmetric Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya spin interactions. Here, we provide an alternative theoretical explanation of magnon chiral damping using linear spin-wave theory and antisymmetric Gilbert damping introduced within the Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert equation of motion. First, employing a minimal model of a single sublattice ferromagnet on a rectangular lattice, we show that the effective magnon damping is independent of the system's energy, including contributions from Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interactions. Considering scalar nonlocal Gilbert damping, the effective damping parameter $α_{\rm eff}$ becomes wave-vector dependent. In the long-wavelength limit, $α_{\rm eff}$ acquires a quadratic dependence on $\bf{k}$. Furthermore, the antisymmetric component of the Gilbert damping tensor gives rise to asymmetric (nonreciprocal) magnon damping. We find the asymmetry to be linear in $\bf{k}$ in the long-wavelength limit, consistent with recent experiments. Extending the analysis to a two-sublattice system, we consider cross-sublattice Gilbert damping. In the presence of only scalar Gilbert damping, broken reciprocity in magnon relaxation arises from Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interactions. In contrast, the cross-sublattice antisymmetric component of the Gilbert damping tensor can generate nonreciprocal magnon damping even if the Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction is parametrically set to zero, providing an alternative mechanism for asymmetric spin-wave relaxation. Notably, the nonreciprocity induced by antisymmetric Gilbert damping is roughly an order of magnitude larger than that arising from Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interactions.

</details>


### [277] [From Dirac surface response to Casimir forces in Bumblebee-CFJ electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03768)
*F. C. E. Lima*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在Lifshitz散射形式下研究平面大质量Dirac材料与Lorentz破坏Bumblebee-CFJ矢量扇区耦合的Casimir相互作用。法向Lorentz破坏背景下表面响应保持宇称偶，排斥区源自CFJ诱导的TE-TM模式混合与Dirac片层空间色散响应。Dirac质量抑制相互作用并改变大距离标度，CFJ参数驱动吸引-排斥转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在Lorentz破坏（Lorentz-violating）背景下，Dirac材料间Casimir相互作用（Casimir interaction）的调控机制，特别是排斥性（repulsive）区域的物理起源。传统上排斥性Casimir力常与Hall型电导相关联，本研究试图厘清在宇称偶响应条件下排斥区的替代来源。

Method: 采用Lifshitz散射形式（Lifshitz scattering formalism），将Dirac准粒子的重整化单圈极化张量（renormalized one-loop polarization tensor）作为非局域表面响应（nonlocal surface response），考察其与Bumblebee-CFJ矢量扇区耦合后的效应，并分析法向Lorentz破坏背景下的宇称性质与模式耦合。

Result: 对于法向取向的Lorentz破坏背景，表面响应保持宇称偶（parity-even），故排斥区并非源于Hall型电导（Hall-like conductivity）。排斥性来自CFJ诱导的TE与TM体模式混合（mixing of TE and TM bulk modes）以及Dirac片层空间色散响应（spatially dispersive response）的共同作用。Dirac质量抑制相互作用并改变其大距离标度行为；CFJ参数驱动吸引与排斥区之间的转变。

Conclusion: Dirac材料的Casimir相互作用可通过Dirac质量与CFJ参数进行调控：质量起抑制作用并影响标度律，CFJ参数则决定吸引或排斥性质。排斥性可独立于Hall型电导，由模式混合与空间色散响应产生。

Abstract: We examine the Casimir interaction between planar massive Dirac materials coupled to a Lorentz-violating Bumblebee-CFJ vector sector. The standard renormalized one-loop polarization tensor of the Dirac quasiparticles is used as the nonlocal surface response in the Lifshitz scattering formalism. For the normally oriented Lorentz-violating background, the surface response remains parity-even. Hence, the repulsive regimes do not originate from a Hall-like conductivity. Instead, they arise from the CFJ-induced mixing of TE and TM bulk modes, combined with the spatially dispersive response of the Dirac sheets. Furthermore, we show that the Dirac mass suppresses the interaction and changes its large-distance scaling, whereas the CFJ parameter drives transitions between attractive and repulsive regimes.

</details>


### [278] [Surface scalar plasmons on curved interfaces: a venue for analogue gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03813)
*Matheus E. Pereira*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究探讨弯曲导体-介电界面几何对表面等离激元（surface plasmons, SSPs）有效质量与传播的调控作用，在流体力学极限下将SSP描述为半经典凝胶电子气中的密度扰动，并导出带几何势的有质量Klein-Gordon方程。结果表明特定条件下几何势可使有效质量消失，且界面曲率可作为等离激元传播与局域化的几何调控手段，并可用于类比黑洞的准正规模频率计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于检验弯曲导体-介电界面的几何结构是否能够改变标量表面等离激元（scalar surface plasmons, SSPs）的有效质量与传播行为，从而寻求对等离激元传播的几何调控途径。

Method: 在流体力学极限（hydrodynamic limit）下，将SSP视为半经典凝胶电子气（semiclassical jellium electron gas）中的密度扰动，采用薄层维度约化（thin-layer dimensional reduction）方法，在带几何势（geometric potential）的弯曲界面上导出有质量Klein-Gordon方程，并将该形式应用于已知的类比黑洞（analogue black hole）构型以求解准正规模（quasinormal mode）频率。

Result: 研究发现，在特定条件下几何势允许SSP以消失的有效质量传播；在应用于类比黑洞构型时，确定出相应的准正规模频率。

Conclusion: 界面曲率为等离激元传播与局域化提供了几何调控手段，提示了等离激元输运与基于表面等离激元的类比引力（analogue gravity）实验的新可能性。

Abstract: We investigate whether the geometry of a curved conductor--dielectric interface can modify the effective mass and propagation of scalar surface plasmons (SSPs). In the hydrodynamic limit, we describe SSPs as density perturbations in a semiclassical jellium electron gas. Using a thin--layer dimensional reduction procedure, we derive a massive Klein--Gordon equation for SSPs on the curved interface with a geometric potential. We find that under specific conditions, the geometric potential allows SSP propagation with a vanishing effective mass. We then apply the formalism to a well known analogue black hole configuration, determining the corresponding quasinormal mode frequencies. Our results show that interface curvature provides a geometric handle on plasmon propagation and localization, suggesting new possibilities for plasmon transport and analogue gravity experiments with surface plasmons.

</details>


### [279] [Integrating spin glass dynamics into nanomechanical resonators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03891)
*S. Y. Grebenchuk,T. M. Savilov,Y. W. Ho,A. K. Grebenko,M. Kravtsov,J. M. Wolf,D. A. Litvinov,Z. Yang,P. Cai,S. Ramachandran,R. Duan,L. Zheng,B. Özyilmaz,G. Eda,E. J. G. Santos,M. Koperski,K. S. Novoselov,M. Šiškins*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究将二维范德华（van der Waals）自旋玻璃磁体 NbFeTe₂ 薄膜集成至纳米机电系统（NEMS），实现自旋玻璃动力学的功能性纳米力学读取。通过低温磁力显微镜观测到小时尺度的宏观磁弛豫，并借助膜谐振器将其转导为应变与共振频移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋玻璃（spin glass）为非平衡磁系统，具有显著的慢弛豫、老化及混沌的类突触特性，但其依赖历史的动力学一直难以在功能器件中加以利用。二维范德华自旋玻璃磁体可通过磁性与力学自由度之间的可控耦合提供突破路径。

Method: 采用剥离的薄层 NbFeTe₂ 薄膜，将其集成进纳米机电系统（NEMS），并利用低温磁力显微镜（magnetic force microscopy）进行表征；同时将薄 NbFeTe₂ 引入膜谐振器（membrane resonator）中，用以转导玻璃态磁畴弛豫。

Result: 在数十纳米厚的薄层中确认了稳健的自旋玻璃态；观测到与磁畴团簇玻璃态重组相关的、小时尺度的缓慢宏观磁弛豫；该玻璃态磁畴弛豫被转导为应变，导致膜谐振器的机械共振频率发生移动。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了自旋玻璃动力学的功能性纳米力学读取手段，并建立了将阻挫磁性（frustrated magnetism）集成至 NEMS 的多功能平台。

Abstract: Spin glasses are non-equilibrium magnetic systems with pronounced slow relaxation, ageing, and chaotic, synaptic-like properties, whose unique history-dependent dynamics have remained difficult to harness in functional devices. Two-dimensional van der Waals spin-glass magnets offer a route to overcome this limitation by allowing controlled coupling between magnetic and mechanical degrees of freedom. Here, we demonstrate the integration of a spin-glass functionality in nanoelectromechanical systems (NEMS) using thin exfoliated NbFeTe$_2$ films, which establishes a robust spin-glass state down to a few tens of nanometer-thick layers. Using cryogenic magnetic force microscopy, we reveal characteristic slow, hour-scale macroscopic magnetic relaxation, associated with glassy reorganisation of its magnetic domain clusters. Incorporation of thin NbFeTe$_2$ into membrane resonators allows the transduction of this glassy magnetic domain relaxation into strain, shifting their mechanical resonance frequency. This provides a functional nanomechanical readout of spin-glass dynamics and establishes a versatile platform for integrating frustrated magnetism into NEMS.

</details>


### [280] [Escaping the Composite Fermi Sea: An Incompressible State at Half Filling in Graphene's Lowest Landau Level](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03921)
*Domagoj Perkovic,Ziwei Wang,Steven Herbert Simon*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作对单层与三层石墨烯中零朗道能级交叉附近观测到的偶数分母不可压缩分数量子霍尔态进行首次定量能量学研究，指出朗道能级混合（Landau-level mixing）与超短程相互作用（ultra-short-range interaction）的协同效应可稳定强配对复合费米子相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单层与三层石墨烯实验在零朗道能级（zeroth Landau level）交叉的窄参数区间内观测到偶数分母不可压缩分数量子霍尔态（incompressible even-denominator fractional quantum Hall states），这与半填充下库仑相互作用（Coulomb interaction）预期导致的可压缩复合费米液体（composite Fermi liquid）相矛盾，亟需微观机制解释。

Method: 采用精确对角化（exact diagonalization）与无限密度矩阵重整化群（infinite density-matrix renormalisation group）方法，先研究有限层间距、晶格尺度效应与电子-声子耦合（electron-phonon coupling）产生的超短程修正，再通过随机相近似（random-phase approximation）引入朗道能级混合的屏蔽效应。

Result: 足够强的吸引型短程相互作用可使复合费米液体先进入弱配对 d 波赝自旋单态（d-wave pseudospin-singlet），随后进入强配对态；但微观估计的短程修正过小，不足以单独稳定任一不可压缩相。屏蔽效应显著重塑有效相互作用、抑制弱配对区并增强强配对倾向，在现实的微观参数范围内强配对相可在相当一部分区间被稳定。

Conclusion: 朗道能级混合与超短程相互作用的相互作用是解释单层与三层石墨烯中实验观测不可压缩态的合理微观机制，强配对复合费米子相（strongly paired composite-fermion phase）可作为其候选基态。

Abstract: Recent experiments in monolayer and trilayer graphene have observed incompressible even-denominator fractional quantum Hall states within narrow regions of parameter space associated with crossings of zeroth Landau levels. This is surprising because at half filling the Coulomb interaction in the zeroth Landau level is expected to favour a compressible composite Fermi liquid. Here we provide the first quantitative energetic study of the candidate incompressible phases and of the microscopic mechanisms that can stabilise them near such Landau-level crossings. Using exact diagonalization and infinite density-matrix renormalisation group, we first study ultra-short-range corrections to the Coulomb interaction arising from finite layer separation, lattice-scale effects, and electron-phonon coupling. We find that a sufficiently strong attractive short-range interaction drives the composite Fermi liquid first into a weakly paired \(d\)-wave pseudospin-singlet state and subsequently into a strongly paired state. However, microscopic estimates of these short-range corrections are far too small to stabilise either incompressible phase. We then incorporate Landau-level mixing through screening within the random-phase approximation. Screening substantially reshapes the effective interaction, suppresses the weak-pairing regime, and strongly enhances the tendency toward strong pairing. For a realistic range of microscopic parameters, the strongly paired phase is stabilised over a substantial fraction of that range. Our results identify the interplay of Landau-level mixing and ultra-short-range interactions as a plausible microscopic mechanism for the experimentally observed incompressible states in monolayer and trilayer graphene and point toward a strongly paired composite-fermion phase as their candidate ground state.

</details>


### [281] [Exchange-controlled quantum beats and entanglement in an exciton--bimodal-cavity system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04111)
*Cesar E. P. Villegas,Victor P. Brasil,Pedro A. S. Contri,Kevin Lizárraga,Jonas Maziero*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文在理论上研究了双激子（two-exciton）系统与双模光学腔（bimodal optical cavity）耦合的动力学，并引入激子-激子交换相互作用（exciton-exciton exchange interaction），揭示可调布居转移（population transfer）与纠缠（entanglement）生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探索双激子-双模腔系统中交换相互作用对量子动力学与纠缠的可控调控，为工程化长寿命纠缠和相干激子操作提供理论依据。

Method: 采用缀饰态变换（dressed-state transformations）与Löwdin划分（Löwdin partitioning）推导有效哈密顿量（effective Hamiltonians），并以第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）得到的石墨烯纳米带激子（graphene nanoribbon excitons）能量、跃迁偶极矩和寿命进行参数化。

Result: 识别出三个动力学时间尺度：局域极化激元振荡（local polaritonic oscillations）、长周期缀饰态拍频（dressed-state beats）和直接激子交换。小/中等交换区呈多尺度动力学，拍频通道可在长耗散时窗内维持可观纠缠；大交换区激子哈密顿量主导，经多个相干循环实现近乎完全的布居转移。

Conclusion: 解析表达式给出了拍频周期、转移幅度和相干循环数与交换相互作用、腔失谐（cavity detuning）及光-物质耦合之间的设计规则，确立了在激子-双模腔系统中工程化长寿命纠缠与相干激子操作的可控途径。

Abstract: We theoretically demonstrate tunable population transfer and entanglement generation in a two-exciton system coupled to a bimodal optical cavity, including an exciton-exciton exchange interaction. By using dressed-state transformations and Löwdin partitioning, we derive effective Hamiltonians that identify three dynamical time scales: local polaritonic oscillations, long-period dressed-state beats, and direct excitonic exchange. We apply the framework to graphene nanoribbon excitons, using excitonic energies, transition dipoles, and lifetimes obtained from first-principles calculations to parameterize the model. In the small- and moderate-exchange regimes, the dynamics become multiscale, and the beat channel sustains sizable quantum entanglement over long dissipative time windows. In the large-exchange regime, the excitonic Hamiltonian dominates and drives nearly complete population transfer between excitonic states over many coherent cycles. The resulting analytical expressions provide direct design rules connecting the beat period, transfer amplitude, and coherent-cycle number to the exchange interaction, cavity detuning, and light--matter coupling. Our results establish a controllable route for engineering long-lived entanglement and coherent excitonic operations in exciton--bimodal-cavity systems.

</details>


### [282] [Micromagnet engineering for enhanced addressability of donor-cluster arrays in silicon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04584)
*Hao Wang,Baolong Zhao,Shihang Zhang,Ziliang Jin,Huan Shu,Peihao Huang,Guanyong Wang,Mingchao Duan,Tianluo Pan,Tao Xin,Guangchong Hu,Zhen Tian,Dapeng Yu,Yu He*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种可扩展的硅基量子处理器架构，将STM定义的磷供体团簇阵列与片上微磁体（micromagnet）集成，通过磁场梯度扩展可寻址频率范围，支持超过120个原子自旋量子比特及单天线频率复用寻址。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硅中的磷供体（phosphorus donor）因长相干时间与CMOS工艺兼容性，是容错量子计算的有前景平台；但其规模化瓶颈在于单个自旋量子比特的独立可寻址性（addressability）。

Method: 将扫描隧道显微镜（STM）定义的磷供体团簇阵列与片上微磁体集成，利用微磁体产生磁场梯度；并通过微磁模拟优化微磁体几何结构，最后以磁测量进行验证。

Result: 相比仅依赖超精细（hyperfine, HF）相互作用随机性导致的频率差异的寻址方案，微磁体提供更宽的可用频率范围；该结构兼容定制阵列设计，可集成超过120个原子自旋量子比特，并借单一微波天线实现频率复用，显著减少扇出寻址线（fan-out addressing lines）的开销；磁测量结果与微磁模拟总体一致。

Conclusion: 该设计为基于原子量子比特的可扩展硅量子计算奠定了关键基础。

Abstract: Phosphorus donors in silicon are one of the promising platforms for fault-tolerant quantum computing, owing to their long coherence times and compatibility with complementary metal-oxide-semiconductor (CMOS) technology. However, a key bottleneck for scaling this platform lies in the independent addressability of individual spin qubits. Here, we present a scalable quantum processor architecture that integrates scanning tunneling microscope (STM)-defined phosphorus donor-cluster arrays with on-chip micromagnets. By generating magnetic field gradients, the micromagnets enable a broader addressable frequency range compared to the addressing scheme based solely on variations in hyperfine (HF) interactions, which arise from the randomness of intra-cluster configurations. Through micromagnetic simulations to optimize micromagnet geometry, we demonstrate that these structures are compatible with customized array designs, facilitating the integration of over 120 atomic spin qubits and enabling frequency multiplexing with a single microwave antenna, which greatly reduces the overhead of fan-out addressing lines. This design is further validated via magnetic measurements, which generally align with our micromagnetic simulations. These results establish a critical foundation for the development of scalable silicon quantum computing with atomic qubits.

</details>


### [283] [Spatiotemporal imaging of microwave magnetic fields via magnonic coherent splitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04618)
*C. K. Wei,Z. J. Chen,J. T. Song,S. H. Ma,W. H. Liu,J. H. Wu,Z. W. Huang,Jinwei Rao,Wei Lu,Bimu Yao*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作利用钇铁石榴石（yttrium iron garnet, YIG）中的亚铁磁序（ferrimagnetic order）与泵浦诱导磁振子模式（magnon mode）的相干耦合，将目标磁场幅度转化为可全微波读出的谱分裂，实现室温、无光学元件的时空磁场成像，灵敏度达58 pT/√Hz、瞬态响应时间130 ns。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时空微波磁场成像探针难以同时兼顾定标光谱读出、无光学（optics-free）操作与室温瞬态成像三项能力，因此需要发展一种可定标、可扩展的磁场探测方案。

Method: 以钇铁石榴石中的亚铁磁序支持泵浦诱导磁振子模式的相干耦合，把目标磁场幅度转换为谱分裂并用全微波方式读出；通过在位置与延迟上采样定标后的分裂值重建磁场时空分布，并结合时间分辨频率梳光谱（time-resolved frequency-comb spectroscopy）实现瞬态场重构。

Result: 连续波测量灵敏度为58 pT/√Hz，并在不同功率下恢复相位；瞬态场重构响应时间为130 ns。共面波导（coplanar-waveguide）成像验证了模式分裂读出的磁选择性，测量分布与模拟磁场分布一致；在微波放大器中分辨出非均匀开关动力学，并检测到开路触点故障引起的下游磁场抑制。

Conclusion: 磁振子相干分裂（magnonic coherent splitting）为功能器件中时空磁场演化的无光学成像提供了一条可扩展的技术路径。

Abstract: Spatiotemporal microwave magnetic-field imaging reveals current flow in high-frequency circuits and nonequilibrium spin dynamics, yet probes rarely combine calibrated spectral readout, optics-free operation and transient mapping at room temperature. Here ferrimagnetic order in yttrium iron garnet supports coherent coupling from a pump-induced magnon mode, converting target-field amplitude into a spectral splitting with all-microwave readout. Sampling the calibrated splitting over position and delay reconstructs spatiotemporal imaging of magnetic fields. Continuous-wave measurement reaches a sensitivity of 58 pT/$\sqrt{\mathrm{Hz}}$ and recovers phases across various powers. Combined with time-resolved frequency-comb spectroscopy, the method reconstructs transient fields with a 130-ns response time. Coplanar-waveguide imaging validates the magnetic selectivity of the mode-splitting readout, where measured maps agree with simulated magnetic-field distribution. In a microwave amplifier, our reconstruction resolves nonuniform switching dynamics and detects downstream field suppression from an open-contact fault. Magnonic coherent splitting provides a scalable route for optics-free imaging of spatiotemporal field evolution in functional devices.

</details>


### [284] [Electron-phonon control of obstructed and quantum spin Hall topology in an altermagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04684)
*Kuntal Bhattacharyya,Bilal Tanatar,Saurabh Basu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文从理论上展示，在具有零净磁化与非相对论性自旋劈裂能带的 d 波交替磁体（d-wave altermagnet, AM）中，电子-声子耦合（electron-phonon coupling, EPC）可调控受阻碍原子绝缘相与一阶拓扑相之间的相互竞争与转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探索交替磁体（altermagnet）这一兼具自旋劈裂与零净磁化特性的非常规磁性平台中，拓扑绝缘相的可控切换机制。作者希望借助电子-声子耦合（EPC）为声子调控的自旋电子学（spintronics）功能提供理论路径。

Method: 方法上，将 d_{x^{2}-y^{2}} 波交替磁体嵌入量子自旋霍尔（quantum spin Hall, QSH）绝缘体，即 Bernevig-Hughes-Zhang（BHZ）型拓扑绝缘体（topological insulator, TI）母体中，并引入电子-声子耦合（EPC）以考察极化子（polaron）效应对 Wannier 中心与有效边缘拓扑的重整。

Result: 结果显示，当交替磁序超过临界强度时，母体 QSH 态被重构为受阻碍原子绝缘体（obstructed atomic insulator, OAI），表现为非平庸体极化、量子化的分数角电荷及特征性带隙内边界态，同时自旋陈数（spin Chern number）消失。引入足够强的 EPC 后，极化子缀饰重组 Wannier 中心与边缘拓扑，削弱 OAI 相并恢复螺旋 QSH 边缘模；EPC 进一步增强则最终使系统进入平庸绝缘体。

Conclusion: 结论是，该研究揭示了交替磁体中拓扑上不同的绝缘相之间的可控切换机制，为非常规磁性平台中声子调控的自旋电子学功能提供了直接途径。

Abstract: We theoretically demonstrate control over the interplay between obstructed atomic insulating and first-order topological phases via electron-phonon coupling (EPC) in a $d$-wave altermagnet (AM) that hosts nonrelativistic spin-split bands with zero net magnetization. By embedding a $d_{x^{2}-y^{2}}$-wave AM in a quantum spin Hall (QSH) insulator, we show that an altermagnetic order beyond a critical strength reconstructs the parent QSH state of a Bernevig-Hughes-Zhang (BHZ)-type topological insulator (TI) into an obstructed atomic insulator (OAI), characterized by nontrivial bulk polarization with quantized fractional corner charges, accompanied by characteristic in-gap boundary states, while the spin Chern number vanishes. Upon incorporating sufficiently strong EPC, the polaronic dressing reorganizes the Wannier centers and the associated effective edge topology, thereby weakening the altermagnetism-induced OAI phase and restoring the helical QSH edge modes. Further, EPC eventually drives the system into a trivial insulator. By uncovering a controllable switching mechanism between topologically distinct insulating phases in AMs, our study establishes a direct route toward phonon-tuned spintronic functionalities in unconventional magnetic platforms.

</details>


### [285] [Anisotropic Friedel oscillations and isoperimetric transport in two-dimensional free lattice Fermi seas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04724)
*Guillermo R. Zemba*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文基于二维费米海（2d Fermi seas）在平坦轨形（flat orbifolds）ℝ²/Γ 上的拓扑分类，探讨两个物理后果：介观尺度下密度矩阵的定向各向异性与实空间衍射图案，以及动量空间填充与电输运之间的几何对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期基于离散对称群 Γ 的平坦轨形 ℝ²/Γ 对自由粒子二维费米海的拓扑分类，其物理后果尚待探索。本文旨在揭示该分类在介观密度行为与电导输运两方面的可观测效应。

Method: 研究费米长度与（无穷）热长度所围介观窗口内单粒子密度矩阵的空间密度行为；并对 p4m 与 p6m 晶格平坦轨形作具体分析。同时利用几何测度论（geometric measure theory）建立动量空间填充与纵向电导 σxx 的联系，通过等周参数（isoperimetric parameter）ℐ 给出平均场表达。

Result: 单粒子密度矩阵表现出强烈的定向各向异性，编码实空间衍射图案（即费米海的 Fraunhofer 型图样），以各向异性 Friedel 振荡形式反映背景晶格 Γ 的点群对称性。纵向电导满足标度律 σxx ∝ 1/√ℐ，该拓扑输运公式自然包含此前所述“几何绝缘体”的电导消失（σxx → 0）。

Conclusion: 等周参数 ℐ 可作为导体中拓扑相与几何相的有效描述符；所建立的形式统一了拓扑分类与电输运行为。

Abstract: Two physical consequences of the recent topological classification of 2d Fermi seas for free particles based on flat orbifolds $\mathbb{R}^2/Γ$ of discrete symmetry $Γ$, are explored. The first is an study of the spatial density behavior inside the mesoscopic window enclosed by the Fermi and (infinite) thermal lengths. It is shown that the single-particle density matrix exhibits a sharp directional anisotropy, encoding a real space diffraction pattern, {\it i.e.}, a Fraunhofer-like diagram of the Fermi sea, that reveals the point-group symmetries of the background lattice $Γ$ in the form of anisotropic Friedel oscillations. As examples, the special cases of the $p4m$ and $p6m$ crystallographic flat orbifolds are treated in some detail. The second physical consequence is the establishment of an explicit geometric correspondence between momentum-space packing and electrical transport. A mean-field expression for the longitudinal electrical conductivity $σ_{xx}$ as a function of the {\it isoperimetric parameter} $\cal{I}$ of the occupied orbifold domain by utilizing geometric measure theory is presented, satisfiying the scaling law $ σ_{xx} \propto 1/\sqrt{\cal{I}}$. The topological transport formula naturally incorporates the vanishing conductivity ($σ_{xx} \to 0$) of the previously described ``geometric insulators,'' promoting the isoperimetric parameter as an efficient descriptor for topological and geometric phases in conductors.

</details>


### [286] [From Nested Soliton Crystals to Skyrmion Crystals in Chiral Magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05090)
*Sven Bjarke Gudnason,Muneto Nitta*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文系统研究了含Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用、面内各向异性项与面外Zeeman磁场的手性磁模型参数空间，绘制相图并发现嵌套手性孤子晶格基态及多种激发态复合孤子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确阐述研究动机，仅指出对该手性磁模型的参数空间进行详尽研究（exhaustive study）。

Method: 采用对手性磁模型（chiral magnetic model）参数空间的穷举式研究，模型包含Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用（Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction）、面内各向异性项（in-plane anisotropy term）与面外Zeeman磁场（out-of-plane Zeeman magnetic field），并据此绘制相图（phase diagram）。

Result: 在相图特定角落发现以“手性孤子晶格上嵌套手性孤子晶格”（chiral soliton lattice hosted on a chiral soliton lattice）为基态；向斯格明子晶格（skyrmion crystal lattice）相的转变为平滑渡越（smooth crossover）而非相变（phase transition）；并发现反斯格明子（anti-skyrmion）与双麦纟（bimeron）或反双麦纟（anti-bimeron）共存的激发复合孤子态（excited composite soliton states）。

Conclusion: 该模型相图包含嵌套手性孤子晶格基态、经平滑渡越连接的斯格明子晶格相，以及多种激发复合孤子态，拓展了对该手性磁系统基态与激发态的认识。

Abstract: We perform an exhaustive study of the parameter space of the chiral magnetic model with a Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction, an in-plane anisotropy term and an out-of-plane Zeeman magnetic field. We map out the phase diagram for the model and find a chiral soliton lattice hosted on a chiral soliton lattice as the ground state in a particular corner of the phase diagram. There is a smooth crossover, as opposed to a phase transition, to the skyrmion crystal lattice phase. Finally, we also discover excited composite soliton states such as an anti-skyrmion coexisting with a bimeron or an anti-bimeron.

</details>


### [287] [Nonlinearity-induced Topological Phase Transitions in One-dimensional Systems with Chiral Symmetry: A Real-Space Treatment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04984)
*Liu Bi Tao,Shilpi Roy,Jiangbin Gong*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作以周期 Su-Schrieffer-Heeger（SSH）链为参照，在实空间直接研究非线性（nonlinearity）与准周期势（quasiperiodic potential）共同作用下拓扑相变（topological phase transition）是否在缺乏平移对称性（translational symmetry）时仍然存在。结果表明确立相变行为强烈依赖于准周期势强度：弱准周期势支持由平庸相到拓扑相的转变，中等强度则不出现拓扑相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性已被证明可在周期系统中诱导拓扑相变，但此类相变在缺少平移对称性的体系中能否持续尚不明确，因此需要直接在实空间中检验非线性与准周期性的相互作用。

Method: 以周期性 SSH 链作为理解准周期性效应的参照，直接在实空间（real space）中分析非线性本征态及边界局域化（boundary localization）随非线性强度与准周期势强度的变化。

Result: 随着非线性强度增大，弱准周期势支持从平庸相到拓扑相的转变；中等准周期势区间不出现拓扑相变；强准周期势下，显著的边界局域化可在非线性诱导的拓扑转变之前就已形成。

Conclusion: 非线性与准周期势在诱导拓扑相变中存在复杂的相互影响，且非线性本征态的边界局域化本身不足以作为拓扑边缘态（topological edge states）的明确判据。

Abstract: Nonlinearity can induce topological phase transitions in periodic systems. It is hence of considerable interest to examine if such transitions persist in the absence of translational symmetry. We address this question directly in real space, using the periodic Su-Schrieffer-Heeger (SSH) chain as a reference for understanding the effect of quasiperiodicity. How nonlinearity drives a chain towards a topological phase transition is found to depend strongly on the strength of the quasiperiodic potential. As the nonlinearity strength increases, we find that weak quasiperiodicity supports a transition from a trivial to a topological phase, whereas the intermediate regime does not have a topological phase transition. Under strong quasiperiodicity, pronounced boundary localization can develop well before a nonlinearity-induced topological transition. Our results show how nonlinearity may interplay with a quasiperiodic potential in inducing topological phase transitions and that boundary localization of nonlinear eigenstates alone may not be taken as a clear signature of topological edge states.

</details>


### [288] [Multi-bit Ferroelectric-NAND for High-throughput Massive Database Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05002)
*Prasanna Venkatesan,Tanvir H. Pantha,Po-Kai Hsu,Sumukh Pinge,Zheyu Li,Chinsung Park,Priyankka Ravikumar,Hari Jayasankar,Lance Fernandes,Weihong Xu,Zihan Xia,Flavio Ponzina,Keming Fan,Amrit Garlapati,Huy Tran,Taeyoung Song,Mengkun Tian,Hang Chen,Winston Chern,Kijoon Kim,Kwangyou Seo,Suhwan Lim,Kwangsoo Kim,Wanki Kim,Daewon Ha,Duygu Kuzum,Shimeng Yu,Mingu Kang,Tajana Rosing,Sourav Dutta,Asif Khan*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作利用超高密度、低能耗的铁电 NAND（FE-NAND）存储器实现原位计算，在单胞层面演示多级单元（MLC）点积操作，并通过校准的物理仿真完成超向量（HV）间汉明相似度计算，在蛋白质组学检索任务中实现近1000倍加速与逾10000倍能效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据密集型应用（从蛋白质组学到自主系统）对大规模数据库检索的需求增长，暴露出冯·诺依曼（von Neumann）架构在存储带宽与能耗方面的根本限制。超维计算（hyperdimensional computing）虽提供鲁棒且可并行的框架，但其实际落地受制于高存储需求。

Method: 制备具有宽存储窗口、抗干扰（disturb resilience）与稳健保持（retention）特性的四电平（quad-level）FE-NAND 串，并以其为构建模块，在单胞层面实验演示原位多级单元（MLC）点积操作；进一步通过经实验校准的物理基仿真，实现参考超向量与查询超向量之间的汉明相似度（Hamming similarity）计算。

Result: 该平台借助超维计算固有的容错性，即使在高逻辑电平（TLC、QLC）下仍可实现超过90%的检索精度。在TB级数据集的蛋白质组学开放修饰搜索（Open Modification Search, OMS）任务上基准测试，相较现有方案取得近1000倍加速和超过10000倍的能效提升。

Conclusion: 结果表明 FE-NAND 可作为面向大规模、高维数据处理的可行存内计算（in-storage compute）架构。

Abstract: The growing demand for large-scale database search in data-intensive applications, ranging from proteomics to autonomous systems, has exposed fundamental limitations in von Neumann architectures due to memory bandwidth and energy constraints. Hyperdimensional (HD) computing offers a robust and parallelizable framework for such tasks, but its practical implementation remains challenged by high memory demands. Ultra-high-density, energy-efficient ferroelectric NAND (FE-NAND) memory provides a potential solution by enabling in-situ computation. We fabricate quad-level FE-NAND strings with wide memory windows, disturb resilience, and robust retention. Using these planar FE-NAND strings as building blocks, we experimentally demonstrate in-situ multi-level cell (MLC) dot product operations at the single-cell level and, using experimentally calibrated physics-based simulations, demonstrate Hamming similarity calculations between reference and query hypervectors (HV). This platform leverages the inherent error tolerance of HD computing to achieve >90% search accuracy even at high logic levels (TLC, QLC). When benchmarked on Open Modification Search (OMS) tasks in proteomics with TB-scale datasets, our system shows nearly 1,000x speedup and over 10,000x energy efficiency improvement compared to incumbent solutions. These results establish FE-NAND as a viable in-storage compute architecture for large-scale, high-dimensional data processing.

</details>


### [289] [Radio-frequency electron cascade in semiconductor quantum dots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05206)
*Tara Murphy,Lorenzo Peri,Giovanni A. Oakes,Jacob F. Chittock-Wood,Ross C. C. Leon,Fabrizio Berritta,Amber Heskes,David Wise,Anasua Chatterjee,Ferdinand Kuemmeth,M. Fernando Gonzalez-Zalba*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究在半导体量子点（semiconductor quantum dots）中探讨射频（radio-frequency, rf）驱动下的关联共隧穿（correlated cotunneling）过程，并将其应用于色散量子点读出（dispersive quantum-dot readout）。通过理论建模与两种晶圆厂制造的硅器件实验，证明了rf驱动可诱导关联循环单电子跃迁并增强电荷极化。工作确立了rf电子级联读出（rf electron-cascade readout）的工作原理，为提升半导体量子计算架构中的读出保真度提供了路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共隧穿（cotunneling）作为一种高阶隧穿机制，可连接初始与最终电荷态，并在量子点阵列中实现跨多个隧道结（tunnel junctions）的同步电荷运动，使原本被库仑阻塞（Coulomb blockade）抑制的跃迁成为可能。作者在此基础上，基于早前的原理验证（proof-of-principle）演示，进一步研究rf驱动关联共隧穿及其在色散读出中的应用，以提升读出性能。

Method: 结合理论建模（theoretical modeling）与实验测量，在两种不同晶圆厂制造的硅（silicon）器件中开展研究；建立了一个通用的rf电子级联读出理论框架，该框架利用具有三个及以上量子点的量子点系统中的共隧穿事件；并将实验演示扩展到栅极感应（gate-sensed）的器件架构。

Result: 外部rf驱动可诱导关联的循环单电子跃迁（correlated cyclic single-electron transitions），从而增强系统的电荷极化（charge-polarization）；rf电子级联读出可产生相对信号增强（relative signal enhancement）；实验演示成功扩展至栅极感应的器件架构。

Conclusion: 研究确立了rf电子级联读出的工作原理，并为在半导体量子计算架构（semiconductor quantum computing architectures）中提高色散读出保真度（dispersive readout fidelity）提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Electron cotunneling in semiconductor quantum dots is a higher-order tunneling mechanism that connects initial and final charge states through energetically inaccessible virtual intermediate configurations. In quantum-dot arrays, such processes can produce simultaneous charge motion across multiple tunnel junctions and enable transitions that would otherwise be suppressed by Coulomb blockade. Here, building on an earlier proof-of-principle demonstration, we investigate radio-frequency (rf)-driven correlated cotunneling and its application to dispersive quantum-dot readout. Using both theoretical modeling and experimental measurements in two different foundry-fabricated silicon devices, we show that an external rf drive can induce correlated cyclic single-electron transitions, resulting in enhanced charge-polarization of the system. We develop a general theoretical framework for rf electron-cascade readout, which exploits cotunneling events in quantum-dot systems with three or more dots to produce a relative signal enhancement, and extend its experimental demonstration to a gate-sensed device architecture. Our results establish the operating principles of rf electron-cascade readout and provide a route to increased dispersive readout fidelity in semiconductor quantum computing architectures.

</details>


### [290] [New dimensions in magnetism and superconductivity via 3D nanoprinting with focused electron and ion beams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05443)
*Claire Donnelly,Rosa Córdoba,Amalio Fernández-Pacheco*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: Error


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Error

Method: Error

Result: Error

Conclusion: Error

Abstract: The extension of nanoscale quantum materials to the third dimension brings new possibilities to geometrically tune emergent properties, emulating or indeed going beyond the physics of bulk materials, with exciting implications for high-density technological integration. Realising such three-dimensional architectures requires nanofabrication techniques that not only enable complex 3D geometries, but also deliver functional quantum materials with emergent phenomena. Recent advances in focused electron- and ion-beam induced deposition (FEBID and FIBID) have made 3D nanopatterning of functional materials in complex geometries increasingly feasible, paving the way for the experimental realisation of new phenomena and device prototyping. In this review, we discuss the use of FEBID and FIBID 3D nanopatterning of superconducting and magnetic materials. We address the underlying mechanisms, and capabilities of these techniques, focusing on the physical phenomena that become accessible. Considering the resolution of the techniques in the context of the fundamental lengthscales of the materials, we discuss the emergent effects that arise due to three dimensional geometries in magnetic and superconducting nanostructures, and highlight perspectives for future developments of the techniques.

</details>


### [291] [Fragmented and symmetry-restored condensates in a three-site Bose ring: exchange versus umklapp](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05469)
*A. Siddiki*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文研究三格点环上的 $N$ 个玻色子，证明随密度辅助跃迁增强会出现从 $q=0$ 凝聚体到近似均分于 $\pm1$ 动量模态的基态一级相变，并借助 umklapp 散射的有无区分出碎裂态与数压缩驻波凝聚体两种情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三格点环中动量模 $q=\pm1$ 存在等价关系 $2\cdot(+1)\equiv-1$，使 umklapp 散射（umklapp scattering）成为可开关的自由度，为研究少模玻色体系中碎裂（fragmentation）与凝聚的竞争提供了可控平台。

Method: 在含最近邻跃迁、反号密度辅助跃迁与在位相互作用的哈密顿量下进行精确对角化（exact diagonalisation），规模达 $N=400$；通过关闭或保留 umklapp 散射、改变在位相互作用，并与 Nozières 交换论证及 Gross-Pitaevskii（GP）平均场结果对比。

Result: 随密度辅助项增大能带反转，基态经历由 $q=0$ 凝聚体到 $\avg{n_{+1}}=\avg{n_{-1}}\simeq N/2$ 态的一级相变，回避交叉（avoided-crossing）能隙随 $N$ 以快于指数的速度闭合；该态在对称本征态下于 Penrose-Onsager 意义下碎裂，领头阶能量等于 GP 驻波。无 umklapp 时 $n_{+1}-n_{-1}$ 守恒，本征态构成转子塔（rotor tower），仅当 $\pm1$ 相互作用为吸引时相变精确导致碎裂，否则形成单一载流凝聚体；有 umklapp 时驻波被钉扎于三个位置，三个动量扇区随 $N$ 指数简并，$q=0$ 模的相干混合扩大驻波稳定区间。

Conclusion: umklapp 散射的存在与否决定了相变终态的物理性质：无 umklapp 时可获得 Nozières 交换论证预言的稳固碎裂态，有 umklapp 时基态则为数压缩且动量投影的驻波凝聚体，而非稳固碎裂态。

Abstract: We study $N$ bosons on a three-site ring with nearest-neighbour hopping, density-assisted hopping of the opposite sign, and an on-site interaction, written equivalently as three momentum modes $q=0,\pm1$ with a channel-dependent quartic vertex. As the density-assisted term grows it inverts the band, and exact diagonalisation up to $N=400$ shows a first-order ground-state transition from the $q=0$ condensate to a state with $\avg{n_{+1}}=\avg{n_{-1}}\simeq N/2$; the avoided-crossing gap closes faster than exponentially with $N$. In its symmetric eigenstates this state is fragmented in the Penrose-Onsager sense, and at leading order in $N$ it has the energy of a Gross-Pitaevskii (GP) standing wave, as expected for a few-mode mean-field system. We distinguish two cases by switching off umklapp scattering, which is available because $2\cdot(+1)\equiv-1$ on three sites, and by varying the on-site interaction. Without umklapp, $n_{+1}-n_{-1}$ is conserved, the eigenstates form a rotor tower whose lowest member is a fragmented Fock state, and the transition leads to fragmentation exactly when the $\pm1$ interaction is attractive, as predicted by Nozières' exchange argument with the sign of the interaction reversed; otherwise the system forms a single current-carrying condensate. With umklapp, the standing wave is pinned to three positions, the three momentum sectors become degenerate exponentially in $N$, and coherent admixture of the $q=0$ mode stabilises the standing wave over a wider range; the exact ground state is then a number-squeezed, momentum-projected standing-wave condensate rather than a robustly fragmented state.

</details>


### [292] [Density Functional Theory for 2D Transition-Metal Dichalcogenides with Extended Hubbard Parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05496)
*Xue Li,Suad Alshammari,Igor Rozhansky,James G. McHugh,Vladimir Falko*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过在 PBE 泛函中引入金属位点（U）与金属—硫族元素（V）Hubbard 修正，并以实验自旋轨道劈裂（spin–orbit splitting）优化参数，实现对 TMD 带边电子结构的高效定量计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过渡金属二硫族化物（transition metal dichalcogenides, TMDs）带边电子结构的准确建模是其光学、电子与自旋电子学（spintronics）性质理解和应用的前提，而标准 PBE 交换关联泛函（exchange-correlation functional）在定量精度上不足。

Method: 在标准 PBE 泛函上补充金属位点 U 与金属—硫族元素 V 的 Hubbard 修正，以调控带边态的轨道组成；针对 MoS2、MoSe2、WS2、WSe2，通过与实验测量的导带与价带边自旋轨道劈裂对比来优化 U 与 V 参数。

Result: 所得到的优化参数不仅再现实验自旋轨道劈裂，还同时改善了带隙（band gap）以及菱形堆垛双层（rhombohedral bilayers）中铁电电势降（ferroelectric potential drop）与实验值的一致性。

Conclusion: 该方案为单层与多层 TMD 的定量精确、高通量（high-throughput）计算提供了高效且可扩展的框架，适用于二维材料数据库建设及大规模电子与自旋电子器件建模。

Abstract: Accurate modelling of the band-edge electronic structure of transition metal dichalcogenides (TMDs), such as MoS$_2$, is essential for understanding their optical, electronic and spintronic properties and enabling future applications. Here, we supplement the standard PBE exchange-correlation functional with on-metal ($U$) and metal--chalcogen ($V$) Hubbard corrections, which can control the orbital composition of band-edge states and consequently improve the quantitative accuracy of various properties. For MoS$_2$, MoSe$_2$, WS$_2$, and WSe$_2$, we optimise the $U$ and $V$ parameters by comparison with experimentally measured spin--orbit splittings at the conduction and valence band edges, and show that these optimal choices also improve agreement of the computed band gap, and ferroelectric potential drop in rhombohedral bilayers, with experimentally measured values. This provides a highly efficient, scalable framework for quantitatively accurate, high-throughput calculations of monolayer and multilayer TMDs, suitable for implementation in 2D-materials databases, as well as large-scale electronic and spintronic device modelling.

</details>


### [293] [Terahertz nanoscopy of quantum-geometric photovoltaics in bilayer graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05620)
*Bogdan Borodin,Jiangxu Li,Xuanpu Zhang,Aleksei Karaborchev,Som Subhra Guria,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Roshan Krishna Kumar,Fan Zhang,Petr Stepanov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作发展了低温、偏振敏感的深亚波长太赫兹显微术（terahertz microscopy），并在双栅 AB 双层石墨烯（AB bilayer graphene）中实现空间分辨的体光伏效应（bulk photovoltaic effect, BPVE）观测。测量揭示与晶格对称性和量子几何（quantum geometry）相关的二重角对称与位移矢量方向，并可通过载流子密度与温度演化探测三角度扭曲（trigonal warping）与电子-空穴不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统远场测量无法将 BPVE 与接触及界面诱导的光热电势（photothermoelectric）信号区分开。BPVE 是量子几何与非线性光-物质相互作用的直接联系，因此需要具备高空间分辨与偏振分辨能力的测量手段以分离本征信号。

Method: 构建低温、偏振敏感的深亚波长太赫兹显微系统，并将其应用于双栅 AB 双层石墨烯。在带隙与太赫兹光子能量近共振条件下，进行偏振依赖的测量以获得增强的光导率与空间分辨的 BPVE 信号。

Result: 在近共振条件下观察到增强的光导率和空间分辨的 BPVE。偏振依赖测量显示特征性的二重角对称（twofold angular symmetry）以及独特的位移矢量（shift-vector）方向，二者均受晶体对称性与量子几何支配。BPVE 随载流子密度和温度的演化揭示了三角度扭曲与电子-空穴不对称性的特征。

Conclusion: 结果与理论一致，表明所建立的 BPVE 太赫兹纳米显微术（terahertz nanoscopy）可作为光学透镜，用于探究量子材料中能带与量子几何之间的相互作用。

Abstract: The bulk photovoltaic effect (BPVE) provides a direct link between quantum geometry and nonlinear light-matter interactions, yet conventional far-field measurements cannot disentangle BPVE from contact- and interface-induced photothermoelectric signals. Here we develop cryogenic, polarization-sensitive terahertz microscopy with deep-subwavelength resolution and apply it to dual-gated AB bilayer graphene. Near resonance between the band gap and terahertz photon energy, we observe enhanced optical conductivity and spatially resolved BPVE. Polarization-dependent measurements reveal a characteristic twofold angular symmetry and a distinct shift-vector direction, both governed by crystalline symmetries and quantum geometry. The evolution of BPVE with carrier density and temperature provides a sensitive probe of the fine electronic structure of bilayer graphene, revealing signatures of trigonal warping and electron-hole asymmetry. Agreement with theory demonstrates that the terahertz nanoscopy of BPVE established here offers an optical lens into the interplay between band and quantum geometry in quantum materials.

</details>


### [294] [Chirality-induced spin selectivity as a nonequilibrium effect: a unified test of competing mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05710)
*Swagata Acharya,Mark van Schilfgaarde*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一个精确的选择定则（selection rule），界定手性诱导自旋选择性（Chirality-Induced Spin Selectivity, CISS）成立的基本条件：在非磁性、时间反演不变（time-reversal-invariant）导体中，所有可测量的 CISS 信号均为时间反演奇（time-reversal odd），并在近平衡时消失，故结构手性本身并不充分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CISS 效应指无磁场条件下非磁性手性系统对电子自旋的极化，其起源已悬置二十年而未获定论。本文旨在厘清该效应的基本成立条件及其微观机制。

Method: 在从相干经典驱动到完全非马尔可夫量子浴（non-Markovian quantum baths）四个非平衡输运（nonequilibrium transport）层级上，评估主流理论机制，并以机器精度（machine precision）验证所提出的选择定则。

Result: 相干手性振动可产生显著共线极化（0.5–6 THz 范围内最高约 10%），且随手性反转；非相干振动产生低于 1% 的极化，非马尔可夫浴记忆进一步抑制信号。机制上，手性几何先通过累积轨道角动量（orbital angular momentum）使电子运动具有手性，再由自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling, SOC）将其转化为自旋。极化随分子长度增长后饱和，与 DNA 与多肽（peptides）报道趋势一致；反向驱动可将衰减自旋转化为手性锁定的电流脉冲（inverse-CISS）。

Conclusion: CISS 兼具自旋极化器与自旋滤波器（spin filter）功能，后者弱一个数量级。系统被驱动后，极化幅度与有效自旋轨道耦合（effective SOC）成标度关系，使重原子与弯曲轻原子骨架在等效有效 SOC 下不可区分，从而可刻画几何、驱动场与长度如何区分这些路径。

Abstract: For two decades, the origin of Chirality-Induced Spin Selectivity (CISS), the spin polarization of electrons by nonmagnetic chiral systems without magnetic fields, has remained unsettled. Here, we establish a single exact rule defining the fundamental conditions for the effect: in nonmagnetic, time-reversal-invariant conductors, all measurable CISS signals are time-reversal odd and vanish near equilibrium, proving that structural chirality alone is insufficient. By evaluating leading theoretical mechanisms across four levels of nonequilibrium transport, from coherent classical driving to fully non-Markovian quantum baths, we verify this selection rule to machine precision. We show that coherent chiral vibrations generate substantial collinear polarization (up to 10 percent across 0.5-6 THz) that reverses with handedness, whereas incoherent vibrations yield under 1 percent, and non-Markovian bath memory further suppresses the signal. The chiral geometry first makes the electronic motion chiral by accumulating orbital angular momentum; spin-orbit coupling (SOC) then converts that into spin. CISS therefore acts as both a spin polarizer and a spin filter, the latter an order of magnitude weaker. The polarization grows with molecular length and then saturates, matching trends reported for DNA and peptides. Reversing the drive converts decaying spin into a handedness-locked charge-current pulse (inverse-CISS). We find that once the system is driven, the polarization magnitude scales with an effective spin-orbit coupling: making heavy atoms and curved light-atom backbones indistinguishable at equivalent effective SOC. This enables us to chart how geometry, driving field, and length separate those routes.

</details>


### [295] [Gate-tunable large second-order and third-order nonlinear Hall effect in topological insulator BiSbTeSe2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05713)
*Fengyi Guo,Shuai Zhang,Xiubing Li,Zheng Dai,Congcong Li,Yufan Ju,Ziqi Wang,Cheng Bin,Zhe Ying,Fengqi Song*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作报道了双栅（dual-gated）BiSbTeSe2 器件中可栅压调控的二阶与三阶非线性霍尔效应（nonlinear Hall effect, NLHE），其中二阶效率高于既有拓扑绝缘体体系并可稳定保持至室温。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性霍尔效应（NLHE）在下一代电子器件中具有重要应用前景，因此亟需探索并调控强且高度可调（highly tunable）的 NLHE。

Method: 采用双栅（dual-gated）BiSbTeSe2 器件，测量其二阶与三阶 NLHE，并通过标度分析（scaling analysis）判断其微观散射机制来源。

Result: 二阶与三阶 NLHE 均高度栅压可调；二阶 NLHE 产生效率高于此前报道的拓扑绝缘体（topological insulator）体系且可稳定观测至室温，三阶 NLHE 产生效率与既有报道相当。标度分析表明，二阶 NLHE 与斜散射（skew scattering）以及贝里曲率偶极（Berry curvature dipole）或边跳（side jump）贡献相符，三阶 NLHE 与贝里联络极化率张量（Berry connection polarizability tensor）及高阶斜散射贡献相符。

Conclusion: 该研究为基于 NLHE 的器件开发建立了一个有前景的实验平台。

Abstract: The nonlinear Hall effect (NLHE) holds significant promise for next-generation electronic device applications, and it is essential to explore and engineer a strong and highly tunable NLHE. In this work, we report large second-order and third-order NLHEs in dual-gated BiSbTeSe2 devices. Both the second-order and third-order NLHE are highly gate-tunable. The second-order NLHE exhibits a higher generation efficiency than those reported in previous topological insulator systems, and it can be stably observed up to room temperature. Meanwhile, the generation efficiency of the third-order NLHE is comparable to previously reported values. The scaling analysis indicates that the second-order NLHE is consistent with contributions from skew scattering and a term compatible with either the Berry curvature dipole or side jump, whereas the third-order NLHE is consistent with contributions from the Berry connection polarizability tensor and higher-order skew scattering. Our work establishes a promising experimental platform for the development of NLHE-based devices.

</details>


### [296] [Current selects the helicity of a chiral phonon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05714)
*Swagata Acharya,Jerome Jackson,Mark van Schilfgaarde*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种无需光学泵浦的机制：在偏置手性导体中，电流使两种螺旋性（helicity）手性声子的阻尼出现不对称，从而在有限偏压下选择性地占据某一螺旋性，并可在阈值以上形成自维持相干行波；以元素碲为例在准从头算框架下验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性诱导自旋选择性（chirality-induced spin selectivity, CISS）由非磁性手性分子与晶体产生自旋极化电子，特定波矢 $q$ 的手性声子可显著增强该效应，但平衡态下 $+q$ 与 $-q$ 两种螺旋性等量占据且互为时间反演（time-reversal）伙伴，其贡献相互抵消。

Method: 将偏置手性导体中的手性声子视为开放量子系统（open quantum system），包含电子哈密顿量、声子浴与电子-声子耦合，并用速率方程（rate equation）闭合声子动力学；对元素碲采用准从头算（quasi-ab initio）方法，电子哈密顿量由含自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）的顶点修正准粒子自洽 $GW$（vertex-corrected quasiparticle self-consistent $GW$, QSGW）势给出，核位移用机器学习原子间势（machine-learned interatomic potential）建模，电子哈密顿量受核位移的微扰以冻结声子形变势（frozen-phonon deformation potential）近似。

Result: 电流使一种螺旋性的声子阻尼小于另一种，该阻尼不对称性关于电流为奇、在 $q\rightarrow-q$ 下为奇、在反转结构手性的镜面操作下不变，并逐模式等于作用在声子坐标上的电流诱导非保守贝里力（nonconservative Berry force）。速率方程使该不对称性转化为净晶格螺旋性：任意有限偏压下无需阈值即优先占据某一螺旋性，且随电流线性增长（$V\rightarrow0$）；超过阈值后该螺旋性成为自维持相干行波。机器学习势在 $Γ$-A 线上给出有限 $\pm q_z$ 的手性声子模式。

Conclusion: 无需光学泵浦：仅凭偏压即可驱动电流并选择声子螺旋性，反转电流即反转该选择。

Abstract: The emergence of spin-polarized electrons from nonmagnetic chiral molecules and crystals is called Chirality-induced spin selectivity (CISS). A chiral phonon of definite wavevector $q$ can strongly enhance it, but near equilibrium the two helicities $+q$ and $-q$ are equally populated: they are time-reversal partners and their contributions cancel. We show that in a biased chiral conductor the current causes the phonon of one helicity to damp less than the other. The damping asymmetry is odd in the current, odd under $q\rightarrow-q$, invariant under a mirror that reverses the structural handedness, and equal, mode for mode, to the current-induced nonconservative Berry force on the phonon coordinate. Closing the phonon kinetics with a rate equation turns the asymmetry into a net lattice helicity: at any finite bias one helicity is preferentially populated, with no threshold, growing linearly with the current as $V\rightarrow0$; above a threshold that helicity becomes a self-sustained coherent travelling wave. We demonstrate this effect in elemental tellurium, treated as an open quantum system (electronic Hamiltonian, phonon bath, and electron-phonon coupling) within a quasi-ab initio framework. The electronic Hamiltonian is supplied by a vertex-corrected quasiparticle self-consistent $GW$ (QSGW) potential with spin-orbit coupling; nuclear displacements are modeled with a machine-learned interatomic potential, which yields chiral phonon modes at finite $\pm q_z$ on the $Γ$-A line. The perturbation to the electronic Hamiltonian from a nuclear displacement is approximated by a frozen-phonon deformation potential. No optical pump is needed: a bias alone drives the current and selects the phonon helicity; reversing the current reverses the selection.

</details>


### [297] [Mirror-Constrained Optical Hall Rectification in Asymmetric Gold Gratings:\\ Edelstein Conversion and a Phase-Seeded Kerr Four-Wave Mixing Instability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05761)
*Michael N. Leuenberger,Richard M. Osgood*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文针对一维非对称 Au 光栅中出现横向光学整流（transverse optical rectification）这一在理想镜面对称下"看似禁止"的现象，构建对称性完备的有效模型，并提出由四波混频、克尔不稳定性与弱背散射共同实现的动力学镜像奇因子 $\Lambda_{dyn}$ 生成机制，从而解释横向直流电流的来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一维非对称 Au 光栅中被报道存在横向光学整流，但理想轮廓 $h(x)$ 仍保留 $xz$ 镜面对称 $M:y\to-y$；在该对称下 $J_y$ 为奇而 $p$ 偏振双线性项 $|E_x|^2$、$|E_z|^2$、$\mathrm{Re}(E_xE_z^*)$ 均为偶，因此横向直流电流必须依赖一个额外的镜面奇因子 $\Lambda$。论文旨在给出该因子的对称性完备描述与动力学实现。

Method: 构建对称性完备的有效模型，综合与原点无关的胞不变量 $\mathcal{A}=\mathrm{Im}(h_1^2h_2^*)$、Drude–Edelstein 自旋极化以及自旋–轨道转换。针对有限宽度器件提出 $\Lambda$ 的动力学实现：以 $k_y\simeq0$ 的泵浦型等离激元模式，经同一三阶非线性（产生自克尔与交叉克尔频移）产生镜像成对的横向表面等离激元极化激元准简正模 $(K_x,\pm q_y)$；四波混频负责成对生成并锁定相位和，弱相干背散射使相对驱动相位可观测，来自入射 $k_y$、光斑位移、边界或无序的小相位差触发克尔方向不稳定性并确定性选择符号。论文进一步给出完整三模方程、仅泵浦与饱和态的 Bogoliubov 问题、不完美叉式分岔（imperfect pitchfork）约化，以及经验证的归一化数值实现。

Result: 群体布居失衡提供动力学镜像奇因子 $\Lambda_{dyn}=\lambda_\eta\eta$，进入结构依赖的类侧跳（side-jump-like）通道 $\mathcal{A}\Lambda\langle j_xs_y\rangle_t$；对于中心对称光栅，该通道因 $\mathcal{A}=0$ 而消失。归一化方程呈现稳健的超临界叉式分岔（supercritical pitchfork），临界指数约为 0.5（one-half），并伴随相位反转的分支选择。

Conclusion: 横向光学整流可在镜面对称理想轮廓下出现，前提是存在额外的镜面奇因子；该因子可由三阶非线性等离激元动力学中的克尔方向不稳定性自发且确定性地产生，其强度由居群失衡 $\Lambda_{dyn}=\lambda_\eta\eta$ 刻画，并与胞不变量 $\mathcal{A}$ 相乘构成唯一允许的横向直流通道，对中心对称光栅自动归零。

Abstract: Transverse optical rectification has been reported in a one-dimensional asymmetric Au grating even though an ideal profile $h(x)$ retains the $xz$ mirror $\Me:y\to-y$. Because $J_y$ is odd under $\Me$, whereas the $p$-polarized bilinears $|E_x|^2$, $|E_z|^2$, and $\mathrm{Re}(E_xE_z^*)$ are even, the transverse dc current requires a distinct mirror-odd factor $\Lamy$. We formulate a symmetry-complete effective model combining the origin-independent cell invariant $\Astr=\mathrm{Im}(h_1^2h_2^*)$, Drude--Edelstein spin polarization, and spin--orbit conversion. We then propose a dynamical realization of $\Lamy$ for a finite-width device. A driven pump-like plasmonic mode at $k_y\simeq0$ generates a mirror pair of transverse surface-plasmon-polariton quasinormal modes $(K_x,\pm q_y)$ through the same third-order susceptibility that produces self- and cross-Kerr shifts. Four-wave mixing creates the pair and locks its phase sum but, by itself, cannot change the pair-population difference. Weak coherent backscattering makes the relative drive phase observable; a small phase difference from incident $k_y$, spot displacement, edges, or disorder then unfolds a Kerr directional instability and deterministically selects its sign. The resulting population imbalance supplies the dynamical mirror-odd factor $\Ldyn=λ_ηη$ in the structure-dependent $\Astr\Lamy\langle j_xs_y\rangle_t$ side-jump-like channel, which vanishes for the centered symmetric grating because $\Astr=0$. We give the complete three-mode equations, the pump-only and saturated-state Bogoliubov problems, the imperfect-pitchfork reduction, and a validated normalized numerical realization. The normalized equations exhibit a robust supercritical pitchfork with an approximately one-half critical exponent and phase-reversed branch selection.

</details>


### [298] [Ferromagnetic Bloch Line as a Bead on a String](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05859)
*Jake Hoffmann,Oleg Tchernyshyov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文提出薄膜铁磁体中布洛赫线（Bloch line）的简化模型，将其中子结构——半子（meron）视为沿线性畴壁滑动、带电荷无质量的粒子，类比“弦上的陀螺珠”。模型预测与数值模拟结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于为强易轴与中等面内各向异性薄膜铁磁体中的布洛赫线建立简单且物理直观的模型，以统一理解半子的陀螺运动、畴壁隔离以及外场与自旋转移矩的作用方式。

Method: 将半子建模为在背景磁场中沿线性畴壁滑动的带电、无质量粒子（陀螺珠），其陀螺行为动态隔离半子两侧的壁段，从而把布洛赫线描述为在移动边界处相接的两根弦；并分析垂直/面内磁场与自旋转移矩（spin-transfer torque）通过直接作用于布洛赫线或整体驱动畴壁两种途径产生影响。

Result: 半子在交接处夹持畴壁，对沿壁传播的低幅自旋波（spin waves）实现完全反射；模型对低幅自旋波反射及外场、自旋转移矩响应的预测与数值模拟（numerical simulations）结果相符。

Conclusion: 该“弦上陀螺珠”图像为布洛赫线提供了一个简洁自洽的描述框架，能统一解释半子的动态隔离效应、自旋波完全反射以及磁场与自旋转移矩的双重作用机制，并得到数值模拟验证。

Abstract: We present a simple model of a Bloch line in a thin-film ferromagnet with strong easy-axis and intermediate in-plane anisotropies. The meron is represented as a charged, massless particle that slides along the linear domain wall in a background magnetic field- a gyroscopic bead on a string. Its gyroscopic behavior dynamically isolates the wall segments on either side of the meron, motivating the model for a Bloch line as two strings joined at a moving boundary. The meron clamps the wall at this junction, completely reflecting low-amplitude spin waves that propagate along it. Out-of-plane and in-plane magnetic fields and spin-transfer torques then act in two ways: by exerting a force on the Bloch line itself, or by driving the wall as a whole. Predictions of this model are in agreement with numerical simulations.

</details>


### [299] [Giant Conductance Enhancement in Graphene Nanoscrolls via Interlayer-Coupled States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05881)
*Jia-Cheng Li,Ying-Je Lee,Yu An Cheng,Yu-Hsiang Fu,Xuan-Fu Huang,Yu-Jie Zhong,Ion Cosma Fulga,Carmine Ortix,Ching-Hao Chang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 理论研究表明，将石墨烯纳米带卷曲为1.0–1.2圈的少圈数纳米卷，可使纵向电导提升近二十倍；该增强源于开边界间的层间跃迁，且电导随圈数增加呈反比下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 石墨烯纳米卷（graphene nanoscrolls, GNSs）形成独特的径向超晶格（radial superlattice），其几何结构有望带来不同于平面石墨烯的输运行为，因此需要从理论上揭示卷曲几何对电子输运的影响。

Method: 采用理论方法，将石墨烯纳米带（graphene nanoribbon）卷曲为少圈数（1.0–1.2圈）的纳米卷，并分析其层间跃迁（interlayer hopping）与边界态对电导的作用机制。

Result: 卷曲使纵向电导（longitudinal conductance）出现巨大增强，幅度达一个数量级（接近二十倍）；开边界间的层间跃迁将局域边缘态（localized edge states）转化为类一维导电通道（one-dimensional-like conducting channels），并引起显著的费米能移（Fermi energy shift）；电导与圈数呈反比标度关系。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了石墨烯纳米卷作为几何驱动量子输运（geometry-driven quantum transport）平台的地位，并为纳米结构电子态（nanoarchitectural electronic states）的可调控工程提供了途径。

Abstract: Graphene nanoscrolls form a unique radial superlattice whose geometry enables transport behaviors distinct from their flat counterparts. We theoretically demonstrate that rolling a graphene nanoribbon into a few-turn nanoscroll (1.0-1.2 turns) triggers a giant enhancement in longitudinal conductance by an order of magnitude (nearly twentyfold). We show that this effect stems from interlayer hopping between open boundaries, which not only transforms localized edge states into one-dimensional-like conducting channels and also induces a significant Fermi energy shift. Our results reveal an inverse scaling of conductance with turn number, establishing GNSs as a platform for geometry-driven quantum transport and offering a tunable approach to engineering nanoarchitectural electronic states.

</details>


### [300] [Intrinsic spin-orbit coupling in graphene stacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05905)
*Guillermo Parra-Martinez,Alejandro Jimeno-Pozo,Ady Stern,Jose Angel Silva-Guillén,Francisco Guinea*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文基于包含碳全部价轨道与原子内自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling, SOC）的模型，计算石墨烯多层堆叠中的内禀自旋轨道耦合，发现其主导项由Rashba型自旋翻转过程与层间跃迁结合而成，并显著改变有限菱形堆叠的相图与费米面及弱磁场下的磁化强度密度依赖，表明SOC在多层石墨体系中不可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去对单层石墨烯中自旋轨道耦合的研究表明其电子结构几乎不受影响，因此SOC在多数学术讨论中常被忽视，这一忽略也延伸至其他石墨类体系。作者认为该假设在多层体系中未必成立，故需重新评估SOC在多层石墨结构中的作用。

Method: 构建包含碳原子全部价轨道及原子内自旋轨道耦合的模型，据此计算石墨烯多层堆叠中的内禀自旋轨道耦合，并识别多层体系中领先的自旋轨道项，分析其对有限菱形（rhombohedral）堆叠的影响。

Result: 多层石墨烯中领先的自旋轨道项由Rashba型自旋翻转过程与层间跃迁（interlayer hopping）组合而成。该内禀项会改变有限菱形堆叠的相图（phase diagram）与费米面（Fermi surfaces），并在弱磁场下导致磁化强度（magnetization）呈现明显的密度依赖特性。

Conclusion: 与单层情形不同，自旋轨道耦合在多层石墨体系中具有重要物理后果，在研究其物理性质时不应被忽略。

Abstract: The effect of spin-orbit coupling (SOC) in monolayer graphene has been thoroughly studied in the past. Its impact is commonly disregarded since the electronic structure of graphene is almost unaffected. Consequently, SOC has also been ignored in other graphitic systems. In this work, we calculate the intrinsic SOC in stacks of graphene layers from a model that includes all the valence orbitals of carbon as well as the intra-atomic SOC.The leading spin-orbit term in a graphene multilayer combines a Rashba-like spin-flip process with interlayer hopping. We find that this intrinsic term modifies the phase diagram and Fermi surfaces of finite rhombohedral stacks, and it leads to a distinct density-dependence of the magnetization in weak magnetic fields. This Letter shows that, unlike in the single-layer case, SOC has important consequences in multilayer graphitic systems and it should not be neglected when studying their physical properties.

</details>


### [301] [Stability and current-driven dynamics of mixed-topology bimeron clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05917)
*Philippe Heymes,Thomas L. Schmidt,Vladyslav M. Kuchkin*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究双半子（bimeron，即面内斯格明子）及其由混合拓扑指数态构成的团簇，揭示此类团簇可用于调控斯格明子霍尔角（skyrmion Hall angle），并系统分析其稳定性、动力学与速度约束规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 斯格明子霍尔角依赖于体系的总拓扑荷（topological charge），而由混合拓扑指数态组成的双半子团簇为调控该角度提供了灵活手段，因此需要明确其稳定性与动力学行为。

Method: 采用测地线微动弹性带方法（geodesic nudged elastic band method）分析最简双半子-反双半子对（bimeron-antibimeron pair）的稳定性；随后数值模拟面内与垂直电流（分别对应 Zhang-Li 机制与 Slonczewski 机制）下团簇的动力学；最后用 Thiele 方法分析多种双半子团簇的动力学。

Result: 双半子对的稳定性由三种能量势垒相当（comparable energy barriers）的机制决定：双半子分离（separation）、互换（interchange）与湮灭（annihilation），该结果为更复杂团簇的稳定性给出上界。Thiele 分析表明，各类双半子团簇的速度始终位于一条特定椭圆上，其参数完全由模型哈密顿量（model Hamiltonian）决定。

Conclusion: 双半子团簇可有效调控斯格明子霍尔角，其稳定性上限由双半子对的三种竞争机制决定，而速度受限于由模型哈密顿量唯一确定的椭圆，为拓扑荷混合态的可控输运提供理论依据。

Abstract: In this work, we study bimerons (in-plane skyrmions) and their clusters composed of states with mixed topological indices. These clusters provide flexibility in controlling the skyrmion Hall angle, which depends on the cluster's total topological charge. First, using the simplest such cluster, a bimeron-antibimeron pair, we examine its stability with the geodesic nudged elastic band method and identify three mechanisms -- bimeron separation, interchange, and annihilation -- with comparable energy barriers that determine the pair's overall stability. This provides an upper bound for the stability of more sophisticated clusters of bimerons and antibimerons. Then, we numerically study the clusters' dynamics for currents applied in-plane and perpendicular to the plane, corresponding to the Zhang-Li and Slonczewski mechanisms. Finally, we analyze the dynamics of a wide variety of bimeron clusters within the Thiele approach and show that their velocities always lie on a specific ellipse whose parameters are fully governed by the model Hamiltonian.

</details>


### [302] [Beyond the Flat-Band Paradigm: Open Orbits as a Route to Correlated States in Dispersive Moiré Minibands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06110)
*Zihao Wang,Pablo M. Perez-Piskunow,Calvin Pei Yu Wong,Matthew Holwill,Hao Sun,Wei Fu,Junxiong Hu,Zheng Yuntian,Zhang Xuanrui,T. Taniguchi,K. Watanabe,A. Ariando,Geliang Yu,Li Lin,Kuan Eng Johnson Goh,Stephan Roche,Jeil Jung,Kostya S. Novoselov,Nicolas Leconte*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种磁场诱导的强关联机制：在单层石墨烯/六方氮化硼（monolayer graphene on hexagonal boron nitride）中高度色散的能带里，于第二莫尔微带（second moiré miniband）的低场经典—量子过渡区，通过莫尔势与弱磁场的协同作用调制电子动力学，观察到分数斜率输运与相互作用驱动的不可压缩性，并将其描述为"初现的 FCI 行为"（incipient FCI behavior）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数量子陈绝缘体（fractional Chern insulators, FCI）的实现此前主要依赖复杂双层或多层莫尔体系中具有理想量子几何（quantum geometry）的孤立平带（flat band）。这种相能否在真正色散（dispersive）的能带中出现、以及其机制为何，仍是未解问题。本文旨在寻找一条不依赖平带场景的替代路径。

Method: 研究对象为单层石墨烯/六方氮化硼体系。作者聚焦第二莫尔微带的低场经典—量子过渡区（classical-to-quantum crossover），分析莫尔势与弱磁场（B < 1.5 T）共同作用下的电子动力学：鞍点范霍夫奇点（saddle-point van Hove singularities）产生扩展开放轨道，抑制载流子传播；弱磁场解除能谷简并（valley degeneracy），三角翘曲（trigonal warping）重新分布贝里曲率（Berry curvature）。实验上采用高分辨率磁输运与变温测量。

Result: 输运测量显示分数斜率（fractional-slope）特征，以及相互作用驱动不可压缩性（interaction-driven incompressibility）的信号。但霍尔电导（Hall conductivity）未完全量子化、纵向电阻仍为有限值，因此作者未宣称已建立完整的 FCI 相。

Conclusion: 作者将这些观测定义为"初现的 FCI 行为"（incipient FCI behavior），即与分数陈绝缘态发展相符的输运特征，而非由完全霍尔量子化与纵向电阻消失所确证的成熟 FCI 相。结果提示存在一条由费米学（fermiology）驱动、在经典—量子过渡区于色散能带中实现关联拓扑态的途径，区别于传统平带方案。

Abstract: The realization of fractional Chern insulators has largely relied on isolated flat bands in complex bilayer or multilayer moiré systems with ideal quantum geometry. Whether such phases can emerge in genuinely dispersive bands, and by what mechanism, remains an open question. Here, we propose a mechanism for magnetic-field-induced strong correlations in the highly dispersive bands of monolayer graphene on hexagonal boron nitride. In the low-field classical-to-quantum crossover regime of the second moiré miniband, we show that the interplay of the moiré potential and weak magnetic fields (B < 1.5 T) profoundly alters electron dynamics. Saddle-point van Hove singularities produce extended open orbits that strongly suppress carrier propagation, potentially favoring a regime in which Coulomb interactions become comparatively more important, while weak magnetic fields lift valley degeneracy and trigonal warping redistributes Berry curvature. High-resolution magnetotransport and temperature-dependent measurements reveal fractional-slope transport features and signatures of interaction-driven incompressibility. Because the Hall conductivity is not fully quantized and the longitudinal resistance remains finite, we describe these observations as incipient FCI behavior: transport signatures consistent with a developing fractional Chern insulating state, without claiming a fully developed FCI phase established by complete Hall quantization and vanishing longitudinal resistance. Our results suggest a possible fermiology-driven route toward correlated topological states in dispersive bands in the classical-to-quantum crossover, distinct from the conventional flat-band scenario.

</details>


### [303] [An \textit{ab initio} based compact analytical formula for inelastic x-ray scattering from spatially localized excitons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06131)
*Joris Sturm,Duc Anh Truong,Andrei Benediktovitch,Nina Rohringer,Andreas Knorr$^{1}$*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出一个紧凑模型（compact model）用于描述空间局域激子（spatially localized excitons）的非弹性 X 射线散射（inelastic x-ray scattering），并以氟化锂（LiF）为对象结合解析理论与第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确描述空间局域激子的非弹性 X 射线散射谱需要兼顾解析处理与电子结构细节，而单粒子波函数（single-particle wave functions）的选取对谱形的影响尚需明确评估。

Method: 将解析理论（analytical theory）与第一性原理计算相结合，构建紧凑模型，并应用于 LiF；同时比较两种不同的单粒子波函数方案以计算散射谱。

Result: 两种波函数方案所得谱与实验数据在定性上均符合良好，但结果表明，要获得定性结果，对波函数的详细描述至关重要。

Conclusion: 该紧凑模型可为局域激子的非弹性 X 射线散射提供可行描述，并强调波函数细节在光谱模拟中的关键作用。

Abstract: In this contribution we provide a compact model for the description of inelastic x-ray scattering of spatially localized excitons. We combine our analytical theory with first principles calculations and apply it to the material lithium fluoride (LiF). A comparison of two different approaches for the underlying single-particle wave functions to calculate the spectrum yield a qualitatively good agreement with experimental data but also shows that a detailed description of the wave functions is essential to obtain qualitative results.

</details>


### [304] [Phenomenology of reactive and dissipative nutation in antiferromagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06145)
*Erlend Syljuåsen,Hans Gløckner Giil,Arne Brataas*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文建立了多子晶格磁体中均匀磁化动力学的运动方程，重点引入惯性阻尼力矩（inertial damping torque），并推导了双亚晶格单轴反铁磁体在易轴静磁场下的动力学磁化率与近似本征频率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有磁化动力学理论对多子晶格磁体中惯性阻尼（inertial damping）的描述尚不完整，尤其是子晶格内与子晶格间贡献的区分及其对各场依赖平衡相的影响需要系统化处理。

Method: 在多子晶格磁体的均匀磁化运动方程中同时纳入子晶格内（intra-sublattice）与子晶格间（intersublattice）的吉尔伯特阻尼（Gilbert damping）、反应性惯性（reactive inertia）与惯性阻尼贡献；针对处于易轴静磁场中的双亚晶格单轴反铁磁体（two-sublattice uniaxial antiferromagnet），推导动力学磁化率及跨各场依赖平衡相的近似本征频率。

Result: 给出了覆盖全部场依赖平衡相的动力学磁化率（dynamical magnetic susceptibilities）与近似本征频率表达式，并揭示惯性阻尼对章动模式（nutational modes）弛豫过程的影响。

Conclusion: 惯性阻尼显著调控章动模式的弛豫行为，论文据此提出可用于确定各动力学系数（dynamical coefficients）的实验方案。

Abstract: We formulate equations of motion for uniform magnetization dynamics in multisublattice magnets, with emphasis on a recently proposed inertial damping torque. We include intra- and intersublattice contributions to Gilbert damping, reactive inertia, and inertial damping. For a two-sublattice uniaxial antiferromagnet in an easy-axis static magnetic field, we derive the dynamical magnetic susceptibilities and approximate eigenfrequencies across all field-dependent equilibrium phases. In particular, we show how inertial damping affects the relaxation of the nutational modes and propose an experimental protocol to determine the dynamical coefficients.

</details>


### [305] [The future of 3D NAND flash technology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06363)
*Prasanna Venkatesan,Taeyoung Song,Gaurav Thareja,Luca Larcher,Andrea Padovani,Cristian Zambelli,Shimeng Yu,Suman Datta,Tajana Rosing,Priyankka Ravikumar,Biswajit Ray,Raisul Islam,Wanki Kim,Daewon Ha,Asif Khan*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该展望（Perspective）指出，3D NAND 闪存（3D NAND flash）的继续微缩已不再主要受制于工艺集成或光刻，而受限于电荷存储（charge storage）相关的物理机制；因此需要面向应用（application-specific）的协同优化，而非单一器件路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
