Motivation: 3D NAND 已成为 AI 时代基础性非易失存储（non-volatile storage）平台，支撑从模型训练到大规模推理与冷数据归档的工作负载。随着路线图指向千层堆叠（kilolayer stacks）与单芯片数十万亿器件，横向电荷迁移（lateral charge migration）、静电耦合（electrostatic coupling）、读干扰（read disturb）与输运限制（transport limitations）等机制共同进入裕度受限（margin-limited）区间，其集体相互作用逐代压缩工作裕度（operating margins）。

Method: 采用展望（Perspective）形式的分析，梳理上述汇聚性瓶颈（converging bottlenecks），并主张以应用特定的协同优化（application-specific co-optimization）替代单一化的器件路线图（monolithic device roadmap）。分析视角将传统电荷俘获闪存（charge-trap flash）、铁电存储（ferroelectric storage）、替代沟道材料（alternative channel materials）与系统级集成（system-level integration）视为面向不同层级数据计算（data-centric computing）的互补方案。

Result: 论文未给出实验或量化结果，其核心论断为：NAND 微缩的约束正由工艺与光刻转向电荷存储物理，且是多种机制的协同作用而非单一机制在压缩工作裕度。

Conclusion: 维持 NAND 微缩需转向应用特定的协同优化框架，在该框架下多种存储与集成技术作为互补方案，分别服务于以数据为中心计算的不同层级。

Abstract: 3D NAND flash has become the foundational non-volatile storage platform of the AI era, underpinning workloads from model training to large-scale inference and cold data archival. With roadmaps now targeting kilolayer stacks and tens of trillions of devices per die, scaling is no longer governed primarily by process integration or lithography. Instead, it is increasingly constrained by the physics of charge storage itself: lateral charge migration, electrostatic coupling, read disturb, and transport limitations are entering a margin-limited regime. In this regime, their collective interaction, not any single mechanism, compresses operating margins with each generation. In this Perspective, we examine these converging bottlenecks and argue that sustaining NAND scaling will require application-specific co-optimization rather than a monolithic device roadmap. In this framework, conventional charge-trap flash, ferroelectric storage, alternative channel materials, and system-level integration are best viewed as complementary solutions targeted to distinct tiers of data-centric computing.

</details>


### [306] [Exact vortex decomposition of the shift current](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06449)
*Matias Castro Schnaidt,Felipe Perez Riffo,Juan M. Florez,Eric Suarez Morell*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文将 shift current（移位电流）的 shift vector 视为布里渊环面上的奇异场，其旋度由带间 Berry curvature（贝里曲率）与跃迁偶极零点处的量子化点电荷两个源构成，并据此给出移位电导率在任意频率下的精确分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是：移位电流中是否存在某种整数不变量，以及 Chern number（陈数）是否决定其符号。作者希望厘清整数不变量与光电流符号之间的真实对应关系，而非默认将其归于陈数。

Method: 通过求 shift vector 的旋度，识别出带间贝里曲率与跃迁偶极零点处量子化点电荷两类源，并反解该关系得到移位电导率的精确分解（一个与整数电荷线性的扇区加三个连续扇区）。以 graphene-Haldane bilayer（石墨烯-哈丹模型双层）为测试平台，在固定能隙下扫描带间陈数取五个值以分离其作用，并以时间反演对称的 biased AB bilayer（偏压 AB 双层）进行检验。

Result: 当电荷位于带边且存在 trigonal warping（三角翘曲）时，整数扇区单独即可解释带边峰。相关整数并非陈数，而是该单个点电荷的绕数（winding）；其符号与谷依赖分布共同决定光电流符号。在双层体系中，拓扑相变处的符号反转遵循该绕数变化，而拓扑不变量仅在与同一能隙闭合同时发生时随之改变。

Conclusion: 带边电荷无需任何拓扑不变量，只要求两个带边子晶格不被 nearest-neighbour hopping（最近邻跃迁）连接；三角翘曲使其可被观测。该结果为移位电流中的整数结构与光电流符号起源提供了不同于陈数主导图像的判据。

Abstract: Does any integer survive in the shift current, and does the Chern number control its sign? We show that the shift vector is a singular field on the Brillouin torus. Its curl has two sources: the interband Berry curvature and quantised point charges at the zeros of the transition dipole. Inverting this relation gives an exact decomposition of the shift conductivity, valid at every frequency, into one sector linear in the integer charges and three continuous sectors. As a testing ground, we take a graphene-Haldane bilayer: a model that lets us sweep the interband Chern number through five values at fixed gap to isolate its role. We also test the formalism against the time-reversal-symmetric biased AB bilayer. Whenever a charge sits at the band edge, and trigonal warping is present, the integer sector alone accounts for the band-edge peak. The relevant integer is not the Chern number but the winding of that single charge. Its sign, combined with a valley-dependent profile, sets the sign of the photocurrent. In these bilayers, sign reversals across topological transitions follow this winding; the invariant changes with it only when the same gap closing does both. A band-edge charge needs no invariant. It only requires that the two band-edge sublattices not be connected by the nearest-neighbour hopping, and trigonal warping makes it visible.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [307] [Latent Score-Based Bayesian Cramér-Rao Bound Estimation for High-Dimensional Imaging Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03956)
*Evan Scope Crafts,Thomas Wynn,Seonyeong Park,Mark Anastasio,Umberto Villa*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一种数据驱动框架，在预训练变分自编码器（variational autoencoder, VAE）的隐空间中估计贝叶斯克拉美-罗界（Bayesian Cramér-Rao bound, CRB），并借助新型 Bochner 空间切片分数匹配（Bochner-space sliced score matching）学习隐先验分数，实现百万维 qPACT 成像问题的稳定、无伪影 CRB 估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维成像系统中，先验复杂且解析不可处理，直接计算贝叶斯 CRB 需要对先验分数（prior score）建模并在极高维下构造与求逆贝叶斯费舍尔信息矩阵（Bayesian Fisher information matrix），因而极具挑战。

Method: 两步策略：一是将逆问题重述于预训练 VAE 的隐空间以大幅降维并保留图像空间结构，在隐空间构造贝叶斯 CRB 后通过变量变换公式（change-of-variables formula）映射回原始参数空间；二是引入在赋予 $H^1(Ω)$ Sobolev 范数的 Bochner 空间中定义的切片分数匹配目标，用于学习隐先验分数。

Result: 在参数超过一百万的定量光声计算断层成像（quantitative photoacoustic computed tomography, qPACT）乳腺成像问题上验证，使用源自 Stable Diffusion 的基础模型自编码器；方法给出稳定、无伪影的贝叶斯 CRB 估计，反映所学先验的高度非高斯结构，并揭示先验对竞争性 qPACT 设计方案相对性能的显著影响。

Conclusion: 该框架可在解析不可处理先验下实现高维成像系统的可行贝叶斯 CRB 估计；所提 Bochner 空间切片分数匹配与标准切片分数匹配一致，但通过惩罚分数空间梯度误差抑制高频伪影，从而提升 CRB 估计质量。

Abstract: We propose a data-driven framework for estimating the Bayesian Cramér-Rao bound (CRB) in high-dimensional imaging systems with complex, analytically intractable priors. Direct CRB computation is challenging in this setting due to the need to model the prior score and to form and invert the Bayesian Fisher information matrix in very high dimensions. To address these issues, we first reformulate the inverse problem in the latent space of a pre-trained variational autoencoder, thereby dramatically reducing the dimensionality of the bound estimation problem while preserving the spatial structure of the images. The Bayesian CRB is formed in this latent space and mapped back to the native parameter space using a change-of-variables formula. Second, to learn the latent prior score, we introduce a new sliced score matching objective defined in a Bochner space endowed with an $H^1(Ω)$ Sobolev norm in space. This "Bochner-space sliced score matching" objective is consistent with standard sliced score matching, but penalizes errors in the spatial gradients of the score, suppressing high-frequency artifacts that otherwise contaminate the resulting CRB estimates. We validate the approach on a stylized quantitative photoacoustic computed tomography (qPACT) breast imaging problem with over one million unknown parameters, using a foundation-model autoencoder derived from Stable Diffusion. The proposed method yields stable, artifact-free Bayesian CRB estimates that reflect the highly non-Gaussian structure of the learned prior and reveal the substantial impact of the prior on the relative performance of competing qPACT design schemes.

</details>


### [308] [A Solvable Model of Adaptive Learning Rate Rescaling: Acceleration, Stability & Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06701)
*Itay Lavie,Clarissa Lauditi,Cengiz Pehlevan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文通过随机特征模型（random-feature model）与幂律教师/数据协方差，分离并刻画了归一化梯度更新（normalized SGD）中"更新幅度与梯度范数解耦"这一机制，建立动态平均场理论（dynamical mean-field theory, DMFT），揭示其加速、失稳与资源缩放规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代优化器普遍采用将更新幅度与原始梯度范数解耦的设计原则，但该机制对学习曲线（learning-curve）与资源缩放（resource scaling）的影响尚不清晰，需要被孤立地研究。

Method: 在具有幂律教师与数据协方差的随机特征模型中研究归一化 SGD；推导描述损失对训练时间、模型宽度与批量大小联合依赖的动态平均场理论（DMFT）；并在 CIFAR-5M 上做线性化 ResNet 实验验证。

Result: 固定范数更新引入了随梯度收缩而增大的有效学习率，初期加速 SGD，将幂律指数由 r_SGD<1 映射为 2r_SGD/(1-r_SGD)，在 r_SGD=1 时呈指数收敛、r_SGD>1 时形式有限时间收敛；但有限步长下该反馈最终破坏加速并导致边缘稳定性（marginal stability）；晚期理论给出宽度受限、随机稳定性边缘（EoSS）与确定性稳定性边缘（EoS）三个区间。线性化 ResNet 实验支持预测的加速、失效与资源缩放趋势。

Conclusion: 该工作将归一化诱导的加速、EoS 效应以及宽度—批量分配统一于一个可解理论框架中，并量化了在各区间内增大批量或宽度能否以更少优化步数补偿每步额外算力。

Abstract: A recurring design principle in modern optimizers is to decouple update magnitude from the raw gradient norm, yet its consequences for learning-curve and resource scaling remain unclear. We isolate this mechanism by studying normalized SGD in a random-feature model with power-law teacher and data covariance. Fixed-norm updates induce an effective learning rate that grows as gradients shrink. We derive a dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT) describing the joint dependence of the loss on training time, model width and batch size. Normalization initially accelerates SGD, mapping the power-law exponent $r_{\rm SGD}<1$ to $2r_{\rm SGD}/(1-r_{\rm SGD})$, with exponential convergence at $r_{\rm SGD}=1$ and formal finite-time convergence for $r_{\rm SGD}>1$. At finite step size, however, the same feedback ultimately breaks the acceleration and leads to marginal stability. The late-time theory yields width-limited, edge-of-stochastic-stability (EoSS), and deterministic edge-of-stability (EoS) regimes. These phases determine when larger batches or wider models reduce serial training time at comparable compute. We quantify in which of these phases increased batch size or width can compensate the excess compute use per step by fewer optimization steps to target loss. Linearized ResNet experiments on CIFAR-5M support the predicted acceleration, breakdown, and resource-scaling trends. Together, these results connect normalization-induced acceleration, EoS effects, and width--batch allocation within a solvable theory.

</details>
